盲化标注 Blinded annotation — annotator_1

只根据下面显示的摘要判断。同话题不等于有人研究了这个问题。 中文为机器翻译的辅助阅读版，以英文原文为准——AI 裁判读的是英文原文， 两边看同一份材料，对比才成立。 答案会自动保存在本浏览器里，随时可以关掉再回来；全部做完点右上角 Export CSV 导出文件。
Judge only from the abstracts shown; same topic is not engagement. Answers autosave locally; press Export CSV when done.

标注手册 / Codebook — 点开看判断标准和例子

你看到的是：一个写于 2021 年之前的科研问题，加上最多 8 篇 之后发表的论文摘要。只根据这些摘要判断——不要上网查， 因为 AI 裁判当时看到的就是这几篇，你多用外部知识对比就不公平了。

You see a question written before 2021 and up to 8 abstracts published after it. Judge only from these abstracts — do not look anything up.

⚠️ 最容易犯的错：把「同话题」当成「有人研究了这个问题」。
摘要讲的是同一颗行星、同一个分子，不算。必须真的碰到这个问题要检验的东西， 或者碰到它依赖的那个假设。
The single most common error is confusing topic overlap with engagement.

例子 / Worked examples

问题：「HD 209458 b 大气里检索出的极低含水量，是真实的大气性质，还是检索方法造成的假象？」
摘要：三篇独立重新分析，都得出含水量接近太阳组成。
→ answered + refuted（问题解决了，而且是靠推翻它质疑的那个假设解决的）

同一个问题，但摘要：几篇测量别的热木星含水量的论文。
→ not_addressed（同话题、不同天体，跟这个检验无关）

问题：「不均匀的晨昏线会怎样系统性地影响检索出的丰度？」
摘要：几篇证明这种偏差普遍存在，但没有解决问题里问的那个具体情形。
→ partially_addressed + still_plausible

两个标签是独立的 / The two labels are independent

一个问题可以正因为它的假设被推翻而得到解决—— answered + refuted 是合理组合，不是矛盾。

信心度 / Confidence

1 = 没把握，2 = 一般，3 = 很有把握。该给 1 就给 1，低信心的条目会单独分析， 它们是有用的信息，不是失败。犹豫的地方请在 notes 里写一句你在纠结什么。

item_001 · sys_A
Analyses [2016koa..prop..367C] and [2017hst..prop15127B] of 55 CNC E's atmosphere reach potentially conflicting conclusions regarding CO: one reports "A detection of the extended atmosphere would be a first for super-Earths and could provide constraints on planetary formation and evoluti...", while the other reports "A non-detection of escaping hydrogen, as with 55 Cnc e and HD 97658b, would bring useful constraints on the nature of the planetary atmos...". Under what observational or methodological conditions can these results be reconciled, and which currently untested assumption drives the disagreement?
对55 CNC E大气的分析[2016koa..prop..367C]和[2017hst..prop15127B]在CO方面得出了可能相互矛盾的结论：一项报告称“对扩展大气的探测将是超地球的首次，并可为行星形成和演化提供约束……”，而另一项报告称“如同55 Cnc e和HD 97658b，未探测到逃逸氢将为行星大气性质带来有用约束……”。在什么观测或方法学条件下这些结果可以调和，目前尚未检验的哪个假设驱动了这种分歧？
8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] High-resolution transmission spectroscopy of the super-Earth 55 Cnc e

2021-10-00

The comparison of exoplanets with well-studied Solar System planets can provide answers to the fundamental questions regarding their evolutionary path. The study of exoplanet atmospheres via high-resolution transmission spectroscopy has shown great success, especially for Jupiter-type planets. In the last years, this study also continued with promising investigations towards the atmospheric characterization of terrestrial exoplanets. One possible target for such investigation is the super Earth-sized exoplanet 55 Cnc e, which is one of the most studied terrestrial exoplanets to date. Currently, there are two possible atmospheric scenarios for 55 Cnc e: Either there is a heavy-weighted envelope with a significant amount of water or there is no gaseous envelope at all i.e. a rocky surface similar to Mercury, of our Solar System.Here, we present high-resolution (R = 120 000) transit observation of this planet, acquired with the PEPSI instrument at the Large Binocular Telescope, aiming to search for absorption signatures from various species in the puzzling atmosphere of 55 CNC e.

[1] 超级地球 55 Cnc e 的高分辨率透射光谱

将系外行星与太阳系中研究充分的行星进行比较，有助于回答其演化路径的基本问题。通过高分辨率透射光谱研究系外行星大气已取得巨大成功，尤其是类木星行星。近年来，这一研究也在向类地系外行星的大气特性探索方向取得了有前景的进展。55 Cnc e是一颗超地球大小的系外行星，也是迄今研究最充分的类地系外行星之一，目前对其大气存在两种可能情景：要么存在一个富含水的大质量包层，要么完全没有气体包层，即类似于太阳系中的水星那样的岩石表面。本文展示了利用大双筒望远镜上的PEPSI仪器获得的该行星高分辨率（R = 120 000）凌日观测，旨在搜索55 Cnc e大气中各种组分的吸收特征。

[2] A Detailed Investigation of HD 209458 b HST and JWST Transmission Spectra with SANSAR

2025-08-00

HD 209458 b is the first exoplanet on which an atmosphere was detected. Since then, its atmosphere has been investigated using multiple telescopes and instruments. However, many of its atmospheric constraints remain debatable. While Hubble Space Telescope (HST) observations suggested a highly subsolar metallicity, recent JWST NIRCam observations by Q. Xue et al. constrained a supersolar metallicity with highly subsolar C/O. In this work, we show a detailed investigation of HD 209458 b transmission spectra observations from JWST and HST using SANSAR, a newly developed planetary atmosphere modeling framework, with free, equilibrium chemistry and self-consistent grid retrievals. The overall best-fitting model with free retrievals (<inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>χ</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>red</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula> = 1.21) constrains its metallicity and C/O to be highly subsolar, while equilibrium chemistry and grid retrievals (<inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>χ</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>red</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula> = 1.27 and 1.30, respectively) are consistent with solar values using Space Telescope Imaging Spectrograph (STIS)+WFC3+NIRCam observations. The retrieved abundances of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO<SUB>2</SUB> are almost 3 orders of magnitude lower (highly subsolar) with STIS+WFC3+NIRCam compared to just NIRCam, using free retrievals. NIRCam observations alone also result in misleading constraints on metallicity and C/O, with equilibrium chemistry and grid retrieval. We find that the model choice of varying C/H or O/H to vary the C/O in equilibrium chemistry retrievals leads to different metallicity constraints with NIRCam, but similar constraints with STIS+WFC3+NIRCam. We conclude that NIRCam observations alone can lead to overestimation of abundances for exoplanet atmospheres and, therefore, should be used in combination with UV/optical and near-infrared observations to obtain robust constraints on abundances, C/O, and metallicity. In particular, even though we can detect the CO<SUB>2</SUB> feature with just NIRCam, we cannot constrain its abundances robustly without the optical baseline.

[2] 利用SANSAR对HD 209458 b的HST和JWST透射光谱的详细研究

HD 209458 b 是首个被探测到大气的系外行星。自那以后，其大气已通过多台望远镜和仪器进行了研究。然而，其许多大气约束仍存在争议。尽管哈勃空间望远镜（HST）观测表明其金属丰度远低于太阳，但最近 Q. Xue 等人利用 JWST NIRCam 观测约束其金属丰度高于太阳且 C/O 极低。在本工作中，我们利用新开发的行星大气建模框架 SANSAR，对 JWST 和 HST 的 HD 209458 b 透射光谱观测进行了详细分析，采用自由、平衡化学和自洽网格检索。整体上，自由检索的最佳拟合模型（χ_red^2 = 1.21）约束其金属丰度和 C/O 均远低于太阳，而平衡化学和网格检索（χ_red^2 = 1.27 和 1.30）则与利用 STIS+WFC3+NIRCam 观测得到的太阳值一致。利用自由检索，STIS+WFC3+NIRCam 得到的 H2O 和 CO2 丰度比仅用 NIRCam 时低近3个数量级（极低于太阳）。仅用 NIRCam 观测也会导致金属丰度和 C/O 的误导性约束（平衡化学和网格检索）。我们发现，在平衡化学检索中，通过改变 C/H 或 O/H 来调整 C/O 的模型选择，会导致 NIRCam 下金属丰度约束不同，但在 STIS+WFC3+NIRCam 下约束相似。我们得出结论，仅用 NIRCam 观测可能会高估系外行星大气的丰度，因此应与紫外/光学和近红外观测结合使用，以获得对丰度、C/O 和金属丰度的稳健约束。特别地，尽管仅用 NIRCam 可以探测到 CO2 特征，但没有光学基线无法稳健约束其丰度。

[3] Four HD 209458 b transits through CRIRES+: Detection of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and non-detections of C<SUB>2</SUB>H<SUB>2</SUB>, CH<SUB>4</SUB>, and HCN

2024-10-00

Context. HD 209458 b is one of the most studied exoplanets to date. Despite this, atmospheric characterisation studies yielded inconsistent species detections and abundances. Values reported for the C/O ratio range from ≍0.1 to 1.0. Of particular interest is the simultaneous detection of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and HCN reported by some studies using high-resolution ground-based observations, which would require the atmospheric C/O ratio to be fine-tuned to a narrow interval around 1. HCN has however not been detected from recent space-based observations. Aims. We aim to provide an independent study of HD 209458 b's atmosphere with high-resolution observations, in order to infer the presence of several species, including H<SUB>2</SUB>O and HCN. Methods. We observed four primary transits of HD 209458 b at a high resolution (ℛ ≍ 92000) with CRIRES+ in the near infrared (band H, 1.431243─1.837253 μm). After reducing the data with pycrires, we prepared the data using the SysRem algorithm and performed a cross-correlation (CCF) analysis of the transmission spectra. We also compared the results with those obtained from simulated datasets constructed by combining the Exo-REM self-consistent model with the petitRADTRANS package. Results. Combining the four transits, we detect H<SUB>2</SUB>O with a signal-to-noise CCF metric of 8.7σ. This corresponds to a signal emitted at K<SUB>p</SUB> = 151.3<SUB>−23.4</SUB><SUP>+31.1</SUP> km s<SUP>−1</SUP> and blueshifted by −6<SUB>−2</SUB><SUP>+1</SUP> km s<SUP>−1</SUP>, consistent with what is expected for HD 209458 b. We do not detect any other species among C<SUB>2</SUB>H<SUB>2</SUB>, CH<SUB>4</SUB>, CO, CO<SUB>2</SUB>, H<SUB>2</SUB>S, HCN, and NH<SUB>3</SUB>. Comparing this with our simulated datasets, this result is consistent with a C/O ratio of 0.1 and an opaque cloud top pressure of 50 Pa, at a 3 times solar metallicity. This would also be consistent with recent JWST observations. However, none of the simulated results obtained with a bulk C/O ratio of 0.8, a value suggested by previous studies using GIANO-B and CRIRES, are consistent with our observations.

[3] HD 209458 b的四次CRIRES+凌日观测：探测到H2O，未探测到C2H2、CH4和HCN

背景。HD 209458 b 是迄今为止研究最为深入的系外行星之一。尽管如此，大气特征研究在分子种类探测和丰度方面结果并不一致。已报道的 C/O 比值范围从约 0.1 到 1.0。特别值得关注的是，部分基于高分辨率地面观测的研究报告了 H2O 和 HCN 的同时探测，这要求大气 C/O 比值被精确调节到接近 1 的狭窄区间。然而，近期的空间观测并未探测到 HCN。目标。我们旨在利用高分辨率观测对 HD 209458 b 的大气进行独立研究，以推断包括 H2O 和 HCN 在内的多种分子的存在。方法。我们使用 CRIRES+ 在近红外（H 波段，1.431243─1.837253 μm）以高分辨率（ℛ ≍ 92000）观测了 HD 209458 b 的四次凌日。数据经 pycrires 处理后，采用 SysRem 算法进行预处理，并对透射光谱进行了互相关（CCF）分析。我们还将结果与结合 Exo-REM 自洽模型和 petitRADTRANS 软件包构建的模拟数据集进行了比较。结果。结合四次凌日观测，我们以 8.7σ 的信噪比 CCF 指标探测到 H2O。该信号对应于 Kp = 151.3−23.4+31.1 km s−1，蓝移 −6−2+1 km s−1，与 HD 209458 b 的预期一致。我们未探测到 C2H2、CH4、CO、CO2、H2S、HCN 和 NH3 等其他分子。与我们的模拟数据集比较，该结果与 C/O 比值为 0.1、云顶压强为 50 Pa、金属丰度为三倍太阳值的情形一致。这也与近期 JWST 观测结果相符。然而，采用 GIANO-B 和 CRIRES 先前研究建议的整体 C/O 比值 0.8 的所有模拟结果均与我们的观测不符。

[4] Can We Reconcile Space- and Ground-Based Atmospheric Interpretations of Two Classic Hot Jupiters? A comprehensive reanalysis of HD209458b and HD189733b using IGRINS2 spectrograph

2026-02-00

Hot Jupiters present a major challenge to planet formation theories, and their atmospheric compositions are keys to understanding their origins. Despite extensive observations from both space and ground based facilities, the molecular makeup of keystone hot Jupiters - HD 209458 b and HD 189733 b has long been a topic of intense debate. More importantly, these two planets occupy the moderate hot Jupiter regime (1000 - 1500 K), cooler than the ultra-hot (T &gt; 2000 K) planets where molecules dissociate but hotter than warm Neptunes where condensation dominates, placing them in the critical temperature range where species such as H<SUB>2</SUB>O, CO, CH<SUB>4</SUB>, HCN, and NH<SUB>3</SUB> can coexist—ideal for probing chemical transitions between oxygen-rich and carbon-rich atmospheres. I will present our campaign to revisit these classic exoplanets using the simultaneous high-resolution H- and K-band coverage of IGRINS2. Our observations target the dayside thermal emission mitigating the biases plaguing transit geometry such as limb asymmetry, Rossiter-McLaughlin effects and clouds. I will present the preliminary result of a strong H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO signals in HD209458b and an ongoing analysis of HD189733b with a strong H<SUB>2</SUB>O signal. Today, JWST is remarkable in detecting broadband exoplanet atmospheric features, but recovering crucial narrow band signals (e.g., CO) has proven to be challenging for planets with robust ground-based CO detection (e.g., WASP-18b, HD209458b). Our analysis of HD209458b and HD189733b further emphasizes the reasoning that ground-based observations are complementary to spaced-based facilities and possess unique advantages in providing unprecedented view of exoplanet atmospheres.

[4] 我们能否调和两颗经典热木星的空间与地面大气解释？利用IGRINS2光谱仪对HD209458b和HD189733b的综合再分析

热木星对行星形成理论提出了重大挑战，其大气成分是理解其起源的关键。尽管来自空间和地面设施的观测非常丰富，关键热木星 HD 209458 b 和 HD 189733 b 的分子组成长期以来一直存在激烈争议。更重要的是，这两颗行星处于中等温度热木星区间（1000-1500 K），比分子解离主导的超热行星（T > 2000 K）更冷，但又比冷凝主导的温暖海王星更热，正处于 H2O、CO、CH4、HCN 和 NH3 等物种可以共存的关键温度区间——非常适合研究富氧与富碳大气之间的化学转变。我将介绍我们利用 IGRINS2 同步高分辨率 H- 和 K-波段观测重新研究这些经典系外行星的计划。我们的观测针对日面热发射，减小了凌星几何带来的偏差，如边缘不对称、Rossiter-McLaughlin 效应和云的影响。我将展示 HD209458b 中强 H2O 和 CO 信号的初步结果，以及 HD189733b 的持续分析（同样有强 H2O 信号）。目前，JWST 在探测宽带系外行星大气特征方面表现出色，但对于那些地面已稳健探测到 CO 的行星（如 WASP-18b、HD209458b），恢复关键的窄带信号（如 CO）仍然具有挑战性。我们对 HD209458b 和 HD189733b 的分析进一步强调，地面观测与空间设施互为补充，并在提供前所未有的系外行星大气视角方面具有独特优势。

[5] The ESO SupJup Survey. III. Confirmation of <SUP>13</SUP>CO in YSES 1 b and Atmospheric Detection of YSES 1 c with CRIRES<SUP>+</SUP>

2024-12-00

High-resolution spectroscopic characterization of young super-Jovian planets enables precise constraints on elemental and isotopic abundances of their atmospheres. As part of the ESO SupJup Survey, we present high-resolution spectral observations of two wide-orbit super-Jupiters in YSES 1 (or TYC 8998-760-1) using the upgraded VLT/CRIRES<SUP>+</SUP> (<inline-formula> </inline-formula>) in K-band. We carry out free atmospheric retrieval analyses to constrain chemical and isotopic abundances, temperature structures, rotation velocities (<inline-formula> </inline-formula>), and radial velocities. We confirm the previous detection of <SUP>13</SUP>CO in YSES 1 b at a higher significance of 12.6σ, but point to a higher <SUP>12</SUP>CO/<SUP>13</SUP>CO ratio of 88 ± 13 (1σ confidence interval), consistent with the primary's isotope ratio 66 ± 5. We retrieve a solar-like composition in YSES 1 b with a C/O = 0.57 ± 0.01, indicating a formation via gravitational instability or core accretion beyond the CO iceline. Additionally, the observations lead to detections of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO in the outer planet YSES 1 c at 7.3σ and 5.7σ, respectively. We constrain the atmospheric C/O ratio of YSES 1 c to be either solar or subsolar (C/O = 0.36 ± 0.15), indicating the accretion of oxygen-rich solids. The two companions have distinct <inline-formula> </inline-formula>, 5.34 ± 0.14 km s<SUP>−1</SUP> for YSES 1 b and 11.3 ± 2.1 km s<SUP>−1 </SUP>for YSES 1 c, despite their similar natal environments. This may indicate different spin axis inclinations or effective magnetic braking by the long-lived circumplanetary disk around YSES 1 b. YSES 1 represents an intriguing system for comparative studies of super-Jovian companions and linking present atmospheres to formation histories.

[5] ESO SupJup巡天III：确认YSES 1 b中的13CO及利用CRIRES+探测YSES 1 c的大气

对年轻超木星行星进行高分辨率光谱特性研究，可以精确约束其大气中的元素和同位素丰度。作为ESO SupJup Survey的一部分，我们利用升级后的VLT/CRIRES+在K波段对YSES 1（或TYC 8998-760-1）系统中的两颗宽轨道超木星进行了高分辨率光谱观测。我们进行了自由大气检索分析，以约束化学和同位素丰度、温度结构、自转速度和径向速度。我们以更高的显著性（12.6σ）确认了此前在YSES 1 b中探测到的13CO，但指出12CO/13CO比值为88 ± 13（1σ置信区间），与主星的同位素比值66 ± 5一致。我们在YSES 1 b中检索到类太阳组成，C/O = 0.57 ± 0.01，表明其形成过程可能为引力不稳定或在CO雪线之外的核吸积。此外，观测还在外侧行星YSES 1 c中分别以7.3σ和5.7σ的显著性探测到H2O和CO。我们约束了YSES 1 c的大气C/O比为类太阳或亚太阳（C/O = 0.36 ± 0.15），表明其吸积了富氧固体。尽管两颗伴星的形成环境相似，但它们的自转速度明显不同，YSES 1 b为5.34 ± 0.14 km s−1，YSES 1 c为11.3 ± 2.1 km s−1。这可能表明自转轴倾角不同，或YSES 1 b周围长寿命的类卫星盘产生了有效的磁制动。YSES 1系统为超木星伴星的对比研究及将当前大气与形成历史联系起来提供了有趣的案例。

[6] The only known atmosphere on a rocky exoplanet?

2025-03-00

55 Cnc e is so far the only rocky exoplanet with convincing--though not uncontroversial--evidence of an atmosphere, in the form of a MIRI/LRS eclipse 8 sigma shallower than that of an airless zero-albedo world. It is also the only exoplanet with evidence of emission variability from multiple telescopes spanning multiple years. We propose to observe three eclipses of the planet with MIRI/MRS in the Medium sub-band. These observations will allow us to confirm the existence of an atmosphere, probe the wavelength dependence of its emission variability, and constrain its carbon dioxide abundance through the 15 um CO2 absorption feature. As an old, ultra-hot (Teq=2000 K), and ultra-short-period planet, 55 Cnc e may seem a-priori like a particularly hostile place for any gaseous envelope. Understanding whether and/or how such an envelope exists on 55 Cnc e, the most observationally favorable super-Earth, has strong implications for the survivability of rocky planet atmospheres more generally.

[6] 岩石系外行星上唯一已知的大气？

55 Cnc e 迄今是唯一有令人信服（尽管仍有争议）大气证据的岩石系外行星，其证据表现为 MIRI/LRS 食相深度比无大气零反照率模型浅 8 sigma。它也是唯一一个在多台望远镜、跨越多年观测中表现出发射可变性的系外行星。我们提议使用 MIRI/MRS 中波段观测该行星的三次食相。这些观测将使我们能够确认大气的存在，探测其发射可变性的波长依赖性，并通过 15 μm CO2 吸收特征约束其二氧化碳丰度。作为一颗年老、超高温（Teq=2000 K）、超短周期的行星，55 Cnc e 乍看之下似乎极不适合存在任何气体包层。理解 55 Cnc e 这样一颗观测条件极佳的超级地球是否以及如何拥有大气包层，对于岩石行星大气的存活性具有重要意义。

[7] No Escaping Helium from 55 Cnc e

2021-04-00

We search for escaping helium from the hot super-Earth 55 Cnc e by taking high-resolution spectra of the 1083 nm line during two transits using Keck/NIRSPEC. We detect no helium absorption down to a 90% upper limit of 250 ppm in excess absorption or 0.27 mÅ in equivalent width. This corresponds to a mass-loss rate of less than ∼10<SUP>9</SUP> g s<SUP>-1</SUP> assuming a Parker wind model with a plausible exosphere temperature of 5000-6000 K, although the precise constraint is heavily dependent on model assumptions. We consider both hydrogen- and helium-dominated atmospheric compositions and find similar bounds on the mass-loss rate in both scenarios. Our hydrodynamical models indicate that if a lightweight atmosphere exists on 55 Cnc e, our observations would have easily detected it. Together with the nondetection of Lyα absorption by Ehrenreich et al., our helium nondetection indicates that 55 Cnc e either never accreted a primordial atmosphere in the first place or lost its primordial atmosphere shortly after the dissipation of the gas disk. * The data presented herein were obtained at the W. M. Keck Observatory, which is operated as a scientific partnership among the California Institute of Technology, the University of California, and the National Aeronautics and Space Administration. The Observatory was made possible by the generous financial support of the W. M. Keck Foundation.

[7] 55 Cnc e 未探测到氦逃逸

我们利用Keck/NIRSPEC在两个凌星期间对1083 nm谱线进行高分辨率观测，搜索热超地球55 Cnc e的大气逃逸氦。我们未探测到氦吸收，90%上限为额外吸收250 ppm或等效宽度0.27 mÅ。假设Parker风模型和合理的外逸层温度（5000–6000 K），这对应的质量损失率小于约10^9 g s^-1，尽管具体约束高度依赖模型假设。我们同时考虑了以氢和氦为主的大气成分，发现两种情形下的质量损失率上限相似。我们的流体动力学模型表明，如果55 Cnc e存在轻质大气，我们的观测本应能轻松探测到。结合Ehrenreich等人未探测到Lyα吸收的结果，我们的氦未探测表明，55 Cnc e要么从未获得过原始大气，要么在气体盘消散后不久就失去了原始大气。* 本文所用数据由W. M. Keck天文台获得，该台由加州理工学院、加州大学和美国国家航空航天局合作运营，并由W. M. Keck基金会慷慨资助建成。

[8] A Nondetection of Iron in the First High-resolution Emission Study of the Lava Planet 55 Cnc e

2023-10-00

Close-in lava planets represent an extreme example of terrestrial worlds, but their high temperatures may allow us to probe a diversity of crustal compositions. The brightest and most well-studied of these objects is 55 Cancri e, a nearby super-Earth with a remarkably short 17 hr orbit. However, despite numerous studies, debate remains about the existence and composition of its atmosphere. We present upper limits on the atmospheric pressure of 55 Cnc e derived from high-resolution time-series spectra taken with Gemini-N/MAROON-X. Our results are consistent with current crustal evaporation models for this planet which predict a thin ~100 mbar atmosphere. We conclude that, if a mineral atmosphere is present on 55 Cnc e, the atmospheric pressure is below 100 mbar.

[8] 熔岩行星55 Cnc e首次高分辨率发射研究中未探测到铁

近轨道熔岩行星是类地世界的极端例子，但其高温可能使我们能够探测多样的地壳组成。其中最亮、研究最充分的是55 Cancri e，这是一颗距离较近、轨道周期仅17小时的超地球。尽管已有大量研究，关于其大气的存在与组成仍有争议。我们基于Gemini-N/MAROON-X高分辨率时序光谱，对55 Cnc e的大气压强给出了上限。我们的结果与当前针对该行星的地壳蒸发模型一致，该模型预测其大气为~100 mbar的稀薄大气。我们得出结论，如果55 Cnc e存在矿物大气，其大气压强低于100 mbar。

item_003 · sys_F
What are the observable consequences of magma ocean dynamos and interior magnetic fields on the atmospheric properties and potential habitability of rocky exoplanets with thick atmospheres?
岩石系外行星具有厚大气层时，岩浆海洋发电机和内部磁场对其大气性质及潜在宜居性有哪些可观测影响？
8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] How does magma ocean evolution affect the habitability of terrestrial exoplanets?

2023-12-00

Whether the long-term habitability of Earth is exceptional or typical for terrestrial planets has immense significance for the search for life elsewhere. One potentially underexplored aspect to habitability is how initial volatile inventories may shape subsequent geochemical and climate evolution. Exoplanet exploration has revealed that many—perhaps most—terrestrial exoplanets formed with substantial H2-rich envelopes, seemingly in contrast to the solar system terrestrials, for which there is scant evidence of prolonged H2-rich envelopes. How might the presence of a long-lived primary atmosphere and resultant molten surface affect the subsequent habitability of terrestrial and super-Earth planets? Answering this question requires a self-consistent model of how high molecular weight volatiles partition into the magma ocean and fractionate during primary atmosphere loss, as well as how terrestrial planets that previously possessed thick primary atmospheres might subsequently evolve. Of particular relevance to ongoing JWST observations is how coupled magma ocean + atmosphere systems evolve when subjected to high bolometric and XUV fluxes during the extended pre-main sequence of late M-dwarfs. Here, we will present a new model of atmosphere-interior early evolution that self-consistently tracks atmospheric escape, magma ocean solidification, and redox evolution. Specifically, the model tracks how C, O, H, and N-bearing volatile reservoirs evolve as an initial H2-rich envelope is lost to space and the magma ocean solidifies. The model will be applied to several imminently characterizable, rocky exoplanets to explore how post-magma ocean volatile reservoirs (and subsequent geochemical cycling + climate evolution) could be affected by the presence or absence of a substantial primary atmosphere.

[1] 岩石系外行星的岩浆海演化如何影响其宜居性？

地球的长期宜居性是特殊还是类地行星的普遍现象，对于寻找地外生命具有重大意义。影响宜居性的一个可能被低估的方面是初始挥发分库存如何塑造后续地球化学和气候演化。系外行星探索显示，许多（甚至大多数）类地系外行星形成时拥有大量富 H2 包层，这与太阳系类地行星形成鲜有长期富 H2 包层的证据形成对比。长期存在的原始大气及其导致的熔融表面会如何影响类地和超级地球行星的后续宜居性？回答这一问题需要自洽建模高分子量挥发分在岩浆洋中的分配及其在原始大气损失过程中的分馏，以及曾经拥有厚原始大气的类地行星后续如何演化。与 JWST 正在进行的观测密切相关的是，岩浆洋+大气系统在晚型 M 矮星延长前主序阶段经历高总辐射和 XUV 通量时的耦合演化。我们将在此展示一个新的大气-内部早期演化模型，该模型自洽追踪大气逃逸、岩浆洋固化和氧化还原演化。具体而言，该模型追踪 C、O、H、N 含挥发分库在初始富 H2 包层丧失至太空及岩浆洋固化过程中的演化。该模型将应用于若干即将可表征的岩石系外行星，以探讨岩浆洋后挥发分库（及后续地球化学循环和气候演化）如何受有无大量原始大气的影响。

[2] Evaporation of a magma ocean in presence of hydrogen &amp; observational implications

2023-10-00

With the new observational capabilities of space telescopes, it should be possible to better characterize the atmospheres of exoplanets, and provide constraints on interiors. Ultra hot rocky exoplanets, for which the stellar irradiation may maintain a magma ocean at the surface for a long period of time, are candidates for such observations. It has been suggested that the primary hydrogen envelope that is captured during the formation of a planet could be kept in the magma ocean, and therefore we could observe planets with a silicate atmosphere mixed with hydrogen. Our model relies on a Gibbs free-energy minimization to find the vapor composition in equilibrium with the magma ocean (a modified version of the CEA/NASA code (Gordon &amp; McBride (1996)). The vapor composition is then used in an atmospheric model, ATMO (Amundsen et al. 2014), which solves for the pressure-temperature profile by finding the energy flux balance in each layer of the model. Synthetic observations are generated via ATMO. We confirm the thermal inversion of silicate atmospheres and the associated emission features of SiO (Ito et al. 2015, Zilinskas et al. 2022), as well as MgO, Na, K, Fe, which are the strongest candidates for detection. We show that hydrogen will water down the other species, and the thermal inversion is reduced or removed, depending on the temperature of the planet. Cases with a lot of hydrogen will be linked to absorption features of H2O. Surface temperatures will also be affected, and increase for higher content of hydrogen.

[2] 氢存在下岩浆海的蒸发及其观测意义

随着空间望远镜观测能力的提升，我们有望更好地刻画系外行星大气，并对其内部结构加以约束。超高温岩石系外行星由于恒星辐射可能在表面长期维持岩浆海，是此类观测的候选目标。有研究提出，行星形成过程中捕获的原始氢包层可能被保存在岩浆海中，因此我们可能观测到含氢的硅酸盐大气。我们的模型基于Gibbs自由能最小化，确定与岩浆海平衡的蒸气成分（采用改进版CEA/NASA代码（Gordon & McBride (1996)））。随后将蒸气成分输入大气模型ATMO（Amundsen等，2014），通过各层能量通量平衡求解压强-温度剖面。合成观测数据由ATMO生成。我们证实了硅酸盐大气的热反转及其相关的SiO发射特征（Ito等，2015；Zilinskas等，2022），以及MgO、Na、K、Fe，这些是最有希望被探测到的分子。我们显示，氢的存在会稀释其他组分，热反转会减弱或消失，具体取决于行星温度。高氢含量的情形将与H2O的吸收特征相关。表面温度也会受到影响，氢含量越高，表面温度越高。

[3] Convective shutdown in the atmospheres of lava worlds

2025-01-00

Atmospheric energy transport is central to the cooling of primordial magma oceans. Theoretical studies of atmospheres on lava planets have assumed that convection is the only process involved in setting the atmospheric temperature structure. This significantly influences the ability for a magma ocean to cool. It has been suggested that convective stability in these atmospheres could preclude permanent magma oceans. We develop a new 1D radiative-convective model in order to investigate when the atmospheres overlying magma oceans are convectively stable. Using a coupled interior-atmosphere framework, we simulate the early evolution of two terrestrial-mass exoplanets: TRAPPIST-1 c and HD 63433 d. Our simulations suggest that the atmosphere of HD 63433 d exhibits deep isothermal layers which are convectively stable. However, it is able to maintain a permanent magma ocean and an atmosphere depleted in <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0001" notation="LaTeX">$\mathrm{H_{2}O}$</tex-math></inline-formula>. It is possible to maintain permanent magma oceans underneath atmospheres without convection. Absorption features of <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0002" notation="LaTeX">$\mathrm{CO_{2}}$</tex-math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0003" notation="LaTeX">$\mathrm{SO_{2}}$</tex-math></inline-formula> within synthetic emission spectra are associated with mantle redox state, meaning that future observations of HD 63433 d may provide constraints on the geochemical properties of a magma ocean analogous with the early Earth. Simulations of TRAPPIST-1 c indicate that it is expected to have solidified within <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0004" notation="LaTeX">$100 \,\mathrm{M}\rm {yr}$</tex-math></inline-formula>, outgassing a thick atmosphere in the process. Cool isothermal stratospheres generated by low-molecular-weight atmospheres can mimic the emission of an atmosphere-less body. Future work should consider how atmospheric escape and chemistry modulates the lifetime of magma oceans, and the role of tidal heating in sustaining atmospheric convection.

[3] 熔岩世界大气中的对流关闭

大气能量输运对于原始岩浆海洋的冷却至关重要。关于熔岩行星大气的理论研究通常假设对流是决定大气温度结构的唯一过程，这对岩浆海洋的冷却能力有重要影响。有观点认为，这些大气中的对流稳定性可能阻止永久性岩浆海洋的存在。我们开发了一个新的1D辐射-对流模型，以研究覆盖岩浆海洋的大气在何种条件下为对流稳定。利用耦合的内部-大气框架，我们模拟了两颗类地质量系外行星（TRAPPIST-1 c和HD 63433 d）的早期演化。模拟结果表明，HD 63433 d的大气表现出深层等温层且对流稳定，但仍能维持永久性岩浆海洋和H2O亏损的大气。在无对流的大气下，仍有可能维持永久性岩浆海洋。合成发射光谱中CO2和SO2的吸收特征与地幔氧化还原态相关，意味着未来对HD 63433 d的观测可为类似早期地球的岩浆海洋地球化学性质提供约束。TRAPPIST-1 c的模拟表明其预计在100 Myr内固化，并在此过程中释放出厚大气。低分子量大气产生的冷等温平流层可模拟无大气天体的发射特征。未来工作应考虑大气逃逸和化学过程如何调节岩浆海洋寿命，以及潮汐加热在维持大气对流中的作用。

[4] Redox hysteresis of super-Earth exoplanets from magma ocean circulation

2021-12-00

Ongoing and near-future exoplanet surveys will significantly widen our view of the distribution and variability of rocky exoplanets. From an astronomical perspective, planets that formed under similar conditions should exhibit comparable compositional trends, such as volatile inventory, which can be compared to hypothetical M-R relations. However, internal redox reactions may irreversibly alter the mantle composition and volatile inventory of terrestrial and super-Earth exoplanets, which can affect their outgassed atmospheres and decouple the initial accreted composition from long-term climate. The global efficacy of these mechanisms hinges on the transfer of reduced iron from the molten silicate mantle to the metal core. Using scaling analysis I demonstrate that turbulent diffusion in the internal magma oceans of sub-Neptune exoplanets can kinetically entrain liquid iron droplets and quench core formation. This suggests that the chemical equilibration between core, mantle, and atmosphere may be energetically limited by convective overturn in the magma flow. Hence, molten super-Earths possibly retain a compositional memory of their accretion path. Redox control by magma ocean circulation positively correlates with planetary heat flow, internal gravity, and planet size. The presence and speciation of remanent atmospheres, surface mineralogy, and core-mass fraction of atmosphere-stripped exoplanets may thus constrain magma ocean dynamics, which can be probed observationally.

[4] 来自岩浆海洋环流的超级地球系外行星氧化还原迟滞

正在进行和即将到来的系外行星巡天将极大拓展我们对岩石系外行星分布和多样性的认识。从天文学角度看，在类似条件下形成的行星应表现出可比较的组成趋势，如挥发物储量，这可以与假设的质量-半径关系进行比较。然而，内部氧化还原反应可能不可逆地改变类地和超级地球系外行星的地幔组成和挥发物储量，这会影响其逸出大气，并使初始吸积组成与长期气候脱钩。这些机制的全球效率取决于还原态铁从熔融硅酸盐地幔向金属核的转移。通过尺度分析，我证明了亚海王星系外行星内部岩浆洋中的湍流扩散可以动力学地夹带液态铁滴并抑制核心形成。这表明，核、地幔和大气之间的化学平衡可能受到岩浆流对流翻转的能量限制。因此，熔融超级地球可能保留其吸积路径的组成记忆。岩浆洋环流对氧化还原的控制与行星热流、内部重力和行星大小正相关。大气剥离后的系外行星的残余大气的存在与形态、表面矿物学和核心质量分数等，可能因此约束岩浆洋动力学，这些可以通过观测手段加以探测。

[5] Early Habitability Under Different Molten Interior Outgassing Scenarios

2023-12-00

The volatile budget of rocky exoplanets is highly unconstrained. In addition, recent models have suggested that the early magma ocean outgassing of volatile species highly soluble in the melt, in particular water, may not be as efficient as previously thought. A substantial amount of water could then be trapped in the planetary interior before being progressively released through long-term volcanic outgassing. The long-term evolution of a planet may then start with a broad range of atmospheric compositions. Yet, the inner edge of the habitable zone is classically defined for and restricted to steam, water-dominated atmospheres. It does not encompass the diversity of atmospheric composition resulting from alternative magma ocean and volcanic outgassing scenarios. Together with exoplanets findings and characterizations, there is a pressing need to broaden our understanding of planetary habitability in the frame of realistic coupled interior-atmosphere evolution. <P />Here we use a 1D radiative-convective climate model, with self-consistent patchy clouds, to simulate the atmospheric thermal structure of Earth-sized planets of different oxidized atmospheric compositions. We thus investigate the climate and resulting surface conditions associated with a broad diversity of interior outgassing scenarios. We then assess how the location of the inner edge of the habitable zone may vary as a function of the relative abundance of water and carbon dioxide in the atmosphere. Finally, we evaluate the influence of the star type and of clouds on our predictions.

[5] 不同熔融内部逸气情景下的早期宜居性

岩石系外行星的挥发分预算高度不确定。此外，最新模型表明，早期岩浆洋中对熔体高度可溶的挥发分（尤其是水）的释气效率可能远低于此前认为的水平。因此，大量水分可能被困于行星内部，随后通过长期火山释气逐步释放。行星的长期演化可能由多种大气成分组成的初始状态开始。然而，宜居带内缘传统上是针对并仅限于水蒸气主导的大气定义的，并未涵盖由不同岩浆洋和火山释气情景导致的大气成分多样性。结合系外行星的发现与表征，亟需在真实的内部-大气耦合演化框架下拓展我们对行星宜居性的理解。 在此，我们使用带有自洽斑块云的 1D 辐射-对流气候模型，模拟不同氧化大气成分的类地行星大气热结构。我们研究了多样化内部释气情景下的气候及其导致的表面条件。随后，我们评估了宜居带内缘位置如何随大气中水和二氧化碳相对丰度变化而变化。最后，我们还评估了恒星类型和云对预测结果的影响。

[6] Redox Hysteresis of Super-Earth Exoplanets from Magma Ocean Circulation

2021-06-00

Internal redox reactions may irreversibly alter the mantle composition and volatile inventory of terrestrial and super-Earth exoplanets and affect the prospects for atmospheric observations. The global efficacy of these mechanisms, however, hinges on the transfer of reduced iron from the molten silicate mantle to the metal core. Scaling analysis indicates that turbulent diffusion in the internal magma oceans of sub-Neptunes can kinetically entrain liquid iron droplets and quench core formation. This suggests that the chemical equilibration between core, mantle, and atmosphere may be energetically limited by convective overturn in the magma flow. Hence, molten super-Earths possibly retain a compositional memory of their accretion path. Redox control by magma ocean circulation is positively correlated with planetary heat flow, internal gravity, and planet size. The presence and speciation of remanent atmospheres, surface mineralogy, and core mass fraction of primary envelope-stripped exoplanets may thus constrain magma ocean dynamics.

[6] 来自岩浆海洋环流的超级地球系外行星氧化还原迟滞

内部氧化还原反应可能不可逆地改变类地和超级地球系外行星的地幔组成和挥发物储量，并影响其大气观测前景。然而，这些机制的整体效率取决于还原态铁从熔融硅酸盐地幔向金属核的转移。尺度分析表明，亚海王星内部岩浆洋中的湍流扩散可以动力学夹带液态铁滴并抑制核心形成。这表明，核、地幔和大气之间的化学平衡可能受到岩浆流动中对流翻转的能量限制。因此，熔融超级地球可能保留其吸积路径的组成记忆。岩浆洋环流对氧化还原的控制与行星热流、内部重力和行星尺寸正相关。原始包层被剥离的系外行星的残余大气的存在及其形态、表面矿物学和核质量分数，因而可以约束岩浆洋动力学。

[7] Large-scale hydrogen reactions with magma oceans change the atmosphere of exoplanets

2025-09-00

Exoplanet super-Earths and sub-Neptunes are likely more prevalent than stars, yet their nature remains uncertain. Notably, sub-Neptunes orbit in close proximity to their host stars, often enveloped in substantial and hot hydrogen-rich atmospheres. These conditions, characterized by intense irradiation and the presence of greenhouse gases, accelerate the melting of the underlying silicate surface and facilitate the exchange of volatiles with the atmosphere. In this study, we conduct extensive large-scale ab initio simulations to investigate the reaction between hydrogen gas and molten multi-component silicates. Our findings reveal that magma oceans possess the capability to dissolve up to 2 wt% hydrogen at typical conditions pertinent to the interface between the atmosphere and the condensed surface of such planets. The influx of hydrogen into the molten silicate results in a reduction in magma density, leading to the formation of a substantial buffer layer between the condensed interior and the outer atmosphere. This layer effectively suppresses convection and restricts chemical exchanges within these planets.Furthermore, the incorporation of hydrogen into the molten silicate alters the redox state of the magma ocean, causing it to become reduced. Consequently, a substantial outflux of oxygen is generated, which combines with atmospheric hydrogen to produce substantial amounts of water vapor. Consequently, the release of oxygen will profoundly alter the atmospheric chemistry and manifest spectral signatures detectable from space telescopes. These atmospheric composition modifications constitute a testable signature of concealed magma oceans on exoplanets. The uptake of volatiles by the magma implies that sub-Neptunes exhibit a higher volatility compared to previously assumed. Additionally, our atomistic simulations suggest that the dissolution and evaporation process is chemically intricate, resulting in the formation of hundreds of distinct molecular species within the atmosphere. These magma-buffered atmospheric recipes will necessitate consideration in future studies focused on the origins of exoplanet atmospheres.

[7] 大尺度氢与岩浆海洋反应改变系外行星大气

系外行星中的超级地球和亚海王星可能比恒星更为常见，但它们的本质仍不确定。值得注意的是，亚海王星通常围绕其母星近距离运行，往往包裹着大量且高温的富氢大气。这些条件下，强烈的辐射和温室气体的存在加速了下方硅酸盐表面的熔融，并促进了挥发分与大气之间的交换。在本研究中，我们进行了大规模从头算模拟，研究氢气与多组分熔融硅酸盐之间的反应。我们的结果显示，在典型的大气与凝聚表面界面条件下，岩浆海洋能够溶解高达2 wt%的氢。氢进入熔融硅酸盐会导致岩浆密度降低，从而在凝聚内部与外层大气之间形成一个显著的缓冲层。该层有效抑制了对流，并限制了这些行星内部的化学交换。此外，氢掺入熔融硅酸盐会改变岩浆海洋的氧化还原状态，使其变得更还原。结果，产生了大量氧气外流，氧与大气中的氢结合生成大量水蒸气。因此，氧的释放将深刻改变大气化学，并在空间望远镜可探测的光谱中表现出特征信号。这些大气组成的变化构成了可检验的系外行星隐匿岩浆海洋的特征信号。岩浆对挥发分的吸收意味着亚海王星的挥发性比此前认为的更高。此外，我们的原子级模拟表明，溶解和蒸发过程在化学上极为复杂，导致大气中形成数百种不同的分子种类。这些岩浆缓冲型大气的成分将在未来关于系外行星大气起源的研究中需要加以考虑。

[8] Redox hysteresis of super-Earth exoplanets from magma ocean circulation

2021-09-00

Internal redox reactions may irreversibly alter the mantle composition and volatile inventory of terrestrial and super-Earth exoplanets and affect the prospects for atmospheric observations. The global efficacy of these mechanisms, however, hinges on the transfer of reduced iron from the molten silicate mantle to the metal core. Scaling analysis indicates that turbulent diffusion in the internal magma oceans of sub- Neptunes can kinetically entrain liquid iron droplets and quench core formation. This suggests that the chemical equilibration between core, mantle, and atmosphere may be energetically limited by convective overturn in the magma flow. Hence, molten super-Earths possibly retain a compositional memory of their accretion path. Redox control by magma ocean circulation is positively correlated with planetary heat flow, internal gravity, and planet size. The presence and speciation of remanent atmospheres, surface mineralogy, and core mass fraction of atmosphere-stripped exoplanets may thus constrain magma ocean dynamics.

[8] 来自岩浆海洋环流的超级地球系外行星氧化还原迟滞

内部氧化还原反应可能不可逆地改变类地和超地球系外行星的地幔组成和挥发物储量，并影响大气观测的前景。然而，这些机制的全球效率取决于还原态铁从熔融硅酸盐地幔向金属核的转移。尺度分析表明，亚海王星内部岩浆洋中的湍流扩散可以动力学地夹带液态铁滴，并抑制核心的形成。这表明，核心、地幔和大气之间的化学平衡可能受到岩浆流对流翻转的能量限制。因此，熔融的超地球可能保留其吸积路径的组成记忆。岩浆洋环流对氧化还原的控制与行星热流、内部重力和行星大小呈正相关。大气被剥离的系外行星的残余大气的存在及其形态、表面矿物学和核心质量分数，因而可以约束岩浆洋动力学。

8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] On the robustness of atmospheric inferences. Estimating the predictive accuracy of atmospheric models for transmission spectra of transiting exoplanets.

2022-06-00

Up until now, most exoplanet transit spectroscopy observations are comprised of relatively low signal to noise and sparse wavelength coverage data. As such, atmospheric inferences are currently derived from a limited number of spectral points and, in some cases, one data-point can significantly affect the interpretation of these observations. Furthermore, when interpreting the goodness-of-fit between the data and the atmospheric models, using different statistical metrics can lead to different conclusions. In some cases, while Bayesian evidence model comparisons suggest the detection of an atomic or molecular species, frequentist metrics like the p-value suggest that the chosen atmospheric model is not supported by the data. Can we trust a chemical detection if some metric suggests that the model does not fit the data? This conundrum highlights the need for methods that can assess the sensitivity of the resulting inferences to the data. Such need becomes more urgent with the imminent wave of spectroscopic information coming from the James Webb Space Telescope for which understanding the limits of our models and our data is paramount. <P />In this work we will present a complementary goodness-of-fit metric applied to transmission spectra of transiting exoplanets. Our work leverages the power of Bayesian atmospheric retrievals to understand which specific data points are responsible for an inferred atmospheric property (e.g., chemical detection), and is robust against considerations in the model (e.g., choice of priors) and noise properties of the data. We will present our computationally practical approach and demonstrate its applicability on recent transmission spectra with unsettled atmospheric detections. We will show how this metric can help us better determine the reliability of an inference and quantify its significance. This complementary approach to estimate the goodness of fit of atmospheric models to the data opens a new avenue to determine the reliability of a given result and inform our understanding of exoplanet atmospheres.

[1] 关于大气推断的稳健性。估算凌星系外行星透射光谱大气模型的预测精度

迄今为止，大多数系外行星凌日光谱观测的数据信噪比较低且波长覆盖稀疏。因此，目前的大气推断主要基于有限数量的光谱点，在某些情况下，单个数据点就可能显著影响对观测结果的解释。此外，在评估数据与大气模型拟合优度时，采用不同的统计指标可能会导致不同的结论。有时，尽管贝叶斯证据模型比较表明探测到了某种原子或分子，但频率学指标如 p 值却显示所选大气模型并不被数据支持。如果某些指标表明模型与数据不符，我们还能相信化学成分的探测吗？这一难题凸显了需要能够评估推断结果对数据敏感性的方法。随着 JWST 即将带来大量光谱信息，理解我们模型和数据的局限性变得尤为重要。 在本工作中，我们提出了一种应用于系外行星凌日透射光谱的互补拟合优度指标。我们的工作利用贝叶斯大气检索的能力，理解哪些具体数据点导致了某一大气属性（如化学探测）的推断结果，并且对模型选择（如先验的选择）和数据噪声特性具有鲁棒性。我们将展示这种计算上可行的方法，并在近期存在争议的大气探测透射光谱上演示其适用性。我们将展示该指标如何帮助我们更好地判断推断结果的可靠性并量化其显著性。这种评估大气模型与数据拟合优度的互补方法为判断结果可靠性和加深对系外行星大气的理解开辟了新途径。

[2] Considerations for next-generation retrievals of exoplanetary transmission spectra

2021-01-00

The last decade has witnessed a revolution in our understanding of exoplanets through the procurement of exquisite spectroscopic observations of their atmospheres. Such observations are routinely interpreted by retrieval tools, frameworks in which an atmospheric model is coupled with an optimization algorithm. Atmospheric retrievals of exoplanetary transmission spectra have been able to provide important constraints on various atmospheric properties such as chemical abundances, information about the presence and structure of clouds and hazes, and characteristic temperatures, at the day-night terminator region of the atmosphere. Most transmission spectra to date have been observed for giant exoplanets due to which retrievals typically assume Hydrogen-rich atmospheres. However, recent observations of mini-Neptunes and super-Earths, and the promise of upcoming facilities including JWST, call for a new generation of retrieval frameworks that can robustly address a wide range of atmospheric compositions and related complexities. In this talk, we will present various considerations for next-generation atmospheric retrievals of exoplanetary transmission spectra with current and upcoming facilities like JWST. We investigate the performance of multiple parametric prescriptions for the presence of inhomogeneous clouds and hazes in atmospheres of transiting exoplanets and quantify their impact on the retrieved chemical abundances. Next, we consider the agreement, or lack thereof, between these prescriptions when the assumption of a H-rich atmosphere is relaxed as it may be the case for some mini-Neptunes and super-Earths. Alongside these parametric forms, we include considerations for atmospheric forward models that incorporate Mie-scattering and refraction. Furthermore, we examine the different model degeneracies present in these models using multiple optimization algorithms which have been benchmarked against each other. Lastly, we argue about the importance of exploring existing model assumptions and incorporating these and other critical model advancements to retrieval tools.

[2] 面向下一代系外行星透射光谱检索的考虑因素

过去十年，通过对系外行星大气的高质量光谱观测，我们对系外行星的理解发生了革命性变化。这类观测通常通过检索工具进行解释，即将大气模型与优化算法结合的框架。系外行星透射光谱的大气检索能够对多种大气属性提供重要约束，如化学丰度、云和薄雾的存在及结构信息，以及晨昏线区域的特征温度。迄今为止，大多数透射光谱观测对象为巨型系外行星，因此检索通常假设富氢大气。然而，近期对迷你海王星和超级地球的观测，以及包括JWST在内的新设施的到来，要求新一代检索框架能够稳健地处理更广泛的大气组成及其相关复杂性。在本报告中，我们将介绍针对当前和即将到来的设施（如JWST）进行系外行星透射光谱下一代大气检索的多项考虑。我们研究了多种参数化方案在处理凌星系外行星大气中非均匀云和薄雾存在时的表现，并量化了其对检索化学丰度的影响。接着，我们考察了在放宽富氢大气假设（如部分迷你海王星和超级地球可能的情况）时，这些参数化方案之间的一致性或不一致性。除这些参数化形式外，我们还考虑了包含Mie散射和折射的大气正向模型。此外，我们利用多种优化算法（已相互基准测试）分析了这些模型中存在的不同模型简并性。最后，我们强调探索现有模型假设并将这些及其他关键模型进展纳入检索工具的重要性。

[3] Atmospheric Retrievals: Limitations, Challenges, and the Road Ahead

2026-06-00

Atmospheric retrieval is now the default framework for interpreting exoplanet and brown dwarf spectra from space and ground-based platforms, yet its limitations often become most apparent precisely in the high-quality data regime where it is most heavily applied. In this talk, I will focus on the challenges of contemporary retrieval approaches, drawing on examples across low- and high-resolution spectroscopy, multiple wavelength regimes, and substellar benchmark atmospheres. Key challenges include degeneracies between temperature structure, composition, and aerosols; biases introduced by incomplete or overly flexible model assumptions; the influence of priors in data-limited regimes; uncertainty in the "correctness" of the data and separating planetary signal from astrophysical noise sources; and the difficulty of validating inference when forward models are only approximate representations of complex, multidimensional atmospheres. I will then outline several future directions that can move the field forward. These include leveraging combined low-and-hi-res datasets, incorporating more realistic atmospheric physics without sacrificing interpretability, and inference methods that enable more rapid computation enabling more robustness tests. I will also briefly discuss the role of machine-learning techniques as computational accelerators and diagnostic tools, rather than black-box replacements for physical models. The goal is to provide a concise, critical perspective on how retrieval can evolve from a powerful but fragile technique into a robust framework for population-level studies and the eventual characterization of potentially habitable worlds.

[3] 大气检索：局限性、挑战与未来方向

大气检索现已成为解释来自空间和地基平台的系外行星及褐矮星光谱的默认框架，但其局限性往往在高质量数据领域最为突出，而该领域也是检索应用最广泛的地方。本报告将聚焦于当前检索方法面临的挑战，涵盖低分辨率和高分辨率光谱、多波段以及亚恒星基准大气的案例。主要挑战包括温度结构、成分和气溶胶之间的退化性；不完整或过于灵活的模型假设引入的偏差；先验在数据有限情形下的影响；对数据“正确性”的不确定性以及如何将行星信号与天体噪声源区分开；以及当前前向模型仅为复杂多维大气近似时，推断验证的困难。随后我将概述推动该领域发展的若干未来方向，包括结合低分辨率和高分辨率数据集、在不牺牲可解释性的前提下引入更真实的大气物理，以及能够更快计算、从而实现更强健性测试的推断方法。我还将简要讨论机器学习技术作为计算加速器和诊断工具的作用，而非物理模型的黑箱替代品。目标是为检索方法如何从强大但脆弱的技术演变为适用于总体研究和最终潜在宜居世界表征的稳健框架，提供简明而批判性的视角。

[4] Novel methods to probe exoplanet atmospheres using ground-based spectrophotometry

2021-09-00

Ground-based spectrophotometric observations of transiting exoplanet atmospheres conventionally rely on correcting for instrumental and telluric systematics in the light curves by using reference stars that are simultaneously observed. However, this approach often leads to sub-optimal corrections due to multiple accounts on which the target and reference star spectra can be affected by systematics differently through the night, ultimately limiting the achievable precision and accuracy on the measurement of planetary atmospheric signatures. We introduce a new method based on Gaussian Processes regression to address this challenge by extracting the transmission or emission spectrum without relying explicitly on the reference stars. Our new method overcomes the necessity of using reference stars and opens up the doors to ground-based atmospheric observations of exoplanets orbiting bright host stars (e.g. those discovered by TESS) that intrinsically lack proper reference stars. We present results from the application of our method to a broad sample of exoplanets observed in the optical and near-infrared using Gemini/GMOS and Keck/MOSFIRE. We also discuss the challenges and possible solutions arising from stellar variability towards combining high precision ground-based low-resolution spectroscopy observations in complementarity with future infrared observations from HST and JWST.

[4] 利用地基光谱光度法探测系外行星大气的新方法

地基凌星系外行星大气的光谱光度观测通常依赖于同时观测的参考星来校正光曲线中的仪器和大气系统误差。然而，由于目标星和参考星的光谱在夜间可能受到不同系统误差的影响，这种方法常常导致校正效果不理想，最终限制了行星大气特征测量的精度和准确性。我们提出了一种基于高斯过程回归的新方法，通过不显式依赖参考星来提取透射光谱或发射光谱。该新方法克服了对参考星的依赖，为观测缺乏合适参考星的明亮宿主星（如TESS发现的行星）的地基大气观测打开了大门。我们展示了该方法在使用Gemini/GMOS和Keck/MOSFIRE在光学和近红外波段观测的一批系外行星样本上的应用结果。我们还讨论了恒星活动带来的挑战及其可能的解决方案，以便将高精度地基低分辨率光谱观测与未来HST和JWST的红外观测互补结合。

[5] From cross-correlations to likelihoods: a fully Bayesian approach to high-resolution retrievals

2021-10-00

High-resolution Doppler-resolved spectroscopy has opened up a new window into the atmospheres of both transiting and non-transiting exoplanets. While the 'classical' cross-correlation approach is efficient for finding atomic and molecular species, it is quite limited with its inability to recover quantitative information on the atmosphere such as abundances and temperature profiles, nor can it place statistically robust uncertainties on the quantities it can measure (e.g. velocities, detection significances). Recent pioneering efforts have sought to develop likelihood 'mappings' from the cross-correlation function, that can be used to directly compare model fits to high-resolution data sets thus solving these issues. Here, I will outline a framework based purely on a simple Gaussian likelihood, remarkably similar to the techniques that have been applied to transit light curves for over a decade. These can be used to explore the posterior distribution of parametrised model atmospheres in a statistically rigorous way. However, one important and distinct problem remains for high-resolution retrievals; the methods used for filtering out the stellar and telluric lines from the data also filters the underlying exoplanet's signal. I will discuss a simple and fast technique to address this problem that enables retrievals from data filtered through common methods such as SysRem and PCA. Finally, I will demonstrate this framework on observations of the ultra-hot Jupiter WASP-121b, including constraints on the abundances and temperature profile of the atmosphere.

[5] 从互相关到似然：高分辨率检索的全贝叶斯方法

高分辨率多普勒分辨光谱为凌日和非凌日系外行星大气研究开辟了新窗口。虽然“经典”的互相关方法在寻找原子和分子组分方面高效，但它无法获得大气的定量信息，如丰度和温度剖面，也无法对其可测量的量（如速度、探测显著性）给出统计上稳健的不确定性。近期的开创性工作尝试从互相关函数开发似然“映射”，可用于直接将模型拟合与高分辨率数据集进行比较，从而解决上述问题。本文将介绍一个完全基于简单高斯似然的框架，与十多年来应用于凌日光变曲线的技术非常相似。该方法可用于以统计严谨的方式探索参数化模型大气的后验分布。然而，高分辨率检索仍存在一个重要且独特的问题：用于滤除恒星和大气吸收线的方法也会滤除系外行星的信号。本文将讨论一种简单快速的技术，能够对通过如SysRem和PCA等常用方法滤波后的数据进行检索。最后，将以超热木星WASP-121b的观测为例，展示该框架，包括对大气丰度和温度剖面的约束。

[6] Exploring Variability in the Atmospheres of Imaged Giant Exoplanets and Brown Dwarfs

2022-00-00

I propose to examine time-resolved spectra of variable ultracool objects (exoplanets, planetary mass companions, and brown dwarfs). I will perform Bayesian atmospheric retrievals on the spectra of these objects as they vary in order to see how the atmosphere varies spatially as they rotate. I will examine objects that span the L/T transition, compare low surface gravity objects to high gravity objects with similar properties, and examine high amplitude variables outside the L/T transition. I will perform retrievals using a Bayesian retrieval scheme with a machine learning based forward model trained using the Machine Learning Algorithm for Radiative Transfer of Generated Exoplanets (MARGE). Targets will be selected from the Cloud Atlas spectral library and from similar or related studies. This will be the first study to combine spatial information from variability monitoring with the detail of a full atmospheric retrieval. This study will contribute to the understanding of atmospheric processes in gas giants and brown dwarfs, as well as the effect of surface gravity on variability of ultracool atmospheres, which is currently not well understood.

[6] 探索直接成像巨型系外行星和褐矮星大气的变化性

我提议对可变超冷天体（系外行星、行星质量伴星和褐矮星）的时序光谱进行研究。我将对这些天体在变化过程中的光谱进行贝叶斯大气检索，以了解它们自转时大气的空间变化。我将研究跨越L/T转变的天体，将低表面重力天体与具有相似性质的高重力天体进行比较，并研究L/T转变之外的高振幅变量。我将采用基于机器学习的前向模型（使用Machine Learning Algorithm for Radiative Transfer of Generated Exoplanets，MARGE训练）进行贝叶斯检索。目标将从Cloud Atlas光谱库及相关研究中选取。这将是首次将变异监测获得的空间信息与完整大气检索的细节相结合的研究。本研究将有助于理解气态巨行星和褐矮星中的大气过程，以及表面重力对超冷大气变异性的影响，而这目前尚不清楚。

[7] Impacts of high-contrast image processing on atmospheric retrievals

2023-10-00

Many post-processing algorithms have been developed in order to better separate the signal of a companion from the bright light of the host star, but the effect of such algorithms on the shape of exoplanet spectra extracted from integral field spectrograph data is poorly understood. The resulting spectra are affected by noise that is correlated in wavelength space due to both optical and data processing effects. Within the framework of Bayesian atmospheric retrievals, we aim to understand how these correlations and other systematic effects impact the inferred physical parameters. We consider three algorithms (KLIP, PynPoint, and ANDROMEDA), optimising the choice of algorithmic parameters using a series of injection tests on archival SPHERE and GPI data of the HR 8799 system. The wavelength-dependent covariance matrix was calculated to provide a measure of instrumental and algorithmic systematics. We perform atmospheric retrievals using petit RADTRANS on optimally extracted spectra to measure how these data processing systematics influence the retrieved parameter distributions. The choice of data processing algorithm and parameters significantly impact the accuracy of retrieval results, with the mean posterior parameter bias ranging from 1 to 3 σ from the true input parameters. Including the full covariance matrix in the likelihood improves the accuracy of the inferred parameters, and cannot be accounted for using ad hoc scaling parameters in the retrieval framework. Using the Bayesian information criterion and other statistical measures as heuristic goodness-of-fit metrics, the retrievals including the full covariance matrix are favoured when compared to using only the diagonal elements.

[7] 高对比度图像处理对大气检索的影响

为更好地将伴星信号与主星强光分离，已经开发了多种后处理算法，但这些算法对从积分场光谱仪数据中提取的系外行星光谱形状的影响尚不清楚。所得光谱受到波长空间相关噪声的影响，这既有光学效应，也有数据处理效应。在贝叶斯大气检索框架下，我们旨在理解这些相关性及其他系统误差如何影响推断的物理参数。我们考察了三种算法（KLIP、PynPoint和ANDROMEDA），通过在HR 8799系统的SPHERE和GPI档案数据上进行一系列注入测试，优化算法参数选择。我们计算了波长相关的协方差矩阵，以衡量仪器和算法系统误差。我们在最优提取的光谱上使用petit RADTRANS进行大气检索，以评估这些数据处理系统误差对检索参数分布的影响。数据处理算法及其参数的选择对检索结果的准确性有显著影响，平均后验参数偏差相较真实输入参数在1到3σ之间。将完整协方差矩阵纳入似然函数可提高推断参数的准确性，这一效果无法通过检索框架中的临时缩放参数来补偿。采用贝叶斯信息准则和其他统计量作为启发式拟合优度指标时，包含完整协方差矩阵的检索优于仅使用对角元素的检索。

[8] Towards Generalized Characterization of Exoplanet Atmospheres with Transit Spectroscopy

2021-07-00

The field of exoplanetary sciences has grown from an era of detection to one of characterization. To date, over 4000 exoplanets have been discovered and over 50 of them have been observed with primary transit spectroscopy methods. The current population of characterized exoplanets spans a wide range of parameter space; from ultra-hot Jupiters with atmospheric temperatures beyond 3000 K, to temperate mini Neptunes that may host water in their atmospheres. Upcoming observational facilities in the next two decades will deliver exquisite spectra of exoplanet atmospheres at wavelengths never probed before, with unprecedented precision, and at much higher resolution than currently possible, effectively expanding the number of exoplanets with observed spectra. Nonetheless, an increasingly diverse planet population and higher fidelity data necessarily demand more flexible, complex, and generalized modeling frameworks. In this thesis, we present our work on atmospheric retrievals of exoplanets, focusing on investigating the robustness of the model assumptions inevitably employed to infer basic planetary conditions, compositional trends across the exoplanet mass range, and considerations for next-generation generalized retrieval frameworks. First, we present our systematic investigation of degeneracies between different model considerations in retrievals of transmission spectra and the observations that can resolve them. This study used a combination of Bayesian atmospheric retrievals and a range of common model assumptions, focusing on H2-rich atmospheres. We find that a combination of models including variable cloud coverage, prominent opacity sources, and high-precision optical and infrared spectra with current facilities enable constraints on cloud/haze properties and chemical abundances. Second, we apply our atmospheric retrieval framework to a large sample of 19 exoplanets ranging from cool mini-Neptunes to hot Jupiters. This effort constitutes the largest (i.e., broad wavelength coverage, multiple chemical species, mini-Neptunes to Jupiter sized planets) homogeneous chemical abundance survey for transiting exoplanets to date. We find a mass─metallicity trend of increasing H2O abundances with decreasing mass, significantly lower than the mass─metallicity relation for carbon in the solar system giant planets and similar predictions for exoplanets. On the other hand, the Na and K mass─metallicity trends are generally consistent with the solar system metallicity trend. We argue that the trends observed in this sample suggest different formation pathways for these close-in exoplanets compared to the long-period solar system giants. Third, we introduce Aurora, a next-generation retrieval framework for the characterization of H-rich and H-poor atmospheres. Here, we build upon state-of-the-art architectures and incorporate the following key advancements (a) a generalized compositional retrieval allowing for H-rich and H-poor atmospheres, (b) a generalized prescription for inhomogeneous clouds/hazes, (c) multiple Bayesian inference algorithms for high-dimensional retrievals, (d) modular considerations for refraction, forward scattering, and Mie scattering, and (e) noise modeling functionalities. We then carry out an investigation of the current and future chemical composition constraints for exoplanet atmospheres using this new retrieval framework. We estimate the abundance constraints achievable for hot Jupiters, mini Neptunes, and rocky exoplanets with current and upcoming observational facilities. Lastly, we present our contribution to recent studies characterizing exoplanet atmospheres using ground and space-based facilities. We perform atmospheric retrievals on a diverse population of exoplanets from ultra-hot Jupiters to temperate mini Neptunes. Among the planets studied are WASP-127b, WASP-33b, WASP-21b, K2-18b, KELT-11b, and HAT-P-41b. Our results add to the vast chemical inventory of atomic and molecular species found in exoplanet atmospheres. Moreover, our analyses unveil some of the challenges when interpreting high-precision spectroscopic data and possible instrument systematics. The atmospheric reconnaissance presented in this work explores some of the considerations needed for generalized characterization of exoplanet atmospheres with upcoming ground-based and space-based facilities. We conclude this dissertation by summarizing our findings and their implications to the broader field of exoplanet characterization. We discuss some of the outstanding questions from our research and the prospect of future modeling and retrieval approaches to robustly characterize exoplanet atmospheres. The lessons from this work highlight that, although the inferences derived from observations are strongly influenced by model assumptions, the use of physically motivated models with minimal assumptions, and broadband transmission spectra with current and future facilities can provide plausible estimates for the atmospheric properties for planets outside our solar system.

[8] 利用透射光谱实现系外行星大气的广义特征研究

系外行星科学领域已从发现时代进入表征时代。迄今已发现4000多颗系外行星，其中50多颗通过初级凌日光谱法被观测。当前已表征的系外行星涵盖了广泛参数空间：从大气温度超过3000 K的超热木星，到可能拥有水的大气的温和型迷你海王星。未来二十年即将上线的观测设施将在前所未及的波段、以前所未有的精度和更高分辨率获取系外行星大气的精细光谱，极大扩展可观测光谱的系外行星数量。然而，日益多样化的行星种群和更高质量的数据必然要求更灵活、复杂和通用的建模框架。本论文介绍了我们在系外行星大气检索方面的工作，重点研究了推断基本行星条件时模型假设的稳健性、跨行星质量范围的组成趋势，以及下一代通用检索框架的相关考量。首先，我们系统研究了透射光谱检索中不同模型假设之间的简并性及可区分这些简并的观测条件。该研究结合了贝叶斯大气检索和一系列常见模型假设，聚焦于H2丰富大气。我们发现，结合可变云覆盖、主要不透明源以及当前设施下高精度光学和红外光谱的模型，可对云/薄雾性质和化学丰度进行约束。其次，我们将大气检索框架应用于19颗从冷迷你海王星到热木星的系外行星样本。这是迄今为止最大规模（即宽波段覆盖、多种化学物种、从迷你海王星到木星大小行星）的凌日系外行星均匀化学丰度调查。我们发现，H2O丰度随质量降低而增加的质量-金属丰度趋势，显著低于太阳系巨行星碳的质量-金属丰度关系及对系外行星的类似预测。另一方面，Na和K的质量-金属丰度趋势与太阳系金属丰度趋势大致一致。我们认为，该样本中观察到的趋势表明这些近轨道系外行星与远日太阳系巨行星有不同的形成路径。第三，我们介绍了Aurora——用于H丰富和H贫大气表征的下一代检索框架。该框架在现有架构基础上，加入了以下关键进展：（a）通用组成检索，适用于H丰富和H贫大气；（b）通用不均匀云/薄雾处理；（c）多种贝叶斯推断算法，适用于高维检索；（d）可模块化考虑折射、前向散射和Mie散射；（e）噪声建模功能。随后，我们利用该新检索框架，研究了当前和未来观测设施下系外行星大气化学组成的约束能力。我们估算了当前及未来观测设施下对热木星、迷你海王星和类地系外行星可达的丰度约束。最后，我们介绍了近期利用地基和空间设施对系外行星大气进行表征的相关研究。我们对从超热木星到温和迷你海王星的多样化行星群体进行了大气检索，研究对象包括WASP-127b、WASP-33b、WASP-21b、K2-18b、KELT-11b和HAT-P-41b。我们的结果丰富了系外行星大气中已知的原子和分子物种清单。此外，我们的分析揭示了高精度光谱数据解释及仪器系统误差方面的挑战。本论文中的大气普查探讨了未来地基和空间设施下通用系外行星大气表征所需的若干考量。最后，我们总结了本研究的主要发现及其对系外行星表征领域的意义，讨论了研究中尚未解决的问题以及未来稳健表征系外行星大气的建模与检索前景。本研究的经验表明，尽管观测推断结果受模型假设影响较大，但采用物理动机的最小假设模型，以及当前和未来设施下的宽波段透射光谱，可为太阳系外行星的大气性质提供合理估算。

8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] Revisiting the Iconic Spitzer Phase Curve of 55 Cancri e: Not So Strange After All?

2022-06-00

Thermal phase curves of short-period planets provide the best constraints on the atmospheric dynamics and heat transport in exoplanet atmospheres. The published Spitzer phase curve of 55 Cancri e, an ultra-short period super-Earth, exhibits a large hot spot offset, suggesting significant eastward heat recirculation, unexpected on such a hot planet. We present our re-reduction and analysis of these iconic observations using the open source and modular Spitzer Phase Curve Analysis Pipeline (SPCA). In particular, we attempt to reproduce the published analysis using bi-linear interpolation, but also test alternative instrument detrending schemes. We study how the retrieved dayside temperature, nightside temperature, and longitudinal offset of the planet's hotspot depend on the reduction and detrending. Our re-analysis suggests that 55 Cancri e may have a negligible hot spot offset of 14 ± 10 degrees west, as expected from theoretical considerations and consistent with other ultra-short-period planets. We also report an eclipse depth of 195 ± 24 p.p.m., a phase semi-amplitude of 95 ± 13 p.p.m., a dayside brightness temperature of 3767 ± 424 K and a nightside brightness temperature of 1033 ± 253 K.

[1] 重访标志性Spitzer 55 Cancri e相位曲线：其实并不奇怪？

短周期行星的热相位曲线为系外行星大气动力学和热量输运提供了最佳约束。已发表的 Spitzer 55 Cancri e 相位曲线显示出显著的热点偏移，表明存在强烈的向东热再循环，这在如此高温的行星上是出乎意料的。我们利用开源模块化的 Spitzer Phase Curve Analysis Pipeline（SPCA）对这些标志性观测数据进行了重新归约和分析。我们尝试通过双线性插值复现已发表的分析结果，同时也测试了其他仪器去趋势方法。我们研究了所检索的日面温度、夜面温度和行星热点经度偏移如何依赖于数据归约和去趋势方法。我们的再分析表明，55 Cancri e 的热点偏移可能可以忽略不计，为 14 ± 10 度偏西，这与理论预期及其他超短周期行星一致。我们还报告了 195 ± 24 ppm 的食深、95 ± 13 ppm 的相位半振幅、3767 ± 424 K 的日面亮温和 1033 ± 253 K 的夜面亮温。

[2] Revisiting the Iconic Spitzer Phase Curve of 55 Cancri e: Hotter Dayside, Cooler Nightside, and Smaller Phase Offset

2022-11-00

Thermal phase curves of short-period exoplanets provide the best constraints on the atmospheric dynamics and heat transport in their atmospheres. The published Spitzer Space Telescope phase curve of 55 Cancri e, an ultra-short-period super-Earth, exhibits a large phase offset suggesting significant eastward heat recirculation, unexpected on such a hot planet. We present our rereduction and analysis of these iconic observations using the open source and modular Spitzer Phase Curve Analysis pipeline. In particular, we attempt to reproduce the published analysis using the same instrument detrending scheme as the original authors. We retrieve the dayside temperature ( ${T}_{\mathrm{day}}={3771}_{-520}^{+669}$ K), nightside temperature ( ${T}_{\mathrm{night}\,}\lt 1649$ K at 2σ), and longitudinal offset of the planet's hot spot, and quantify how they depend on the reduction and detrending. Our reanalysis suggests that 55 Cancri e has a negligible hot spot offset of $-{12}_{-18}^{+21}$ degrees east. The small phase offset and cool nightside are consistent with the poor heat transport expected on ultra-short-period planets. The high dayside 4.5 μm brightness temperature is qualitatively consistent with SiO emission from an inverted rock vapor atmosphere.

[2] 重访标志性Spitzer 55 Cancri e相位曲线：更热的昼面、更冷的夜面和更小的相位偏移

短周期系外行星的热相位曲线为其大气动力学和热量输运提供了最佳约束。超短周期超级地球 55 Cancri e 的已发表 Spitzer 空间望远镜相位曲线显示出较大的相位偏移，暗示存在显著的向东热再循环，这在如此高温行星上并不常见。我们利用开源模块化的 Spitzer Phase Curve Analysis 管道对这些标志性观测进行了重新归约和分析。特别地，我们尝试采用与原作者相同的仪器去趋势方案复现已发表的分析结果。我们检索了白昼面温度（${T}_{\mathrm{day}}={3771}_{-520}^{+669}$ K）、夜晚面温度（${T}_{\mathrm{night}\,}\lt 1649$ K，2σ）以及行星热点的经度偏移，并量化了这些参数对归约和去趋势方法的依赖性。我们的再分析表明，55 Cancri e 的热点偏移可以忽略，为 $-{12}_{-18}^{+21}$ 度向东。小的相位偏移和较冷的夜晚面与超短周期行星预期的低效热输运一致。白昼面 4.5 μm 的高亮温与反转岩石蒸气大气中的 SiO 发射在定性上相符。

[3] Spitzer's Exoplanet Legacy: Population Trends from Phase Curve Observations

2023-01-00

While the Spitzer telescope has officially reached the end of its mission, it remains a powerful force in exoplanet characterization. Spitzer 3.6 and 4.5 micron phase curve observations allow us to measure key parameters that result from a planet's unique atmospheric circulation such as heat redistribution efficiency and hot spot shifts due to equatorial jets. Now in the era of comparative exoplanet studies, an understanding of the observed trends in these parameters is key to our understanding of the Hot Jupiter population. However, to ensure any observed trends are accurate, we must be sure that our techniques to extract these signals from the instrumental noise are reliable and reproducible. Towards those means, we have developed a more robust intrapixel sensitivity detrending method to remove the main systematic in Spitzer IRAC observations. We are using our new fixed intrapixel sensitivity map to perform as uniform of an analysis as allowed by the phase curve data. I will discuss trends in observed Hot Jupiter atmospheric parameters, as well as how these observed trends compare to three dimensional circulation model predictions to determine what they teach us about the Hot Jupiter population. Future work will continue to extend this uniform analysis to the entire population of Spitzer phase curves and improve our modeling techniques to better compare between models and data.

[3] Spitzer系外行星遗产：来自相位曲线观测的人口统计趋势

尽管Spitzer望远镜已正式结束任务，但它在系外行星特性研究中仍然具有强大作用。Spitzer 3.6和4.5微米相位曲线观测使我们能够测量由行星独特大气环流导致的关键参数，如热量再分布效率和由于赤道喷流引起的热点偏移。如今已进入系外行星对比研究时代，理解这些参数的观测趋势对于我们理解热木星种群至关重要。然而，为确保观测趋势的准确性，我们必须确保从仪器噪声中提取这些信号的方法是可靠且可复现的。为此，我们开发了一种更为稳健的像素内灵敏度去趋势方法，以去除Spitzer IRAC观测中的主要系统误差。我们正在利用新的固定像素内灵敏度图，对相位曲线数据进行尽可能统一的分析。我将讨论热木星大气参数的观测趋势，以及这些趋势与三维环流模型预测的对比，从而探讨它们对热木星种群的启示。未来工作将继续将这种统一分析扩展到全部Spitzer相位曲线样本，并改进我们的建模技术，以更好地实现模型与数据的对比。

[4] Spitzer thermal phase curve of WASP-121 b

2023-08-00

<BR /> Aims: We analyse unpublished Spitzer observations of the thermal phase-curve of WASP-121 b, a benchmark ultra-hot Jupiter. <BR /> Methods: We adopted the wavelet pixel-independent component analysis technique to remove challenging instrumental systematic effects in these datasets and we fit them simultaneously with parametric light-curve models. We also performed phase-curve retrievals to better understand the horizontal and vertical thermal structure of the planetary atmosphere. <BR /> Results: We measured planetary brightness temperatures of ~2700 K (dayside) and ~700-1100 K (nightside), along with modest peak offsets of 5.9° ± 1.6 (3.6 µm) and 5.0°<SUB>‒3.1</SUB><SUP>+3.4</SUP> (4.5 µm) after mid-eclipse. These results suggest inefficient heat redistribution in the atmosphere of WASP-121 b. The inferred atmospheric Bond albedo and circulation efficiency align well with observed trends for hot giant exoplanets. Interestingly, the measured peak offsets correspond to a westward hot spot, which has rarely been observed. We also report consistent transit depths at 3.6 and 4.5 µm, along with updated geometric and orbital parameters. Finally, we compared our Spitzer results with previous measurements, including recent JWST observations. <BR /> Conclusions: We extracted new information on the thermal properties and dynamics of an exoplanet atmosphere from an especially problematic dataset. This study probes the reliability of exoplanet phase-curve parameters obtained from Spitzer observations when state-of-the-art pipelines are adopted to remove the instrumental systematic effects. It demonstrates that Spitzer phase-curve observations provide a useful baseline for comparison with JWST observations, and shows the increase in parameters precision achieved with the newer telescope.

[4] WASP-121 b的Spitzer热相位曲线

目标：我们分析了尚未发表的WASP-121 b（一颗基准超热木星）热相位曲线的Spitzer观测数据。 方法：我们采用小波像素独立成分分析技术，以去除这些数据集中具有挑战性的仪器系统效应，并与参数化光变曲线模型同时拟合。我们还进行了相位曲线检索，以更好地理解行星大气的水平和垂直热结构。 结果：我们测得行星白昼面亮温约为2700 K，夜晚面约为700-1100 K，峰值偏移分别为5.9° ± 1.6（3.6 µm）和5.0°<SUB>‒3.1</SUB><SUP>+3.4</SUP>（4.5 µm），均发生在中掩食后。这些结果表明WASP-121 b大气中的热量再分配效率较低。推断的大气Bond反照率和环流效率与热巨行星的观测趋势一致。有趣的是，测得的峰值偏移对应于西向热点，这种现象很少被观测到。我们还报告了3.6和4.5 µm波段一致的凌日深度，以及更新后的几何和轨道参数。最后，我们将Spitzer结果与先前的观测，包括最新的JWST观测进行了比较。 结论：我们从一个特别有问题的数据集中提取了关于系外行星大气热性质和动力学的新信息。本研究检验了采用最先进数据处理流程去除仪器系统效应后，Spitzer观测获得的系外行星相位曲线参数的可靠性。结果表明，Spitzer相位曲线观测为与JWST观测的对比提供了有用的基线，并展示了新一代望远镜带来的参数精度提升。

[5] Investigation of the infrared profiles of exoplanet HD 209458 b secondary eclipses

2024-12-00

In this work we process infrared light curves for 13 secondary eclipses of the exoplanet HD 209458 b, obtained with the Spitzer Space Telescope. The objective of the study was to identify any discrepancies that might be attributed to the inhomogeneity of the planet's surface brightness or a difference of the planet's shape from a sphere. Firstly, an attempt was made to ascertain whether the observed midpoint of the secondary eclipse exhibited a shift from the ephemeris moment. This could indicate the presence of a hot spot on the planet's surface that is shifted from the central meridian. The analysis allowed for the correlated noise that remained in the IRAC detector data after aperture photometry by approximating the noise with a Gaussian random process. As a result, we detected two components of red noise with different time scales: $\sim\!10$ s and $\sim\!5$ min. In addition, we constructed a unified model of the effect of inhomogeneous pixel sensitivity as a periodic function of the brightness centroid coordinates, which works well for all the analyzed eclipses. According to the results, the main parameters of the HD 209458 b eclipse coincided with those obtained by other authors: a depth of $0.125 \pm 0.004$% and a shift of the observed midpoint of $-0.53 \pm 0.61$ min.

[5] HD 209458 b次生凌星红外光谱的研究

本工作处理了利用 Spitzer 空间望远镜获得的系外行星 HD 209458 b 13 次次生食的红外光变曲线。研究目标是识别可能归因于行星表面亮度不均或行星形状偏离球体的任何差异。首先，尝试确定观测到的次生食中点是否相对于星历时刻发生偏移，这可能表明行星表面存在偏离中央子午线的热点。分析中考虑了在光圈光度测量后 IRAC 探测器数据中残留的相关噪声，通过高斯随机过程对噪声进行近似。结果检测到两种不同时间尺度的红噪声分量：约10秒和约5分钟。此外，我们构建了一个统一模型，将像素灵敏度不均的影响表示为亮度质心坐标的周期函数，对所有分析的次生食均适用。结果显示，HD 209458 b 次生食的主要参数与其他作者获得的结果一致：食深为0.125 ± 0.004%，观测中点偏移为-0.53 ± 0.61分钟。

[6] A JWST Optical and Infrared Phase Curve of NGTS-10b

2024-02-00

Since the first photometric phase curve observation from Spitzer, hot Jupiter atmospheres have challenged our understanding of atmospheric dynamics. Large studies have shown that many optical phase curves exhibit westward offsets, while most infrared phase curves have eastward offsets. The former is attributed to the enhanced formation of reflective clouds near the morning terminator, whereas the latter is connected primarily to equatorial jets redistributing heat from the hot dayside to the cooler nightside. This inverse correlation between the cloud and temperature maps can provide strong insights into cloud formation processes in hot Jupiters. We will present results from a full-orbit phase curve of NGTS-10b, measured simultaneously at optical and near-IR wavelengths using the unique capabilities of NIRSpec/PRISM. This includes showing the planet's wavelength-dependent hotspot offsets and heat redistribution efficiencies, phase-resolved emission spectra, and transmission spectrum. We will also discuss how our isobaric map of NGTS-10b informs current theoretical models and its implications on future JWST phase curve observations.

[6] NGTS-10b的JWST光学和红外相位曲线

自Spitzer首次获得光度相位曲线观测以来，热木星大气一直挑战着我们对大气动力学的理解。大量研究表明，许多光学相位曲线表现出西向偏移，而大多数红外相位曲线则为东向偏移。前者归因于晨昏线附近反射性云层的增强形成，后者主要与赤道喷流将热量从炽热的昼面输送到较冷的夜面有关。云层与温度分布图之间的这种反相关性可为热木星云层形成过程提供重要见解。我们将展示NGTS-10b的全轨道相位曲线结果，该曲线利用NIRSpec/PRISM的独特能力在光学和近红外波段同时测量。内容包括行星的波长依赖性热点偏移与热量再分布效率、相位分辨发射光谱和透射光谱。我们还将讨论NGTS-10b等压面分布图对当前理论模型的启示及其对未来JWST相位曲线观测的意义。

[7] Search for deviations in exoplanetary infrared profiles, based on the secondary eclipse analysis

2022-00-00

Aperture photometry of Spitzer space telescope observations of HD209458 b in the 3.6µm channel was conducted. To find deviations in the exoplanetary infrared emission, the modelling of secondary eclipse light curves was carried out. The noise from intrapixel sensitivity variations was modelled by Gaussian processes. The obtained value of the eclipse depth is 0.094±0.013% of the total flux of the system and is consistent with the results of previous work and the works of other authors.

[7] 基于二次凌星分析的系外行星红外谱型偏差搜索

对HD209458 b在3.6µm通道的Spitzer空间望远镜观测数据进行了孔径光度测量。为寻找系外行星红外发射的偏差，对次生凌光变曲线进行了建模。像素内灵敏度变化带来的噪声通过高斯过程建模。获得的凌深为系统总通量的0.094±0.013%，与以往本组及其他作者的结果一致。

[8] An Analysis of Spitzer Phase Curves for WASP-77Ab and WASP-121b

2024-02-00

Although Spitzer is no longer an active mission, its collection of over 30 exoplanet phase curve observations remain a valuable resource for comparative planetology of hot Jupiters and for informing future observations with JWST. For individual planets, measurements of key phase curve parameters such as amplitude and hotspot offset provide evidence for the presence of clouds, magnetic fields, etc., which affect atmospheric dynamics. WASP-77Ab and WASP-121b are two hot Jupiters with comparable orbital periods of around 1.5 days but disparate equilibrium temperatures. Spitzer observed each planet for a full orbit at both 3.6 µm and 4.5 µm. We will present an analysis of these phase curves and their derived parameters. We will discuss predicted dynamic effects on the atmospheres of both planets, as well as comparisons of calculated planetary parameters between Spitzer and JWST NIRSpec observations, whose NRS1 and NRS2 channels broadly align with the two warm-Spitzer mission channels. Finally, we will discuss an assessment of the empirical relationship between orbital period and hotspot offset for hot Jupiters.

[8] WASP-77Ab和WASP-121b的Spitzer相位曲线分析

尽管Spitzer已不再是活跃任务，其收集的30余条系外行星相位曲线观测仍是热木星比较行星学和指导未来JWST观测的重要资源。对于单个行星，相位曲线关键参数（如振幅和热点偏移）的测量为云层、磁场等影响大气动力学的因素提供了证据。WASP-77Ab和WASP-121b是两颗轨道周期约1.5天但平衡温度差异显著的热木星。Spitzer在3.6 µm和4.5 µm波段对每颗行星均进行了完整轨道的观测。我们将展示这些相位曲线及其推导参数的分析结果，讨论对两颗行星大气的动力学效应预测，以及Spitzer与JWST NIRSpec观测（NRS1和NRS2通道大致对应Spitzer两个波段）下行星参数的比较。最后，我们将评估热木星轨道周期与热点偏移之间的经验关系。

[1] An Exploration of Retrieval Choices for JWST Transit Spectra of Hot Jupiters: Varying Sampling Algorithms, Wavelength Coverage, Spectral Resolution, and Multi-Dimensionality of Models.

2024-02-00

Among the various methods for characterizing exoplanet atmospheres, transit spectroscopy has proven to be the most prolific. Hot Jupiters, while relatively rare, have emerged as favorable targets for spectral analysis. Their hot temperatures, close proximity to their host stars, and low-density atmospheres enable unparalleled insight into their structure and composition. Previous studies that investigated the atmospheres of Hot Jupiters using transmission spectroscopy were hampered by restricted wavelength coverage. Superior quality data demonstrated by JWST ERS and cycle 1 is pushing the community to upgrade their analysis methods to extract as much detail as possible. In our project, we assess to what degree the expanded wavelength coverage offered by JWST necessitates further complexity beyond the first generation of one-dimensional retrieval frameworks. We conducted several studies investigating trade-offs between wavelength coverage and signal-to-noise ratios. As part of this project, we have made significant improvements to the existing transit modeling and retrieval code, METIS, making it more suitable for application to real transit observations. Notably, we have incorporated Hamiltonian Monte Carlo techniques for parameter retrievals in our transit spectral analysis, a departure from previous Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) and nested sampling methods. We anticipate that it will be useful for multi-dimensional models and joint analysis of multiple datasets, as HMC has demonstrated to converge more robustly than MCMC for models with large numbers of parameters in other applications.

[1] JWST热木星凌日光谱检索选择探索：采样算法、波长覆盖、光谱分辨率和模型多维度的变化

在多种系外行星大气表征方法中，透射光谱已被证明是最为高产的。热木星虽然相对罕见，但因其高温、靠近母星及低密度大气，成为光谱分析的优选目标。这些特性使我们能够前所未有地深入了解其结构和成分。以往利用透射光谱研究热木星大气的工作受限于波长覆盖范围。JWST ERS和第一周期展示的高质量数据推动了社区升级分析方法，以尽可能提取更多细节。在本项目中，我们评估了JWST扩展波长覆盖是否需要超越第一代一维检索框架的复杂性。我们开展了多项研究，探讨波长覆盖与信噪比之间的权衡。作为项目的一部分，我们对现有的凌星建模与检索代码METIS进行了重大改进，使其更适用于实际凌星观测。值得注意的是，我们在凌星光谱分析中引入了Hamiltonian Monte Carlo技术进行参数检索，这与以往的Markov Chain Monte Carlo（MCMC）和嵌套采样方法不同。我们预计该方法对于多维模型和多数据集联合分析将非常有用，因为HMC在参数数量较多的模型中已被证明比MCMC收敛更稳健。

[2] Exoplanet atmospheric retrievals through different JWST/NIRISS data reduction pipelines

2025-09-00

Since the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) became available in 2022, its observations of exoplanet transit spectra have revolutionized the field of exoplanet science. Its observations have enabled significant new discoveries, in particular, contributing to the ongoing effort of characterizing a wide array of exoplanet atmospheres [1][2][3]. Distinct data reduction pipelines have been produced to process JWST observational spectra [4][5] - which for some cases have resulted in slightly distinct transmission spectra, which may consequentially lead to distinct interpretations when characterizing said planetary atmospheres.The goal of this study is to have a grasp of the extent of how distinct data reduction pipelines affect the extraction of transmission spectra and the consequent characterization of exoplanet atmospheres. Here we present an analysis of a small array of hot Jupiter transit spectra by the JWST/NIRISS instrument [6], whose 2 major spectral orders cover the wavelength range 0.7 μm to 2.5 μm. The same dataset of transit observations for this small exoplanet population were processed through multiple pipelines [4][5].Here we explore how distinct pipelines extract distinct exoplanet transmission spectra from the same observational dataset - across a small sample of 5 hot Jupiters. We then apply the exoplanet atmospheric retrieval code TauREx 3 [7] to the distinct transmission spectra extracted by the several distinct data reduction pipelines. This allows to retrieve the distinct set of parameters that characterize these planetary atmospheres - and to compare how the distinct pipelines affect the retrieved parameters that characterize the planetary atmospheres across this small sample of hot Jupiters. This may provide a useful cross-validation between distinct data reduction approaches for this JWST instrument - increasingly relevant as new dedicated missions to the study of exoplanet atmospheres through transit spectroscopy - such as the ESA Ariel mission [8] - are expected to come online on the coming years.Figure 1: WASP-39b JWST/NIRISS transmission spectrum extracted by 6 distinct data reduction pipelines, including our group's IRACLIS pipeline. [2] 通过不同JWST/NIRISS数据处理流程进行系外行星大气检索

自2022年詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）投入使用以来，其对系外行星凌日光谱的观测彻底改变了系外行星科学领域。JWST的观测推动了大量新发现，特别是在持续刻画多样化系外行星大气方面做出了重要贡献[1][2][3]。针对JWST观测光谱，已经开发出多种数据处理流程[4][5]——在某些情况下，这些流程会产生略有不同的透射光谱，进而在刻画行星大气时可能导致不同的解释。本研究的目标是了解不同数据处理流程对透射光谱提取及后续系外行星大气刻画的影响程度。我们分析了一组由JWST/NIRISS仪器[6]获得的热木星凌日光谱，其两个主要光谱阶覆盖0.7 μm至2.5 μm波段。针对这组小样本系外行星的同一组凌日观测数据，我们采用了多种数据处理流程[4][5]进行处理。我们探讨了不同流程如何从同一观测数据集中提取出不同的系外行星透射光谱——样本包括5颗热木星。随后，我们将多种数据处理流程提取的不同透射光谱输入系外行星大气检索代码TauREx 3[7]，以检索描述这些行星大气的参数，并比较不同流程对这些参数的影响。这为JWST仪器不同数据处理方法之间的交叉验证提供了有用参考——随着如ESA Ariel任务[8]等专注于透射光谱研究系外行星大气的新任务即将上线，这一问题日益重要。图1：WASP-39b的JWST/NIRISS透射光谱由6种不同数据处理流程提取，包括我们团队的IRACLIS流程。数据来自Feinstein等（2022）[4]的数据发布。参考文献：[1] - Tsai S. 等, 2023, Nature, 617, https://doi.org/10.1038/s41586-023-05902-2, [2] - Taylor.
