[1] Biases in Exoplanet Transmission Spectra Introduced by Limb-darkening Parametrization

2024-11-00

One of the main endeavors of the field of exoplanetary sciences is the characterization of exoplanet atmospheres on a population level. The current method of choice to accomplish this task is transmission spectroscopy, where the apparent radius of a transiting exoplanet is measured at multiple wavelengths in search of atomic and molecular absorption features produced by the upper atmosphere constituents. To extract the planetary radius from a transit light curve, it is necessary to account for the decrease in luminosity away from the center of the projected stellar disk, known as limb darkening. Physically motivated parametrizations of limb darkening, in particular of the quadratic form, are commonly used in exoplanet transit light-curve fitting. Here, we show that such parametrizations can introduce significant wavelength-dependent biases in the transmission spectra currently obtained with all instrument modes of the JWST, and thus have the potential to affect atmospheric inferences. To avoid such biases, we recommend the use of standard limb-darkening parametrizations with wide uninformative priors that allow for nonphysical stellar intensity profiles in the transit fits, and thus for a complete and symmetrical exploration of the parameter space. We further find that fitting the light curves at the native resolution results in errors on the measured transit depths that are significantly smaller compared to light curves that are binned in wavelength before fitting, thus potentially maximizing the amount of information that can be extracted from the data.

[1] 边缘暗化参数化对系外行星透射光谱引入的偏差

系外行星科学领域的主要目标之一是在群体层面刻画系外行星大气。目前实现这一目标的首选方法是透射光谱，即在多个波长测量凌日系外行星的表观半径，以寻找由高层大气成分产生的原子和分子吸收特征。要从凌日光变曲线中提取行星半径，必须考虑投影恒星盘中心向外亮度递减的现象，即边缘变暗。物理动机的边缘变暗参数化，尤其是二次型，常用于系外行星凌日光变曲线拟合。本文表明，这类参数化会在目前所有 JWST 仪器模式获得的透射光谱中引入显著的波长相关偏差，进而可能影响大气推断。为避免此类偏差，我们建议在拟合中采用标准边缘变暗参数化，并使用宽泛的无信息先验，允许出现非物理的恒星强度分布，从而对参数空间进行完整且对称的探索。我们还发现，在本征分辨率下拟合光变曲线，测得的凌日深度误差显著小于先按波长分箱后再拟合的情况，因此可最大化数据中可提取的信息量。

[2] A critical assessment of the applicability of the energy-limited approximation for estimating exoplanetary mass-loss rates

2021-06-00

Context. The energy-limited atmospheric escape approach is widely used to estimate mass-loss rates for a broad range of planets that host hydrogen-dominated atmospheres as well as for performing atmospheric evolution calculations. <BR /> Aims: We aim to study the applicability range of the energy-limited atmospheric escape approximation. <BR /> Methods: We revise the energy-limited atmospheric escape formalism and the involved assumptions. We also compare the results of the energy-limited formalism with those of hydrodynamic simulations, employing a grid covering planets with masses, radii, and equilibrium temperatures ranging between 1 M<SUB>⊕</SUB> and 39 M<SUB>⊕</SUB>, 1 R<SUB>⊕</SUB> and 10 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>, and 300 and 2000 K, respectively. <BR /> Results: Within the grid boundaries, we find that the energy-limited approximation gives a correct order of magnitude estimate for mass-loss rates for about 76% of the planets, but there can be departures from hydrodynamic simulations by up to two to three orders of magnitude in individual cases. Furthermore, we find that planets for which the mass-loss rates are correctly estimated by the energy-limited approximation to within one order of magnitude have intermediate gravitational potentials (≈2.5-5.5 ×10<SUP>8</SUP> J kg<SUP>‒1</SUP>) as well as low-to-intermediate equilibrium temperatures and irradiation fluxes of extreme ultraviolet and X-ray radiation. However, for planets with low or high gravitational potentials, or high equilibrium temperatures and irradiation fluxes, the approximation fails in most cases. <BR /> Conclusions: The energy-limited approximation should not be used for planetary evolution calculations that require computing mass-loss rates for planets that cover a broad parameter space. In this case, it is very likely that the energy-limited approximation would at times return mass-loss rates of up to several orders of magnitude above or below those predicted by hydrodynamic simulations. For planetary atmospheric evolution calculations, interpolation routines or approximations based on grids of hydrodynamic models should be used instead.

[2] 对能量限制近似法在估算系外行星质量损失率适用性的关键评估

背景：能量限制大气逃逸方法被广泛用于估算以氢为主大气的各类行星的质量损失率，并用于大气演化计算。 目的：我们旨在研究能量限制大气逃逸近似的适用范围。 方法：我们修订了能量限制大气逃逸的公式及相关假设，并将其结果与流体动力学模拟进行比较，模拟网格覆盖行星质量、半径和平衡温度分别在1 M⊕到39 M⊕、1 R⊕到10 R⊕、300到2000 K范围内。 结果：在网格范围内，能量限制近似对约76%的行星能给出质量损失率的数量级估算，但在个别情况下与流体动力学模拟结果可相差2到3个数量级。此外，我们发现，能量限制近似能在一个数量级内正确估算质量损失率的行星，其引力势（约2.5-5.5 ×10^8 J kg^‒1）为中等，且平衡温度和极紫外/X射线辐照通量为低到中等。而对于低或高引力势，或高平衡温度和高辐照通量的行星，该近似在大多数情况下失效。 结论：对于需要计算覆盖广泛参数空间行星质量损失率的行星演化计算，不应使用能量限制近似。在这种情况下，该近似很可能会给出比流体动力学模拟高出或低出几个数量级的质量损失率。对于行星大气演化计算，应采用基于流体动力学模型网格的插值方法或近似。

[3] Exploring Systematic Errors in the Inferred Parameters of the Transiting Planets

2024-04-00

Transiting planet systems offer a unique opportunity to measure precise masses and radii of planets and their host stars. However, relative photometry and radial velocity measurements alone only constrain the host star density, leaving a one-parameter mass-radius degeneracy. We assess the magnitude of systematic errors in the derived system parameters relative to their statistical precision due to different methods of breaking this degeneracy. We first model extant data for the typical hot Jupiter system KELT-15 using EXOFASTv2, considering four methods of breaking the stellar mass-radius degeneracy. We find systematic differences in the inferred physical parameters of the KELT-15 system, including a 6.5% (~1.8 σ) difference in the stellar and planetary radii based the host star mass-radius degeneracy-breaking method employed. We then evaluate the systematic errors for several systems of M-dwarfs transiting FGK host stars. M-dwarfs are the most abundant stars in the galaxy and popular targets for exoplanet searches. However, we only know of dozens of M-dwarfs with fundamental parameters of mass, radius and effective temperature characterized to better than a few percent. Similarly to transiting planets, M-dwarfs in eclipsing binaries can also be robustly characterized. Here we present several targets from the Eclipsing Binary Low Mass (EBLM) survey where we measure M-dwarf masses with precisions better than 5%, radii better than 3% and effective temperatures on order 1%. Using the same methodology employed for KELT-15, we determine that the model uncertainty in the primary star is of similar magnitude to the statistical uncertainty in the model fits of the secondary M-dwarf. Therefore, whilst these can be considered benchmark M-dwarfs, we caution the community to consider model uncertainty when pushing the limits of precise stellar characterization. Finally, we present a homogenous analysis of ~10 hot Jupiter planetary systems with secondary eclipses observed by TESS with consistent systematic uncertainties in their derived properties. Where possible we homogenously estimate albedos, temperatures, and eccentricities of these systems in addition to updated ephemerides. Understanding the eccentricity of these systems is important in understanding the formation of hot Jupiters.

[3] 探索凌星行星推断参数中的系统误差

凌星行星系统为测量行星及其宿主恒星的精确质量和半径提供了独特机会。然而，相对光度和径向速度测量只能约束宿主恒星的密度，导致质量-半径之间存在一参数简并性。我们评估了由于不同打破该简并性方法导致的系统参数系统误差相对于其统计精度的大小。我们首先利用EXOFASTv2对典型热木星系统KELT-15的现有数据进行建模，考虑了四种打破恒星质量-半径简并性的方法。我们发现KELT-15系统推断的物理参数存在系统性差异，包括根据所采用的恒星质量-半径简并性打破方法，恒星和行星半径存在6.5%（约1.8σ）的差异。随后我们评估了多颗M矮星凌越FGK型宿主星系统的系统误差。M矮星是银河系中最丰富的恒星类型，也是系外行星搜寻的热门目标。然而，目前只有几十颗M矮星的质量、半径和有效温度等基本参数被精确测量到优于几个百分点。类似于凌星行星，食双星中的M矮星也可以被稳健地表征。我们在Eclipsing Binary Low Mass（EBLM）巡天中展示了多个目标，其M矮星质量测量精度优于5%，半径优于3%，有效温度约为1%。采用与KELT-15相同的方法，我们确定主星的模型不确定性与次星M矮星模型拟合的统计不确定性相当。因此，尽管这些可以被视为基准M矮星，我们仍提醒社区在追求高精度恒星参数表征时需考虑模型不确定性。最后，我们对约10个TESS观测到次生凌星的热木星行星系统进行了同质化分析，获得了其推导属性中一致的系统不确定性。在可能的情况下，我们还同质化估算了这些系统的反照率、温度和偏心率，并更新了历元。理解这些系统的偏心率对于理解热木星的形成过程非常重要。

[4] Where does the simplified stellar contamination model fail in exoplanet transmission spectroscopy?

2026-02-00

Stellar photospheric heterogeneities (e.g., starspots and faculae) distort the apparent stellar spectrum during a transit and imprint wavelength-dependent biases on the measured planet─to─star radius ratio. This transit light source effect (TLSE) must be accounted for to obtain reliable atmospheric properties. A widely used approach is the Rackham─TLSE (R─TLSE) prescription, which applies a disk-averaged contamination correction based solely on the filling factor and spectral contrast. However, accurate transmission-spectroscopy interpretations require models that also account for limb darkening, the spatial distribution of active regions, and transit geometry. In this work, we incorporated these effects into a self-consistent, pixel-resolved framework, ECLIPSE-Xλ, and performed idealized, noise-free model─model comparisons against the R─TLSE approximation. Using three archetypal systems ─ the super-Earth LHS 1140 b, the mini-Neptune K2-18 b, and the hot Jupiter WASP-69 b ─ we show that disk-averaged TLSE corrections can differ from the self-consistent model by up to ~400 ppm in the optical for active hosts and non-equatorial transits, while remaining below ~10 ppm at near-infrared wavelengths where limb darkening is weaker. We then applied both approaches to the JWST/NIRISS SOSS transmission spectrum of LHS 1140 b. When limb darkening is artificially set to zero, ECLIPSE-Xλ recovers stellar-contamination parameters that closely match the reference R─TLSE solution, confirming that the two frameworks are consistent in the disk-averaged limit. With wavelength-dependent limb darkening included, however, reproducing the observed short-wavelength slope through stellar contamination alone requires very hot faculae (∆T<SUB>fac</SUB> ≃ 600 K) covering f<SUB>fac</SUB> ≃ 0.35 of the visible hemisphere, corresponding to an equivalent circular facular region with radius ≃0.6 R<SUB>⋆</SUB> (i.e., about 60% of the stellar radius) on the stellar disk. Such an extended, unocculted active region would be physically unlikely even for an active M dwarf. In this context, a purely stellar-contamination explanation for any residual optical slope would demand rather extreme facular populations; scenarios in which a genuine atmospheric contribution helps complement a more modest facular signal appear more physically plausible. Taken together, these results delineate the regime of validity of the R─TLSE approximation and underscore the need for geometry-aware stellar-heterogeneity models that explicitly account for limb darkening in high-precision transmission spectroscopy.

[4] 简化恒星污染模型在系外行星透射光谱中何处失效？

恒星光球非均匀性（如恒星黑子和亮斑）会在凌日过程中扭曲表观恒星光谱，并在测量的行星-恒星半径比上引入波长相关的偏差。这种凌日光源效应（TLSE）必须加以考虑，才能获得可靠的大气性质。广泛使用的方法是Rackham-TLSE（R-TLSE）方案，仅基于填充因子和光谱对比度进行盘面平均污染修正。然而，准确的透射光谱解释需要模型同时考虑边缘变暗、活跃区空间分布和凌日几何。在本工作中，我们将这些效应纳入自洽、像素分辨的ECLIPSE-Xλ框架，并与R-TLSE近似进行了理想化、无噪声的模型-模型对比。以三种典型系统——超级地球LHS 1140 b、迷你海王星K2-18 b和热木星WASP-69 b为例，我们发现对于活跃宿主和非赤道凌日，盘面平均TLSE修正与自洽模型在光学波段可相差高达约400 ppm，而在边缘变暗较弱的近红外波段则低于约10 ppm。随后我们将两种方法应用于LHS 1140 b的JWST/NIRISS SOSS透射光谱。当边缘变暗被人为设为零时，ECLIPSE-Xλ恢复的恒星污染参数与R-TLSE参考解高度一致，证实两种框架在盘面平均极限下是一致的。然而，若考虑波长相关的边缘变暗，仅通过恒星污染重现观测到的短波斜率需非常高温的亮斑（∆T<SUB>fac</SUB> ≃ 600 K），覆盖可见半球f<SUB>fac</SUB> ≃ 0.35，相当于恒星盘面上半径约0.6 R<SUB>⋆</SUB>（即约60%恒星半径）的等效圆形亮斑区域。即使对于活跃M矮星，如此大面积未被遮挡的活跃区在物理上也极不可能。在此背景下，任何残余光学斜率若仅用恒星污染解释，则需极端的亮斑分布；而大气贡献与适度亮斑信号共同作用的情景更为合理。综上，这些结果明确了R-TLSE近似的适用范围，并强调了在高精度透射光谱中需采用显式考虑边缘变暗的几何敏感恒星非均匀性模型。

[5] On the Effect of Stellar Activity on Low-resolution Transit Spectroscopy and the use of High Resolution as Mitigation

2022-05-00

We present models designed to quantify the effects of stellar activity on exoplanet transit spectroscopy and atmospheric characterization at low (R = 100) and high (R = 100,000) spectral resolution. We study three model classes mirroring planetary system archetypes: a hot Jupiter around an early-K star (HD 189733 b); a mini-Neptune around an early-M dwarf (K2-18 b); and terrestrial planets around a late-M dwarf (TRAPPIST-1). We map photospheres with temperatures and radial velocities (RV) and integrate specific intensity stellar models. We obtain transit spectra affected by stellar contamination, the Rossiter-McLaughlin effect (RME), and center-to-limb variations (CLV). We find that, at low resolution, for later-type stars, planetary water features become difficult to distinguish from contamination. Many distributions of unocculted active regions can induce planetary-like features of similar amplitudes in the case of a late-M dwarf. Atmospheric characterization of planets around late-type stars will likely continue to suffer from degeneracy with stellar activity unless active regions' parameters can be constrained using additional information. For the early-K star, stellar contamination mostly manifests itself through a slope at optical wavelengths similar to Rayleigh scattering. In all cases, contamination induces offsets in measured planet radii. At high resolution, we show that we can determine the origin of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO detection signals and lift the degeneracy observed at low resolution, provided sufficient planet RV variation during transit and adequate correction for the RME and CLV when required. High-resolution spectroscopy may therefore help resolve issues arising from stellar contamination for favorable systems.

[5] 恒星活动对低分辨率凌星光谱的影响及高分辨率作为缓解手段的应用

我们提出了旨在量化恒星活动对系外行星凌日光谱和大气表征影响的模型，涵盖低（R = 100）和高（R = 100,000）光谱分辨率。我们研究了三类反映行星系统原型的模型：围绕早K型恒星的热木星（HD 189733 b）；围绕早M型矮星的迷你海王星（K2-18 b）；以及围绕晚M型矮星的类地行星（TRAPPIST-1）。我们绘制了具有不同温度和径向速度（RV）的光球，并整合了特定强度的恒星模型。我们获得了受恒星污染、Rossiter-McLaughlin效应（RME）和中心到边缘变化（CLV）影响的凌日光谱。我们发现，在低分辨率下，对于晚型恒星，行星水特征变得难以与污染区分。在晚M型矮星的情况下，许多未被遮挡的活跃区分布可诱发与行星特征幅度相当的信号。除非能利用额外信息约束活跃区参数，否则围绕晚型恒星的行星大气表征很可能继续受到与恒星活动的退化影响。对于早K型恒星，恒星污染主要表现为类似瑞利散射的光学波段斜率。在所有情况下，污染都会引入行星半径测量的偏移。在高分辨率下，我们展示了只要凌日过程中行星RV变化足够大，并对RME和CLV进行充分校正，就能确定H<sub>2</sub>O和CO探测信号的起源，并消除低分辨率下的退化。因此，高分辨率光谱有望帮助解决有利系统中由恒星污染引发的问题。

[6] Phase-curve Pollution of Exoplanet Transmission Spectra

2021-04-00

The occurrence of a planet transiting in front of its host star offers the opportunity to observe the planet's atmosphere filtering starlight. The fraction of occulted stellar flux is roughly proportional to the optically thick area of the planet, the extent of which depends on the opacity of the planet's gaseous envelope at the observed wavelengths. Chemical species, haze, and clouds are now routinely detected in exoplanet atmospheres through rather small features in transmission spectra, i.e., collections of planet-to-star area ratios across multiple spectral bins and/or photometric bands. Technological advances have led to a shrinking of the error bars down to a few tens of parts per million (ppm) per spectral point for the brightest targets. The upcoming James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) is anticipated to deliver transmission spectra with precision down to 10 ppm. The increasing precision of measurements requires a reassessment of the approximations hitherto adopted in astrophysical models, including transit light-curve models. Recently, it has been shown that neglecting the planet's thermal emission can introduce significant biases in the transit depth measured with the JWST/Mid-InfraRed Instrument, integrated between 5 and 12 μm. In this paper, we take a step forward by analyzing the effects of the approximation on transmission spectra over the 0.6-12 μm wavelength range covered by various JWST instruments. We present open-source software to predict the spectral bias, showing that, if not corrected, it may affect the inferred molecular abundances and thermal structure of some exoplanet atmospheres.

[6] 系外行星透射光谱的相位曲线污染

行星凌越其母星的事件为观测行星大气对恒星光的过滤提供了机会。被遮挡的恒星通量比例大致与行星的光学厚面积成正比，而该面积取决于行星气体包层在观测波长下的不透明度。通过透射光谱中的较小特征（即多个光谱通道和/或光度带的行星-恒星面积比集合），目前已能常规探测系外行星大气中的化学组分、薄雾和云。技术进步使得最亮目标的每个光谱点误差条缩小到几十ppm。即将发射的James Webb Space Telescope（JWST）预计将实现精度达10 ppm的透射光谱。测量精度的提升要求重新评估迄今在天体物理模型（包括凌星光变曲线模型）中采用的近似。近期研究表明，忽略行星的热发射会在JWST/Mid-InfraRed Instrument（5–12 μm波段积分）测得的凌星深度中引入显著偏差。本文进一步分析了该近似对JWST各仪器覆盖的0.6–12 μm波段透射光谱的影响。我们发布了开源软件用于预测光谱偏差，结果显示若不加以修正，该偏差可能影响部分系外行星大气的分子丰度和热结构的推断。

[7] An Analytical Model for Transit Light Curves

2024-11-00

Transits and secondary eclipses of an exoplanet orbiting a host star generate dips in the light curve. This article proposes a new class of mathematical functions that provide simple and computationally efficient models for such light curves. The transit and secondary eclipse functions account for ingress/egress, and the transit function incorporates curvilinear limb darkening. Their parameters are estimated using least squares methods. These models can be used to test for the statistical significance of dips, potentially revealing the existence of an exoplanet. In addition, they can be used to estimate features such as duration and depth of a transit or a secondary eclipse, limb darkening curvature and ingress/egress slope, and the orbital period. Examples using publicly available light curve data are presented, demonstrating the accuracy and efficiency of the proposed functions as fast analytical models for transits and/or secondary eclipses.

[7] 凌星光变曲线的解析模型

一颗系外行星绕主星运行时，其凌星和次生食会在光变曲线上产生下降。本文提出了一类新的数学函数，为此类光变曲线提供了简单且计算高效的建模方法。凌星和次生食函数均考虑了进/出食过程，凌星函数还包含了曲线状边缘变暗。其参数通过最小二乘法估算。这些模型可用于检验光变曲线下降的统计显著性，从而有可能揭示系外行星的存在。此外，还可用于估算凌星或次生食的持续时间和深度、边缘变暗曲率、进/出食斜率以及轨道周期。文中给出了使用公开光变曲线数据的示例，展示了所提出函数作为凌星和/或次生食快速解析模型的准确性和高效性。

[8] Power of wavelets in analyses of transit and phase curves in the presence of stellar variability and instrumental noise. I. Method and validation

2023-07-00

Context. Stellar photometric variability and instrumental effects, such as cosmic ray hits, data discontinuities, data leaks, instrument aging, and so on, lead to difficulties in the characterisation of exoplanets. Therefore, they can impact the accuracy and precision of the modelling and the detectability of their transits, occultations, and phase curves. <BR /> Aims: This paper is aimed at improving the transit, occultation, and phase-curve modelling in the presence of strong stellar variability and instrumental noise. To this end, we invoke the wavelet formulation. <BR /> Methods: We explored the capabilities of the software package Transit and Light Curve Modeller (TLCM). It is able to perform (1) a joint radial-velocity and light-curve fit or (2) a light curve-only fit. It models the transit, occultation, beaming, ellipsoidal, and reflection effects in the light curves (including the gravity-darkening effect). Here, the red noise, stellar variability, and instrumental effects were modelled via wavelets. The wavelet fit was constrained by prescribing that the final white noise level must be equal to the average of the uncertainties of the photometric data points. This helps to avoid overfitting and regularises the noise model. The approach was tested by injecting synthetic light curves into short-cadence Kepler data and modelling them. <BR /> Results: The method performs well over a certain signal-to-noise (S/N) ratio. We provide limits in terms of the S/N for every studied system parameter that is needed for accurate parameter retrieval. The wavelet approach is able to manage and remove the impact of data discontinuities, cosmic ray events, and long-term stellar variability and instrument ageing, as well as short-term stellar variability, pulsation, and flares (among others). <BR /> Conclusions: We conclude that precise light-curve models combined with the wavelet method and with well-prescribed constraints on the white noise are able to retrieve the planetary system parameters, even in the presence of strong stellar variability and instrumental noise, including data discontinuities.

[8] 在恒星变化性和仪器噪声存在下利用小波分析凌星和相位曲线的能力。I. 方法与验证

背景：恒星光度变化和仪器效应（如宇宙射线击中、数据不连续、数据泄漏、仪器老化等）导致系外行星特性刻画变得困难。因此，这些因素会影响凌日、掩食和相位曲线建模的准确性和精度，以及其可探测性。 目标：本文旨在提升在强恒星变化和仪器噪声存在下的凌日、掩食和相位曲线建模。为此，我们引入了小波方法。 方法：我们探索了Transit and Light Curve Modeller (TLCM)软件包的能力。该软件能够进行（1）径向速度与光变曲线联合拟合，或（2）仅光变曲线拟合。它可对光变曲线中的凌日、掩食、光束效应、椭球效应和反射效应（包括重力变暗效应）进行建模。本文中，红噪声、恒星变化和仪器效应通过小波建模。小波拟合通过规定最终白噪声水平等于光度数据点不确定性的平均值来约束，以避免过拟合并正则化噪声模型。该方法通过将合成光变曲线注入Kepler短时序数据并进行建模进行测试。 结果：该方法在一定信噪比（S/N）范围内表现良好。我们为每个研究的系统参数提供了准确参数检索所需的S/N下限。小波方法能够处理并消除数据不连续、宇宙射线事件、长期恒星变化和仪器老化，以及短期恒星变化、脉动和耀斑等影响。 结论：我们认为，精确的光变曲线模型结合小波方法，并对白噪声有良好约束，即使在强恒星变化和仪器噪声（包括数据不连续）存在的情况下，也能准确检索行星系统参数。

Analyses [2016A&A...596A..90V] and [2017A&A...606A.144A] of HD 189733B's atmosphere reach potentially conflicting conclusions regarding water: one reports "We find good agreement between our models and the estimated water abundance for WASP-43b.", while the other reports "We place a 5-σ upper limit of 100 ppm on the strength of the 6500 Å water vapor band.". Under what observational or methodological conditions can these results be reconciled, and which currently untested assumption drives the disagreement?

8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] Characterizing Exoplanetary Atmospheres at High Resolution with SPIRou: Detection of Water on HD 189733 b

2021-12-00

We present the first exoplanet atmospheric detection made as part of the SPIRou Legacy Survey, a Large Observing Program of 300 nights exploiting the capabilities of SPIRou, the new near-infrared high-resolution (R ~ 70,000) spectropolarimeter installed on the Canada-France-Hawaii Telescope (3.6 m). We observed two transits of HD 189733b, an extensively studied hot Jupiter that is known to show prominent water vapor absorption in its transmission spectrum. When combining the two transits, we successfully detect the planet's water vapor absorption at 5.9σ using a cross-correlation t-test, or with a ∆BIC &gt; 10 using a log-likelihood calculation. Using a Bayesian retrieval framework assuming parameterized temperature-pressure (T-P) profile atmospheric models, we constrain the planet atmospheric parameters, in the region probed by our transmission spectrum, to the following values: ${\mathrm{log}}_{10}\mathrm{VMR}[{{\rm{H}}}_{2}{\rm{O}}]$ = $-{4.4}_{-0.4}^{+0.4}$ , and P <SUB>cloud</SUB> ≳ 0.2 bar (gray clouds), both of which are consistent with previous studies of this planet. Our retrieved water volume-mixing ratio is slightly subsolar; although, combining it with the previously retrieved super-solar CO abundances from other studies would imply a super-solar C/O ratio. We furthermore measure a net blueshift of the planet signal of $-{4.62}_{-0.44}^{+0.46}$ km s<SUP>-1</SUP>, which is somewhat larger than many previous measurements and unlikely to result solely from winds in the planet's atmosphere, although it could possibly be explained by a transit signal dominated by the trailing limb of the planet. This large blueshift is observed in all of the different detection/retrieval methods that were performed and in each of the two transits independently.

[1] 利用 SPIRou 高分辨率表征系外行星大气：在 HD 189733 b 上探测到水

我们报告了SPIRou Legacy Survey（300夜大型观测项目，利用加拿大-法国-夏威夷望远镜3.6 m新近安装的近红外高分辨率（R ~ 70,000）光谱偏振仪SPIRou）中首次系外行星大气探测结果。我们观测了HD 189733b的两次凌星，这是一颗已被广泛研究、在透射光谱中表现出显著水蒸气吸收的热木星。将两次凌星数据合并后，我们利用互相关t检验成功以5.9σ探测到行星的水蒸气吸收，或通过对数似然计算得到∆BIC > 10。采用参数化温度-压强（T-P）剖面的大气模型进行贝叶斯检索，我们约束了透射光谱探测区域内的大气参数：log10VMR[H2O] = -4.4_{-0.4}^{+0.4}，P_cloud ≳ 0.2 bar（灰云），两者均与此前研究一致。我们检索到的水体积分混合比略低于太阳值；但结合其他研究中已检索到的超太阳CO丰度，则暗示超太阳C/O比。此外，我们测得行星信号的净蓝移为-4.62_{-0.44}^{+0.46} km s^-1，略高于许多先前测量，且不太可能仅由行星大气风引起，但可能由凌星信号主要来自行星后缘主导所致。该大蓝移在所有不同的探测/检索方法及两次凌星中均独立出现。

[2] Water observed in the atmosphere of τ Boötis Ab with CARMENES/CAHA

2022-08-00

Characterizing the atmospheres of hot Jupiters is important in understanding the formation and migration of these exotic planets. However, there are still many open questions about the chemical and physical properties of these atmospheres. Here, we confirm the detection of water vapour in thermal emission from the non-transiting hot Jupiter τ Boötis Ab with the high-resolution NIR CARMENES spectrograph. Combining over 17 h of observations (560 spectra) and using a Bayesian cross-correlation to log-likelihood approach, we measure a systemic velocity of $V_{\mathrm{sys}} = -11.51^{+0.59}_{-0.60}$ km s<SUP>-1</SUP> and a radial velocity semi-amplitude of $K_{\mathrm{P}} = 106.21^{+1.76}_{-1.71}$ km s<SUP>-1</SUP> for the planet, which results in an absolute mass of $M_{\mathrm{P}} = 6.24^{+0.17}_{-0.18}\, \mathrm{M_{J}}$ and an orbital inclination of $41.6^{+1.0}_{-0.9}$ degrees. Our retrieved V<SUB>sys</SUB> shows a significant shift (+5 km s<SUP>-1</SUP>) from the literature value, which could be caused by an inaccurate time of periastron. Within the explored model grid, we measure a preference for solar water abundance (VMR = 10<SUP>-3</SUP>) and find no evidence for additional minor species in the atmosphere. Given the extensive orbital coverage of the data, we searched for a phase dependency in the water signal but found no strong evidence of variation with orbital phase. This detection is at odds with recent observations from SPIRou/CFHT and their tight upper limit on water abundance. We recommend further observations of the atmosphere τ Boötis Ab to try and resolve these discrepancies.

[2] 利用CARMENES/CAHA在τ Boötis Ab大气中观测到水

对热木星大气的刻画对于理解这些特殊行星的形成和迁移过程至关重要。然而，这些大气的化学和物理性质仍存在许多未解之谜。在本研究中，我们利用高分辨率近红外CARMENES光谱仪，确认了非凌星热木星τ Boötis Ab热发射中的水蒸气探测。我们结合超过17小时（560个光谱）的观测数据，并采用贝叶斯互相关到对数似然的方法，测得行星的系统速度为V_sys = -11.51^{+0.59}_{-0.60} km s^-1，径向速度半振幅为K_P = 106.21^{+1.76}_{-1.71} km s^-1，对应行星绝对质量M_P = 6.24^{+0.17}_{-0.18} M_J，轨道倾角为41.6^{+1.0}_{-0.9}度。我们反演得到的V_sys与文献值相比有显著偏移（+5 km s^-1），可能由近日点时刻不准确导致。在所探索的模型网格中，我们测得太阳丰度的水（VMR = 10^-3）最为优选，且未发现大气中存在其他微量分子的证据。鉴于数据覆盖了广泛的轨道相位，我们搜索了水信号的相位依赖性，但未发现随轨道相位有显著变化。该探测结果与SPIRou/CFHT的最新观测及其对水丰度的严格上限不符。我们建议对τ Boötis Ab大气进行进一步观测，以尝试解决这些差异。

[3] Where are the Water Worlds?: Self-consistent Models of Water-rich Exoplanet Atmospheres

2023-08-00

It remains to be ascertained whether sub-Neptune exoplanets primarily possess hydrogen-rich atmospheres or whether a population of H<SUB>2</SUB>O-rich water worlds lurks in their midst. Addressing this question requires improved modeling of water-rich exoplanetary atmospheres, both to predict and interpret spectroscopic observations and to serve as upper boundary conditions on interior structure calculations. Here, we present new models of hydrogen-helium-water atmospheres with water abundances ranging from solar to 100% water vapor. We improve upon previous models of high-water-content atmospheres by incorporating updated prescriptions for water self-broadening and a nonideal gas equation of state. Our model grid (https://umd.box.com/v/water-worlds) includes temperature─pressure profiles in radiative-convective equilibrium, along with their associated transmission and thermal emission spectra. We find that our model updates primarily act at high pressures, significantly impacting bottom-of-atmosphere temperatures, with implications for the accuracy of interior structure calculations. Upper-atmosphere conditions and spectroscopic observables are less impacted by our model updates, and we find that, under most conditions, retrieval codes built for hot Jupiters should also perform well on water-rich planets. We additionally quantify the observational degeneracies among both thermal emission and transmission spectra. We recover standard degeneracies with clouds and mean molecular weight for transmission spectra, and we find thermal emission spectra to be more readily distinguishable from one another in the water-poor (i.e., near-solar) regime.

[3] 水世界在哪里？：水丰富系外行星大气的自洽模型

目前尚不清楚亚海王星类系外行星是否主要拥有富氢大气，或其间是否存在一类富H2O的水世界。解决这一问题需要改进对富水系外行星大气的建模，以预测和解释光谱观测结果，并为内部结构计算提供上边界条件。本文提出了新的氢-氦-水大气模型，水丰度从太阳丰度到100%水蒸气。我们在以往高水含量大气模型基础上，加入了更新的水自展宽处理和非理想气体状态方程。我们的模型网格（https://umd.box.com/v/water-worlds）包括辐射-对流平衡下的温度-压力剖面及其对应的透射和热发射光谱。我们发现，模型更新主要影响高压区，显著改变大气底部温度，这对内部结构计算的准确性有重要影响。高层大气条件和光谱可观测量受模型更新影响较小，我们发现，在大多数情况下，为热木星开发的检索代码同样适用于富水行星。我们还量化了热发射和透射光谱之间的观测退化性。我们重现了透射光谱中与云和平均分子量的标准退化性，并发现热发射光谱在贫水（即近太阳丰度）情形下更易于区分。

[4] Confirmation of Water Absorption in the Thermal Emission Spectrum of the Hot Jupiter WASP-77Ab with HST/WFC3

2022-06-00

Secondary eclipse observations of hot Jupiters can reveal both their compositions and thermal structures. Previous observations have shown a diversity of hot Jupiter eclipse spectra, including absorption features, emission features, and featureless blackbody-like spectra. We present a secondary eclipse spectrum of the hot Jupiter WASP-77Ab observed between 1 and 5 μm with the Hubble Space Telescope (HST) and the Spitzer Space Telescope. The HST observations show signs of water absorption indicative of a noninverted thermal structure. We fit the data with both a one-dimensional free retrieval and a grid of one-dimensional self-consistent forward models to confirm this noninverted structure. The free retrieval places a 3σ lower limit on the atmospheric water abundance of $\mathrm{log}({n}_{{{\rm{H}}}_{2}{\rm{O}}})\gt -4.78$ and cannot constrain the CO abundance. The grid fit produces a slightly superstellar metallicity and constrains the carbon-to-oxygen ratio to less than or equal to the solar value. We also compare our data to recent high-resolution observations of WASP-77Ab taken with the IGRINS/IGRINS spectrograph. We find that the best-fit model to the IGRINS data gives a reduced chi squared of χ<SUP>2</SUP> <SUB>ν</SUB> = 1.32 when compared to the WFC3 data. However, the metallicity derived from the IGRINS data is significantly lower than that derived from our self-consistent model fit. We find that this difference may be due to disequilibrium chemistry, and the varying results between the models applied here demonstrate the model dependence of derived metallicities when comparing to low-resolution, low-wavelength coverage data alone. Future work to combine observations from IGRINS, HST, and the James Webb Space Telescope will improve our estimate of the atmospheric composition of WASP-77Ab.

[4] 利用HST/WFC3确认热木星WASP-77Ab热发射光谱中的水吸收

对热木星的次生凌星观测可以揭示其组成和热结构。以往观测显示热木星的凌星光谱多样，包括吸收特征、发射特征和无特征的黑体型光谱。我们展示了用Hubble Space Telescope (HST) 和 Spitzer Space Telescope在1至5 μm波段观测的热木星WASP-77Ab的次生凌星光谱。HST观测显示出水吸收特征，表明其热结构未发生反转。我们用一维自由检索和一维自洽前向模型网格拟合数据，均证实了这一非反转结构。自由检索对大气水丰度给出了3σ下限：$\mathrm{log}({n}_{{{\rm{H}}}_{2}{\rm{O}}})\gt -4.78$，但无法约束CO丰度。模型网格拟合结果显示金属丰度略高于太阳值，并将C/O比约束在不高于太阳值。我们还将数据与近期IGRINS/IGRINS光谱仪对WASP-77Ab的高分辨率观测进行了比较。我们发现，IGRINS数据的最佳拟合模型与WFC3数据相比，得到的归一化卡方为χ<sup>2</sup><sub>ν</sub> = 1.32。然而，IGRINS数据推导的金属丰度显著低于我们的自洽模型拟合结果。我们认为这种差异可能源于非平衡化学，不同模型的结果也表明仅依赖低分辨率、窄波段数据时推导金属丰度具有模型依赖性。未来结合IGRINS、HST和James Webb Space Telescope观测将有助于改进对WASP-77Ab大气组成的估计。

[5] Helium Atmospheres May Hide in Current Exoplanet Analysis Frameworks

2026-01-00

The increasing number of detailed exoplanet observations offers an opportunity to refine our analyses and interpretations. Here, we show that atmospheres that appear volatile-rich and/or cloudy may instead be helium-rich. As transmission spectra constrain the atmospheric scale height (H), a He-enriched atmosphere can be misinterpreted as H<SUB>2</SUB>-dominated water-rich to bring the mean molecular weight (μ) to intermediate values (∼4−10) when He/H<SUB>2</SUB> is fixed. Similarly, a cloud deck can reduce the spectral features, and thus the apparent (i.e., cloud-free equivalent) H. We present a proof-of-concept reanalysis of HD 209458 b's JWST transmission spectrum treating He/H<SUB>2</SUB> as a free parameter, resulting in sets of He-rich solutions. We argue that He enhancement must be considered to reliably constrain atmospheric composition, be sensitive to a more diverse planetary population, and ultimately yield robust trends to inform formation and evolution pathways. Looking ahead, we suggest leveraging insights from differences in pressure-broadening effects, outflow measurements, and atmospheric chemistry to disentangle reliably between He-, volatile-rich, and cloudy atmospheres—while recognizing that associated models need targeted upgrades to reach the fidelity level required to this end.

[5] 氦大气可能隐藏在当前系外行星分析框架中

越来越多的系外行星详细观测为我们改进分析和解释提供了机会。本文展示了那些看似富含挥发物和/或多云的大气，实际上可能是富含He的大气。由于透射光谱约束了大气的尺度高度（H），当He/H2固定时，He富集的大气可能会被误解为H2主导的富水大气，从而使平均分子量（μ）达到中间值（约4−10）。类似地，云层也会减弱光谱特征，从而降低表观（即无云等效）H。我们以HD 209458 b的JWST透射光谱为例，进行概念验证性再分析，将He/H2作为自由参数，得到了多组富He解。我们认为，必须考虑He的增强，才能可靠约束大气组成，对更为多样的行星族群保持敏感，并最终获得稳健的趋势以指导形成和演化路径。展望未来，我们建议利用压强展宽效应差异、外流测量和大气化学等方面的见解，来可靠区分He富、富挥发物和多云大气——同时认识到相关模型需要有针对性地升级，以达到所需的精度水平。

[6] Methods for Incorporating Model Uncertainty into Exoplanet Atmospheric Analysis

2024-04-00

A key goal of exoplanet spectroscopy is to measure atmospheric properties, such as abundances of chemical species, in order to connect them to our understanding of atmospheric physics and planet formation. In this new era of high-quality JWST data, it is paramount that these measurement methods are robust. When comparing atmospheric models to observations, multiple candidate models may produce reasonable fits to the data. Typically, conclusions are reached by selecting the best-performing model according to some metric. This ignores model uncertainty in favour of specific model assumptions, potentially leading to measured atmospheric properties that are overconfident and/or incorrect. In this talk, we will present a case study highlighting these effects. The hot Jupiter HD 209458b is one of the most extensively studied exoplanets, boasting one of the highest-quality spectra obtained with HST. Despite this, past efforts to measure the abundance of water vapor in its atmosphere have led to a range of different conclusions, with some studies finding that water is depleted relative to equilibrium expectations and others disputing this. We finally resolved this apparent contradiction using a new ensemble approach to atmospheric retrieval, whereby results from multiple models are combined in a statistically sound manner to account for model uncertainty. We showed that when a range of reasonable models are considered, the uncertainty on the measured water abundance ranges from depleted to slightly enhanced relative to equilibrium. In this case, the results are strongly influenced by the chosen approach to modeling clouds and hazes. As we start to analyse JWST data, it is critical that we do not fall into the same trap, and that we consider effects of model uncertainty to avoid overconfident and biased inferences.

[6] 将模型不确定性纳入系外行星大气分析的方法

系外行星光谱学的一个关键目标是测量大气属性，如化学组分的丰度，以便将其与我们对大气物理和行星形成的理解联系起来。在JWST高质量数据的新时代，确保这些测量方法的稳健性至关重要。在将大气模型与观测数据进行比较时，多个候选模型可能都能较好地拟合数据。通常，结论是通过根据某种指标选择表现最好的模型得出的。这种做法忽略了模型不确定性，偏向于特定模型假设，可能导致对大气属性的测量过于自信和/或不正确。在本报告中，我们将通过一个案例研究来突出这些影响。热木星HD 209458b是研究最为深入的系外行星之一，拥有用HST获得的最高质量光谱之一。尽管如此，过去对其大气中水汽丰度的测量却得出了不同的结论，一些研究发现水丰度低于平衡预期，另一些则对此提出异议。我们最终通过一种新的集合反演方法解决了这一表面上的矛盾，该方法以统计上合理的方式结合多个模型的结果，从而考虑模型不确定性。我们显示，当考虑一系列合理模型时，测得的水丰度的不确定性范围从低于到略高于平衡值。在本案例中，结果受云和薄雾建模方法的影响很大。随着我们开始分析JWST数据，必须避免重蹈覆辙，充分考虑模型不确定性，以避免过度自信和有偏推断。

[7] The Watery Atmosphere of HD 209458 b Revealed by Joint K- and L-band High-resolution Spectroscopy

2025-10-00

We present a joint analysis of high-resolution K- and L-band observations of the benchmark hot Jupiter HD 209458 b from the Keck Planet Imager and Characterizer. One half-night of observations was obtained in each bandpass, covering similar preeclipse phases. The two epochs were then jointly analyzed using our atmospheric retrieval pipeline based on petitRADTRANS to constrain the atmospheric pressure─temperature profile and chemical composition. Consistent with recent results from JWST observations at lower spectral resolution, we obtain an oxygen-rich composition for HD 209458 b (C/O &lt; 10<SUP>−3</SUP> at 95% confidence) and a lower limit on the volatile metallicity similar to the solar value ([(C + O)/H] &gt; −0.2 at 95% confidence). Leveraging the large spectral grasp of the multiband observations, we constrain the atmospheric H<SUB>2</SUB>O mixing ratio to <inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:mi>log</mml:mi><mml:msub><mml:mi>H</mml:mi><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:msub><mml:mi>O</mml:mi><mml:mi>V MR</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>&gt;</mml:mo><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>3.1</mml:mn></mml:math> </inline-formula> at 95% confidence, and obtain 95% upper limits on the atmospheric mixing ratios of CO (&lt;10<SUP>−4.8</SUP>), CH<SUB>4</SUB> (&lt;10<SUP>−4.5</SUP>), NH<SUB>3</SUB> (&lt;10<SUP>−5.8</SUP>), H<SUB>2</SUB>S (&lt;10<SUP>−3.3</SUP>), and HCN (&lt;10<SUP>−5.6</SUP>). The limits on CH<SUB>4</SUB>, NH<SUB>3</SUB>, and HCN are consistent with recent results from JWST transmission spectroscopy, demonstrating the value of multiband, ground-based high-resolution spectroscopy for precisely constraining trace-species abundances in exoplanet atmospheres. The retrieved low-C/O, moderate-metallicity composition for HD 209458 b is consistent with formation scenarios involving late accretion of substantial quantities of oxygen-rich refractory solids and/or ices.

[7] 通过联合K波段和L波段高分辨率光谱揭示HD 209458 b的富水大气

我们对基准热木星 HD 209458 b 的 Keck Planet Imager and Characterizer 获得的高分辨率 K 波段和 L 波段观测进行了联合分析。每个波段各获得了半夜的观测，覆盖了类似的凌前相位。随后，我们利用基于 petitRADTRANS 的大气检索流程对这两个历元的数据进行了联合分析，以约束大气的压强-温度剖面和化学组成。与近期 JWST 低光谱分辨率观测结果一致，我们获得了 HD 209458 b 的富氧组成（C/O < 10^−3，95%置信度）和与太阳值相当的挥发性金属丰度下限（[(C + O)/H] > −0.2，95%置信度）。借助多波段观测的广泛波长覆盖，我们将大气 H2O 混合比约束为 log(H2O_VMR) > −3.1（95%置信度），并获得了 CO（<10^−4.8）、CH4（<10^−4.5）、NH3（<10^−5.8）、H2S（<10^−3.3）和 HCN（<10^−5.6）的大气混合比 95% 上限。CH4、NH3 和 HCN 的上限与近期 JWST 透射光谱结果一致，显示多波段地基高分辨率光谱在精确约束系外行星大气微量组分丰度方面的价值。HD 209458 b 检索得到的低 C/O、中等金属丰度组成与其形成过程中晚期吸积大量富氧难挥发固体和/或冰的情景一致。

[8] WASP-127b: a misaligned planet with a partly cloudy atmosphereand tenuous sodium signature seen by ESPRESSO

2021-09-00

During the past few years, exoplanet atmospheric studies have grown tremendously. Detections of atomic and molecular species are reported from the ultraviolet to the infrared at low- and high-resolution for Earth-mass to Jupiter-mass planets and from temperate to ultra-hot worlds. Nowadays, ground-based high-resolution spectrographs are starting to produce groundbreaking results that inform us of the temperature structure and dynamics of exoplanet atmospheres. A reference system for atmospheric studies: WASP-127bWASP-127b (Lam et al. 2017) orbits a bright (V = 10.17) G5-type star with a period of 4.18 days. The host star is at the end of its main sequence phase with an age of ~10Gyr and radius of 1.30RS and has entered the sub-giant phase. Therefore, the planet might be going through an inflation process for the second time (Lopez &amp; Fortney 2016), leading to its large radius (~1.31RJ). WASP-127b is a lukewarm (~600 times Earth irradiation) Saturn at the right mass border of the evaporation desert. Assuming a hydrogen-helium composition, its atmospheric scale height is about 2100 km. In this case, the signal in transmission for one scale height is around 420 ppm, potentially making this planet one of the best among those of its class to study exo-atmospheres through transmission spectroscopy.Near-infrared low-resolution data obtained with the Hubble Space Telescope (Spake et al. 2020) have revealed the strongest water band amplitude in an exoplanet with a mean amplitude of about 800ppm. Moreover, the presence of clouds and hazes is necessary to explain the complete spectrum. Such planets with a high-amplitude water signature in the J-band are well placed to be studied from the ground with visible and NIR spectrographs, allowing the impact of hazes and clouds to be analyzed by measuring the water content at different wavelengths. Observations of WASP-127b with ESPRESSOESPRESSO is a fiber-fed, ultra-stabilized high-resolution echelle spectrograph installed at Paranal. It can collect the light from each 8m Unit Telescope (UT) individually or the 4UTs simultaneously of the Very Large Telescope (VLT). Two transits of WASP-127b were obtained within the framework of the GTO consortium. We published our results in Allart et al. 2020, which includes the first measurement of spin-orbit alignment of this system revealing the peculiar orbital architecture of the system. The old star WASP-127 is a slow rotator while its planet has a misaligned retrograde orbit (Fig. 2). This is surprising as stars with Teff below 6250K are supposed to have aligned systems. An explanation could be that WASP-127b remained trapped in a Kozai resonance with an outer companion and only recently migrated close to its star.A view on the atmosphere of WASP-127bWe used the ESPRESSO data to analyze the transmission spectrum of the exoplanet. Visible high-resolution datasets are known to reveal the exoplanet thermosphere through atomic species as the well-known sodium doublet. The WASP-127b transit datasets have revealed an excess of absorption of 0.34 +/- 0.04% at the expected position of the sodium doublet corresponding to a small extension over 7 scale heights. However, we do not detect the presence of other atomic species but we were able to set upper limits of only a few scale heights.We also undertake a thorough search for water vapor at visible wavelengths. To do so, we used the well-known cross-correlation function technique to average thousands of water lines in velocity space. To be as model-independent as possible, we applied binary masks containing the position of water lines at different temperatures and with an increasing number of lines. We showcased that such a technique is sensitive enough to retrieve planetary signals by doing an injection recovery test. Once applied to the transit datasets of WASP-127b, we were able to put a 3-sigma upper limit on the presence of water vapor at visible wavelengths of only 38ppm on the average depth of the 1600 strongest water lines at the equilibrium temperature. This upper limit corresponds to an atmospheric extension of only 0.08 scale height. Combining low- and high-resolution spectroscopyBased on this unique precision obtained with ESPRESSO, we explored the compatibility between ground-based high-resolution spectroscopy and space-based low-resolution spectroscopy. We computed a grid of models with different grey cloud-deck pressure based on the results of Spake et al. 2020 and using the pi-eta line-by-line radiative transfer code (Pino et al 2018). On one hand, models with grey cloud deck pressure ranging from 0.3 to 1 mbar are compatible with the HST datasets, while in another hand only models with pressure below 0.5 mbar are compatible with the ESPRESSO datasets. Therefore, by combining the low- and high-resolution datasets of WASP-127b, we can constrain the grey cloud pressure between 0.3 and 0.5 mbar.ConclusionWe used two transits of WASP-127b obtained with ESPRESSO to acquire a thorough understanding of the orbital architecture of the system and the exoplanet atmosphere.We propose a new framework to search for water vapor and other molecular species at high resolution. Despite the good data quality, we did not detect water vapor. However, we combined this result with a low-resolution detection of water at 1.3 microns to constrain the presence of clouds in the atmosphere of WASP-127b.To conclude, we report for the first time that high-resolution visible data can be used to differentiate between cloudy and cloud-free exoplanets by measuring the water content and can also provide essential information on the cloud-deck pressure. The framework developed here to measure this water content will be applied to other exoplanets in the ESPRESSO GTO atmospheric survey and other surveys such as the NIRPS GTO atmospheric survey. ReferencesAllart R., Pino, L., Lovis, C. et al., 2020, A&amp;A, 644, A155Pino, L., Ehrenreich, D., Wyttenbach, A., et al. 2018, A&amp;A, 612, A53Spake, J. J., Sing, D. K., Wakeford, H. R., et al. 2020, MNRAS

[8] WASP-127b：一颗轨道倾斜、部分多云且通过 ESPRESSO 观测到微弱钠特征的行星

在过去几年中，系外行星大气研究取得了巨大进展。从紫外到红外、从低分辨率到高分辨率、从地球质量到木星质量、从温和到超热的行星，原子和分子物种的探测不断被报道。如今，地基高分辨率光谱仪开始产出突破性成果，为我们揭示了系外行星大气的温度结构和动力学。大气研究的参考系统：WASP-127bWASP-127b（Lam et al. 2017）围绕一颗亮（V = 10.17）的G5型恒星公转，周期为4.18天。该母星正处于主序末期，年龄约10 Gyr，半径为1.30RS，已进入次巨星阶段。因此，该行星可能正经历第二次膨胀过程（Lopez & Fortney 2016），导致其半径较大（约1.31RJ）。WASP-127b是一颗温暖（约为地球照射的600倍）的土星，位于蒸发荒漠的质量边界。假设其为氢-氦组成，大气尺度高度约为2100 km。在这种情况下，一个尺度高度的透射信号约为420 ppm，使其成为同类行星中通过透射光谱研究系外大气的最佳目标之一。哈勃空间望远镜获得的近红外低分辨率数据（Spake et al. 2020）揭示了系外行星中最强的水带幅度，平均幅度约为800 ppm。此外，解释完整光谱需要考虑云和薄雾的存在。此类在J波段具有高幅度水特征的行星，非常适合用地面可见光和近红外光谱仪进行研究，通过在不同波长测量水含量，分析薄雾和云的影响。使用ESPRESSO观测WASP-127bESPRESSO是一台光纤馈送、超稳定的高分辨率echelle光谱仪，安装在Paranal，可单独收集每个8m单元望远镜（UT）的光，或同时收集甚大望远镜（VLT）4个UT的光。我们在GTO联盟框架下获得了WASP-127b的两次凌日观测。我们在Allart et al. 2020中发表了结果，包括首次测量该系统的自转-轨道对齐，揭示了该系统特殊的轨道结构。年老的母星WASP-127自转缓慢，而其行星轨道为失调的逆行轨道（见图2）。这令人惊讶，因为Teff低于6250K的恒星系统通常应为对齐系统。一种解释可能是WASP-127b曾被外伴星困在Kozai共振中，最近才迁移到靠近母星的位置。WASP-127b大气的观测我们利用ESPRESSO数据分析了该系外行星的透射光谱。可见光高分辨率数据集已知可通过原子物种（如著名的钠双线）揭示系外行星热层。WASP-127b凌日数据集在钠双线预期位置显示出0.34 +/- 0.04%的吸收过量，对应于7个尺度高度的微小扩展。然而，我们未探测到其他原子物种，但能够给出仅为几个尺度高度的上限。我们还对可见波段的水蒸气进行了全面搜索。为此，我们采用了著名的互相关函数技术，在速度空间平均数千条水线。为尽量减少模型依赖性，我们应用了包含不同温度和不同数量水线位置的二进制掩模。我们通过注入-恢复测试展示了该技术对行星信号的敏感性。应用于WASP-127b的凌日数据后，我们对平衡温度下1600条最强水线的平均深度给出了仅38 ppm的3-sigma上限。该上限对应于仅0.08个尺度高度的大气扩展。结合低分辨率和高分辨率光谱基于ESPRESSO获得的独特精度，我们探索了地基高分辨率光谱与空间低分辨率光谱之间的兼容性。我们基于Spake et al. 2020的结果，利用pi-eta逐线辐射传输代码（Pino et al 2018），计算了不同灰云层压力的模型。一方面，灰云层压力在0.3到1 mbar范围的模型与HST数据集兼容，另一方面，只有压力低于0.5 mbar的模型与ESPRESSO数据集兼容。因此，通过结合WASP-127b的低分辨率和高分辨率数据集，我们可以将灰云层压力约束在0.3到0.5 mbar之间。结论我们利用ESPRESSO获得的两次WASP-127b凌日观测，深入理解了该系统的轨道结构和系外行星大气。我们提出了一个新的高分辨率水蒸气及其他分子物种搜索框架。尽管数据质量良好，我们未探测到水蒸气。然而，我们将该结果与1.3微米低分辨率水探测结果结合，约束了WASP-127b大气中云的存在。总之，我们首次报告高分辨率可见光数据可通过测量水含量区分有云和无云系外行星，并可提供关于云层压力的重要信息。此处开发的水含量测量框架将应用于ESPRESSO GTO大气巡天及NIRPS GTO大气巡天等其他巡天的系外行星。参考文献Allart R., Pino, L., Lovis, C. 等, 2020, A&A, 644, A155Pino, L., Ehrenreich, D., Wyttenbach, A., 等. 2018, A&A, 612, A53Spake, J. item_026 · sys_B
Are the observed Lyman-α flux variations during transits of Kepler-444 e and f due to neutral hydrogen exospheres originating from the planets, or are they caused by variability in the host star or the interstellar medium?
Kepler-444 e 和 f 过境期间观测到的 Lyman-α 通量变化是由于来自行星的中性氢外逸层，还是由母星或星际介质的变化引起的？
8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] The fundamentals of Lyman α exoplanet transits

2023-01-00

Lyman α transits have been detected from several nearby exoplanets and are one of our best insights into the atmospheric escape process. However, due to ISM absorption, we typically only observe the transit signature in the blue-wing, making them challenging to interpret. This challenge has been recently highlighted by non-detections from planets thought to be undergoing vigorous escape. Pioneering 3D simulations have shown that escaping hydrogen is shaped into a cometary tail receding from the planet. Motivated by this work, we develop a simple model to interpret Lyman α transits. Using this framework, we show that the Lyman α transit depth is primarily controlled by the properties of the stellar tidal field rather than details of the escape process. Instead, the transit duration provides a direct measurement of the velocity of the planetary outflow. This result arises because the underlying physics is the distance a neutral hydrogen atom can travel before it is photoionized in the outflow. Thus, higher irradiation levels, expected to drive more powerful outflows, produce weaker, shorter Lyman α transits because the outflowing gas is ionized more quickly. Our framework suggests that the generation of energetic neutral atoms may dominate the transit signature early, but the acceleration of planetary material produces long tails. Thus, Lyman α transits do not primarily probe the mass-loss rates. Instead, they inform us about the velocity at which the escape mechanism is ejecting material from the planet, providing a clean test of predictions from atmospheric escape models.

[1] Lyman α系外行星凌日的基本原理

Lyman α凌日已在若干邻近系外行星中被探测到，是我们了解大气逃逸过程的最佳途径之一。然而，由于星际介质（ISM）吸收，我们通常只能观测到蓝翼的凌日特征，这使得其解释变得具有挑战性。近期，一些被认为正在经历剧烈逃逸的行星未探测到Lyman α信号，进一步凸显了这一难题。开创性的三维模拟显示，逃逸的氢被塑造成一条彗尾状结构，从行星远离。受此工作启发，我们开发了一个简单模型用于解释Lyman α凌日。利用该框架，我们展示Lyman α凌日深度主要由恒星潮汐场的性质控制，而不是逃逸过程的细节。相反，凌日持续时间可直接测量行星外流的速度。这一结果源于基本物理过程：中性氢原子在外流中被光致电离前能移动的距离。因此，预期能驱动更强外流的高辐照水平，反而会产生更弱、更短的Lyman α凌日，因为外流气体更快被电离。我们的框架还表明，能量中性原子的产生可能在早期主导凌日信号，但行星物质的加速会形成长尾结构。因此，Lyman α凌日并不主要探测质量损失率，而是揭示逃逸机制将物质从行星抛出的速度，为大气逃逸模型的预测提供了直接检验。

[2] Space-weather-driven Variations in Lyα Absorption Signatures of Exoplanet Atmospheric Escape: MHD Simulations and the Case of AU Mic b

2022-08-00

We simulate the space environment around AU Microscopii b and the interaction between the magnetized stellar wind and a planetary atmospheric outflow for ambient stellar wind conditions and coronal mass ejection (CME) conditions. We also calculate synthetic Lyα absorption due to neutral hydrogen in the ambient and the escaping planetary atmosphere affected by this interaction. We find that the Lyα absorption is highly variable owing to the highly varying stellar wind conditions. A strong Doppler blueshift component is observed in the Lyα profile, in contradiction to the actual escape velocity observed in the simulations themselves. This result suggests that the strong Doppler blueshift is likely attributed to the stellar wind, not the escaping neutral atmosphere, either through its advection of neutral planetary gas or through the creation of a fast neutral flow via charge exchange between the stellar wind ions and the planetary neutrals. Indeed, our CME simulations indicate a strong stripping of magnetospheric material from the planet, including some of the neutral escaping atmosphere. Our simulations show that the pressure around close-in exoplanets is not much lower, and may be even higher, than the pressure at the top of the planetary atmosphere. Thus, the neutral atmosphere is hydrodynamically escaping with a very small velocity (&lt;15 km s<SUP>-1</SUP>). Moreover, our simulations show that an MHD treatment is essential in order to properly capture the coupled magnetized stellar wind and the escaping atmosphere, despite the atmosphere being neutral. This coupling should be considered when interpreting Lyα observations in the context of exoplanets' atmospheric escape.

[2] 空间天气驱动的系外行星大气逃逸Lyα吸收特征变化：MHD模拟及AU Mic b案例

我们模拟了AU Microscopii b周围的空间环境，以及磁化恒星风与行星大气外流在背景恒星风和日冕物质抛射（CME）条件下的相互作用。我们还计算了受该相互作用影响的背景和逃逸行星大气中中性氢导致的合成Lyα吸收。我们发现，由于恒星风条件高度变化，Lyα吸收表现出高度可变性。在Lyα谱线中观察到强烈的多普勒蓝移分量，这与模拟中实际观测到的大气逃逸速度相矛盾。该结果表明，强多普勒蓝移很可能归因于恒星风，而非逃逸的中性大气，可能是通过恒星风对行星中性气体的平流作用，或通过恒星风离子与行星中性粒子的电荷交换产生的快速中性流。实际上，我们的CME模拟显示，行星的磁层物质，包括部分逃逸的中性大气，会被强烈剥离。我们的模拟表明，近轨系外行星周围的压力并不比行星大气顶端低，甚至可能更高。因此，中性大气以极小的速度（<15 km s^-1）进行流体动力学逃逸。此外，我们的模拟显示，尽管大气为中性，但MHD处理对于正确捕捉磁化恒星风与逃逸大气的耦合至关重要。在解释系外行星大气逃逸相关的Lyα观测时，应考虑这种耦合。

[3] Inferring Intrinsic Stellar EUV and Lyman-Alpha Fluxes and Their Effects on Exoplanet Atmospheres

2024-04-00

EUV radiation is primarily responsible for driving hydrodynamic mass loss and thus determining whether an exoplanet can retain its atmosphere and water. The Lyman-α flux is primarily responsible for photo-dissociating water and methane, and therefore plays a major role in determining the chemistry in the upper atmospheres of exoplanets. Since interstellar hydrogen absorbs much of the EUV and Lyman-α radiation, reconstruction and theoretical techniques are needed to determine the intrinsic flux levels received by exoplanets.

[3] 推断恒星本征EUV和Lyman-Alpha通量及其对系外行星大气的影响

EUV 辐射主要负责驱动流体动力学质量损失，从而决定系外行星是否能够保留其大气和水。Lyman-α 辐射通量主要负责光解水和甲烷，因此在决定系外行星高层大气的化学组成中起着重要作用。由于星际氢会吸收大量的 EUV 和 Lyman-α 辐射，因此需要重建和理论技术来确定系外行星实际接收到的本征辐射通量水平。

[4] Characterization of Terrestrial Exoplanet Atmospheres through Lyman-alpha Transit Observations

2025-09-00

Introduction Lyman-alpha transmission spectroscopy has been a powerful tool for observing hydrogen escape from close-in exoplanets. For instance, Ly-α observations of GJ 436b showed a maximum transit depth of 56% - compared to a 0.69% transit depth in optical wavelengths - due to the hydrodynamic escape of hydrogen from the planet (1). Ly-α observations assist in understanding the evolution of such exoplanets, including characterization of the atmosphere. To date, the Ly-α transit of terrestrial-sized exoplanets has yielded only non-detections for exoplanets such as Trappist 1b/c (2) and 55 Cn e (3) using the Hubble Space Telescope. These non-detections possibly indicate that terrestrial exoplanets do not have enough hydrogen escape to be observed in Ly-α. Despite these non-detections, Earth's hydrogen exosphere has been shown to extend out past 38 Earth-radii, and modelling has suggested that an exoplanet with an Earth-like hydrogen exosphere orbiting an M-dwarf star would be observable with future space telescopes (4). This work models the Ly-α transit of varying terrestrial exoplanets to diagnose atmospheric composition. For example, how does the Ly-α transit of a desiccated planet like Venus compare to a water-rich planet like Earth? Can we discern these differences from future space telescope observations? To test this, we model the thermal escape of hydrogen from terrestrial exoplanets and compute the associated Ly-α transit depth. Atmospheric parameters in the upper atmosphere, such as the mixing ratio of hydrogen, are varied to analyze the resulting Ly-α transit depth. From this, we examine trends in the transit depth to characterize the atmospheres of terrestrial exoplanets. In addition, we derive key attributes of exoplanets that would be detectable with future space telescopes.Methods In this work, the hydrogen exosphere is modelled using the Chamberlain approach (5). Key input parameters needed are the exobase height, the number density of hydrogen, and the exobase temperature. The exobase height and hydrogen number density are calculated by setting up a 2-component atmosphere at the homopause (the altitude where different species can diffusively separate, situated at 100 km from the surface with a total species number density of 1019 molecules cm-3), with a given hydrogen mixing ratio. The species diffuse upward from the homopause until they reach the exobase where the mean free path of the atmosphere is equal to the scale height. We also account for diffusion-limited escape. In a scenario where Jean's escape is larger than the diffusion-limited escape, the number density of hydrogen is scaled to the diffusion-limited value. The exobase temperature is a free parameter in our model, though observations from the solar system indicate that a CO2 dominated exobase is cooler than an atomic oxygen-dominated exobase such as Earth's, due to infrared cooling. Once the hydrogen exosphere is modelled, the radiative transfer code from the open-source model Sunbather (6) is used to model the transit. By default, we consider the planet to be mid-transit with an impact factor of 0. Factors affecting the transit depth, such as thermal line broadening are included. We consider a wide parameter space of planets between 0.5 to 2 Earth radii, with atmospheric temperatures between 100 to 700 K, and hydrogen mixing ratios between 10-10 and 1. For each run of the model, the hydrogen exosphere is computed at several exospheric temperatures between 100 and 1000 K to span the range of exobase temperatures observed in the solar system.Results An example of the number density of hydrogen in the exosphere and the resulting Ly-α transit in shown in Figure 1. In this case, we have modelled an Earth-sized planet with a hydrogen mixing ratio of 10-6 at the homopause that diffuses through an upper atmosphere of atomic oxygen. The exobase is 228 km from the surface with a hydrogen number density of 3.4 x 104, limited by the diffusion. The exobase is dominated by atomic oxygen, and modelled to be at 1000 K. The Ly-α transit depth around a sun-sized star is 275 ppm, much smaller than observed for close-in Neptunes, but possibly observable with future space telescope technology at a distance where geocoronal contamination is minimal. Figure 1: (a) Hydrogen number density in the exosphere as a function of distance from the planet (b) The Ly-α transit in ppm. Figure 2 shows the transit depth of exoplanets with varying masses and hydrogen mixing ratios at the homopause. Here a fixed exobase temperature of 1000 K was used though the simulations were also run with other exobase temperatures (not shown here, though the Ly-α transit typically increases with increasing exobase temperature due to the hydrogen atoms escaping the exobase more easily). The transit depth shows a strong dependence on hydrogen mixing ratio, with higher hydrogen mixing ratios resulting in a larger transit depth. The mass of the planet also affects the transit depth, with lower mass planets allowing for hydrogen escape more easily than more massive planets.Figure 2: Ly-α transit depths for exoplanets with varying masses and hydrogen mixing ratios.We will also present the effects of photoionization, making the hydrogen no longer observable in Ly-α. Lastly, we will test the ability to detect these Ly-α transits with space telescopes. (1) Ehrenreich D, Bourrier V, Wheatley PJ, des Etangs AL, Hébrard G, Udry S, et al. A giant comet-like cloud of hydrogen escaping the warm Neptune-mass exoplanet GJ 436b. Nature. 2015 Jun;522(7557):459-61.(2) Bourrier V, Ehrenreich D, Wheatley PJ, Bolmont E, Gillon M, Wit J de, et al. Reconnaissance of the TRAPPIST-1 exoplanet system in the Lyman-α line. Astron Astrophys. 2017 Mar 1;599:L3.(3) Salz M, Czesla S, Schneider PC, Schmitt JHMM. Simulating the escaping atmospheres of hot gas planets in the solar neighborhood. Astron Astrophys. 2016 Feb 1;586:A75.(4) Santos LA dos, Bourrier V, Ehrenreich D, Kameda S. Observability of hydrogen-rich exospheres in Earth-like exoplanets. Astron Astrophys. 2019 Feb 1;622:A46.(5) Chamberlain JW. Planetary coronae and atmospheric evaporation. Planet Space Sci. 1963 Aug 1;11(8):901-60.(6) Linssen D, Shih J, MacLeod M, Oklopčić A. The open-source sunbather code: Modeling escaping planetary atmospheres and their transit spectra. Astron Astrophys. 2024 Aug 1;688:A43.

[4] 通过Lyman-alpha凌日观测对类地系外行星大气的特征研究

引言 Lyman-α透射光谱一直是观测近轨道系外行星氢逃逸的重要工具。例如，对GJ 436b的Ly-α观测显示最大凌日深度为56%，而在可见光波段仅为0.69%，这是由于该行星发生了氢的流体动力学逃逸（1）。Ly-α观测有助于理解此类系外行星的大气演化，包括大气的特性。迄今为止，利用哈勃空间望远镜对类地尺寸系外行星的Ly-α凌日观测仅获得了未探测到的结果，如Trappist 1b/c（2）和55 Cn e（3）。这些未探测到的结果可能表明，类地系外行星的氢逃逸量不足以在Ly-α中被观测到。尽管如此，地球的氢外逸层已被证明可延伸至38个地球半径之外，且建模表明，围绕M矮星运行、拥有类似地球氢外逸层的系外行星，有望被未来空间望远镜观测到（4）。本工作对不同类地系外行星的Ly-α凌日进行建模，以诊断大气组成。例如，像金星这样干燥的行星与像地球这样富水行星的Ly-α凌日有何不同？我们能否通过未来空间望远镜观测区分这些差异？为此，我们对类地系外行星的氢热逃逸进行建模，并计算相应的Ly-α凌日深度。通过改变上层大气中的氢混合比等参数，分析其对Ly-α凌日深度的影响。由此，我们研究凌日深度的变化趋势，以刻画类地系外行星的大气。此外，我们还推导出未来空间望远镜可探测的系外行星的关键属性。方法 本工作采用Chamberlain方法（5）对氢外逸层进行建模。所需关键输入参数包括外逸基高度、氢数密度和外逸基温度。外逸基高度和氢数密度通过在同温层顶（不同组分可发生扩散分离的高度，距地表100 km，总数密度为1019分子/cm3）设置两组分大气，并给定氢混合比来计算。各组分自同温层顶向上扩散，直至到达外逸基，此处大气的平均自由程等于尺度高度。我们还考虑了扩散极限逃逸。在Jean逃逸大于扩散极限逃逸的情况下，氢数密度被缩放至扩散极限值。外逸基温度为模型的自由参数，太阳系观测表明，CO2主导的外逸基比以原子氧为主的地球外逸基更冷，原因是红外冷却。氢外逸层建模完成后，采用开源模型Sunbather（6）的辐射传输代码对凌日进行建模。默认情况下，行星处于凌日中点，冲击参数为0。包括热线展宽等影响凌日深度的因素。我们考虑了行星半径0.5至2地球半径、大气温度100至700 K、氢混合比10^-10至1的广泛参数空间。每次模型运行时，氢外逸层在100至1000 K的多个外逸基温度下计算，以覆盖太阳系观测到的外逸基温度范围。结果 图1展示了外逸层中氢数密度及其对应的Ly-α凌日。此例中，我们模拟了一个地球大小、同温层顶氢混合比为10^-6、在原子氧主导的上层大气中扩散的行星。外逸基距地表228 km，氢数密度为3.4 x 10^4，受扩散限制。外逸基以原子氧为主，温度设为1000 K。绕太阳大小恒星的Ly-α凌日深度为275 ppm，远小于近轨道海王星的观测值，但在未来空间望远镜技术下、地球辉污染较小的距离上有望被观测到。图1：（a）外逸层中氢数密度随离行星距离的变化；（b）Ly-α凌日深度（ppm）。图2展示了不同质量和同温层顶氢混合比的系外行星的凌日深度。此处外逸基温度固定为1000 K，尽管模拟也在其他温度下进行（未展示，通常外逸基温度升高会使Ly-α凌日加深，因为氢原子更易逃逸）。凌日深度对氢混合比高度敏感，混合比越高，凌日深度越大。行星质量也影响凌日深度，质量较小的行星更易发生氢逃逸。图2：不同质量和氢混合比系外行星的Ly-α凌日深度。我们还将展示光致电离的影响，使氢在Ly-α中不再可见。最后，我们将测试利用空间望远镜探测这些Ly-α凌日的能力。（1）Ehrenreich D等，2015，Nature，522(7557):459-61。（2）Bourrier V等，2017，Astron Astrophys，599:L3。（3）Salz M等，2016，Astron Astrophys，586:A75。（4）Santos LA dos等，2019，Astron Astrophys，622:A46。（5）Chamberlain JW，1963，Planet Space Sci，11(8):901-60。（6）Linssen D等，2024，Astron Astrophys，688:A43。

[5] Testing Lyα Emission-line Reconstruction Routines at Multiple Velocities in One System

2022-09-00

The 1215.67 Å H I Lyα emission line dominates the ultraviolet flux of low-mass stars, including the majority of known exoplanet hosts. Unfortunately, strong attenuation by the interstellar medium (ISM) obscures the line core in most stars, requiring the intrinsic Lyα flux to be reconstructed based on fits to the line wings. We present a test of the widely used Lyα emission-line reconstruction code LYAPY using phase-resolved, medium-resolution STIS G140M observations of the close white dwarf-M dwarf binary EG UMa. The Doppler shifts induced by the binary orbital motion move the Lyα emission line in and out of the region of strong ISM attenuation. Reconstructions of each spectrum should produce the same Lyα profile regardless of phase, under the well-justified assumption that there is no intrinsic line variability between observations. Instead, we find that the reconstructions underestimate the Lyα flux by almost a factor of 2 for the lowest velocity, most attenuated spectrum, due to a degeneracy between the intrinsic Lyα and ISM profiles. Our results imply that many stellar Lyα fluxes derived from G140M spectra reported in the literature may be underestimated, with potential consequences for, for example, estimates of extreme-ultraviolet stellar spectra and ultraviolet inputs into simulations of exoplanet atmospheres.

[5] 在同一系统中多速度下测试Lyα发射线重建程序

1215.67 Å H I Lyα发射线主导了低质量恒星（包括大多数已知系外行星宿主星）的紫外通量。不幸的是，星际介质（ISM）的强烈吸收遮蔽了大多数恒星的谱线中心，需要通过拟合谱线翼来重建本征Lyα通量。我们利用近白矮星-红矮星双星EG UMa的相位分辨、中等分辨率STIS G140M观测，对广泛使用的Lyα发射线重建代码LYAPY进行了测试。双星轨道运动引起的多普勒移动使Lyα发射线进出强ISM吸收区域。每个光谱的重建在假设观测间本征谱线无变化的前提下，应产生相同的Lyα谱线。然而，我们发现对于速度最低、吸收最强的光谱，重建结果低估了Lyα通量近2倍，原因在于本征Lyα与ISM谱线之间的退化。我们的结果意味着，文献中基于G140M光谱报告的许多恒星Lyα通量可能被低估，这对极紫外恒星光谱的估算以及系外行星大气模拟中的紫外输入等方面可能产生影响。

[6] The impact of coronal mass ejections and flares on the atmosphere of the hot Jupiter HD189733b

2022-02-00

High-energy stellar irradiation can photoevaporate planetary atmospheres, which can be observed in spectroscopic transits of hydrogen lines. For the exoplanet HD189733b, multiple observations in the Ly α line have shown that atmospheric evaporation is variable, going from undetected to enhanced evaporation in a 1.5-yr interval. Coincidentally or not, when HD189733b was observed to be evaporating, a stellar flare had just occurred 8 h prior to the observation. This led to the question of whether this temporal variation in evaporation occurred due to the flare, an unseen associated coronal mass ejection (CME), or even the simultaneous effect of both. In this work, we investigate the impact of flares (radiation), winds, and CMEs (particles) on the atmosphere of HD189733b using three-dimensional radiation hydrodynamic simulations that self-consistently include stellar photon heating. We study four cases: first, the quiescent phase including stellar wind; secondly, a flare; thirdly, a CME; and fourthly, a flare that is followed by a CME. Compared to the quiescent case, we find that the flare alone increases the evaporation rate by only 25 per cent, while the CME leads to a factor of 4 increments. We calculate Ly α synthetic transits and find that the flare alone cannot explain the observed high blueshifted velocities seen in the Ly α. The CME, however, leads to an increase in the velocity of escaping atmospheres, enhancing the blueshifted transit depth. While the effects of CMEs show a promising potential, our models are not able to fully explain the blueshifted transit depths, indicating that they might require additional physical mechanisms.

[6] 日冕物质抛射和耀斑对热木星HD189733b大气的影响

高能恒星辐射可导致行星大气光致蒸发，这一过程可通过氢谱线的凌日光谱观测到。对于系外行星HD189733b，多次Ly α谱线观测显示大气逃逸具有可变性，在1.5年内从未探测到到增强逃逸。巧合或非巧合地，在HD189733b被观测到大气逃逸时，观测前8小时刚发生了一次恒星耀斑。这引发了一个问题：这种逃逸的时间变化是否由耀斑、未被观测到的相关日冕物质抛射（CME），或两者共同作用引起。本文利用三维辐射流体动力学模拟，包含恒星光子加热，自洽地研究了耀斑（辐射）、恒星风和CME（粒子）对HD189733b大气的影响。我们研究了四种情形：一是静息期（含恒星风），二是耀斑，三是CME，四是耀斑后紧随CME。与静息期相比，仅有耀斑时逃逸速率仅增加25%，而CME可使逃逸速率增加4倍。我们计算了Ly α合成凌日光谱，发现仅有耀斑无法解释观测到的高蓝移速度，而CME则可提升逃逸大气的速度，增强蓝移凌日深度。尽管CME的影响显示出潜力，我们的模型仍无法完全解释蓝移凌日深度，表明可能还需要其他物理机制。

This coupling should be considered when interpreting Lyα" role="presentation" tabindex="0"&gt;αobservations in the context of exoplanets atmospheric escape.

[7] 空间天气驱动的系外行星大气逃逸Ly-alpha吸收特征变化：MHD模拟及AU Mic b案例

我们模拟了 AU Microscopii b 周围的空间环境，以及磁化恒星风与行星大气外流在背景恒星风和日冕物质抛射（CME）条件下的相互作用。我们还计算了由于该相互作用影响下的中性氢在背景和逃逸行星大气中的合成 Lyα 吸收。我们发现 Lyα 吸收由于恒星风条件的剧烈变化而高度可变。在 Lyα 轮廓中观察到强烈的多普勒蓝移分量，这与模拟中实际观测到的逃逸速度相矛盾。该结果表明，强多普勒蓝移很可能归因于恒星风，而非逃逸的中性大气，可能是通过恒星风对行星中性气体的平流，或通过恒星风离子与行星中性粒子的电荷交换产生的快速中性流。我们的 CME 模拟显示，行星磁层物质，包括部分逃逸的中性大气，会被强烈剥离。模拟结果表明，近轨道系外行星周围的压力并不比行星大气顶端低多少，甚至可能更高。因此，中性大气以极小的速度（<15 km s⁻¹）进行流体动力学逃逸。此外，我们的模拟显示，尽管大气为中性，但 MHD 处理对于正确捕捉磁化恒星风与逃逸大气的耦合至关重要。在解释系外行星大气逃逸相关的 Lyα 观测时，应考虑这种耦合。

[8] Testing Lyα Emission-line Reconstruction Routines at Multiple Velocities in One System

2023-01-00

The 1215.67 Å H I Lyα emission line dominates the ultraviolet flux of low-mass stars, including the majority of known exoplanet hosts. Unfortunately, strong attenuation by the interstellar medium (ISM) obscures the line core in most stars, requiring the intrinsic Lyα flux to be reconstructed based on fits to the line wings. We present a test of the widely used Lyα emission-line reconstruction code LYAPY using phase-resolved, medium-resolution STIS G140M observations of the close white dwarf-M dwarf binary EG UMa. The Doppler shifts induced by the binary orbital motion move the Lyα emission line in and out of the region of strong ISM attenuation.Reconstructions of each spectrum should produce the same Lyα profile regardless of phase, under the well-justified assumption that there is no intrinsic line variability between observations. Instead, we find that the reconstructions underestimate the Lyα flux by almost a factor of 2 for the lowest velocity, most attenuated spectrum, due to a degeneracy between the intrinsic Lyα and ISM profiles. Our results imply that many stellar Lyα fluxes derived from G140M spectra reported in the literature may be underestimated, with potential consequences for, for example, estimates of extreme-ultraviolet stellar spectra and ultraviolet inputs into simulations of exoplanet atmospheres.

[8] 在同一系统中测试Lyα发射线重建程序在多速度下的表现

1215.67 Å H I Lyα 发射线主导了低质量恒星的紫外通量，包括大多数已知系外行星宿主。不幸的是，星际介质（ISM）的强烈吸收遮蔽了大多数恒星的该线核心，因此需要通过拟合线翼来重建本征 Lyα 通量。我们利用近白矮星-红矮星双星 EG UMa 的相位分辨、中等分辨率 STIS G140M 观测，对广泛使用的 Lyα 发射线重建代码 LYAPY 进行了测试。双星轨道运动引起的多普勒移动使 Lyα 发射线在强 ISM 吸收区域内外移动。在假设观测间本征线型无变化的合理前提下，每个光谱的重建结果应产生相同的 Lyα 轮廓。然而，我们发现对于速度最低、吸收最强的光谱，重建结果低估了 Lyα 通量，几乎达 2 倍，这是由于本征 Lyα 与 ISM 轮廓之间的简并性所致。我们的结果表明，文献中基于 G140M 光谱得到的许多恒星 Lyα 通量可能被低估，这对极紫外恒星光谱的估算以及系外行星大气模拟中的紫外输入等方面可能产生影响。
