item_032 · sys_F
How can empirical high-resolution spectra of Solar System bodies be used as analogs to constrain and validate models of exoplanet atmospheres, and what are the limitations of such analogies?
如何利用太阳系天体的实测高分辨率光谱作为类比，约束和验证系外行星大气模型，这种类比方法的局限性是什么？
8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] Exploring and Validating Exoplanet Atmospheric Retrievals with Solar System Analog Observations

2023-01-00

Solar system observations that serve as analogs for exoplanet remote sensing data can provide important opportunities to validate ideas and models related to exoplanet environments. Critically, and unlike true exoplanet observations, solar system analog data benefit from available high-quality ground- or orbiter-derived "truth" constraints that enable strong validations of exoplanet data interpretation tools. In this work, we first present a versatile atmospheric retrieval suite, capable of application to reflected light, thermal emission, and transmission observations spanning a broad range of wavelengths and thermochemical conditions. The tool-dubbed rfast-is designed, in part, to enable exoplanet mission concept feasibility studies. Following model validation, the retrieval tool is applied to a range of solar system analog observations for exoplanet environments. Retrieval studies using Earth reflected light observations from NASA's EPOXI mission provide a key proof of concept for exo-Earth direct imaging concept missions under development. Inverse modeling applied to an infrared spectrum of Earth from the Mars Global Surveyor Thermal Emission Spectrometer achieves good constraints on atmospheric gases, including many biosignature gases. Finally, retrieval analysis applied to a transit spectrum of Titan derived from the Cassini Visual and Infrared Mapping Spectrometer provides a proof of concept for interpreting more feature-rich transiting exoplanet observations from NASA's James Webb Space Telescope. In the future, solar system analog observations for exoplanets could be used to verify exoplanet models and parameterizations, and future exoplanet analog observations of any solar system worlds from planetary science missions should be encouraged.

[1] 利用太阳系类比观测探索和验证系外行星大气检索

作为系外行星遥感数据类比的太阳系观测，为验证与系外行星环境相关的理论和模型提供了重要机会。关键在于，与真正的系外行星观测不同，太阳系类比数据受益于高质量地面或轨道器获得的“真实”约束，从而能够强有力地验证系外行星数据解释工具。在本工作中，我们首先介绍了一套多功能大气检索工具，能够应用于反射光、热发射和透射观测，涵盖广泛波长和热化学条件。该工具（命名为rfast）部分旨在支持系外行星任务概念可行性研究。在模型验证后，该检索工具被应用于一系列系外行星环境的太阳系类比观测。利用NASA EPOXI任务获得的地球反射光观测进行的检索研究，为正在开发的类地系外行星直接成像任务提供了关键的概念验证。对火星全球探测器热发射光谱仪获得的地球红外光谱进行反演建模，对包括多种生物标志气体在内的大气气体给出了良好约束。最后，对卡西尼可见与红外测绘光谱仪获得的土卫六凌日光谱进行检索分析，为解释NASA詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜未来更丰富特征的系外行星凌日观测提供了概念验证。未来，太阳系类比观测可用于验证系外行星模型和参数化方法，并应鼓励行星科学任务对太阳系天体进行更多系外行星类比观测。

[2] Bayesian Spectroscopic Characterization of Directly Imaged Planets and Brown Dwarfs and Implications for Ultracool Model Atmospheres

2021-09-00

Spectroscopic characterization of imaged exoplanets and brown dwarfs is essential for understanding their atmospheres, formation, and evolution, but such work is challenged by the unavoidably simplified model atmospheres needed to interpret spectra. While most previous work has focused on single or at most a few objects, comparing a large collection of spectra to models can uncover trends in data-model inconsistencies needed to improve model predictions, thereby leading to robust properties from exoplanet and brown dwarf spectra. Therefore, we are conducting a systematic analysis of a valuable but underutilized resource: the numerous high-quality spectra of (directly imaged and free-floating) exoplanets and brown dwarfs already accumulated by the community. Focusing on the cool-temperature end, we have constructed a Bayesian modeling framework using the new Sonora-Bobcat model atmospheres and have applied it to study near-infrared low-resolution spectra of &gt;50 late-T imaged planets and brown dwarfs (≍600-1200K, ≍10-70 MJup) and infer their physical properties (effective temperature, surface gravity, metallicity, radii, mass). By virtue of having such a large sample of high-quality spectra, our analysis identifies the systematic offsets between observed and model spectra as a function of wavelength and physical properties to pinpoint specific shortcomings in model predictions. We have also found that the spectroscopically inferred metallicities, ages, and masses of our sample all considerably deviate from expectations, suggesting the physical and chemical assumptions made within these models need to be improved to fully interpret data. Our work has established a systematic validation of cloudless model atmospheres to date and we discuss extending such analysis to wider temperature and wavelength (e.g., JWST) ranges, as well as finding new planetary-mass and brown dwarf benchmarks, in order to validate ultracool model atmospheres over larger parameter space.

[2] 直接成像行星与褐矮星的贝叶斯光谱特征及其对超冷模型大气的启示

对直接成像系外行星和褐矮星的光谱特征分析对于理解其大气、形成和演化至关重要，但此类研究受限于为解释光谱而采用的不可避免的简化大气模型。以往大多数工作集中于单个或少数目标，而将大量光谱与模型对比可以揭示数据与模型不一致的趋势，从而改进模型预测，并获得更可靠的系外行星和褐矮星光谱物理参数。因此，我们正在系统分析一个宝贵但利用不足的资源：社区已积累的大量高质量（直接成像和自由漂浮）系外行星和褐矮星光谱。我们聚焦于低温端，构建了基于新Sonora-Bobcat大气模型的贝叶斯建模框架，并应用于研究50余颗晚T型成像行星和褐矮星（约600-1200K，约10-70 MJup）的近红外低分辨率光谱，推断其物理参数（有效温度、表面重力、金属丰度、半径、质量）。凭借大样本高质量光谱，我们的分析识别了观测与模型光谱在不同波长和物理参数下的系统性偏差，定位了模型预测的具体不足。我们还发现，样本的光谱推断金属丰度、年龄和质量均与预期有较大偏离，表明模型中的物理和化学假设需改进以充分解释观测数据。我们的工作建立了迄今为止无云大气模型的系统性验证，并讨论了将此类分析扩展到更宽温度和波长范围（如JWST），以及寻找新的行星质量和褐矮星基准体，以在更大参数空间内验证超冷大气模型。

[3] Exoplanets &amp; Stars: Characterisation of atmospheres at High Spectral Resolution

2021-03-00

The study of exoplanets atmospheres is one of the most intriguing challenges in exoplanet field nowadays and the High Resolution Spectroscopy (HRS) has recently emerged as one of the leading methods for detecting atomic and molecular species in their atmospheres.Nevertheless extraordinary results have been achieved (Birkby, 2018), HRS alone is not enough. 1D models of the host star have been coupled to HRS observations, but they do not reproduce the complexity of stellar convection mechanism (Chiavassa &amp; Brogi, 2019). On the contrary, 3D Radiative Hydrodynamical (3D RHD) simulations take it into account intrinsically, allowing us to correctly reproduce asymmetric and blue-shifted spectral lines due to the granulation pattern of the stellar disk, which is a very important source of uncertainties (Chiavassa et al. 2017). However, numerical simulations have been computed independently for star and planet so far, while the acquired spectrum contains both the signals. For instance, some molecular species (e.g, CO) form in the same region of the spectrum, thus planetary and stellar spectral lines are completely mixed and overlapped. A next step forward is needed: computing stellar and planetary models together during the planet transit.With our work, we aim at upgrading the already-in-place 3D radiative transfer code Optim3D (Chiavassa et al. 2009) — largely used for stellar purposes so far — to take into account also the exoplanetary contribution and finally carry out a full characterisation of planets and their host stars. I will present the advantages of the simultaneous use of 3D RHD stellar simulations and exoplanet's Global Climate Models (GCMs) in generating unprecedented precise synthetic spectra and the two main reasons why stars and planets' spectra should be computed and interpreted together.

[3] 系外行星与恒星：高光谱分辨率下的大气特征研究

系外行星大气的研究是当前系外行星领域最具吸引力的挑战之一，高分辨率光谱（HRS）近年来已成为探测其大气中原子和分子的重要方法之一。尽管已经取得了非凡的成果（Birkby, 2018），但仅靠HRS还不够。宿主恒星的一维模型已与HRS观测结合使用，但这些模型无法再现恒星对流机制的复杂性（Chiavassa & Brogi, 2019）。相反，三维辐射流体力学（3D RHD）模拟本质上考虑了这一点，使我们能够正确再现由于恒星盘颗粒化结构导致的不对称和蓝移光谱线，这是一个非常重要的不确定性来源（Chiavassa et al. 2017）。然而，目前数值模拟仍是分别针对恒星和行星独立计算的，而实际获得的光谱同时包含了两者的信号。例如，一些分子（如CO）在光谱的同一区域形成，因此行星和恒星的光谱线完全混合和重叠。下一步需要将恒星和行星模型在行星凌星期间同时计算。我们的工作旨在升级现有的三维辐射转移代码Optim3D（Chiavassa et al. 2009），该代码迄今主要用于恒星研究，以同时考虑系外行星的贡献，最终实现对行星及其宿主恒星的全面特性刻画。我将展示同时使用3D RHD恒星模拟和系外行星全球气候模型（GCMs）在生成前所未有的高精度合成光谱方面的优势，以及为什么恒星和行星光谱应当一起计算和解释的两个主要原因。

[4] From Solar System to Exoplanets: What can we learn from Planetary Spectroscopy?

2022-12-00

The purpose of this paper is to address the question: Using our knowledge of infrared planetary spectroscopy, what can we learn about the atmospheres of exoplanets? In a first part, a simplified classification of exoplanets, assuming thermochemical equilibrium, is presented, based on their masses and their equilibrium temperatures, in order to propose some possible estimations about their atmospheric composition. In the second part, infrared spectra of planets are discussed, in order to see what lessons can be drawn for exoplanetary spectroscopy. In the last part, we consider the solar system as it would appear from a star located in the ecliptic plane. It first appears that the solar system (except in a few specific cases) would not be seen as a multiple system, because, contrary to many exoplanetary systems, the planets are too far from the Sun and the inclinations of their orbits with respect to the ecliptic plane are too high. Primary transit synthetic spectra of solar system planets are used to discuss the relative merits of transmission and direct emission spectroscopy for probing exoplanetary atmospheres.

[4] 从太阳系到系外行星：行星光谱学能带来什么启示？

本文旨在探讨这样一个问题：利用我们对红外行星光谱学的知识，可以了解系外行星大气哪些信息？第一部分，假设热化学平衡，基于行星质量和平衡温度，提出了一个简化的系外行星分类，以便对其大气成分做出一些可能的估计。第二部分，讨论了行星的红外光谱，以分析对系外行星光谱学有何启示。最后一部分，我们考虑如果从黄道面上一颗恒星的视角观测太阳系，会是什么样子。首先可以发现，太阳系（除少数特例外）不会被视为一个多行星系统，因为与许多系外行星系统不同，太阳系行星距离太阳太远，且其轨道相对于黄道面的倾角过大。利用太阳系行星的主凌合成光谱，讨论了透射光谱和直接发射光谱在探测系外行星大气方面的相对优劣。

[5] Windows Into Other Worlds: Pitfalls in the physical interpretation of exoplanet atmospheric spectroscopy

2024-11-00

Atmospheric spectroscopy provides a window into the properties of exoplanets. However, the physical interpretation of retrieved data and its implications for the internal properties of exoplanets remains nebulous. This letter addresses three misconceptions held by some atmospheric spectroscopists regarding the connection between observed chemical abundances and theory: (1) Whether atmospheric spectroscopy can provide the bulk atmospheric chemistry, (2) whether it can identify if a planet is cloudless, and (3) whether atmospheric evaporation arguments can be used to dismiss certain compositions inferred through spectroscopy. This letter concludes by exploring applications of remote sensing in the quest for the search for life outside of our solar system.

[5] 通向其他世界的窗口：系外行星大气光谱物理解释中的陷阱

大气光谱学为研究系外行星性质提供了窗口。然而，对检索数据的物理解释及其对系外行星内部性质的意义仍不明确。本通讯针对部分大气光谱学家关于观测到的化学丰度与理论之间联系的三种误解进行讨论：（1）大气光谱学是否能提供整体大气化学信息，（2）是否能识别行星是否无云，以及（3）是否可以用大气逃逸论据否定通过光谱学推断出的某些成分。最后，本文探讨了遥感技术在太阳系外生命搜寻中的应用前景。

[6] Solar System Observations as a Proving Ground for Exoplanet Atmospheric Inference

2022-11-00

Observations of solar system worlds that are analogs for exoplanet data provide an important avenue for validating exoplanet retrieval tools. To explore and validate such tools, we apply inverse models to spacecraft data for both Earth and Titan.

[6] 将太阳系观测作为系外行星大气推断的试验场

对太阳系内与系外行星观测数据类似的天体进行观测，为验证系外行星检索工具提供了重要途径。为探索和验证此类工具，我们将反演模型应用于地球和土卫六的航天器观测数据。

[7] Clearing the Air: Solar System Bodies as Ground Truth for Aerosol Models in Exoplanet Atmospheric Retrievals

2026-05-00

As we advance into the next chapter in our search for life beyond Earth with state-of-the-art technology such as the James Webb Space Telescope, it is essential that we are able to accurately discern compositional information, including potential biosignatures, from exoplanet transmission spectra through atmospheric retrieval pipelines. As such, ensuring the efficacy of the models in these pipelines is more important than ever, and we face a major challenge in this pursuit: the parameterizations of aerosols such as clouds, dusts, and hazes. Aerosols are ubiquitous in the atmospheres of both Solar System bodies and exoplanets, and representing them accurately in exoplanet atmospheric models is key to deriving the best possible compositional information from transit spectra. Fortunately, we have the opportunity to use pre-existing Solar System observations to validate and improve exoplanet-focused approaches to representing aerosol structures. We derive aerosol profiles from occultation data of Solar System worlds with known atmospheric composition such as Mars. When compared to aerosol parameterizations in retrieval pipelines, these profiles provide an opportunity for ground-truth verification of exoplanet atmospheric characterization tools.

[7] 澄清大气：太阳系天体作为系外行星大气检索气溶胶模型的基准

随着我们利用詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜等最先进技术进入地外生命探索的新阶段，能够通过大气检索流程从系外行星透射光谱中准确辨别组成信息（包括潜在生物标志物）变得至关重要。因此，确保这些流程中模型的有效性比以往任何时候都更加重要，而我们在这一过程中面临的主要挑战之一是气溶胶（如云、尘埃和薄雾）的参数化。气溶胶在太阳系天体和系外行星的大气中普遍存在，在系外行星大气模型中准确表示它们，是从凌日光谱中获得最佳组成信息的关键。幸运的是，我们有机会利用已有的太阳系观测数据来验证和改进面向系外行星的气溶胶结构表示方法。我们从已知大气成分的太阳系天体（如火星）的掩星数据中推导气溶胶剖面。与检索流程中的气溶胶参数化方法相比，这些剖面为系外行星大气特征工具的实地验证提供了机会。

[8] Atmospheric Retrievals: Limitations, Challenges, and the Road Ahead

2026-06-00

Atmospheric retrieval is now the default framework for interpreting exoplanet and brown dwarf spectra from space and ground-based platforms, yet its limitations often become most apparent precisely in the high-quality data regime where it is most heavily applied. In this talk, I will focus on the challenges of contemporary retrieval approaches, drawing on examples across low- and high-resolution spectroscopy, multiple wavelength regimes, and substellar benchmark atmospheres. Key challenges include degeneracies between temperature structure, composition, and aerosols; biases introduced by incomplete or overly flexible model assumptions; the influence of priors in data-limited regimes; uncertainty in the "correctness" of the data and separating planetary signal from astrophysical noise sources; and the difficulty of validating inference when forward models are only approximate representations of complex, multidimensional atmospheres. I will then outline several future directions that can move the field forward. These include leveraging combined low-and-hi-res datasets, incorporating more realistic atmospheric physics without sacrificing interpretability, and inference methods that enable more rapid computation enabling more robustness tests. I will also briefly discuss the role of machine-learning techniques as computational accelerators and diagnostic tools, rather than black-box replacements for physical models. The goal is to provide a concise, critical perspective on how retrieval can evolve from a powerful but fragile technique into a robust framework for population-level studies and the eventual characterization of potentially habitable worlds.

[8] 大气检索：局限性、挑战与未来方向

大气检索现已成为解释来自空间和地基平台的系外行星及褐矮星光谱的默认框架，但其局限性往往在高质量数据领域最为突出，而该领域也是检索应用最广泛的地方。本报告将聚焦于当前检索方法面临的挑战，涵盖低分辨率和高分辨率光谱、多波段以及亚恒星基准大气的案例。主要挑战包括温度结构、成分和气溶胶之间的退化性；不完整或过于灵活的模型假设引入的偏差；先验在数据有限情形下的影响；对数据“正确性”的不确定性以及如何将行星信号与天体噪声源区分开；以及当前前向模型仅为复杂多维大气近似时，推断验证的困难。随后我将概述推动该领域发展的若干未来方向，包括结合低分辨率和高分辨率数据集、在不牺牲可解释性的前提下引入更真实的大气物理，以及能够更快计算、从而实现更强健性测试的推断方法。我还将简要讨论机器学习技术作为计算加速器和诊断工具的作用，而非物理模型的黑箱替代品。目标是为检索方法如何从强大但脆弱的技术演变为适用于总体研究和最终潜在宜居世界表征的稳健框架，提供简明而批判性的视角。

高被引研究“HD 209458b in new light: evidence of nitrogen chemistry, patchy clouds and sub-solar water”的核心结论在独立数据集和不同分析假设下是否成立？

[1] The Watery Atmosphere of HD 209458 b Revealed by Joint K- and L-band High-resolution Spectroscopy

2025-10-00

We present a joint analysis of high-resolution K- and L-band observations of the benchmark hot Jupiter HD 209458 b from the Keck Planet Imager and Characterizer. One half-night of observations was obtained in each bandpass, covering similar preeclipse phases. The two epochs were then jointly analyzed using our atmospheric retrieval pipeline based on petitRADTRANS to constrain the atmospheric pressure─temperature profile and chemical composition. Consistent with recent results from JWST observations at lower spectral resolution, we obtain an oxygen-rich composition for HD 209458 b (C/O &lt; 10<SUP>−3</SUP> at 95% confidence) and a lower limit on the volatile metallicity similar to the solar value ([(C + O)/H] &gt; −0.2 at 95% confidence). Leveraging the large spectral grasp of the multiband observations, we constrain the atmospheric H<SUB>2</SUB>O mixing ratio to <inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:mi>log</mml:mi><mml:msub><mml:mi>H</mml:mi><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:msub><mml:mi>O</mml:mi><mml:mi>V MR</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>&gt;</mml:mo><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>3.1</mml:mn></mml:math> </inline-formula> at 95% confidence, and obtain 95% upper limits on the atmospheric mixing ratios of CO (&lt;10<SUP>−4.8</SUP>), CH<SUB>4</SUB> (&lt;10<SUP>−4.5</SUP>), NH<SUB>3</SUB> (&lt;10<SUP>−5.8</SUP>), H<SUB>2</SUB>S (&lt;10<SUP>−3.3</SUP>), and HCN (&lt;10<SUP>−5.6</SUP>). The limits on CH<SUB>4</SUB>, NH<SUB>3</SUB>, and HCN are consistent with recent results from JWST transmission spectroscopy, demonstrating the value of multiband, ground-based high-resolution spectroscopy for precisely constraining trace-species abundances in exoplanet atmospheres. The retrieved low-C/O, moderate-metallicity composition for HD 209458 b is consistent with formation scenarios involving late accretion of substantial quantities of oxygen-rich refractory solids and/or ices.

[1] 通过联合K波段和L波段高分辨率光谱揭示HD 209458 b的富水大气

我们对基准热木星 HD 209458 b 的 Keck Planet Imager and Characterizer 获得的高分辨率 K 波段和 L 波段观测进行了联合分析。每个波段各获得了半夜的观测，覆盖了类似的凌前相位。随后，我们利用基于 petitRADTRANS 的大气检索流程对这两个历元的数据进行了联合分析，以约束大气的压强-温度剖面和化学组成。与近期 JWST 低光谱分辨率观测结果一致，我们获得了 HD 209458 b 的富氧组成（C/O < 10^−3，95%置信度）和与太阳值相当的挥发性金属丰度下限（[(C + O)/H] > −0.2，95%置信度）。借助多波段观测的广泛波长覆盖，我们将大气 H2O 混合比约束为 log(H2O_VMR) > −3.1（95%置信度），并获得了 CO（<10^−4.8）、CH4（<10^−4.5）、NH3（<10^−5.8）、H2S（<10^−3.3）和 HCN（<10^−5.6）的大气混合比 95% 上限。CH4、NH3 和 HCN 的上限与近期 JWST 透射光谱结果一致，显示多波段地基高分辨率光谱在精确约束系外行星大气微量组分丰度方面的价值。HD 209458 b 检索得到的低 C/O、中等金属丰度组成与其形成过程中晚期吸积大量富氧难挥发固体和/或冰的情景一致。

[2] Revised chemistry of HD209458 b

2022-06-00

We characterize the atmosphere of the iconic giant planet HD209458 b based on a joint modeling effort of the available observations. <P />HD 209458 b is the first planet with a reported detection of a chemical species in its atmosphere, namely Na I, and also the one with the largest number of reported species (H I, O I, C II, Mg I, Fe II, Ca I, Sc I, H2O, CO, HCN, CH4, C2H2 and NH3). However, recent studies casted doubts on the authenticity of some detections. Most of the discrepant results are based on data obtained with different observing techniques, especially using low- and high-resolution spectroscopy. We performed a joint analysis of archive data to exploit the synergies between multiple observing techniques. <P />We reconciled discrepant results reported in the literature by identifying a range of planetary atmospheres that are consistent with both low- and high-resolution spectroscopic observations. The joint analysis enabled us to place tighter constraints on the chemical composition and physical state of the HD209458 b atmosphere. We discuss our findings providing an updated picture of this planetary system.

[2] HD209458 b的化学成分修订

我们基于对现有观测数据的联合建模，对标志性巨行星HD 209458 b的大气进行了特性分析。<P />HD 209458 b是首个在其大气中探测到化学物质（Na I）的行星，也是已报道物种数量最多的行星（H I、O I、C II、Mg I、Fe II、Ca I、Sc I、H2O、CO、HCN、CH4、C2H2和NH3）。然而，近期研究对部分探测结果的真实性提出了质疑。大多数存在分歧的结果基于不同观测技术获得的数据，尤其是低分辨率和高分辨率光谱。我们对档案数据进行了联合分析，以利用多种观测技术之间的协同效应。<P />我们通过识别一系列同时与低分辨率和高分辨率光谱观测一致的行星大气，调和了文献中报道的分歧结果。联合分析使我们能够对HD 209458 b大气的化学组成和物理状态施加更严格的约束。我们讨论了这些发现，并给出了该行星系统的最新图景。

[4] Hello, Old Friend: Dayside thermal emission of HD 209458b analyzed using IGRINS2 high-resolution observations

2026-06-00

Among the Hot Jupiter population, HD209458b remains the definitive benchmark: the first transiting exoplanet, one of the most extensively observed, and a reference point against which atmospheric retrieval techniques and theoretical models are routinely calibrated. Yet, despite this privileged status, no consensus exists on its molecular composition or thermal structure (Line et al. 2016, Giacobbe et al. 2021, Xue et al. 2024, Verma et al. 2025), limiting our ability to validate chemical models, constrain elemental abundances, and extrapolate trends to broader exoplanet populations. We present new high-resolution dayside emission spectroscopy of HD209458b using the simultaneous H- and K-band coverage of IGRINS2, yielding the most chemically resolved ground-based view of this planet to date. Our analysis reveals strong and unambiguous detections of H2O and CO, along with the first evidence for H2S, uniquely enabled by targeting the dayside thermal emission and isolating narrow-band molecular features that remain challenging for broadband space-based observations alone. I will also present results from a comprehensive Bayesian investigation of the planet's atmospheric metallicity and temperature─pressure structure. Ongoing joint analyses combining IGRINS2 with archival WFC3/HST emission spectra aim to definitively resolve the composition and thermal structure of HD 209458 b, enabling us to recalibrate our understanding of this keystone hot Jupiter. This is a much-needed stride as we approach the frontier of sub-Neptune and Earth-analogue characterization. Prior to that, it is worth discussing: "Do we have a concrete understanding of benchmark Hot Jupiters, often regarded as having relatively less complex atmospheres, such as HD209458b?"

[4] 你好，老朋友：利用IGRINS2高分辨率观测分析HD 209458b白昼面热发射

在热木星群体中，HD209458b仍然是最具代表性的基准：它是首个凌星系外行星，也是观测最为充分的行星之一，并且是大气检索技术和理论模型常用的校准参考点。然而，尽管具有这一特殊地位，其分子组成或热结构至今尚无共识（Line et al. 2016, Giacobbe et al. 2021, Xue et al. 2024, Verma et al. 2025），这限制了我们验证化学模型、约束元素丰度并将趋势外推到更广泛系外行星群体的能力。我们利用IGRINS2的H和K波段同时覆盖，展示了HD209458b新的高分辨率日面发射光谱，获得了迄今为止地面观测下该行星化学分辨率最高的视图。我们的分析清晰且明确地探测到了H2O和CO，并首次发现了H2S，这得益于针对日面热发射并分离出窄带分子特征，而这些特征在仅依赖宽带空间观测时仍具有挑战性。我还将展示对该行星大气金属丰度和温度-压力结构的全面贝叶斯分析结果。正在进行的联合分析将IGRINS2与WFC3/HST的历史发射光谱结合，旨在最终解决HD 209458 b的组成和热结构，从而重新校准我们对这一关键热木星的理解。这对于我们迈向亚海王星和类地行星表征前沿至关重要。在此之前，值得讨论的是：“我们是否对被认为大气相对不复杂的基准热木星（如HD209458b）有了具体的理解？”

[5] Osiris revisited: Confirming a solar metallicity and low C/O in HD 209458 b

2025-08-00

HD 209458 b is the prototypical hot Jupiter and one of the best targets available for precise atmosphere characterisation. Now that spectra from both the Hubble Space Telescope (HST) and the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) are available, we can reveal the atmospheric properties in unprecedented detail. For this study we performed a new data reduction and analysis of the original HST/WFC3 spectrum, accounting for the wavelength dependence of the instrument systematics that was not considered in previous analyses. This allowed us to precisely and robustly measure the much-debated H<SUB>2</SUB>O abundance in HD 209458 b's atmosphere. We combined the newly reduced spectrum with archival JWST/NIRCam data and ran free chemistry atmospheric retrievals over the 1.0 − 5.1 μm wavelength range, covering possible features of multiple absorbing species, including CO<SUB>2</SUB>, CO, CH<SUB>4</SUB>, NH<SUB>3</SUB>, HCN, Na, SO<SUB>2</SUB>, and H<SUB>2</SUB>S. We detected H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO<SUB>2</SUB> robustly at above 7σ significance, and found a 3.6σ preference for cloudy models compared to a clear atmosphere. For all the other absorbers we tested, only the upper limits of the abundance can be measured. We used Bayesian model averaging to account for a range of different assumptions about the cloud properties, resulting in a water volume mixing ratio of 0.95<SUB>−0.17</SUB><SUP>+0.35</SUP> × solar and a carbon dioxide abundance of 0.94<SUB>−0.09</SUB><SUP>+0.16</SUP> × solar. Both results are consistent with solar values and are comparable to predictions from the VULCAN 1D photochemistry model. Combining these values with a prior on the CO abundance from ground-based measurements, we derive an overall atmospheric composition comparable to solar metallicity of [M/H] = 0.10<SUB>−0.40</SUB><SUP>+0.41</SUP> and very low C/O of 0.054<SUB>−0.034</SUB><SUP>+0.080</SUP> with a 3 σ upper limit of 0.454. This indicates a strong enrichment in oxygen and depletion in carbon during HD 209458 b's formation.

[5] 重访Osiris：确认HD 209458 b的太阳金属丰度和低C/O

HD 209458 b是热木星的原型，也是目前最适合精确大气表征的目标之一。随着哈勃空间望远镜（HST）和詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）光谱的获取，我们能够以前所未有的细节揭示其大气性质。本研究对原始HST/WFC3光谱进行了新的数据处理和分析，考虑了此前分析未涉及的仪器系统误差的波长依赖性。这使我们能够精确且稳健地测量HD 209458 b大气中备受争议的H2O丰度。我们将新处理的光谱与归档的JWST/NIRCam数据结合，在1.0−5.1 μm波段范围内进行了自由化学大气检索，涵盖了包括CO2、CO、CH4、NH3、HCN、Na、SO2和H2S等多种吸收组分的可能特征。我们以超过7σ的显著性稳健探测到H2O和CO2，并发现与无云大气相比，云模型有3.6σ的偏好。对于其他吸收体，仅能测得丰度的上限。我们采用贝叶斯模型平均法，考虑了不同云属性假设，得到水体积混合比为0.95_{−0.17}^{+0.35} × solar，二氧化碳丰度为0.94_{−0.09}^{+0.16} × solar。两者均与太阳值一致，并与VULCAN 1D光化学模型预测相当。结合地面观测对CO丰度的先验，我们推导出整体大气组成与太阳金属丰度[M/H]=0.10_{−0.40}^{+0.41}相当，C/O极低，为0.054_{−0.034}^{+0.080}，3σ上限为0.454。这表明HD 209458 b在形成过程中氧显著富集、碳显著亏损。

[6] The strange case of Na I in the atmosphere of HD 209458 b. Reconciling low- and high-resolution spectroscopic observations

2022-01-00

<BR /> Aims: We aim to investigate the origin of the discrepant results reported in the literature about the presence of Na I in the atmosphere of HD 209458 b, based on low- and high-resolution transmission spectroscopy. <BR /> Methods: We generated synthetic planetary atmosphere models and we compared them with the transmission light curves and spectra observed in previous studies. Our models account for the stellar limb-darkening and Rossiter-McLaughlin (RM) effects, and contemplate various possible scenarios for the planetary atmosphere. <BR /> Results: We reconciled the discrepant results by identifying a range of planetary atmospheres that are consistent with previous low- and high-resolution spectroscopic observations. Either both datasets are interpreted as consistent with a total absence of Na I in the planetary atmosphere (with Hubble Space Telescope data being affected by limb darkening), or the terminator temperature of HD 209458 b has to have an upper limit of about 1000 K. In particular, we find that 1D transmission spectra with lower-than-equilibrium temperatures can also explain the previously reported detection of absorption signal at low resolution due to differential transit depth in adjacent bands, while the cores of the Na I D lines may be masked by the strong RM signal seen at high resolution. We also rule out high-altitude clouds, which would otherwise mask the absorption signal at low resolution, as the source of the discrepancies. <BR /> Conclusions: This work highlights the synergies between different observing techniques, specifically low- and high-resolution spectroscopy, to fully characterise transiting exoplanet systems.

[6] HD 209458 b 大气中 Na I 的奇异案例。协调低分辨率与高分辨率光谱观测

目标：我们旨在基于低分辨率和高分辨率透射光谱，研究文献中关于HD 209458 b大气中Na I存在性的不同结果的起因。方法：我们生成了合成的行星大气模型，并将其与先前研究中观测到的透射光变和光谱进行比较。我们的模型考虑了恒星边缘变暗和Rossiter-McLaughlin（RM）效应，并涵盖了行星大气的多种可能情景。结果：我们通过识别一系列与先前低分辨率和高分辨率光谱观测一致的行星大气，调和了这些不同的结果。两组数据都可以解释为行星大气中完全没有Na I（Hubble Space Telescope数据受边缘变暗影响），或者HD 209458 b的晨昏线温度必须有约1000 K的上限。特别地，我们发现，具有低于平衡温度的1D透射光谱也可以解释先前在低分辨率下报告的吸收信号探测，这是由于相邻波段凌日深度的差异，而Na I D线的线芯可能被高分辨率下强烈的RM信号所掩盖。我们还排除了高空云层作为导致低分辨率下吸收信号被掩盖的原因。结论：本工作强调了不同观测技术，特别是低分辨率和高分辨率光谱之间的协同作用，以全面刻画凌日系外行星系统。

[7] A Detailed Investigation of HD 209458 b HST and JWST Transmission Spectra with SANSAR

2025-08-00

HD 209458 b is the first exoplanet on which an atmosphere was detected. Since then, its atmosphere has been investigated using multiple telescopes and instruments. However, many of its atmospheric constraints remain debatable. While Hubble Space Telescope (HST) observations suggested a highly subsolar metallicity, recent JWST NIRCam observations by Q. Xue et al. constrained a supersolar metallicity with highly subsolar C/O. In this work, we show a detailed investigation of HD 209458 b transmission spectra observations from JWST and HST using SANSAR, a newly developed planetary atmosphere modeling framework, with free, equilibrium chemistry and self-consistent grid retrievals. The overall best-fitting model with free retrievals (<inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>χ</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>red</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula> = 1.21) constrains its metallicity and C/O to be highly subsolar, while equilibrium chemistry and grid retrievals (<inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>χ</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>red</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula> = 1.27 and 1.30, respectively) are consistent with solar values using Space Telescope Imaging Spectrograph (STIS)+WFC3+NIRCam observations. The retrieved abundances of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO<SUB>2</SUB> are almost 3 orders of magnitude lower (highly subsolar) with STIS+WFC3+NIRCam compared to just NIRCam, using free retrievals. NIRCam observations alone also result in misleading constraints on metallicity and C/O, with equilibrium chemistry and grid retrieval. We find that the model choice of varying C/H or O/H to vary the C/O in equilibrium chemistry retrievals leads to different metallicity constraints with NIRCam, but similar constraints with STIS+WFC3+NIRCam. We conclude that NIRCam observations alone can lead to overestimation of abundances for exoplanet atmospheres and, therefore, should be used in combination with UV/optical and near-infrared observations to obtain robust constraints on abundances, C/O, and metallicity. In particular, even though we can detect the CO<SUB>2</SUB> feature with just NIRCam, we cannot constrain its abundances robustly without the optical baseline.

[7] 利用SANSAR对HD 209458 b的HST和JWST透射光谱的详细研究

HD 209458 b 是首个被探测到大气的系外行星。自那以后，其大气已通过多台望远镜和仪器进行了研究。然而，其许多大气约束仍存在争议。尽管哈勃空间望远镜（HST）观测表明其金属丰度远低于太阳，但最近 Q. Xue 等人利用 JWST NIRCam 观测约束其金属丰度高于太阳且 C/O 极低。在本工作中，我们利用新开发的行星大气建模框架 SANSAR，对 JWST 和 HST 的 HD 209458 b 透射光谱观测进行了详细分析，采用自由、平衡化学和自洽网格检索。整体上，自由检索的最佳拟合模型（χ_red^2 = 1.21）约束其金属丰度和 C/O 均远低于太阳，而平衡化学和网格检索（χ_red^2 = 1.27 和 1.30）则与利用 STIS+WFC3+NIRCam 观测得到的太阳值一致。利用自由检索，STIS+WFC3+NIRCam 得到的 H2O 和 CO2 丰度比仅用 NIRCam 时低近3个数量级（极低于太阳）。仅用 NIRCam 观测也会导致金属丰度和 C/O 的误导性约束（平衡化学和网格检索）。我们发现，在平衡化学检索中，通过改变 C/H 或 O/H 来调整 C/O 的模型选择，会导致 NIRCam 下金属丰度约束不同，但在 STIS+WFC3+NIRCam 下约束相似。我们得出结论，仅用 NIRCam 观测可能会高估系外行星大气的丰度，因此应与紫外/光学和近红外观测结合使用，以获得对丰度、C/O 和金属丰度的稳健约束。特别地，尽管仅用 NIRCam 可以探测到 CO2 特征，但没有光学基线无法稳健约束其丰度。

[8] Can We Reconcile Space- and Ground-Based Atmospheric Interpretations of Two Classic Hot Jupiters? A comprehensive reanalysis of HD209458b and HD189733b using IGRINS2 spectrograph

2026-02-00

Hot Jupiters present a major challenge to planet formation theories, and their atmospheric compositions are keys to understanding their origins. Despite extensive observations from both space and ground based facilities, the molecular makeup of keystone hot Jupiters - HD 209458 b and HD 189733 b has long been a topic of intense debate. More importantly, these two planets occupy the moderate hot Jupiter regime (1000 - 1500 K), cooler than the ultra-hot (T &gt; 2000 K) planets where molecules dissociate but hotter than warm Neptunes where condensation dominates, placing them in the critical temperature range where species such as H<SUB>2</SUB>O, CO, CH<SUB>4</SUB>, HCN, and NH<SUB>3</SUB> can coexist—ideal for probing chemical transitions between oxygen-rich and carbon-rich atmospheres. I will present our campaign to revisit these classic exoplanets using the simultaneous high-resolution H- and K-band coverage of IGRINS2. Our observations target the dayside thermal emission mitigating the biases plaguing transit geometry such as limb asymmetry, Rossiter-McLaughlin effects and clouds. I will present the preliminary result of a strong H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO signals in HD209458b and an ongoing analysis of HD189733b with a strong H<SUB>2</SUB>O signal. Today, JWST is remarkable in detecting broadband exoplanet atmospheric features, but recovering crucial narrow band signals (e.g., CO) has proven to be challenging for planets with robust ground-based CO detection (e.g., WASP-18b, HD209458b). Our analysis of HD209458b and HD189733b further emphasizes the reasoning that ground-based observations are complementary to spaced-based facilities and possess unique advantages in providing unprecedented view of exoplanet atmospheres.

[8] 我们能否调和两颗经典热木星的空间与地面大气解释？利用IGRINS2光谱仪对HD209458b和HD189733b的综合再分析

热木星对行星形成理论提出了重大挑战，其大气成分是理解其起源的关键。尽管来自空间和地面设施的观测非常丰富，关键热木星 HD 209458 b 和 HD 189733 b 的分子组成长期以来一直存在激烈争议。更重要的是，这两颗行星处于中等温度热木星区间（1000-1500 K），比分子解离主导的超热行星（T > 2000 K）更冷，但又比冷凝主导的温暖海王星更热，正处于 H2O、CO、CH4、HCN 和 NH3 等物种可以共存的关键温度区间——非常适合研究富氧与富碳大气之间的化学转变。我将介绍我们利用 IGRINS2 同步高分辨率 H- 和 K-波段观测重新研究这些经典系外行星的计划。我们的观测针对日面热发射，减小了凌星几何带来的偏差，如边缘不对称、Rossiter-McLaughlin 效应和云的影响。我将展示 HD209458b 中强 H2O 和 CO 信号的初步结果，以及 HD189733b 的持续分析（同样有强 H2O 信号）。目前，JWST 在探测宽带系外行星大气特征方面表现出色，但对于那些地面已稳健探测到 CO 的行星（如 WASP-18b、HD209458b），恢复关键的窄带信号（如 CO）仍然具有挑战性。我们对 HD209458b 和 HD189733b 的分析进一步强调，地面观测与空间设施互为补充，并在提供前所未有的系外行星大气视角方面具有独特优势。

Does the atmosphere of HAT-P-26b exhibit detectable spectroscopic features in transmission that argue against the presence of thick, high-level clouds, or do secondary eclipse measurements indicate a low atmospheric metallicity with efficient energy circulation and possible cloud coverage affecting CO detection?
HAT-P-26b的大气在透射中是否表现出可探测的光谱特征，从而反对存在厚的高层云，还是二次凌星测量表明其大气金属丰度较低，能量循环高效，并且可能有云层影响CO的探测？
8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] HST SHEL: Revealing Haze and Confirming Elevated Metallicity in the Warm Neptune HAT-P-26b

2025-05-00

We present a new and extended transmission spectrum of the warm Neptune HAT-P-26b spanning wavelengths between 0.29 and 5.0 μm. This spectrum is derived from new HST STIS G430L observations from the PanCET program, a reanalysis of the previously published HST STIS G750L data, along with the previously published HST WFC3 IR G102 and G141 data, and the two Spitzer IRAC photometric points at 3.6 and 4.5 μm. We present this analysis as part of the Sculpting Hubble's Exoplanet Legacy program, where the goals are to analyze all HST archival observations of transiting exoplanets using a uniform and homogeneous reduction technique. With the new wavelength coverage, we identify a scattering slope that is weaker than Rayleigh scattering and is best-matched by models incorporating a haze-only scenario. Our retrieval analysis reveals an atmospheric metallicity of 15<inline-formula> </inline-formula> × solar which suggests that HAT-P-26b may have formed further out in the protoplanetary disk, in a region rich in hydrogen and helium but with fewer heavy elements, and later migrated inward. This supersolar metallicity places HAT-P-26b below the mass─metallicity trend of the solar system. Looking ahead, recent observations from JWST NIRISS/SOSS and NIRSpec/G495H will provide critical, high-precision data that extend the spectral coverage into the infrared to further constrain the atmospheric composition and structure of HAT-P-26b. These observations have the potential to confirm or refine the metallicity and haze scenario presented here, offering unprecedented insights into the atmospheric properties of warm Neptunes and the processes governing their formation and migration histories.

[1] HST SHEL：揭示温暖海王星HAT-P-26b的大气薄雾并确认其高金属丰度

我们展示了温暖海王星HAT-P-26b在0.29至5.0 μm波段的全新扩展透射光谱。该光谱基于PanCET项目中新获得的HST STIS G430L观测、对已发表HST STIS G750L数据的重新分析、已发表的HST WFC3 IR G102和G141数据，以及Spitzer IRAC在3.6和4.5 μm的两个光度点。我们将本分析作为Sculpting Hubble's Exoplanet Legacy项目的一部分，目标是采用统一、同质化的数据处理方法分析所有HST系外行星凌日观测档案。借助新的波长覆盖，我们识别出一个比Rayleigh散射更弱的散射斜率，最符合仅含烟雾的模型。我们的检索分析揭示了15倍太阳的金属丰度，暗示HAT-P-26b可能在原行星盘中较远、富氢氦但重元素较少的区域形成，随后向内迁移。该超太阳金属丰度使HAT-P-26b位于太阳系质量─金属丰度关系之下。展望未来，JWST NIRISS/SOSS和NIRSpec/G495H的最新观测将提供关键的高精度红外数据，进一步约束HAT-P-26b的大气组成和结构。这些观测有望证实或修正本文提出的金属丰度和烟雾情景，为理解温暖海王星的大气性质及其形成和迁移历史提供前所未有的见解。

[2] Detection of CO<SUB>2</SUB>, CO, and H<SUB>2</SUB>O in the atmosphere of the warm sub-Saturn HAT-P-12 b

2025-11-00

Context. The chemical composition of warm gas giant exoplanet atmospheres (with T<SUB>eq</SUB> &lt; 1000 K) is not well known due to the lack of observational constraints. Aims. HAT-P-12 b is a warm, sub-Saturn-mass transiting exoplanet that is ideal for transmission spectroscopy. We aim to characterise its atmosphere and probe the presence of carbonaceous species using near-infrared observations. Methods. One transit of HAT-P-12 b was observed in spectroscopy with JWST NIRSpec in the 2.87─5.10 µm range with a resolving power of ~1000. The JWST data are combined with archival observations from HST WFC3 covering the 1.1─1.7 µm range. The data were analysed using two data reduction pipelines and two atmospheric retrieval tools. Atmospheric simulations using chemical forward models were performed to interpret the spectra. Results. CO<SUB>2</SUB>, CO, and H<SUB>2</SUB>O are detected at 12.2, 4.1, and 6.0 σ confidence, respectively. Their volume mixing ratios are consistent with an atmosphere of ~10× solar metallicity and production of CO<SUB>2</SUB> by photochemistry. CH<SUB>4</SUB> is not detected and seems to be lacking, which could be due to a high intrinsic temperature with strong vertical mixing or other phenomena. SO<SUB>2</SUB> is also not detected and its production seems limited by low upper atmosphere temperatures (~500 K at P ≲ 10<SUP>−3</SUP> bar derived from one-dimensional retrievals), insufficient to produce it in detectable quantities (≳ 800 K required according to photochemical models). H<SUB>2</SUB>S is marginally detected using one data analysis method, but not by the other. Retrievals indicate the presence of clouds between 2 and 11 mbar using one data analysis method, and between 5 and 269 mbar using the other. The derived C/O ratio is below unity, but is not well constrained. Conclusions. This study points towards an atmosphere for HAT-P-12 b that could be enriched in carbon and oxygen with respect to its host star, a possibly cold upper atmosphere that may explain the non-detection of SO<SUB>2</SUB>, and a CH<SUB>4</SUB> depletion that is yet to be fully understood. When including the production of CO<SUB>2</SUB> via photochemistry, an atmospheric metallicity that is close to Saturn's can explain the observations. Metallicities inferred for other gas giant exoplanets based on their CO<SUB>2</SUB> mixing ratios may need to account for its photochemical production pathways. This may impact studies on mass-metallicity trends and links between exoplanet atmospheres, interiors, and formation history.

[2] 在温暖亚土星HAT-P-12 b大气中探测到CO2、CO和H2O

背景：由于观测约束的缺乏，温暖类气态巨行星（T<sub>eq</sub> < 1000 K）大气的化学组成尚不清楚。目标：HAT-P-12 b是一颗温暖、亚土星质量的凌星系外行星，非常适合透射光谱观测。我们旨在利用近红外观测对其大气进行表征，并探测碳基分子的存在。方法：我们利用JWST NIRSpec在2.87─5.10 µm波段（分辨率约1000）对HAT-P-12 b进行了一次凌星光谱观测，并结合了HST WFC3在1.1─1.7 µm波段的历史观测数据。数据通过两套数据处理流程和两种大气检索工具进行分析，并采用化学前向模型进行大气模拟以解释光谱。结果：CO<sub>2</sub>、CO和H<sub>2</sub>O分别以12.2、4.1和6.0 σ的置信度被探测到。它们的体积混合比与约10倍太阳金属丰度的大气以及CO<sub>2</sub>由光化学产生相一致。CH<sub>4</sub>未被探测到且似乎缺乏，这可能是由于较高的本征温度和强烈的垂直混合或其他现象所致。SO<sub>2</sub>也未被探测到，其产生似乎受限于低的高层大气温度（约500 K，P ≲ 10<sup>−3</sup> bar，由一维检索得出），不足以产生可探测量（光化学模型要求≳800 K）。H<sub>2</sub>S在一种数据分析方法下被边缘探测到，但在另一种方法下未被探测到。检索结果显示，云层在2至11 mbar（方法一）或5至269 mbar（方法二）之间。推导的C/O比低于1，但约束不佳。结论：本研究表明，HAT-P-12 b的大气中碳和氧可能相对于母星富集，高层大气可能较冷，这可解释SO<sub>2</sub>未探测到，以及CH<sub>4</sub>的消耗机制尚未完全理解。考虑CO<sub>2</sub>的光化学产生后，类似土星的金属丰度可以解释观测结果。基于CO<sub>2</sub>混合比推断的其他气态巨行星金属丰度可能需要考虑其光化学产生路径，这可能影响关于质量-金属丰度趋势及系外行星大气、内部和形成历史之间联系的研究。

[3] HST SHEL: Revealing Haze and Confirming Elevated Metallicity in the Warm Neptune HAT-P-26b

2025-01-00

We present a new and extended transmission spectrum of the warm Neptune HAT-P-26b spanning wavelengths between 0.29 - 5.0μm. To obtain this spectrum we use new HST STIS G430L observations acquired as part of the PanCET program, a reanalysis of the previously published HST STIS G750L observations, along with the previously published HST WFC3 IR G102 and G141 results, and the two Spitzer IRAC photometric points at 3.6 and 4.5μm. We present this analysis as part of the Sculpting Hubble's Exoplanet Legacy (SHEL) program, where the goals are to analyze all HST archival observations of transiting exoplanets using a uniform and homogeneous reduction technique. With the new wavelength coverage, we identify a scattering slope that is weaker than Rayleigh scattering and is best-matched by models incorporating a haze-only scenario. Our retrieval analysis reveals an atmospheric metallicity of 33.88<SUP>+23.66</SUP><SUB>-11.50</SUB> × solar which suggests that HAT-P-26b may have formed further out in the protoplanetary disk, in a region rich in hydrogen and helium but with fewer heavy elements, and later migrated inward. HAT-P-26b has been observed as part of the JWST Cycle 1 GTO program and this analysis will be useful and complementary when interpreting the optical wavelength range during the modelling part of the JWST observations.

[3] HST SHEL：揭示温暖海王星HAT-P-26b的大气薄雾并确认其高金属丰度

我们展示了温暖海王星HAT-P-26b在0.29-5.0μm波段的新扩展透射光谱。为获得该光谱，我们利用了PanCET项目中新获得的HST STIS G430L观测数据、对先前发表的HST STIS G750L观测的重新分析、已发表的HST WFC3 IR G102和G141结果，以及Spitzer IRAC在3.6和4.5μm的两个光度点。我们将本分析作为Sculpting Hubble's Exoplanet Legacy（SHEL）项目的一部分，目标是采用统一、同质的处理方法分析所有HST档案中的凌星系外行星观测数据。借助新的波长覆盖，我们识别出一个比瑞利散射更弱的散射斜率，最符合仅含薄雾的模型。我们的检索分析显示其大气金属丰度为33.88<sup>+23.66</sup><sub>-11.50</sub>倍太阳值，暗示HAT-P-26b可能在富含氢和氦、重元素较少的原行星盘外部形成，随后向内迁移。HAT-P-26b已被纳入JWST第一周期GTO项目观测，本分析将在JWST观测建模阶段对光学波段的解释具有重要补充作用。

[4] Detection of Na in the atmosphere of the hot Jupiter HAT-P-55b

2024-07-00

The spectral signatures of optical absorbers, when combined with those of infrared molecules, play a critical role in constraining the cloud properties of exoplanet atmospheres. We aim to use optical transmission spectroscopy to confirm the tentative color signature previously observed by multiband photometry in the atmosphere of hot Jupiter HAT-P-55b. We observed a transit of the HAT-P-55b with the OSIRIS spectrograph on the Gran Telescopio Canarias (GTC). We created two sets of spectroscopic light curves, using the conventional band-integrated method and the newly proposed pixel-based method, to derive the transmission spectrum. We performed Bayesian spectral retrieval analyses on the transmission spectrum to interpret the observed atmospheric properties. The transmission spectra derived from the two methods are consistent, both spectrally resolving the tentative color signature observed by MuSCAT2. The retrievals on the combined OSIRIS and MuSCAT2 transmission spectrum yield a detection of Na at 5.5σ and a tentative detection of MgH at 3.4σ. The current optical-only wavelength coverage cannot constrain the absolute abundances of the atmospheric species. Space-based observations covering the molecular infrared bands or ground-based high-resolution spectroscopy are needed to further constrain the atmospheric properties of HAT-P-55b.

[4] 在热木星HAT-P-55b大气中探测到Na

光学吸收体的光谱特征与红外分子的特征结合时，在约束系外行星大气云层性质方面起着关键作用。我们旨在利用光学透射光谱确认此前多波段光度法在热木星HAT-P-55b大气中观测到的初步颜色特征。我们使用加那利大望远镜（GTC）的OSIRIS光谱仪观测了HAT-P-55b的一次凌日。我们采用传统的波段积分法和新提出的基于像素的方法，制作了两组光谱光变曲线以获得透射光谱。我们对透射光谱进行了贝叶斯光谱检索分析，以解释观测到的大气性质。两种方法得到的透射光谱一致，均在光谱上分辨出MuSCAT2观测到的初步颜色特征。对OSIRIS和MuSCAT2联合透射光谱的检索结果显示，Na在5.5σ水平被探测到，MgH在3.4σ水平有初步探测。目前仅有的光学波段覆盖无法约束大气组分的绝对丰度。需要覆盖分子红外波段的空间观测或地面高分辨率光谱观测，以进一步约束HAT-P-55b的大气性质。

[5] ACCESS: Tentative detection of H2O in the ground-based optical transmission spectrum of the low density hot-Saturn HATS-5b

2022-06-00

One of the most important questions to answer in the field of exoplanet atmospheres is to know which parameters shape their cloudiness levels. These not only define the general feedback mechanisms, but also their observational prospects: cloudy worlds typically have muted atmospheric features in transmission, making them observationally challenging to study. Recent works have predicted a window of "clearer" atmospheres for exoplanets at an equilibrium temperature around 1000 K, which is in between the regimes where silicate clouds and hydrocarbon hazes are believed to dominate. With an estimated T~1025 K, HATS-5b is in this "clear" regime, which also sits at the interesting dividing temperature between "warm" and "hot" giant planets. In this contribution, we present a new, precise ground-based optical transmission spectrum of the hot-Saturn HATS-5b, obtained as part of the ACCESS survey with the IMACS multi-object spectrograph mounted on the Magellan/Baade Telescope. Our spectra cover the 0.5 to 0.9 micron region, and are the product of 5 individual transits observed between 2014 and 2018. We introduce, for the first time in our collaboration, the usage of additional second order light in our analyses. This allows us to extract an "extra" transit lightcurve from our data, improving the overall precision of our combined transit spectrum. In addition, we also highlight how optimal spectral extraction can be much more effective than simple extraction, even in the case of high signal-to-noise ratios, as they can naturally compensate for bad pixels and cosmic rays. We find that the favored atmospheric model for this transmission spectrum is a solar-metallicity atmosphere with C/O = 0.39 ± 0.18, whose features are dominated by H2O, and so we report a tentative detection of H2O in the atmosphere of HATS-5b. If confirmed, this does indeed point to a "clearer" atmosphere at the pressure levels probed by transmission spectroscopy. This is an excellent target for Hubble Space Telescope follow-up, as it can help confirm this observed feature, and if it is confirmed, allow for a determination of the absolute water abundance on HATS-5b.

[5] ACCESS：在地面光学透射光谱中对低密度热土星HATS-5b的H2O初步探测

系外行星大气领域最重要的问题之一是了解哪些参数决定了其云量水平。这不仅决定了大气反馈机制，也影响其观测前景：多云世界在透射光谱中通常表现为大气特征减弱，增加了观测难度。近期研究预测，在平衡温度约 1000 K 的系外行星存在一个“大气更清澈”的窗口，介于硅酸盐云和碳氢化合物薄雾主导的温度区间之间。HATS-5b 估算的 T~1025 K 位于这一“清澈”区间，同时也是“温暖”与“热”类巨行星的分界温度。在本项工作中，我们展示了 ACCESS 巡天利用安装在 Magellan/Baade 望远镜上的 IMACS 多目标光谱仪获得的热土星 HATS-5b 的新高精度地基光学透射光谱。我们的光谱覆盖 0.5 到 0.9 微米波段，基于 2014 至 2018 年间 5 次凌星观测。我们首次在合作中引入了二级光的使用，从数据中提取了“额外”的凌星光变曲线，提高了合成凌星光谱的整体精度。此外，我们还强调，即使在高信噪比情况下，最优光谱提取方法也比简单提取更有效，因为它们能自然补偿坏像素和宇宙射线。我们发现，该透射光谱的最佳大气模型为太阳金属丰度、C/O = 0.39 ± 0.18 的大气，其特征由 H2O 主导，因此我们报告在 HATS-5b 大气中初步探测到 H2O。如果得到确认，这确实表明在透射光谱探测的压力层面存在“更清澈”的大气。这是哈勃空间望远镜后续观测的极佳目标，有助于确认该特征，并在确认后测定 HATS-5b 的绝对水丰度。

[6] Characterizing the atmosphere of the warm Neptune HAT-P-11 b with high resolution spectroscopy

2022-06-00

High resolution spectra of transit events enable the search for constituents in exoplanet atmospheres with a variety of methods. Using cross correlation, the observations can be compared to models across a wide wavelength range, taking advantage of the large number of absorption lines of some species. For other species, the excess absorption in strong individual lines is measurable directly by comparing the spectra taken during the transit with those before and after the transit. We analyze transmission spectra of HAT-P-11 b obtained with CARMENES using these two methods to search for a multitude of species in the planetary atmosphere. We remove stellar and telluric lines with SYSREM and find a tentative detection of CH4 using cross correlation. In addition, the influence of clouds on the detection strength is investigated. We confirm the previously detected presence of He by examining the near-infrared helium triplet. Helium is known to be a tracer for extended atmospheres and to be related to stellar activity. The large eccentricity of HAT-P-11 b's orbit helps to disentangle the planetary from the stellar absorption lines. Furthermore, we search for other species that are expected to be present in the atmosphere of warm Neptunes.

[6] 利用高分辨率光谱对温暖海王星HAT-P-11 b大气的特性研究

凌日事件的高分辨率光谱使得可以通过多种方法搜索系外行星大气中的成分。利用交叉相关，可以在宽波长范围内将观测与模型进行比较，利用某些物种大量吸收线的优势。对于其他物种，通过比较凌日过程中与凌日前后获得的光谱，可以直接测量强单线的过量吸收。我们利用 CARMENES 获得的 HAT-P-11 b 透射光谱，采用这两种方法搜索行星大气中的多种物种。我们使用 SYSREM 去除了恒星和大气吸收线，并通过交叉相关初步探测到 CH4。此外，我们还研究了云对探测强度的影响。通过检查近红外氦三重态，我们确认了此前已探测到的 He 的存在。氦已知是膨胀大气的示踪物，并与恒星活动相关。HAT-P-11 b 轨道的高偏心率有助于区分行星与恒星吸收线。此外，我们还搜索了预期存在于温暖海王星大气中的其他物种。

[7] Confirmation of TiO absorption and tentative detection of MgH and CrH in the atmosphere of HAT-P-41b

2024-02-00

Understanding the role of optical absorbers is critical for linking the properties of the dayside and terminator atmospheres of hot Jupiters. This study aims to identify the signatures of optical absorbers in the atmosphere of the hot Jupiter HAT-P-41b. We conducted five transit observations of this planet to obtain its optical transmission spectra using the Gran Telescopio Canarias (GTC). We performed atmospheric retrievals assuming free abundances of 12 chemical species. Our Bayesian model comparisons revealed strong evidence for TiO absorption (∆ ln 𝒵 = 21.02), modest evidence for CrH (∆ ln 𝒵 = 3.73), and weak evidence for MgH (∆ ln 𝒵 = 2.32). When we combined the GTC transmission spectrum with previously published Hubble Space Telescope and Spitzer data, the retrieval results and model inferences remained consistent. In conclusion, HAT-P-41b has a metal-rich atmosphere with no high-altitude clouds or hazes. Further observations of its dayside atmosphere should be made to confirm the hints of a thermal inversion in the upper atmosphere suggested by our results.

[7] 确认HAT-P-41b大气中的TiO吸收，并初步探测到MgH和CrH

理解光学吸收体的作用对于联系热木星白昼面和晨昏线大气的性质至关重要。本研究旨在识别热木星HAT-P-41b大气中光学吸收体的特征。我们对该行星进行了五次凌日观测，利用加那利大型望远镜（GTC）获得其光学透射光谱。我们在检索中假设12种化学物质的丰度为自由参数。我们的贝叶斯模型比较显示，TiO吸收有强烈证据（∆ ln 𝒵 = 21.02），CrH有中等证据（∆ ln 𝒵 = 3.73），MgH有弱证据（∆ ln 𝒵 = 2.32）。当我们将GTC透射光谱与先前发表的哈勃空间望远镜和Spitzer数据结合时，检索结果和模型推断保持一致。总之，HAT-P-41b具有金属丰度较高的大气，且没有高空云或薄雾。应进一步观测其白昼面大气，以确认我们结果所暗示的上层大气温度反转迹象。

[8] Investigating the exoplanetary atmosphere of HAT-P-30b

2024-03-00

Characterizing the atmospheres of exoplanets is essential in order to understand their nature and provide clues about their formation and evolution. To this aim, transiting exoplanets are crucial in atmospheric studies as they allow very precise characterisation of the atmosphere due to the known planetary properties (planetary mass and radius). Transmission spectroscopy is a technique for the detection and characterisation of exoplanetary atmospheres. This method compares spectra taken inside and outside of transits to extract the planetary signal. Low-resolution transmission spectroscopy uses an instrument with low resolving power (R = 50, 000. The latter enables us to resolve the moving planetary motion during the transit. In addition, this technique allows to resolve planetary features from which information like planetary rotation, winds and chemical composition can be derived. This project aimed at using archival data from FORS2 to study the atmosphere of HAT-P-30b, a hot Jupiter with a very small density and large scale height ─ and thus an ideal target for transmission spectroscopy as the expected signal is very high. In the ESO archive there were 1556 FORS2 spectra from two transit nights using the 600B and 600RI grisms. We analyzed the FORS2 data, following the method of sedaghati2017detection and nikolov2018absolute, to obtain the low-resolution transmission spectrum and identify the presence of strong absorbers and/or Rayleigh scattering. The calculated equilibrium temperature of HAT-P-30b puts this planet just between two sub-classes of hot Jupiters with different planetary chemistry and composition. The (non-)detection of certain absorbers in the atmosphere provides strong constraints about temperature and detect eventual temperature inversion layers. This was done using theoretical models from the PetitRADTRANS.

[8] HAT-P-30b系外行星大气的研究

表征系外行星大气对于理解其本质并为其形成和演化提供线索至关重要。为此，凌日系外行星在大气研究中至关重要，因为已知的行星属性（质量和半径）使得大气表征非常精确。透射光谱是一种用于探测和表征系外行星大气的技术。该方法通过比较凌日内外的光谱来提取行星信号。低分辨率透射光谱使用分辨率较低的仪器（R = 50,000），而高分辨率透射光谱则使用分辨率更高的仪器（R = 100,000），后者可以分辨凌日过程中的行星运动。此外，该技术还可以分辨出行星特征，从而获得如行星自转、风和化学成分等信息。本项目旨在利用 FORS2 的归档数据研究 HAT-P-30b 的大气，该行星是一颗密度极低、尺度高度极大的热木星，因此是透射光谱的理想目标，因为预期信号很强。在 ESO 归档中，有两次凌日夜共 1556 条 FORS2 光谱，使用了 600B 和 600RI 光栅。我们按照 sedaghati2017detection 和 nikolov2018absolute 的方法分析了 FORS2 数据，获得了低分辨率透射光谱，并识别出强吸收体和/或瑞利散射的存在。HAT-P-30b 的平衡温度计算结果显示，该行星正好位于两类具有不同化学和组成的热木星亚类之间。大气中某些吸收体的（未）探测为温度及可能存在的温度反转层提供了强约束。我们使用 PetitRADTRANS 的理论模型进行了相关分析。

Under what observational or methodological conditions can these results be reconciled, and which currently untested assumption drives the disagreement?
GJ 3470B 大气的分析 [2020ApJ...894...97N] 和 [2020ApJ...899...29K] 在 10830 问题上得出可能相互矛盾的结论：一项报告称“我们报告在一颗温暖海王星的外逸层中探测到 He I 10830 Å 吸收（等效宽度约 0.012 ± 0.002 Å）”，而另一项报告称“我们估算了恒星参数，并在该行星凌日过程中搜索 He 10830 吸收，结果为未探测到”。在什么观测或方法条件下这些结果可以调和？目前尚未检验的哪个假设驱动了这种分歧？
8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] Escaping Helium and a Highly Muted Spectrum Suggest a Metal-enriched Atmosphere on Sub-Neptune GJ 3090 b from JWST Transit Spectroscopy

2025-05-00

Sub-Neptunes, the most common planet type, remain poorly understood. Their atmospheres are expected to be diverse, but their compositions are challenging to determine, even with JWST. Here, we present the first JWST spectroscopic study of the warm sub-Neptune GJ 3090 b (2.13 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>, T<SUB>eq,A = 0.3</SUB> ∼ 700 K), which orbits an M2V star, making it a favorable target for atmosphere characterization. We observed four transits of GJ 3090 b: two each using JWST NIRISS/SOSS and NIRSpec/G395H, yielding wavelength coverage from 0.6 to 5.2 μm. We detect the signature of the 10833 Å metastable helium triplet at a statistical significance of 5.5σ with an amplitude of 434 ± 79 ppm, marking the first such detection in a sub-Neptune with JWST. This amplitude is significantly smaller than predicted by solar-metallicity forward models, suggesting a metal-enriched atmosphere that decreases the mass-loss rate and attenuates the helium feature amplitude. Moreover, we find that stellar contamination, in the form of the transit light source effect, dominates the NIRISS transmission spectra, with unocculted spot and faculae properties varying across the two visits separated in time by approximately 6 months. Free retrieval analyses on the NIRSpec/G395H spectrum find tentative evidence for highly muted features and a lack of CH<SUB>4</SUB>. These findings are best explained by a high-metallicity atmosphere (&gt;100× solar at 3σ confidence for clouds at ∼μbar pressures) using chemically consistent retrievals and self-consistent model grids. Further observations of GJ 3090 b are needed for tighter constraints on the atmospheric abundances and to gain a deeper understanding of the processes that led to its potential metal enrichment.

[1] JWST凌日光谱揭示GJ 3090 b存在逃逸氦和高度抑制的光谱，暗示富金属大气

亚海王星是最常见的行星类型，但其性质仍然知之甚少。尽管其大气预期具有多样性，但即使利用JWST，其成分也难以确定。在此，我们报告了对温暖亚海王星GJ 3090 b（2.13 R⊕，T_eq,A = 0.3 ∼ 700 K）的首次JWST光谱研究。该行星围绕M2V型恒星运行，是大气表征的理想目标。我们观测了GJ 3090 b的四次凌日：分别使用JWST NIRISS/SOSS和NIRSpec/G395H各两次，波长覆盖0.6至5.2 μm。我们以5.5σ的统计显著性探测到10833 Å亚稳态氦三重态特征，振幅为434 ± 79 ppm，这是JWST首次在亚海王星中探测到该特征。该振幅显著小于太阳金属丰度前向模型的预测，表明其大气富含金属元素，降低了质量损失率并削弱了氦特征振幅。此外，我们发现恒星污染（凌日光源效应）主导了NIRISS透射光谱，且未被遮挡的黑子和亮斑性质在两次相隔约6个月的观测中存在变化。对NIRSpec/G395H光谱的自由检索分析发现特征极为微弱且缺乏CH4的迹象。采用化学一致检索和自洽模型网格，这些结果最能用高金属丰度大气（对于∼μbar压力下的云，3σ置信度下>100×太阳）来解释。需要进一步观测GJ 3090 b，以更严格约束其大气丰度，并深入理解导致其潜在金属富集的过程。

[2] Interpretation of Mysterious Non-Detection He, C and O Components in the Atmosphere of Gj-436b

2022-07-00

Hot exoplnets are heated by intense radiation of the host star result in intensive thermal escape and mass loss of their hydrogen-dominated upper atmospheres. Spectral absorptions of hot exoplanets passing through stellar disk plays in atmospheres investigations the role of Rosetta stone, providing information on these plasmaspheres such as composition, temperature, density, chemical and physical processes that occur at interaction of planetary streams with the stellar wind. Atmospheres of many hot exoplanets consist mostly of hydrogen, and the Ly-a spectral line is valuable for providing evidence of atmospheric escape.However, it is strongly absorbed in interstellar medium and contaminated by geocoronal emission. As it was suggested in [1], the absorption by a metastable helium in the 23S state at 10830 Å offers an alternative way to probe the evaporating exoplanetary atmospheres. It is not affected by the interstellar medium and can be observed by ground telescopes with high resolution. Complex and complicated models are required to interpret the spectral transit features of hot Jupiters correctly. A 3D gas-dynamic model which takes into account the processes of recombination and plasma-photochemistry of plasma components [2, 3] treats hydrogen and helium component like separate fluids was used in this work to reproduce transit observations of atmosphere of warm Neptune GJ-436b. Its interpretations of the transit absorptions in metastable helium 10830 A line and far UV lines of OI and CII allows revealing the reasons of mysterious non-detection of these components [4,5], while extremely deep and long transit was measured in Ly-a [6]. This work was supported by projects of the Russian Science Foundation 18-12-00080, projects of the Ministry of Science and Higher Education of the Russian Federation No. AAAA-A17-117021750017-0, 075-15-2020-780, 121033100062-5, as well as RFBR grant 20-02-00520. [2] 对Gj-436b大气中He、C和O组分神秘未探测现象的解释

热系外行星受到母星强烈辐射加热，导致其以氢为主的高层大气发生强烈热逃逸和质量损失。热系外行星凌越恒星盘时的光谱吸收在大气研究中起到“罗塞塔石碑”作用，提供了等离子体层的成分、温度、密度及行星流与恒星风相互作用下的化学和物理过程等信息。许多热系外行星的大气主要由氢组成，Ly-α谱线对于揭示大气逃逸具有重要价值。然而，Ly-α在星际介质中被强烈吸收，并受到地球日冕发射的污染。正如文献[1]所建议，23S亚稳态氦在10830 Å的吸收为探测蒸发中的系外行星大气提供了替代途径。该谱线不受星际介质影响，可通过地面高分辨率望远镜观测。正确解释热木星凌越的光谱特征需要复杂的模型。本工作采用了考虑等离子体组分复合重组和等离子体光化学过程的三维气体动力学模型[2,3]，将氢和氦组分视为独立流体，用于再现温暖海王星GJ-436b大气的凌越观测。对10830 Å亚稳态氦线和远紫外OI、CII线的凌越吸收解释，揭示了这些组分神秘未探测到的原因[4,5]，而Ly-α却测得极深且持续时间极长的凌越[6]。本工作得到俄罗斯科学基金会18-12-00080、俄罗斯联邦科学与高等教育部项目AAAA-A17-117021750017-0、075-15-2020-780、121033100062-5及RFBR 20-02-00520资助。参考文献：[1] Oklopčić A., Hirata C. [3] Constraints on atmospheric water abundance and cloud deck pressure in the warm Neptune GJ 3470 b via CARMENES transmission spectroscopy

2024-05-00

Observations of cooler atmospheres of super-Earths and Neptune sized objects often show flat transmission spectra. The most likely cause of this trend is the presence of aerosols (i.e. clouds and hazes) in the atmospheres of such objects. High-resolution spectroscopy provides an opportunity to test this hypothesis by targeting molecular species whose spectral line cores extend above the level of such opaque decks. In this work, we analyse high-resolution infrared observations of the warm Neptune GJ 3470 b taken over two transits using CARMENES (R ~ 80 000) and look for signatures of H<SUB>2</SUB>O (previously detected using Hubble Space Telescope (HST) WFC3 + Spitzer observations) in these transits with a custom pipeline fully accounting for the effects of data cleaning on any potential exoplanet signal. We find that our data are potentially able to weakly detect (~3σ) an injected signal equivalent to the best-fitting model from previous HST WFC3 + Spitzer observations. However, we do not make a significant detection using the actual observations. Using a Bayesian framework to simultaneously constrain the H<SUB>2</SUB>O volume mixing ratio (VMR) and the cloud top pressure level, we select a family of models compatible with the non-detection. These are either very high VMR cloud-free models, solar-abundance models with a high cloud deck, or sub-solar abundance models with a moderate cloud deck. This is a broader range compared to published results from low-resolution spectroscopy, but is also compatible with them at a 1σ level.

[3] 通过CARMENES透射光谱对温暖海王星GJ 3470 b大气水丰度和云层压力的约束

对超地球和海王星大小天体较冷大气的观测通常显示平坦的透射光谱。这一趋势最可能的原因是这些天体大气中存在气溶胶（即云和薄雾）。高分辨率光谱学为检验这一假设提供了机会，可以针对那些谱线中心高于不透明云层的分子种类进行观测。在本工作中，我们分析了使用 CARMENES（R ~ 80 000）在两个凌日过程中获得的温暖海王星 GJ 3470 b 的高分辨率红外观测数据，并利用自定义流程，充分考虑数据清洗对潜在系外行星信号的影响，寻找 H2O（此前通过 Hubble Space Telescope WFC3 + Spitzer 观测已探测到）在这些凌日中的信号。我们发现，数据有可能弱探测（约 3σ）到等效于此前 HST WFC3 + Spitzer 观测最佳拟合模型的注入信号。然而，实际观测中并未获得显著探测。利用贝叶斯框架同时约束 H2O 体积混合比（VMR）和云顶压力，我们选出了一组与未探测到结果相符的模型。这些模型包括极高 VMR 的无云模型、具有高云层的太阳丰度模型，或具有中等云层的亚太阳丰度模型。这一范围比已发表的低分辨率光谱结果更宽，但在 1σ 水平上也与其相符。

[4] The strange case of Na I in the atmosphere of HD 209458 b. Reconciling low- and high-resolution spectroscopic observations

2022-01-00

<BR /> Aims: We aim to investigate the origin of the discrepant results reported in the literature about the presence of Na I in the atmosphere of HD 209458 b, based on low- and high-resolution transmission spectroscopy. <BR /> Methods: We generated synthetic planetary atmosphere models and we compared them with the transmission light curves and spectra observed in previous studies. Our models account for the stellar limb-darkening and Rossiter-McLaughlin (RM) effects, and contemplate various possible scenarios for the planetary atmosphere. <BR /> Results: We reconciled the discrepant results by identifying a range of planetary atmospheres that are consistent with previous low- and high-resolution spectroscopic observations. Either both datasets are interpreted as consistent with a total absence of Na I in the planetary atmosphere (with Hubble Space Telescope data being affected by limb darkening), or the terminator temperature of HD 209458 b has to have an upper limit of about 1000 K. In particular, we find that 1D transmission spectra with lower-than-equilibrium temperatures can also explain the previously reported detection of absorption signal at low resolution due to differential transit depth in adjacent bands, while the cores of the Na I D lines may be masked by the strong RM signal seen at high resolution. We also rule out high-altitude clouds, which would otherwise mask the absorption signal at low resolution, as the source of the discrepancies. <BR /> Conclusions: This work highlights the synergies between different observing techniques, specifically low- and high-resolution spectroscopy, to fully characterise transiting exoplanet systems.

[4] HD 209458 b 大气中 Na I 的奇异案例。协调低分辨率与高分辨率光谱观测

目标：我们旨在基于低分辨率和高分辨率透射光谱，研究文献中关于HD 209458 b大气中Na I存在性的不同结果的起因。方法：我们生成了合成的行星大气模型，并将其与先前研究中观测到的透射光变和光谱进行比较。我们的模型考虑了恒星边缘变暗和Rossiter-McLaughlin（RM）效应，并涵盖了行星大气的多种可能情景。结果：我们通过识别一系列与先前低分辨率和高分辨率光谱观测一致的行星大气，调和了这些不同的结果。两组数据都可以解释为行星大气中完全没有Na I（Hubble Space Telescope数据受边缘变暗影响），或者HD 209458 b的晨昏线温度必须有约1000 K的上限。特别地，我们发现，具有低于平衡温度的1D透射光谱也可以解释先前在低分辨率下报告的吸收信号探测，这是由于相邻波段凌日深度的差异，而Na I D线的线芯可能被高分辨率下强烈的RM信号所掩盖。我们还排除了高空云层作为导致低分辨率下吸收信号被掩盖的原因。结论：本工作强调了不同观测技术，特别是低分辨率和高分辨率光谱之间的协同作用，以全面刻画凌日系外行星系统。

[5] A tentative detection of He I in the atmosphere of GJ 1214 b

2022-03-00

The He I λ10833 Å triplet is a powerful tool for characterising the upper atmosphere of exoplanets and tracing possible mass loss. Here, we analysed one transit of GJ 1214 b observed with the CARMENES high-resolution spectrograph to study its atmosphere via transmission spectroscopy around the He I triplet. Although previous studies using lower resolution instruments have reported non-detections of He I in the atmosphere of GJ 1214 b, we report here the first potential detection. We reconcile the conflicting results arguing that previous transit observations did not present good opportunities for the detection of He I, due to telluric H<SUB>2</SUB>O absorption and OH emission contamination. We simulated those earlier observations, and show evidence that the planetary signal was contaminated. From our single non-telluric-contaminated transit, we determined an excess absorption of 2.10<SUB>‒0.50</SUB><SUP>+0.45</SUP>% (4.6 σ) with a full width at half maximum (FWHM) of 1.30<SUB>‒0.25</SUB><SUP>+0.30</SUP> Å. The detection of He I is statistically significant at the 4.6 σ level, but repeatability of the detection could not be confirmed due to the availability of only one transit. By applying a hydrodynamical model and assuming an H/He composition of 98/2, we found that GJ 1214 b would undergo hydrodynamic escape in the photon-limited regime, losing its primary atmosphere with a mass-loss rate of (1.5-18) × 10<SUP>10</SUP> g s<SUP>‒1</SUP> and an outflow temperature in the range of 2900-4400 K. Further high-resolution follow-up observations of GJ 1214 b are needed to confirm and fully characterise the detection of an extended atmosphere surrounding GJ 1214 b. If confirmed, this would be strong evidence that this planet has a primordial atmosphere accreted from the original planetary nebula. Despite previous intensive observations from space- and ground-based observatories, our He I excess absorption is the first tentative detection of a chemical species in the atmosphere of this benchmark sub-Neptune planet.

[5] 在GJ 1214 b大气中氦I的初步探测

He I λ10833 Å三重态是表征系外行星上层大气和追踪可能质量损失的有力工具。本文我们分析了用CARMENES高分辨率光谱仪观测的GJ 1214 b一次凌日，以透射光谱研究其大气中He I三重态。尽管此前使用低分辨率仪器的研究报告未探测到GJ 1214 b大气中的He I，我们在此报告了首次可能的探测。我们认为，先前凌日观测未能提供良好的He I探测机会，原因在于受到地球大气H2O吸收和OH发射的污染。我们模拟了这些早期观测，并证明行星信号受到了污染。在我们唯一一次未受地球大气污染的凌日中，我们测得2.10-0.50+0.45%（4.6σ）的过量吸收，半高宽为1.30-0.25+0.30 Å。He I的探测在统计上达到4.6σ的显著性，但由于仅有一次凌日，无法确认探测的可重复性。通过应用流体动力学模型并假设H/He组成为98/2，我们发现GJ 1214 b将在光子限制机制下经历流体动力学逃逸，以(1.5-18) × 10^10 g s^-1的质量损失率和2900-4400 K的外流温度损失其原始大气。需要进一步的高分辨率后续观测以确认并全面刻画GJ 1214 b周围的扩展大气。如果得到确认，这将有力证明该行星拥有从原始行星星云吸积而来的原始大气。尽管此前有大量空间和地面观测，我们的He I过量吸收是该基准亚海王星行星大气中首次可能探测到的化学物种。

[6] Tighter constraints on the atmosphere of GJ 436 b from combined high-resolution CARMENES and CRIRES<SUP>+</SUP> observations

2026-01-00

Context. Transmission spectra of Neptune-sized exoplanets are frequently observed to be featureless at low-to-mid resolutions from space; whereas high-altitude clouds can mute spectral features, high atmospheric metallicities can also result in compressed envelopes, where low scale heights may also yield undetectable signatures. Aims. We aim to study the atmospheric properties of the warm Neptune GJ 436 b by combining a set of five transit events observed with the CARMENES spectrograph with one transit from CRIRES<SUP>+</SUP> so as to provide the most constrained results possible at high resolution. Methods. We removed telluric and stellar signals from the data using SysRem and potential planetary signals were investigated using the cross-correlation technique. Following standard procedures for undetected species, we performed injection recovery tests and Bayesian retrievals to place constraints on the detectability of the main near-infrared absorbers. In addition, we simulated ELT/ANDES observations by computing end-to-end in silico datasets with EXoPLORE. Results. No molecular signals were detected in the atmosphere of GJ 436 b, which is consistent with previous studies. Combined CARMENES-CRIRES<SUP>+</SUP> injection-recovery and Bayesian retrieval analyses show that the atmosphere is likely covered by high-altitude clouds (~1 mbar) at low and intermediate metallicities or, alternatively, is very metal-rich (≳ 900× solar), which would suppress spectral features without invoking clouds. Simulations of ELT/ANDES observations suggest a boost by nearly an order of magnitude to the upper limit in the photon-limited regime, reaching 0.1 mbar at 10-300× solar metallicities. Conclusions. The joint analysis of all useful transit observations from CARMENES and CRIRES<SUP>+</SUP> provides the most stringent constraints to date on the atmospheric properties of GJ 436 b. Complementary CCF-based and retrieval approaches consistently indicate that the atmosphere is either cloudy or highly metal enriched. Any weak near-infrared absorption lines, if present, are likely to be below current detection limits. However, according to our simulations, these features may be revealed with ELT/ANDES even in single-transit observations.

[6] 结合高分辨率CARMENES和CRIRES+观测对GJ 436 b大气的更严格约束

背景：海王星大小系外行星的透射光谱在空间低至中分辨率下常被观测到无特征；高空云层可削弱光谱特征，高金属丰度也可导致包层压缩，低尺度高度同样可能使特征无法探测。目标：我们旨在通过结合CARMENES光谱仪观测的五次凌日和一次CRIRES<SUP>+</SUP>凌日，对温暖海王星GJ 436 b的大气性质进行高分辨率下最严格的约束。方法：我们利用SysRem去除大气和恒星信号，并采用互相关技术探测潜在的行星信号。对于未探测到的分子，按照标准流程进行注入-恢复测试和贝叶斯检索，以约束主要近红外吸收体的可探测性。此外，我们通过EXoPLORE模拟ELT/ANDES观测，生成端到端的虚拟数据集。结果：未在GJ 436 b大气中探测到分子信号，这与先前研究一致。CARMENES-CRIRES<SUP>+</SUP>联合注入-恢复和贝叶斯检索分析显示，大气很可能被高空云层（~1 mbar）覆盖，金属丰度为低至中等，或极高金属丰度（≳ 900倍太阳），后者无需假设云层即可抑制光谱特征。ELT/ANDES观测模拟显示，在光子极限下，上限可提升近一个数量级，在10-300倍太阳金属丰度下达到0.1 mbar。结论：CARMENES和CRIRES<SUP>+</SUP>所有有效凌日观测的联合分析为GJ 436 b大气性质提供了迄今最严格的约束。基于CCF和检索的互补方法一致表明大气要么多云，要么极高金属丰度。任何微弱的近红外吸收线（如存在）很可能低于当前探测极限。然而，根据我们的模拟，即使单次凌日，ELT/ANDES也有望揭示这些特征。

[7] Non-detection of He I in the Atmosphere of GJ 1214b with Keck/NIRSPEC, at a Time of Minimal Telluric Contamination

2022-11-00

Observations of helium in exoplanet atmospheres may reveal the presence of large gaseous envelopes and indicate ongoing atmospheric escape. Orell-Miquel et al. (2022) used CARMENES to measure a tentative detection of helium for the sub-Neptune GJ 1214b, with a peak excess absorption reaching over 2% in-transit depth at 10830 Å. However, several non-detections of helium had previously been reported for GJ 1214b. One explanation for the discrepancy was contamination of the planetary signal by overlapping telluric absorption and emission lines. We used Keck/NIRSPEC to observe another transit of GJ 1214b at 10830 Å at a time of minimal contamination by telluric lines, and did not observe planetary helium absorption. Accounting for correlated noise in our measurement, we place an upper limit on the excess absorption size of 1.22% (95% confidence). We find that the discrepancy between the CARMENES and NIRSPEC observations is unlikely to be caused by using different instruments or stellar activity. It is currently unclear whether the difference is due to correlated noise in the observations, or variability in the planetary atmosphere.

[7] 利用Keck/NIRSPEC在大气本底最小地球大气污染时未探测到GJ 1214b大气中的He I

系外行星大气中氦的观测可能揭示大型气体包层的存在，并指示正在进行的大气逃逸。Orell-Miquel 等（2022）利用 CARMENES 对亚海王星 GJ 1214b 进行了氦的初步探测，发现 10830 Å 处凌星期间的最大额外吸收深度超过 2%。然而，此前已有多项关于 GJ 1214b 氦未探测到的报告。对此差异的一种解释是行星信号被重叠的地球大气吸收和发射线污染。我们使用 Keck/NIRSPEC 在地球大气线污染最小的时段观测了 GJ 1214b 的另一次凌星，在 10830 Å 处未观测到行星氦吸收。考虑到测量中的相关噪声，我们对额外吸收深度给出了 1.22%（95% 置信度）的上限。我们发现 CARMENES 和 NIRSPEC 观测之间的差异不太可能由仪器差异或恒星活动引起。目前尚不清楚该差异是由于观测中的相关噪声，还是行星大气的变化所致。

[8] The GAPS Programme at TNG. XXXII. The revealing non-detection of metastable He I in the atmosphere of the hot Jupiter WASP-80b

2022-02-00

Context. Because of its proximity to an active K-type star, the hot Jupiter WASP-80b has been identified as a possible excellent target for detecting and measuring He I absorption in the upper atmosphere. <BR /> Aims: Our aim was to look for, and eventually measure and model, metastable He I atmospheric absorption. <BR /> Methods: We observed four primary transits of WASP-80b in the optical and near-infrared using the HARPS-N and GIANO-B high-resolution spectrographs attached to the Telescopio Nazionale Galileo telescope, focusing the analysis on the He I triplet. We further employed a three-dimensional hydrodynamic aeronomy model to understand the observational results. <BR /> Results: We did not find any signature of planetary absorption at the position of the He I triplet with an upper limit of 0.7% (i.e. 1.11 planetary radii; 95% confidence level). We re-estimated the high-energy stellar emission, which we combined with a stellar photospheric model, to generate the input for the hydrodynamic modelling. We determined that, assuming a solar He to H abundance ratio, He I absorption should have been detected. Considering a stellar wind 25 times weaker than solar, we could reproduce the non-detection only by assuming a He to H abundance ratio about 16 times smaller than solar. Instead, considering a stellar wind ten times stronger than solar, we could reproduce the non-detection only with a He to H abundance ratio about ten times smaller than solar. We attempted to understand this result by collecting all past He I measurements and looking for correlations with high-energy stellar emission and planetary gravity, but without success. <BR /> Conclusions: WASP-80b is not the only planet with an estimated sub-solar He to H abundance ratio, which suggests the presence of efficient physical mechanisms (e.g. phase separation, magnetic fields) capable of significantly modifying the He to H content in the upper atmosphere of hot Jupiters. The planetary macroscopic properties and the shape of the stellar spectral energy distribution are not sufficient for predicting the presence or absence of detectable metastable He in a planetary atmosphere, since the He abundance also appears to play a major role. <P />Based on observations made with the Italian Telescopio Nazionale Galileo (TNG) operated on the island of La Palma by the Fundacion Galileo Galilei of the INAF at the Spanish Observatorio Roque de los Muchachos of the IAC in the frame of the program Global Architecture of Planetary Systems (GAPS).

[8] TNG GAPS 计划。XXXII. 热木星 WASP-80b 大气中亚稳态 He I 的未探测到

背景：由于其靠近一颗活跃的K型恒星，热木星WASP-80b被认为是探测和测量上层大气He I吸收的极佳目标。目标：我们的目标是寻找、测量并建模亚稳态He I大气吸收。方法：我们利用HARPS-N和GIANO-B高分辨率光谱仪，安装在意大利国家伽利略望远镜上，对WASP-80b的四次凌日进行了光学和近红外观测，重点分析He I三重态。同时，我们采用三维流体动力学高层大气模型来理解观测结果。结果：我们在He I三重态位置未发现行星吸收信号，上限为0.7%（即1.11个行星半径，置信度95%）。我们重新估算了高能恒星辐射，并与恒星光球模型结合，为流体动力学建模提供输入。我们确定，在假设太阳He/H丰度比的情况下，应当能够探测到He I吸收。若考虑恒星风为太阳的1/25，仅在He/H丰度比约为太阳的1/16时才能复现未探测到的结果；若恒星风为太阳的10倍，仅在He/H丰度比约为太阳的1/10时才能复现未探测到的结果。我们尝试通过收集所有以往He I测量结果，并寻找与高能恒星辐射和行星重力的相关性，但未能成功。结论：WASP-80b并非唯一一颗估算He/H丰度比低于太阳的行星，这表明存在高效的物理机制（如相分离、磁场）能够显著改变热木星上层大气中的He/H含量。行星的宏观性质和恒星光谱能量分布形状不足以预测行星大气中可探测亚稳态He的存在与否，因为He丰度似乎也起着主要作用。基于意大利国家伽利略望远镜（TNG）在拉帕尔马岛由INAF的Fundacion Galileo Galilei在IAC的西班牙Roque de los Muchachos天文台执行的Global Architecture of Planetary Systems（GAPS）项目的观测。

8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] Spatially-resolving the terminator: Variation of Fe, temperature and winds in WASP-76b across planetary limbs and orbital phase

2023-01-00

Exoplanet atmospheres are inherently three-dimensional systems in which thermal/chemical variation and winds can strongly influence spectra. Recently, the ultra-hot Jupiter WASP-76b has shown evidence for condensation and asymmetric Fe absorption with time. However, it is unclear whether these asymmetries are driven by chemical or thermal differences between the two limbs, as precise constraints on variation in these have remained elusive due to the challenges of modelling these dynamics in a Bayesian framework as well as the procurement of such high quality observations. To address this we develop a new model, HyDRA-2D, capable of simultaneously retrieving morning and evening terminators with day-night winds, and use this on recent high-precision terminator observations of WASP-76b with ESPRESSO/VLT. We explore variations in Fe, temperature profile, winds and opacity deck with limb and orbital phase. In this talk I will show that with HyDRA-2D we find Fe is more prominent on the evening for the last quarter of the transit. On the other hand the morning shows a lower abundance with a wider uncertainty, driven by degeneracy with the opacity deck and because the stronger evening signal dominates the overall spectrum. We also constrain a trend of higher temperatures for the more irradiated atmospheric regions, and a higher wind speed for the last quarter of the transit than the first. This new spatially- and phase-resolved treatment is statistically favoured by 4.9 over traditional 1D-retrievals, and thus demonstrates the power of such modelling for robust constraints with current and future facilities.

[1] 空间分辨晨昏线：WASP-76b行星边缘及轨道相位上Fe、温度和风的变化

系外行星大气本质上是三维系统，热/化学变化和风可以强烈影响光谱。最近，超热木星WASP-76b显示出凝结和铁吸收随时间变化的不对称性。然而，目前尚不清楚这些不对称性是由两侧晨昏线之间的化学还是热力学差异驱动的，因为对这些变化的精确约束一直难以获得，原因包括在贝叶斯框架下建模这些动力学的挑战以及获取如此高质量观测的难度。为了解决这一问题，我们开发了新的模型HyDRA-2D，能够同时对晨昏线进行检索，并考虑昼夜风，并将其应用于最近用ESPRESSO/VLT获得的WASP-76b高精度晨昏线观测。我们探索了铁、温度剖面、风和不透明层随晨昏线和轨道相位的变化。在本报告中，我将展示，利用HyDRA-2D，我们发现铁在凌日后四分之一阶段的昏线更为显著。相反，晨线的丰度较低且不确定性更大，这主要受不透明层的简并性影响，并且由于昏线信号更强，主导了整体光谱。我们还约束了受照射更强区域温度更高的趋势，以及凌日后四分之一阶段风速高于前四分之一阶段。这种新的空间和相位分辨处理在统计上比传统的一维检索优越4.9倍，显示了此类建模在当前和未来设施下获得稳健约束的能力。

[2] Inhomogeneous terminators on the exoplanet WASP-39 b

2024-08-00

Transmission spectroscopy has been a workhorse technique used over the past two decades to constrain the physical and chemical properties of exoplanet atmospheres<SUP>1, 2, 3, 4─5</SUP>. One of its classical key assumptions is that the portion of the atmosphere it probes—the terminator region—is homogeneous. Several works from the past decade, however, have put this into question for highly irradiated, hot (T<SUB>eq</SUB> ≳ 1,000 K) gas giant exoplanets, both empirically<SUP>6, 7, 8, 9─10</SUP> and through three-dimensional modelling<SUP>11, 12, 13, 14, 15, 16─17</SUP>. While models have predicted clear differences between the evening (day-to-night) and morning (night-to-day) terminators, direct morning and evening transmission spectra in a wide wavelength range have not been reported for an exoplanet so far. Under the assumption of precise and accurate orbital parameters for the exoplanet WASP-39 b, here we report the detection of inhomogeneous terminators on WASP-39 b, which has allowed us to retrieve its morning and evening transmission spectra in the near-infrared (2─5 μm) using the James Webb Space Telescope. We have observed larger transit depths in the evening, which are, on average, 405 ± 88 ppm larger than the morning ones, and also have qualitatively larger features than the morning spectrum. The spectra are best explained by models in which the evening terminator is hotter than the morning terminator by <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mn>17</mml:mn><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>7</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>57</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>65</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> K, with both terminators having C/O ratios consistent with solar. General circulation models predict temperature differences broadly consistent with the above value and point towards a cloudy morning terminator and a clearer evening terminator.

[2] 系外行星WASP-39 b的晨昏线不均匀性

透射光谱法在过去二十年中一直是约束系外行星大气物理和化学性质的主力技术。其一个经典的关键假设是所探测的大气区域——晨昏线——是均匀的。然而，过去十年的多项工作，无论是观测还是三维建模，都对高照射、热（Teq ≳ 1,000 K）气态巨行星的这一假设提出了质疑。尽管模型预测晨昏线的傍晚（昼夜交界）和清晨（夜昼交界）存在明显差异，但迄今尚未有系外行星在宽波段范围内直接获得晨昏两侧的透射光谱。在假定系外行星 WASP-39 b 轨道参数精确且准确的前提下，本文报告在 WASP-39 b 上探测到不均匀的晨昏线，并利用詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜在近红外（2─5 μm）波段分别反演出其晨昏两侧的透射光谱。我们观测到傍晚侧凌星深度更大，平均比清晨侧大 405 ± 88 ppm，且其光谱特征也明显强于清晨侧。光谱最符合傍晚晨昏线比清晨晨昏线高 17（−57, +65）K 的模型，且两侧的 C/O 比均与太阳值一致。大气环流模型预测的温差与上述结果大致一致，并指出清晨晨昏线多云，傍晚晨昏线较为晴朗。

[3] Spatially resolving the terminator: variation of Fe, temperature, and winds in WASP-76 b across planetary limbs and orbital phase

2022-09-00

Exoplanet atmospheres are inherently three-dimensional systems in which thermal/chemical variation and winds can strongly influence spectra. Recently, the ultra-hot Jupiter WASP-76 b has shown evidence for condensation and asymmetric Fe absorption with time. However, it is currently unclear whether these asymmetries are driven by chemical or thermal differences between the two limbs, as precise constraints on variation in these have remained elusive due to the challenges of modelling these dynamics in a Bayesian framework. To address this, we develop a new model, HyDRA-2D, capable of simultaneously retrieving morning and evening terminators with day-night winds. We explore variations in Fe, temperature profile, winds, and opacity deck with limb and orbital phase using VLT/ESPRESSO observations of WASP-76 b. We find Fe is more prominent on the evening for the last quarter of the transit, with $\log (X_\mathrm{Fe}) = {-4.03}^{+0.28}_{-0.31}$, but the morning shows a lower abundance with a wider uncertainty, $\log (X_\mathrm{Fe}) = {-4.59}^{+0.85}_{-1.0}$, driven by degeneracy with the opacity deck and the stronger evening signal. We constrain 0.1-mbar temperatures ranging from $2950^{+111}_{-156}$ to $2615^{+266}_{-275}$ K, with a trend of higher temperatures for the more irradiated atmospheric regions. We also constrain a day-night wind speed of $9.8^{+1.2}_{-1.1}$ km s<SUP>-1</SUP> for the last quarter, higher than $5.9^{+1.5}_{-1.1}$ km s<SUP>-1</SUP> for the first, in line with general circulation models. We find our new spatially and phase-resolved treatment is statistically favoured by 4.9σ over traditional 1D-retrievals, and thus demonstrate the power of such modelling for robust constraints with current and future facilities.

[3] 晨昏线空间分辨：WASP-76 b行星边缘及轨道相位上Fe、温度和风的变化
