[4] Confirmation of Water Absorption in the Thermal Emission Spectrum of the Hot Jupiter WASP-77Ab with HST/WFC3

2022-06-00

Secondary eclipse observations of hot Jupiters can reveal both their compositions and thermal structures. Previous observations have shown a diversity of hot Jupiter eclipse spectra, including absorption features, emission features, and featureless blackbody-like spectra. We present a secondary eclipse spectrum of the hot Jupiter WASP-77Ab observed between 1 and 5 μm with the Hubble Space Telescope (HST) and the Spitzer Space Telescope. The HST observations show signs of water absorption indicative of a noninverted thermal structure. We fit the data with both a one-dimensional free retrieval and a grid of one-dimensional self-consistent forward models to confirm this noninverted structure. The free retrieval places a 3σ lower limit on the atmospheric water abundance of $\mathrm{log}({n}_{{{\rm{H}}}_{2}{\rm{O}}})\gt -4.78$ and cannot constrain the CO abundance. The grid fit produces a slightly superstellar metallicity and constrains the carbon-to-oxygen ratio to less than or equal to the solar value. We also compare our data to recent high-resolution observations of WASP-77Ab taken with the IGRINS/IGRINS spectrograph. We find that the best-fit model to the IGRINS data gives a reduced chi squared of χ<SUP>2</SUP> <SUB>ν</SUB> = 1.32 when compared to the WFC3 data. However, the metallicity derived from the IGRINS data is significantly lower than that derived from our self-consistent model fit. We find that this difference may be due to disequilibrium chemistry, and the varying results between the models applied here demonstrate the model dependence of derived metallicities when comparing to low-resolution, low-wavelength coverage data alone. Future work to combine observations from IGRINS, HST, and the James Webb Space Telescope will improve our estimate of the atmospheric composition of WASP-77Ab.

[4] 利用HST/WFC3确认热木星WASP-77Ab热发射光谱中的水吸收

[5] On the Dayside Atmosphere of WASP-12b

2022-06-00

The atmospheric structure of WASP-12b has been hotly contested for years, with disagreements on the presence of a thermal inversion as well as the carbon-to-oxygen ratio, C/O, due to retrieved abundances of H<SUB>2</SUB>O, CO<SUB>2</SUB>, and other included species such as HCN and C<SUB>2</SUB>H<SUB>2</SUB>. Previously, these difficult-to-diagnose discrepancies have been attributed to model differences; assumptions in these models were thought to drive retrievals toward different answers. Here, we show that some of these differences are independent of model assumptions and are instead due to subtle differences in the inputs, such as the eclipse depths and line-list databases. We replicate previously published retrievals and find that the retrieved results are data driven and are mostly unaffected by the addition of species such as HCN and C<SUB>2</SUB>H<SUB>2</SUB>. We also propose a new physically motivated model that takes into consideration the formation of H<SUP>-</SUP> via the thermal dissociation of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and H<SUB>2</SUB> at the temperatures reached in the dayside atmosphere of WASP-12b, but the data's current resolution does not support its inclusion in the atmospheric model. This study raises the concern that other exoplanet retrievals may be similarly sensitive to slight changes in the input data.

[5] 关于WASP-12b的昼面大气

[8] Supersolar Metallicity and Tentative Evidence for Photochemistry on WASP-96 b from JWST and Ground-based VLT Transmission Spectroscopy

2026-05-00

With its expanded wavelength coverage and increased precision compared to previous space-based observatories, JWST provides the opportunity to revisit benchmark planets and view them in a new light. Here, we conduct an in-depth study of the atmosphere of the hot-Saturn WASP-96 b combining a new JWST NIRSpec/G395H transit with archival NIRISS/SOSS and Very Large Telescope/FORS2 transmission spectra. The combined spectrum shows clearly visible features from H<SUB>2</SUB>O, CO<SUB>2</SUB>, and Na. CO, though, remains unconstrained, precluding a firm metallicity derivation from free retrievals alone. However, self-consistent grids yield a broadly superstellar atmospheric metallicity of 2─6× stellar. When combined with a roughly stellar C/O ratio (<inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:mn>0.4</mml:mn><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>1</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.09</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.10</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula> from self-consistent grids), we find that WASP-96 b potentially formed via core-accretion beyond the H<SUB>2</SUB>O snowline and subsequently accreted volatile-rich material. Free retrievals also find a moderate preference (<inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:mi>ln</mml:mi><mml:mi>B</mml:mi></mml:math> </inline-formula> = 2.69) for models with SO<SUB>2</SUB> versus without. WASP-96 b falls directly on the proposed "SO<SUB>2</SUB> shoreline" and the retrieved SO<SUB>2</SUB> abundance is well-matched to predictions from photochemical models. Our combined spectrum displays an optical slope, which our models fit with opacity from scattering aerosols—either small-particle condensate clouds or photochemical hazes—though we cannot completely rule out the broad wings of Na or the effects of stellar contamination. Future observations are necessary to disentangle these effects. Finally, we explore the possibility for limb asymmetry in WASP-96 b's transmission spectrum and provide several tests to identify asymmetries in our data. We encourage the community to prioritize the development of a robust pathway to quantify the presence of limb asymmetry—particularly for low signal-to-noise cases.

[8] JWST与地面VLT透射光谱揭示WASP-96 b超太阳金属丰度及光化学的初步证据

JWST相较于以往的空间望远镜，具有更广的波长覆盖和更高的精度，为重新研究基准行星并以新视角观测它们提供了机会。本文我们对热土星WASP-96 b的大气进行了深入研究，结合了新的JWST NIRSpec/G395H凌日观测、已有的NIRISS/SOSS和甚大望远镜FORS2透射光谱。合并后的光谱清晰显示了H2O、CO2和Na的特征。CO则仍未被约束，仅凭自由检索无法得出可靠的金属丰度。然而，自洽模型网格给出了2─6倍恒星值的超恒星大气金属丰度。结合近似恒星的C/O比（自洽模型网格给出0.4−0.09+0.10），我们发现WASP-96 b可能在H2O雪线之外通过核吸积形成，随后吸积了富挥发分物质。自由检索还对含SO2模型表现出中等偏好（lnB=2.69）。WASP-96 b正好落在提出的“SO2海岸线”上，检索到的SO2丰度与光化学模型预测相符。我们的合并光谱表现出光学波段的斜率，模型用散射气溶胶（小粒径冷凝云或光化学雾霾）的不透明度进行拟合，但我们无法完全排除Na宽展翼或恒星污染的影响。未来观测有必要区分这些效应。最后，我们探讨了WASP-96 b透射光谱中晨昏线不对称的可能性，并提供了若干测试方法以识别数据中的不对称性。我们鼓励社区优先开发稳健的定量晨昏线不对称性的路径，尤其针对低信噪比情形。

item_055 · sys_E
Does the central conclusion of the highly cited study "The Gaia-Kepler Stellar Properties Catalog. II. Planet Radius Demographics as a Function of Stellar Mass and Age" hold under independent datasets and alternative analysis assumptions?
高被引研究“Gaia-Kepler 恒星性质星表 II：行星半径与恒星质量和年龄的关系”中的核心结论，在独立数据集和替代分析假设下是否成立？
8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] A Spectroscopic Analysis of the California-Kepler Survey Sample. II. Correlations of Stellar Metallicities with Planetary Architectures

2021-10-00

We present independent and self-consistent metallicities for a sample of 807 planet-hosting stars from the California-Kepler Survey from an LTE spectroscopic analysis using a selected sample of Fe I and Fe II lines. Correlations between host-star metallicities, planet radii, and planetary architecture (orbital periods-warm or hot-and multiplicity-single or multiple) were investigated using nonparametric statistical tests. In addition to confirming previous results from the literature, e.g., that overall host-star metallicity distributions differ between hot and warm planetary systems of all types, we report on a new finding: when comparing the median metallicities of hot versus warm systems, the difference for multiple super-Earths is considerably larger when compared to that difference in single super-Earths. The metallicity cumulative distribution functions of hot single super-Earths versus warm single super-Earths indicate different parent stellar populations, while for sub-Neptunes this is not the case. The transition radius between sub-Neptunes and sub-Saturns was examined by comparing the APOGEE metallicity distribution for the Milky Way thin disk in the solar neighborhood with metallicity distributions of host stars segregated based upon the largest known planet in their system. These comparisons reveal increasingly different metallicity distributions as the radius of the largest planet in the systems increases, with the parent stellar metallicities becoming significantly different for R<SUB>p</SUB> &gt; 2.7 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>. The behavior of the p-values as a function of planet radius undergoes a large slope change at R<SUB>p</SUB> = 4.4 ± 0.5 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>, indicating the radius boundary between small and large planets.

[1] 加州-Kepler 调查样本的光谱分析。II. 恒星金属丰度与行星结构的相关性

我们基于LTE光谱分析，利用选定的Fe I和Fe II谱线，为California-Kepler Survey的807颗行星宿主星样本提供了独立且自洽的金属丰度。通过非参数统计检验，研究了宿主星金属丰度、行星半径及行星系统结构（轨道周期——温暖或炽热——以及多重性——单行星或多行星）之间的相关性。除证实文献中的既有结果（如所有类型的热行星和温暖行星系统的宿主星金属丰度分布存在差异）外，我们还报告了一个新发现：在比较热系统与温暖系统的中位金属丰度时，多重超地球系统的差异远大于单一超地球系统。热单超地球与温暖单超地球的金属丰度累积分布函数表明其母体恒星种群不同，而亚海王星则不存在这种情况。通过比较银河系太阳邻域薄盘的APOGEE金属丰度分布与按系统中最大已知行星分类的宿主星金属丰度分布，研究了亚海王星与亚土星之间的过渡半径。比较结果显示，随着系统中最大行星半径的增大，金属丰度分布差异也随之增大，当R_p > 2.7 R_⊕时，母体恒星金属丰度显著不同。p值随行星半径的变化在R_p = 4.4 ± 0.5 R_⊕处出现大幅斜率变化，指示了大小行星的半径分界。

[2] Demographics of Small Kepler Planets and their Dependence on Stellar Mass

2021-07-00

The "radius valley" is a feature in the short-period, small exoplanet population in Kepler and K2 data showing an abundance of super-Earths and mini-Neptunes, with a relatively scarce population of intermediate-sized planets between the two. Several studies explore the radius valley's dependence on host star properties, specifically stellar age and mass, with a wide range of treatments to the population of small close-in planets. We employ updated stellar properties and implement refined measures of completeness and reliability to observe how the Kepler small planet population varies as a function of stellar mass. These results are extrapolated into the Habitable Zone, placing an estimate on the occurrence rate of habitable, Earth-like planets that still retain their atmospheres. We further attempt to constrain the degree of stripped cores "contaminating" the super-Earth population as a function of period, which will help constrain models of photoevaporation and core powered mass loss. We discuss these results in the context of TESS and compare preliminary demographics between the Kepler field and the solar neighborhood.

[2] 小型 Kepler 行星的人口统计及其对恒星质量的依赖

“半径谷”是Kepler和K2数据中短周期、小型系外行星群体的一个特征，表现为超级地球和迷你海王星的丰度较高，而两者之间中等大小行星的数量相对稀少。多项研究探讨了半径谷对宿主星属性，特别是恒星年龄和质量的依赖性，并对小型近轨行星群体进行了多种处理。我们采用更新的恒星属性，并实施了更精细的完备性和可靠性度量，以观察Kepler小行星群体如何随恒星质量变化。这些结果被外推到宜居带，对仍保有大气的类地宜居行星的出现率进行了估算。我们还尝试约束被剥离核心“污染”超级地球群体的程度，并分析其随周期的变化，这将有助于约束光致蒸发和核心驱动质量损失模型。我们在TESS背景下讨论这些结果，并比较Kepler视场与太阳邻域的初步人口统计学差异。

[3] Comparing Results from Two Uniform Phase-curve Surveys

2026-05-00

We present a comparison of the two most recent and comprehensive Spitzer phase curve studies—L. Dang et al. and M. R. Swain et al.—which report analyses of the Spitzer 4.5 μm phase curves. The studies employ different approaches for correcting instrument systematics and they also use different approaches for selecting the optimal exoplanet system parameters. To evaluate the level of consistency between the two studies, we compared the constraints on the ratio of planet-to-star radii (<inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:mfrac><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>R</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>P</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>R</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>⋆</mml:mo></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:mrow></mml:mfrac></mml:math> </inline-formula>), eclipse depth (<inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:mfrac><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>F</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>P</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>F</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>⋆</mml:mo></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:mrow></mml:mfrac></mml:math> </inline-formula>), phase curve amplitude (A), and phase curve offset (ϕ). We find that the two studies produce similar results at the population level although results for individual planets can vary, especially for phase curve offset values. We examined the difference of planet system parameters to see if inconsistencies in individual planet results were due to data reduction methods or system parameter choices. We also examined whether the system parameters used by both studies were consistent with Kepler's third law. During this comparison, we identified one case where stellar mass, planet semimajor axis, and orbital period did not follow Kepler's law even though the values were all compiled from the same publication. To assess whether this kind of discrepancy was recurrent, we recalculated the orbital periods using Kepler's third law and compared them with the values listed in the NASA Exoplanet Archive. Our detailed analysis of archival system parameters strongly suggests that testing reported/selected parameters for consistency with Kepler's third law is worthwhile.

[3] 两项统一相位曲线巡天结果的比较

我们对两项最新且最全面的Spitzer相位曲线研究——L. Dang等和M. R. Swain等——进行了比较，这两项工作分析了Spitzer 4.5 μm相位曲线。两项研究在仪器系统误差校正方法上采用了不同策略，并且在最优系外行星系统参数的选择上也有所不同。为评估两项研究结果的一致性，我们比较了行星与恒星半径比（Rp/R*）、食深（Fp/F*）、相位曲线振幅（A）和相位曲线偏移（ϕ）的约束。我们发现，两项研究在总体上得出相似结果，但对单个行星的结果可能存在差异，尤其是在相位曲线偏移值上。我们检查了行星系统参数的差异，以判断单个行星结果的不一致性是否源于数据处理方法或系统参数选择。我们还检查了两项研究所用系统参数是否与开普勒第三定律一致。在比较过程中，我们发现有一例中，恒星质量、行星半长轴和轨道周期并不满足开普勒定律，尽管这些数值均来自同一文献。为评估此类差异是否普遍存在，我们利用开普勒第三定律重新计算了轨道周期，并与NASA系外行星档案库中的数值进行了比较。我们对档案系统参数的详细分析强烈表明，检验已报道/选定参数是否与开普勒第三定律一致是值得的。

[4] The Super-Earth to Sub-Neptune Fraction as a Function of Stellar Age

2021-01-00

By studying exoplanet demographics we can shed light on interesting patterns in planetary systems, such as the radius valley; a scarcity of planets between 1.5 and 2.0 Earth radii. The formation of this bimodal distribution of planetary radii has been heavily debated with X-ray + UV irradiation and core-powered mass loss being two leading explanations. In this work, we investigate the influence of age on the fraction of super-Earths to sub-Neptunes using the CKS (California Kepler Survey) sample. Our analysis accounts for stellar age and planet radius uncertainties, the orbital period dependence of the radius valley (and its uncertainty), and the effects of varying small planet sensitivity as a function of age. We also explore the sensitivity of our analysis to several data reliability cuts. We tentatively find that the fraction of super-Earths to sub-Neptunes increases on Gyr timescales which is consistent with atmospheric mass loss, but is in tension with the 100 Myr timescale often associated with X-ray + UV driven photoevaporation.

[4] 超级地球到亚海王星比例与恒星年龄的关系

通过研究系外行星的人口统计学，我们可以揭示行星系统中的有趣模式，例如半径谷——即1.5到2.0地球半径之间行星的稀缺。关于这种行星半径双峰分布的形成机制，X射线+紫外照射和核心驱动的质量损失是两种主要解释。在本工作中，我们利用CKS（California Kepler Survey）样本研究年龄对超级地球与亚海王星比例的影响。我们的分析考虑了恒星年龄和行星半径的不确定性、半径谷的轨道周期依赖性（及其不确定性），以及小行星探测灵敏度随年龄变化的影响。我们还探讨了分析对多种数据可靠性筛选的敏感性。我们初步发现，超级地球与亚海王星的比例在十亿年时间尺度上增加，这与大气质量损失一致，但与通常与X射线+紫外光致蒸发相关的一亿年时间尺度存在张力。

[5] Statistical and Radio Analysis of Exoplanets and Their Host Stars

2023-11-00

As of February 2022, over 4900 exoplanets have been confirmed. In this study, we conducted statistical analyses on both the exoplanets and their host stars' parameters. Our findings suggest that the radius and true mass distribution of the exoplanets remain largely unchanged compared to prior research. However, we observed a correlation between the average eccentricity and the number of planets in a system, and fluctuations in the "size" of the planets may contribute to such variation. Moreover, we discovered that, among planets with precise measurements of radius, true mass, and semi-major axis, the true mass-radius relationship follows a power─law distribution. Interestingly, the power─law index tends to decrease from super-Earths to cold Jupiters, potentially due to atmospheric composition. We also revised the radius valley, and determined that M-type host stars with low mass and metal abundance exhibit high planetary ownership rates or harbor large-mass planets, suggesting a different planet formation mechanism than GK-type stars. Lastly, we assessed the possibility of detecting exoplanets using FAST and found that there are three planets in FAST sky that may be detected, namely CoRoT-3 b, GPX-1 b, and TOI-2109 b.

[5] 系外行星及其宿主恒星的统计与射电分析

截至2022年2月，已确认系外行星数量超过4900颗。本研究对系外行星及其宿主恒星的参数进行了统计分析。我们的结果表明，系外行星的半径和真实质量分布与以往研究相比基本保持不变。然而，我们观察到平均偏心率与系统中行星数量之间存在相关性，且行星“大小”的波动可能导致这种变化。此外，我们发现，在半径、真实质量和半长轴测量精确的行星中，真实质量-半径关系遵循幂律分布。有趣的是，幂律指数从超级地球到冷木星呈下降趋势，这可能与大气成分有关。我们还修正了半径谷，并确定低质量、低金属丰度的M型宿主恒星表现出较高的行星拥有率或拥有大质量行星，暗示其行星形成机制可能不同于GK型恒星。最后，我们评估了利用FAST探测系外行星的可能性，发现FAST视场内有三颗可能被探测到的行星，分别为CoRoT-3 b、GPX-1 b和TOI-2109 b。

[6] The New Generation Planetary Population Synthesis (NGPPS): VIII. Impact of host star metallicity on planet occurrence rates, orbital periods, eccentricities, and radius valley morphology

2025-09-00

Context. The dust-to-gas ratio in the protoplanetary disc, which is likely imprinted into the host star metallicity, is a property that plays a crucial role during planet formation. On the observational side, statistical studies based on large exoplanet datasets have determined various correlations between planetary characteristics and host star metallicity. Aims. We aim to constrain planet formation and evolution processes by statistically analysing planetary systems produced at different metallicities by a theoretical model, and we compare them with the correlations derived from observational samples. Methods. We used the Generation III Bern model of planet formation and evolution to generate synthetic planetary systems at different metallicities. This global model incorporates the accretion of planetesimals and gas, planetary migration, N-body interactions between embryos, giant impacts, and protoplanetary disc evolution, as well as the planets' long-term contraction and atmospheric loss of gaseous envelopes. Using synthetic planets biased to observational completeness, we analysed the impact of stellar metallicity on planet occurrence rates, orbital periods, eccentricities, and the morphology of the radius valley. Results. Based on our nominal model, we find that (1) the occurrence rates of large giant planets and Neptune-sized planets are positively correlated with [Fe/H], while small sub-Earths exhibit an anti-correlation. In between, at radii of 1 to 3.5 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>, the occurrence rate first increases and then decreases with increasing [Fe/H], with an inflection point at ~0.1 dex. (2) Planets with orbital periods shorter than ten days are more likely to be found around stars with a higher metallicity, and this tendency weakens with increasing planet radius. (3) Both giant planets and small planets exhibit a positive correlation between the eccentricity and [Fe/H], which could be explained by the self-excitation and perturbation of outer giant planets. (4) The radius valley deepens and becomes more prominent with increasing [Fe/H], accompanied by a lower super-Earth-to-sub-Neptune ratio. Furthermore, the average radius of the planets above the valley (2.1─6 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>) increases with [Fe/H]. Conclusions. Our nominal model successfully reproduces many observed correlations with stellar metallicity either quantitatively or qualitatively, and supports the description of physical processes and parameters included in the Bern model. Quantitatively, the dependence of orbital eccentricity and period on [Fe/H] predicted by the synthetic population, however, is significantly weaker than observed. This discrepancy likely arises because the model only accounts for planetary interactions for the first 100 Myr and neglects the effects of the stellar environment (e.g. clusters, binaries). This suggests that long-term dynamical interactions between planets, along with the impact of binaries and/or companions, can drive the system towards a dynamically hotter state.

[6] 新一代行星种群综合（NGPPS）：VIII. 宿主星金属丰度对行星发生率、轨道周期、偏心率和半径谷形态的影响

背景：原行星盘中的尘气比（dust-to-gas ratio），很可能被印刻到母星的金属丰度中，是行星形成过程中至关重要的属性。在观测方面，基于大样本系外行星的统计研究已确定了行星特性与母星金属丰度之间的多种相关性。目的：我们旨在通过统计分析理论模型在不同金属丰度下产生的行星系统，并与观测样本中得到的相关性进行比较，从而约束行星形成与演化过程。方法：我们采用Generation III Bern行星形成与演化模型，在不同金属丰度下生成合成行星系统。该全局模型包含了微行星和气体的吸积、行星迁移、胚胎间N体相互作用、巨型碰撞、原行星盘演化，以及行星长期收缩和大气包层损失。利用针对观测完备性加权的合成行星，我们分析了恒星金属丰度对行星出现率、轨道周期、偏心率和半径谷形态的影响。结果：基于标称模型，我们发现：（1）大型巨行星和海王星大小行星的出现率与[Fe/H]正相关，而小型亚地球行星则呈负相关。在1至3.5 R⊕半径区间，出现率随[Fe/H]先升后降，拐点约在0.1 dex。（2）轨道周期小于十天的行星更易出现在高金属丰度恒星周围，且这一趋势随行星半径增大而减弱。（3）巨行星和小行星的偏心率均与[Fe/H]正相关，可能由外部巨行星的自激发和扰动引起。（4）半径谷随[Fe/H]升高而加深且更明显，同时超地球/亚海王星比值降低。此外，半径谷上方行星（2.1─6 R⊕）的平均半径随[Fe/H]增加而增大。结论：我们的标称模型在定量或定性上成功再现了许多与恒星金属丰度相关的观测关系，并支持Bern模型中所包含的物理过程和参数的描述。但定量上，合成族群预测的轨道偏心率和周期对[Fe/H]的依赖显著弱于观测，可能是因为模型仅考虑了前100 Myr的行星相互作用，忽略了恒星环境（如星团、双星）的影响。这表明，行星间的长期动力学相互作用以及双星和/或伴星的影响，能够使系统演化到更高动力学温度的状态。

[7] MAISTEP: A new grid-based machine learning tool for inferring stellar parameters: I. Ages of giant planet host stars

2025-03-00

Context. Our understanding of exoplanet demographics partly depends on their corresponding host star parameters. With the majority of exoplanet-host stars having only atmospheric constraints available, robust inference of their parameters (including ages) is susceptible to the approach used. Aims. The goal of this work is to develop a grid-based machine learning tool capable of determining the stellar radius, mass, and age using only atmospheric constraints. We also aim to analyse the age distribution of stars hosting giant planets. Methods. Our machine learning approach involves combining four tree-based machine learning algorithms (random forest, extra trees, extreme gradient boosting, and CatBoost) trained on a grid of stellar models to infer the stellar radius, mass, and age using effective temperatures, metallicities, and Gaia-based luminosities. We performed a detailed statistical analysis to compare the inferences of our tool with those based on seismic data from the APOKASC (with global oscillation parameters) and LEGACY (with individual oscillation frequencies) samples. Finally, we applied our tool to determine the ages of stars hosting giant planets. Results. Comparing the stellar parameter inferences from our machine learning tool with those from the APOKASC and LEGACY, we find a bias (and a scatter) of −0.5% (5%) and −0.2% (2%) in radius, 6% (5%) and −2% (3%) in mass, and −9% (16%) and 7% (23%) in age, respectively. Therefore, our machine learning predictions are commensurate with seismic inferences. When applying our model to a sample of stars hosting Jupiter-mass planets, we find the average age estimates for the hosts of hot Jupiters, warm Jupiters, and cold Jupiters to be 1.98 Gyr, 2.98 Gyr, and 3.51 Gyr, respectively. Conclusions. Our machine learning tool is robust and efficient in estimating the stellar radius, mass, and age when only atmospheric constraints are available. Furthermore, the inferred age distributions of giant planet host stars confirm previous predictions ─ based on stellar model ages for a relatively small number of hosts, as well as on the average age-velocity dispersion relation ─ that stars hosting hot Jupiters are statistically younger than those hosting warm and cold Jupiters.

[7] MAISTEP：用于推断恒星参数的新型基于网格的机器学习工具：I. 巨行星宿主星的年龄

背景：我们对系外行星种群的理解部分依赖于其宿主恒星参数。由于大多数系外行星宿主星仅有大气参数约束，参数（包括年龄）的稳健推断易受所用方法影响。目的：本工作旨在开发一种基于网格的机器学习工具，仅利用大气参数即可确定恒星半径、质量和年龄。同时，我们还分析了巨行星宿主星的年龄分布。方法：我们结合了四种树模型机器学习算法（随机森林、极端随机树、极端梯度提升和 CatBoost），在恒星模型网格上训练，以有效温度、金属丰度和基于 Gaia 的光度为输入，推断恒星半径、质量和年龄。我们对比了本工具与基于 APOKASC（整体振荡参数）和 LEGACY（单独振荡频率）样本的地震学推断结果，并将本工具应用于巨行星宿主星的年龄测定。结果：与 APOKASC 和 LEGACY 的地震学推断相比，我们的机器学习工具在半径上的偏差（及离散度）分别为 −0.5%（5%）和 −0.2%（2%），质量为 6%（5%）和 −2%（3%），年龄为 −9%（16%）和 7%（23%）。因此，我们的机器学习预测与地震学推断一致。将模型应用于木星质量行星宿主星样本时，热木星、温木星和冷木星宿主星的平均年龄分别为 1.98 Gyr、2.98 Gyr 和 3.51 Gyr。结论：当仅有大气参数约束时，我们的机器学习工具在恒星半径、质量和年龄估算上表现稳健且高效。此外，巨行星宿主星的年龄分布与先前基于较少宿主星恒星模型年龄及平均年龄-速度弥散关系的预测一致，即热木星宿主星在统计上比温木星和冷木星宿主星更年轻。

[8] Revisiting the exoplanet radius valley with host stars from SWEET-Cat

2026-03-00

Context. The radius valley, a deficit in the number of planets with radii around 2 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>, was observed among exoplanets that have sizes of ≲5 R and orbital periods of &lt;100 days by NASA's Kepler mission. This feature separates two distinct populations: super-Earths (rocky planets with radii ≲1.9 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>) and sub-Neptunes (planets with substantial volatile envelopes and radii ≳2 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>). The valley has been proposed to stem from either planet formation conditions or evolutionary atmospheric loss processes. Disentangling these mechanisms has led to numerous studies of population-level trends, although the resulting interpretations remain sensitive to sample selection and the robustness of host-star parameters. Aims. Our aim is to re-examine the existence and depth of the radius valley, and how its location varies with orbital period, incident flux, stellar mass, and stellar age. Methods. We derived robust fundamental stellar parameters of 1221 main-sequence stars (hosting 1405 confirmed planets) from the SWEET-Cat database using a grid-based machine-learning tool (MAISTEP), which incorporates effective temperatures and metallic-ities from spectroscopy, as well as Gaia-based luminosities. Our analysis covers stars with effective temperatures between 4400 and 7500 K (FGK spectral types) and estimated radii between 0.62 and 2.75 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>. We attained a median uncertainty of 2% in both stellar radius and stellar mass. Combining the updated stellar radii with planet-to-star radius ratios from the NASA Exoplanet Archive, we recomputed the planetary radii, achieving a median uncertainty of 5%. Results. Our findings confirm a partially filled planet radius valley near 2 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>. The valley depends on the orbital period, incident flux, and stellar mass, with slopes of −0.12<SUB>−0.01</SUB><SUP>+0.02</SUP>, 0.10<SUB>−0.03</SUB><SUP>+0.02</SUP>, 0.19<SUB>−0.07</SUB><SUP>+0.09</SUP>, respectively. We also find a stronger mass-dependent trend in average sizes of sub-Neptunes than super-Earths of slopes 0.17<SUB>−0.04</SUB><SUP>+0.04</SUP> and 0.11<SUB>−0.05</SUB><SUP>+0.05</SUP>, respectively. With stellar age, the super-Earth/sub-Neptune number ratio increases from 0.51<SUB>−0.08</SUB><SUP>+0.11</SUP> (&lt;3 Gyr) to 0.64<SUB>−0.11</SUB><SUP>+0.11</SUP> (≥3 Gyr). In addition, the valley becomes shallower and shifts to larger radii, indicating age-dependent evolution in planet sizes. A four-dimensional (planet radius, orbital period, stellar mass, and stellar age) linear fit of the valley in log-log space produces slopes in orbital period and stellar mass that are consistent with the results from the two-dimensional analyses, and a weaker slope of 0.07<SUB>−0.04</SUB><SUP>+0.03</SUP> in stellar age. Conclusions. The valley's shift and shallowing over gigayear timescales point to prolonged atmospheric loss, which is consistent with a core-powered mass-loss scenario. Our findings also highlight the importance of stellar age in the interpretation of exoplanet demographics and motivate improved age determinations, as is expected from future missions such as PLATO.

[8] 利用SWEET-Cat主星重新审视系外行星半径谷

背景：半径谷是指在半径约为2 R⊕的行星数量存在缺口，这一现象由NASA的Kepler任务在半径≲5 R、轨道周期<100天的系外行星中观测到。该特征将两类不同的行星群体分开：超级地球（岩石行星，半径≲1.9 R⊕）和亚海王星（具有大量挥发物包层，半径≳2 R⊕）。半径谷被认为可能源于行星形成条件或后期大气损失过程。为区分这些机制，已有大量关于群体层面趋势的研究，但结果的解释仍对样本选择和母星参数的稳健性高度敏感。目的：我们旨在重新检验半径谷的存在性和深度，以及其位置如何随轨道周期、入射辐射通量、恒星质量和恒星年龄变化。方法：我们利用基于网格的机器学习工具MAISTEP，从SWEET-Cat数据库中获得了1221颗主序星（拥有1405颗已确认行星）的稳健基本恒星参数，该工具结合了光谱学获得的有效温度和金属丰度，以及基于Gaia的光度。分析覆盖了有效温度在4400至7500 K（FGK型）之间、估算半径在0.62至2.75 R⊕之间的恒星。我们在恒星半径和质量上均获得了2%的中位不确定度。结合更新后的恒星半径与NASA Exoplanet Archive中的行星-恒星半径比，重新计算了行星半径，中位不确定度为5%。结果：我们的结果确认在2 R⊕附近存在一个部分填充的行星半径谷。该谷依赖于轨道周期、入射辐射通量和恒星质量，斜率分别为−0.12−0.01+0.02、0.10−0.03+0.02、0.19−0.07+0.09。我们还发现亚海王星的平均半径随恒星质量的变化趋势比超级地球更强，斜率分别为0.17−0.04+0.04和0.11−0.05+0.05。随着恒星年龄的增加，超级地球/亚海王星数量比从0.51−0.08+0.11（<3 Gyr）上升到0.64−0.11+0.11（≥3 Gyr）。此外，半径谷变得更浅且向更大半径移动，表明行星半径存在年龄相关的演化。对半径谷在对数空间中进行四维（行星半径、轨道周期、恒星质量和恒星年龄）线性拟合，得到的轨道周期和恒星质量斜率与二维分析结果一致，恒星年龄斜率较弱，为0.07−0.04+0.03。结论：半径谷在十亿年尺度上的移动和变浅指向持续的大气逃逸，这与核心驱动的大气损失情景一致。我们的发现还强调了恒星年龄在系外行星人口统计解释中的重要性，并激励了更精确的年龄测定，这也是未来如PLATO等任务的目标。

item_060 · sys_B
Is CO present in the atmosphere of CoRoT-2b as detectable by emission spectroscopy, or do thermal phase observations and hotspot offsets indicate atmospheric dynamics or cloud coverage that obscure or alter CO signatures?
CoRoT-2b 的大气中是否存在 CO，可以通过发射光谱探测到，还是热相位观测和热点偏移表明大气动力学或云层覆盖掩盖或改变了 CO 特征？

[1] Unraveling the Mystery of the Peculiar and Young Hot Jupiter CoRoT-2b. I. H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO Detection from Dayside Observations with Gemini-S/IGRINS

2026-06-00

We present ground-based high-resolution spectroscopic pre-eclipse observations of the hot Jupiter CoRoT-2b obtained with the IGRINS spectrograph on Gemini South. Using cross-correlation analysis, we detect the Doppler-shifted signature of the planet's thermal emission with a signal-to-noise ratio of 4.32. Our independent analyses confirm the presence of H<SUB>2</SUB>O with a confidence level of 2.6σ and an abundance of log<SUB>10</SUB><inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>5.0</mml:mn><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>8</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.43</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.43</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula>, as well as CO with 2.3σ confidence and an abundance of log<SUB>10</SUB><inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>4.2</mml:mn><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>1</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.81</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.48</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula> in CoRoT-2b's atmosphere, using two fully independent data reduction and retrieval pipelines. No significant detections of CH<SUB>4</SUB>, CO<SUB>2</SUB>, TiO, or VO are reported. While our cross-correlation analysis tentatively suggests the presence of HCN and OH, retrieval analysis does not confirm these molecules. The detected H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO features indicate that CoRoT-2b's dayside spectrum is not featureless, as previously inferred from lower-resolution observations, but instead reveals a complex atmospheric structure. Interestingly, we find a lack of significant molecular features at wavelengths shorter than 1.7 μm, potentially due to high-altitude absorbers such as H<SUP>−</SUP>, clouds, or observational systematics. From our retrieved abundances of CO and H<SUB>2</SUB>O, we constrain a supersolar C/O ratio of <inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:mn>0.9</mml:mn><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>1</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.17</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.08</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula> and a subsolar metallicity. This study provides the first high-resolution constraints on the atmospheric composition of CoRoT-2b and serves as the foundation for future investigations into its peculiar westward hot spot offset. Further phase-resolved observations will be required to explore the underlying atmospheric dynamics in more detail.

[1] 揭开特殊且年轻热木星CoRoT-2b之谜I：利用Gemini-S/IGRINS白昼面观测探测到H2O和CO

我们展示了使用Gemini South上的IGRINS光谱仪获得的热木星CoRoT-2b凌前地基高分辨率光谱观测。通过互相关分析，我们以4.32的信噪比探测到行星热发射的多普勒移动信号。我们的独立分析以2.6σ的置信度确认了H2O的存在，其丰度为log10(−5.08−0.43+0.43)，同时以2.3σ的置信度确认了CO的存在，其丰度为log10(−4.21−0.81+0.48)。这些结果基于两套完全独立的数据处理和检索流程。未探测到CH4、CO2、TiO或VO的显著信号。虽然我们的互相关分析初步提示HCN和OH的存在，但检索分析未能确认这些分子。检测到的H2O和CO特征表明，CoRoT-2b的日面光谱并非如先前低分辨率观测所推断的那样无特征，而是揭示了复杂的大气结构。有趣的是，我们发现波长短于1.7 μm时缺乏显著的分子特征，这可能归因于高空吸收体（如H−）、云或观测系统误差。根据我们检索得到的CO和H2O丰度，约束了超太阳C/O比为0.91−0.17+0.08，金属丰度低于太阳。这项研究首次对CoRoT-2b的大气成分给出了高分辨率约束，并为未来研究其异常西移热点偏移现象奠定了基础。进一步的相位分辨观测将有助于更详细地探索其大气动力学。

[2] Atmospheric Characterization of Hot Jupiter CoRoT-1 b Using the Wide Field Camera 3 on the Hubble Space Telescope

2022-07-00

Exoplanet CoRoT-1 b is intriguing because we predict it to be a transitional planet between hot Jupiters (equilibrium temperatures ~1500 K) and ultrahot Jupiters (equilibrium temperatures &gt;2000 K). In 2012, observations of CoRoT-1 b included one primary transit and three secondary eclipses with the Hubble Space Telescope (HST) Wide Field Camera 3 (WFC3) combined with the G141 grism (1.1-1.7 μm) in stare mode. We aimed to further investigate CoRoT-1 b through its secondary eclipses, producing spectrophotometric light curves corrected for charge trapping, also known as the ramp effect in time-series observations with the WFC3. We found that, when correcting for the ramp effect and using the typically discarded first orbit, we are better capable of constraining and optimizing the emission and transmission spectra. We did a grid retrieval in this transitional temperature regime and found the spectra for CoRoT-1 b to be featureless and to agree with an inverted temperature-pressure (T-P) profile. We note, however, that the contribution function for the WFC3 indicates pressures probed near 10<SUP>-3</SUP> to 10<SUP>0</SUP> bar, which correspond to a nearly isothermal region in our T-P profile, thereby indicating that the inversion at high altitude is model-dependent. Despite no distinct features, the analysis done on CoRoT-1 b paves the way to high-precision results with stare mode spectroscopy. As a new generation of observations from the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) approaches, CoRoT-1 b might be an interesting follow-up target because the time-series spectroscopic modes of JWST's NIRSpec, MIRI, and NIRCam instruments will be analogous to HST's stare mode.

[2] 利用哈勃空间望远镜宽视场相机3对热木星CoRoT-1 b的大气特性研究

系外行星 CoRoT-1 b 具有吸引力，因为我们预测它是热木星（平衡温度约 1500 K）与超热木星（平衡温度 >2000 K）之间的过渡型行星。2012 年，利用哈勃空间望远镜（HST）宽视场相机 3（WFC3）结合 G141 色散元件（1.1-1.7 μm）以 stare 模式对 CoRoT-1 b 进行了观测，包括一次初级凌日和三次次级凌星。我们旨在通过次级凌星进一步研究 CoRoT-1 b，生成经过电荷俘获效应（即 WFC3 时序观测中的 ramp 效应）校正的光谱光度曲线。我们发现，在校正 ramp 效应并使用通常被丢弃的第一轨道数据后，我们能够更好地约束和优化发射与透射光谱。我们在该过渡温度区间进行了网格检索，发现 CoRoT-1 b 的光谱无明显特征，并与反转的温度-压强（T-P）剖面一致。然而，我们注意到 WFC3 的贡献函数表明探测到的压强范围为 10^-3 到 10^0 bar，这对应于我们 T-P 剖面中的近等温区，因此高空的反转特征具有模型依赖性。尽管未见明显特征，对 CoRoT-1 b 的分析为 stare 模式光谱学实现高精度结果铺平了道路。随着詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）新一代观测的临近，CoRoT-1 b 可能成为有趣的后续观测目标，因为 JWST 的 NIRSpec、MIRI 和 NIRCam 仪器的时序光谱模式与 HST 的 stare 模式类似。

[3] Identification of carbon dioxide in an exoplanet atmosphere

2023-02-00

Carbon dioxide (CO<SUB>2</SUB>) is a key chemical species that is found in a wide range of planetary atmospheres. In the context of exoplanets, CO<SUB>2</SUB> is an indicator of the metal enrichment (that is, elements heavier than helium, also called `metallicity')<SUP>1-3</SUP>, and thus the formation processes of the primary atmospheres of hot gas giants<SUP>4-6</SUP>. It is also one of the most promising species to detect in the secondary atmospheres of terrestrial exoplanets<SUP>7-9</SUP>. Previous photometric measurements of transiting planets with the Spitzer Space Telescope have given hints of the presence of CO<SUB>2</SUB>, but have not yielded definitive detections owing to the lack of unambiguous spectroscopic identification<SUP>10-12</SUP>. Here we present the detection of CO<SUB>2</SUB> in the atmosphere of the gas giant exoplanet WASP-39b from transmission spectroscopy observations obtained with JWST as part of the Early Release Science programme<SUP>13,14</SUP>. The data used in this study span 3.0-5.5 micrometres in wavelength and show a prominent CO<SUB>2</SUB> absorption feature at 4.3 micrometres (26-sigma significance). The overall spectrum is well matched by one-dimensional, ten-times solar metallicity models that assume radiative-convective-thermochemical equilibrium and have moderate cloud opacity. These models predict that the atmosphere should have water, carbon monoxide and hydrogen sulfide in addition to CO<SUB>2</SUB>, but little methane. Furthermore, we also tentatively detect a small absorption feature near 4.0 micrometres that is not reproduced by these models.

[3] 在系外行星大气中识别出二氧化碳

二氧化碳（CO2）是一种在多种行星大气中存在的关键化学物种。在系外行星研究中，CO2是金属丰度（即比氦更重的元素，也称为“金属性”）的指示剂，因此与热气态巨行星原始大气的形成过程相关。此外，CO2也是在类地系外行星次生大气中最有希望被探测到的物种之一。此前利用斯皮策空间望远镜对凌日行星的光度测量曾暗示CO2的存在，但由于缺乏明确的光谱识别，尚未获得确证探测。本文我们报告了在气态巨行星系外行星WASP-39b大气中通过透射光谱探测到CO2，观测数据来自JWST的早期科学发布计划。所用数据波长范围为3.0-5.5微米，在4.3微米处显示出显著的CO2吸收特征（26σ显著性）。整体光谱与一维、十倍太阳金属丰度、假设辐射-对流-热化学平衡且具有中等云层不透明度的模型高度一致。这些模型预测大气中除CO2外还应有水、CO和硫化氢，但甲烷含量很低。此外，我们还在4.0微米附近初步探测到一个小的吸收特征，该特征未被这些模型所复现。

[4] Carbon monoxide emission lines indicate an offset hotspot in the inverted atmosphere of the ultra hot Jupiter WASP-33 b

2022-07-00

We report the first detection of CO emission lines at high spectral resolution in the thermal spectrum of an exoplanet atmosphere. These emission lines, seen in the spectrum of the ultra hot Jupiter WASP-33 b, provide unambiguous evidence of a thermal inversion layer in its atmosphere, and are the first results from the MMT Exoplanet Atmosphere Survey (MEASURE) using ARIES with the adaptive secondary mirror at the 6.5-m MMT. Moreover, by incorporating these R=15,000 spectra into a Bayesian framework with 1D PHOENIX atmospheric models, we show via Cross-Correlation-to-log-Likelihood mapping that the spectra indicate an offset hotspot. This arises as the post-eclipse spectra favour a scaling parameter that creates shallower spectral line models than those for pre-eclipse, indicating variation due to an hotspot rotating in and out of view as the planet orbits its host star. We further modelled the atmosphere with 3D Global Circulation Models using gCMCRT, finding that these phase dependant atmospheric models result in consistent scaling parameters throughout the orbit. This demonstrates that high resolution spectroscopy, even at R=15,000 when it cannot detect the additional Doppler shifts from offset hotspots, remains sensitive to the dynamics and 3D nature of exoplanet atmospheres. This bodes well for systems that may require the use of lower resolution spectra to improve photon collection, such as small planets orbiting in the close in habitable zones of small, faint M-dwarfs.

[4] 一氧化碳发射线表明超热木星WASP-33 b反转大气中存在偏移的热点

我们首次在系外行星大气的热谱中以高光谱分辨率探测到CO发射线。这些发射线出现在超热木星WASP-33 b的光谱中，明确证明其大气中存在热反转层，并且是利用6.5米MMT的自适应次镜ARIES进行的MMT系外行星大气巡天（MEASURE）项目的首批成果。此外，将这些R=15,000的光谱纳入与一维PHOENIX大气模型的贝叶斯框架，通过交叉相关-对数似然映射，我们发现光谱指示存在偏移的热点。这是因为凌后光谱更倾向于一个使谱线模型变浅的缩放参数，相较于凌前，表明随着行星绕宿主星公转，热点旋转进出视野导致的变化。我们进一步利用gCMCRT的三维全球环流模型对大气进行建模，发现这些相位相关的大气模型在整个轨道上产生一致的缩放参数。这表明，即使在R=15,000分辨率下无法探测由偏移热点引起的额外多普勒位移，高分辨率光谱仍对系外行星大气的动力学和三维结构敏感。这对于需要利用低分辨率光谱以提高光子收集效率的系统（如围绕小而暗的M型矮星、位于近宜居带的小行星）来说，是一个积极信号。

[5] Carbon monoxide emission lines indicate an offset hotspot in the inverted atmosphere of the ultra hot Jupiter WASP-33 b

2022-06-00

We report the first detection of CO emission lines at high spectral resolution in the thermal spectrum of an exoplanet atmosphere. These emission lines, seen in the spectrum of the ultra hot Jupiter WASP-33 b, provide unambiguous evidence of a thermal inversion layer in its atmosphere, and are the first results from the MMT Exoplanet Atmosphere Survey (MEASURE) using ARIES with the adaptive secondary mirror at the 6.5-m MMT. Moreover, by incorporating these R=15,000 spectra into a Bayesian framework with 1D PHOENIX atmospheric models, we show via Cross-Correlation-to-log-Likelihood mapping that the spectra indicate an offset hotspot. This arises as the post-eclipse spectra favour a scaling parameter that creates shallower spectral line models than those for pre-eclipse, indicating variation due to an hotspot rotating in and out of view as the planet orbits its host star. We further modelled the atmosphere with 3D Global Circulation Models using gCMCRT, finding that these phase dependant atmospheric models result in consistent scaling parameters throughout the orbit. This demonstrates that high resolution spectroscopy, even at R=15,000 when it cannot detect the additional Doppler shifts from offset hotspots, remains sensitive to the dynamics and 3D nature of exoplanet atmospheres. This bodes well for systems that may require the use of lower resolution spectra to improve photon collection, such as small planets orbiting in the close in habitable zones of small, faint M-dwarfs.

[5] 一氧化碳发射线表明超热木星WASP-33 b反转大气中存在偏移的热点

我们首次在系外行星大气的热谱中以高光谱分辨率探测到CO发射线。这些发射线出现在超热木星WASP-33 b的光谱中，明确证明其大气中存在温度反转层，也是利用6.5米MMT的自适应次镜ARIES进行的MMT系外行星大气巡天（MEASURE）的首批成果。此外，通过将这些R=15,000的光谱纳入贝叶斯框架，并结合一维PHOENIX大气模型，我们通过互相关-对数似然映射显示，光谱指示存在偏移的热点。这是因为掩食后光谱更倾向于一个使光谱线模型变浅的缩放参数，而掩食前则不是，表明随着行星绕母星公转，热点旋转进出视野导致的变化。我们进一步利用gCMCRT对大气进行三维全球环流模型建模，发现这些相位相关的大气模型在整个轨道上产生一致的缩放参数。这表明，即使在R=15,000分辨率下无法探测到由偏移热点引起的额外多普勒位移，高分辨率光谱仍然对系外行星大气的动力学和三维结构敏感。这对于需要利用低分辨率光谱以提高光子收集效率的系统（如围绕小而暗的M型矮星、位于宜居带内的小行星）来说，是一个积极信号。

[6] Carbon monoxide emission lines reveal an inverted atmosphere in the ultra hot Jupiter WASP-33 b consistent with an eastward hot spot

2023-06-00

We report the first detection of CO emission at high spectral resolution in the day-side infrared thermal spectrum of an exoplanet. These emission lines, found in the atmosphere of the transiting ultra hot Jupiter (UHJ) WASP-33 b, provide unambiguous evidence of its thermal inversion. Using spectra from the MMT Exoplanet Atmosphere Survey (MEASURE, R ~ 15 000), covering pre- and post-eclipse phases, we cross-correlate with 1D PHOENIX spectral templates to detect CO at S/N = 7.9 ($v_{\rm {sys}}=0.15^{+0.64}_{-0.65}$ km s<SUP>-1</SUP>, $K_{\rm {p}}=229.5^{+1.1}_{-1.0}$ km s<SUP>-1</SUP>). Moreover, using cross-correlation-to-log-likelihood mapping, we find that the scaling parameter which controls the spectral line contrast changes with phase. We thus use the general circulation model SPARC/MITgcm post-processed by the 3D gCMCRT radiative transfer code to interpret this variation, finding it consistent with an eastward-shifted hot spot. Pre-eclipse, when the hot spot faces Earth, the thermal profiles are shallower leading to smaller line contrast despite greater overall flux. Post-eclipse, the western part of the day-side faces Earth and has much steeper thermal profiles, leading to larger line contrast despite less overall flux. This demonstrates that within the log-likelihood framework, even relatively moderate resolution spectra can be used to understand the 3D nature of close-in exoplanets, and that resolution can be traded for photon-collecting power when the induced Doppler-shift is sufficiently large. We highlight CO as a good probe of UHJ thermal structure and dynamics that does not suffer from stellar activity, unlike species that are also present in the host star e.g. iron lines.

[6] 一氧化碳发射线揭示超热木星WASP-33 b存在倒置大气，且与东移热点一致

我们首次在系外行星日面红外热谱中以高光谱分辨率探测到CO发射。这些发射线出现在凌日超热木星（UHJ）WASP-33 b的大气中，明确证明其存在热反转。我们利用MMT Exoplanet Atmosphere Survey（MEASURE, R ~ 15 000）获得的凌前和凌后光谱，与一维PHOENIX光谱模板进行互相关，在S/N = 7.9（$v_{\rm {sys}}=0.15^{+0.64}_{-0.65}$ km s$^{-1}$, $K_{\rm {p}}=229.5^{+1.1}_{-1.0}$ km s$^{-1}$）处探测到CO。此外，通过互相关-对数似然映射，我们发现控制光谱线对比度的缩放参数随相位变化。因此，我们采用SPARC/MITgcm大气环流模型并用3D gCMCRT辐射传输代码后处理，解释该变化，发现其与热斑向东偏移一致。凌日前，热斑朝向地球，热结构剖面较平缓，导致线对比度较小，尽管总通量更大。凌日后，日面西部朝向地球，热结构剖面更陡，导致线对比度更大，尽管总通量较小。这表明，在对数似然框架下，即使是中等分辨率光谱也可用于理解近轨系外行星的三维性质，并且当多普勒位移足够大时，可用分辨率换取光子收集能力。我们强调CO是探测UHJ热结构和动力学的良好探针，不像铁等在母星中也存在的元素那样受恒星活动影响。

[7] Atmospheric Characterization of Hot Jupiter CoRoT-1b Based on Secondary Eclipse Spectroscopy

2021-01-00

Despite the inability to spatially resolve exoplanets from their host stars, substantial amounts of information can be acquired ranging from chemical composition, thermal structure, and constraints on planet formation. Hot Jupiters present good test cases as they are large, hot, and easily characterizable. In particular, exoplanet CoRoT-1b found in 2007, is especially interesting as it is predicted to be a transitionary planet between hot Jupiters (equilibrium temperatures around 1500K) and ultra hot Jupiters (equilibrium temperature over 2000K). Hot Jupiter atmospheres are expected to have temperatures that decrease with altitude while ultra hot Jupiters may be temperature inverted. In 2012, observations of CoRoT-1b included one primary transit and three secondary eclipses with the Hubble Space Telescope Wide Field Camera 3 (WFC3) combined with the 141 grism (1.1-1.7 μm) in stare mode. This wavelength range is predicted to contain the water absorption feature at 1.4 μm. In 2014, the primary transit of CoRoT-1b was analyzed to probe the structure and composition of the atmosphere but found it to return a featureless spectrum. Despite this, we aimed to further analyze CoRoT-1b through secondary eclipse data producing spectrophotometric light curves corrected for charge trapping also known as the ramp effect in time-series observations with the WFC3. We implemented and improved code for the Time Series Helper &amp; Integration Reduction Tool (tshirt) which extracted spectroscopic data and the Ramp Effect Charge Trapping Eliminator (RECTE). We found that by correcting the ramp effect we are better able to constrain and optimize the transit and secondary eclipse spectra which we compare with representative models in this transitionary temperature regime. It is critical to better understand the effects of systematics to characterize exoplanets as a new generation of observations with the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST), which has stare mode, approach.

[7] 基于二次凌日发射光谱的热木星 CoRoT-1b 大气特征研究

尽管无法将系外行星与其母星空间分辨开来，但我们仍能获得大量信息，包括化学组成、热结构以及对行星形成的约束。热木星是很好的测试对象，因为它们体积大、温度高且易于表征。特别是，2007年发现的系外行星CoRoT-1b尤为有趣，因为它被认为是热木星（平衡温度约1500K）与超热木星（平衡温度超过2000K）之间的过渡行星。热木星大气预计随高度升高温度降低，而超热木星可能存在温度反转。2012年，利用哈勃空间望远镜宽视场相机3（WFC3）和141棱镜（1.1-1.7 μm）以stare模式观测了CoRoT-1b的一次初级凌日和三次次级食。该波段预计包含1.4 μm的水吸收特征。2014年，对CoRoT-1b的初级凌日进行了分析以探测大气结构和成分，但发现其光谱无明显特征。尽管如此，我们仍通过次级食数据进一步分析CoRoT-1b，生成了经过电荷俘获（即WFC3时序观测中的ramp效应）校正的光度曲线。我们实现并改进了Time Series Helper & Integration Reduction Tool（tshirt）代码以提取光谱数据，以及Ramp Effect Charge Trapping Eliminator（RECTE）。我们发现，通过校正ramp效应，可以更好地约束和优化凌日及次级食光谱，并与该过渡温度区间的代表性模型进行比较。理解系统误差的影响对于表征系外行星至关重要，尤其是在即将到来的James Webb Space Telescope（JWST）新一代观测中，该望远镜同样具备stare模式。

[8] Detection of CO<SUB>2</SUB>, CO, and H<SUB>2</SUB>O in the atmosphere of the warm sub-Saturn HAT-P-12 b

2025-11-00

Context. The chemical composition of warm gas giant exoplanet atmospheres (with T<SUB>eq</SUB> &lt; 1000 K) is not well known due to the lack of observational constraints. Aims. HAT-P-12 b is a warm, sub-Saturn-mass transiting exoplanet that is ideal for transmission spectroscopy. We aim to characterise its atmosphere and probe the presence of carbonaceous species using near-infrared observations. Methods. One transit of HAT-P-12 b was observed in spectroscopy with JWST NIRSpec in the 2.87─5.10 µm range with a resolving power of ~1000. The JWST data are combined with archival observations from HST WFC3 covering the 1.1─1.7 µm range. The data were analysed using two data reduction pipelines and two atmospheric retrieval tools. Atmospheric simulations using chemical forward models were performed to interpret the spectra. Results. CO<SUB>2</SUB>, CO, and H<SUB>2</SUB>O are detected at 12.2, 4.1, and 6.0 σ confidence, respectively. Their volume mixing ratios are consistent with an atmosphere of ~10× solar metallicity and production of CO<SUB>2</SUB> by photochemistry. CH<SUB>4</SUB> is not detected and seems to be lacking, which could be due to a high intrinsic temperature with strong vertical mixing or other phenomena. SO<SUB>2</SUB> is also not detected and its production seems limited by low upper atmosphere temperatures (~500 K at P ≲ 10<SUP>−3</SUP> bar derived from one-dimensional retrievals), insufficient to produce it in detectable quantities (≳ 800 K required according to photochemical models). H<SUB>2</SUB>S is marginally detected using one data analysis method, but not by the other. Retrievals indicate the presence of clouds between 2 and 11 mbar using one data analysis method, and between 5 and 269 mbar using the other. The derived C/O ratio is below unity, but is not well constrained. Conclusions. This study points towards an atmosphere for HAT-P-12 b that could be enriched in carbon and oxygen with respect to its host star, a possibly cold upper atmosphere that may explain the non-detection of SO<SUB>2</SUB>, and a CH<SUB>4</SUB> depletion that is yet to be fully understood. When including the production of CO<SUB>2</SUB> via photochemistry, an atmospheric metallicity that is close to Saturn's can explain the observations. Metallicities inferred for other gas giant exoplanets based on their CO<SUB>2</SUB> mixing ratios may need to account for its photochemical production pathways. This may impact studies on mass-metallicity trends and links between exoplanet atmospheres, interiors, and formation history.

[8] 在温暖亚土星HAT-P-12 b大气中探测到CO2、CO和H2O

背景：由于观测约束的缺乏，温暖类气态巨行星（T<sub>eq</sub> < 1000 K）大气的化学组成尚不清楚。目标：HAT-P-12 b是一颗温暖、亚土星质量的凌星系外行星，非常适合透射光谱观测。我们旨在利用近红外观测对其大气进行表征，并探测碳基分子的存在。方法：我们利用JWST NIRSpec在2.87─5.10 µm波段（分辨率约1000）对HAT-P-12 b进行了一次凌星光谱观测，并结合了HST WFC3在1.1─1.7 µm波段的历史观测数据。数据通过两套数据处理流程和两种大气检索工具进行分析，并采用化学前向模型进行大气模拟以解释光谱。结果：CO<sub>2</sub>、CO和H<sub>2</sub>O分别以12.2、4.1和6.0 σ的置信度被探测到。它们的体积混合比与约10倍太阳金属丰度的大气以及CO<sub>2</sub>由光化学产生相一致。CH<sub>4</sub>未被探测到且似乎缺乏，这可能是由于较高的本征温度和强烈的垂直混合或其他现象所致。SO<sub>2</sub>也未被探测到，其产生似乎受限于低的高层大气温度（约500 K，P ≲ 10<sup>−3</sup> bar，由一维检索得出），不足以产生可探测量（光化学模型要求≳800 K）。H<sub>2</sub>S在一种数据分析方法下被边缘探测到，但在另一种方法下未被探测到。检索结果显示，云层在2至11 mbar（方法一）或5至269 mbar（方法二）之间。推导的C/O比低于1，但约束不佳。结论：本研究表明，HAT-P-12 b的大气中碳和氧可能相对于母星富集，高层大气可能较冷，这可解释SO<sub>2</sub>未探测到，以及CH<sub>4</sub>的消耗机制尚未完全理解。考虑CO<sub>2</sub>的光化学产生后，类似土星的金属丰度可以解释观测结果。基于CO<sub>2</sub>混合比推断的其他气态巨行星金属丰度可能需要考虑其光化学产生路径，这可能影响关于质量-金属丰度趋势及系外行星大气、内部和形成历史之间联系的研究。

item_065 · sys_A
Analyses [2020hst..prop16448B] and [2019A&A...629A.111C] of L 98-59's atmosphere reach potentially conflicting conclusions regarding CO: one reports "With the robust detection of an atmosphere, L 98-59 c will become a touchstone exoplanet for atmospheric studies and a key planet for dem...", while the other reports "We are able to place an upper limit on the mass of the smallest, innermost planet of &lt;1.01 Earth masses with 95% confidence.". Under what observational or methodological conditions can these results be reconciled, and which currently untested assumption drives the disagreement?
对L 98-59大气的分析[2020hst..prop16448B]和[2019A&A...629A.111C]在CO方面得出了可能相互矛盾的结论：一项报告称“随着大气的稳健探测，L 98-59 c将成为大气研究的标杆行星，也是演化模型的关键目标……”，而另一项报告称“我们能够以95%置信度对最小、最内侧行星的质量给出上限<1.01地球质量。”在什么观测或方法学条件下这些结果可以调和，目前尚未检验的哪个假设驱动了这种分歧？
8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] The Transmission Spectrum of the Potentially Rocky Planet L 98-59 c

2025-05-00

We present observations of the 1.35 ± 0.07 Earth radius planet L 98-59 c, collected using Wide Field Camera 3 on the Hubble Space Telescope (HST). L 98-59 is a nearby (10.6 pc), bright (H = 7.4 mag) M3V star that harbors three small, transiting planets. As one of the closest known transiting multi-planet systems, L 98-59 offers one of the best opportunities to probe and compare the atmospheres of rocky planets that formed in the same stellar environment. We measured the transmission spectrum of L 98-59 c, and the extracted spectrum showed marginal evidence (2.1σ) for wavelength-dependent transit depth variations that could indicate the presence of an atmosphere. We forward-modeled possible atmospheric compositions of the planet based on the transmission spectrum. Although L 98-59 was previously thought to be a fairly quiet star, we have seen evidence for stellar activity, and therefore we assessed a scenario where the source of the signal originates with inhomogeneities on the stellar surface. We also see a correlation between transits of L 98-59 c and L 98-59 b collected 12.5 hr apart, which is suggestive (but at &lt;2σ confidence) of a contaminating component from the star impacting the exoplanet spectrum. While intriguing, our results are inconclusive and additional data are needed to verify any atmospheric signal. Fortunately, additional data have been collected from both the HST and James Webb Space Telescope. Should this result be confirmed with additional data, L 98-59 c would be the first planet smaller than 2 Earth radii with a detected atmosphere.

[1] 可能岩石行星L 98-59 c的透射光谱

我们利用哈勃空间望远镜（HST）宽视场相机3对1.35 ± 0.07地球半径的行星L 98-59 c进行了观测。L 98-59是一颗距离10.6 pc、亮度H = 7.4 mag的M3V恒星，拥有三颗小型凌日行星。作为已知距离最近的多行星凌日系统之一，L 98-59为研究和比较在同一恒星环境下形成的类地行星大气提供了极佳机会。我们测量了L 98-59 c的透射光谱，提取的光谱在凌日深度随波长变化方面显示出边缘性证据（2.1σ），可能指示存在大气。我们基于透射光谱对该行星可能的大气组成进行了前向建模。尽管此前认为L 98-59是一颗相当安静的恒星，我们观察到恒星活动的证据，因此评估了信号源自恒星表面不均匀性的情景。我们还发现L 98-59 c和L 98-59 b的凌日事件（间隔12.5小时）之间存在相关性，这提示（但置信度<2σ）恒星的污染成分可能影响系外行星光谱。尽管结果具有吸引力，但目前尚无定论，需要更多数据以验证任何大气信号。幸运的是，HST和JWST均已获得了更多数据。如果后续数据证实该结果，L 98-59 c将成为首个探测到大气、半径小于2地球半径的行星。

[2] A Transmission Spectrum of the Sub-Earth Planet L98-59 b in 1.1-1.7 μm

2022-11-00

With the increasing number of planets discovered by the Transit Exoplanet Survey Satellite, the atmospheric characterization of small exoplanets is accelerating. L98-59 is an M-dwarf hosting a multiplanet system, and so far, four small planets have been confirmed. The innermost planet b is ~15% smaller and ~60% lighter than Earth, and should thus have a predominantly rocky composition. The Hubble Space Telescope observed five primary transits of L98-59 b in 1.1-1.7 μm, and here we report the data analysis and the resulting transmission spectrum of the planet. We measure the transit depths for each of the five transits and, by combination, we obtain a transmission spectrum with an overall precision of ~20 ppm in for each of the 18 spectrophotometric channels. With this level of precision, the transmission spectrum does not show significant modulation, and is thus consistent with a planet without any atmosphere or a planet having an atmosphere and high-altitude clouds or haze. The scenarios involving an aerosol-free, H<SUB>2</SUB>-dominated atmosphere with H<SUB>2</SUB>O or CH<SUB>4</SUB> are inconsistent with the data. The transmission spectrum also disfavors, but does not rule out, an H<SUB>2</SUB>O-dominated atmosphere without clouds. A spectral retrieval process suggests that an H<SUB>2</SUB>-dominated atmosphere with HCN and clouds or haze may be the preferred solution, but this indication is nonconclusive. Future James Webb Space Telescope observations may find out the nature of the planet among the remaining viable scenarios.

[2] 亚地球行星L98-59 b在1.1-1.7 μm的透射光谱

随着凌日系外行星巡天卫星发现的行星数量不断增加，小型系外行星的大气刻画正在加速。L98-59 是一颗拥有多行星系统的 M 矮星，目前已确认有四颗小行星。最内侧的 b 行星比地球小约 15%，质量约为地球的 60%，因此应主要为岩石组成。哈勃空间望远镜在 1.1-1.7 μm 波段观测了 L98-59 b 的五次初级凌日，本文报告了数据分析及其得到的行星透射光谱。我们测量了每次凌日的凌日深度，并通过合并获得了每个 18 个光谱光度通道整体精度约为 20 ppm 的透射光谱。在这一精度下，透射光谱未显示出显著调制，因此与无大气或存在高空云层或薄雾的大气情景一致。不含气溶胶、以 H2 为主并含 H2O 或 CH4 的大气情景与数据不符。透射光谱也不支持但未完全排除无云的 H2O 主导大气。光谱检索过程表明，含 HCN 和云或薄雾的 H2 主导大气可能是较优解，但这一结论并不确定。未来詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜的观测有望揭示该行星在剩余可行情景中的真实属性。

[3] Ground-based atmospheric characterization of super-Earth L 98-59 d at high spectral resolution

2026-04-00

Atmospheric characterization of exoplanets using ground-based high-resolution transmission spectroscopy has traditionally focussed on large and close-in planets, such as hot Jupiters. In this work, we aim to extend this technique to smaller and more temperate planets by studying the atmospheric composition of the temperate super-Earth planet L 98-59 d (<inline-formula><tex-math>$\sim$</tex-math></inline-formula><inline-formula><tex-math>$1.5\, \mathrm{R_{\rm{\oplus }}}$</tex-math></inline-formula>; <inline-formula><tex-math>$\sim$</tex-math></inline-formula><inline-formula><tex-math>$1.9\, \mathrm{M_{\rm{\oplus }}}$</tex-math></inline-formula>). Using high-resolution transmission spectra obtained using IGRINS on the Gemini-South telescope, we demonstrate the feasibility for atmospheric characterization of super-Earths using ground-based facilities, and confirm the previous tentative James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) inference of hydrogen sulfide (H<inline-formula><tex-math>$_2$</tex-math></inline-formula>S) in the atmosphere of L 98-59 d at <inline-formula><tex-math>$\lesssim 3.9\, \sigma$</tex-math></inline-formula> (<inline-formula><tex-math>$B\sim 390$</tex-math></inline-formula>). This is the first ground-based inference of a molecular species in the atmosphere of a super-Earth planet, and reveals the sensitivity of spectrographs on 8 m-class telescopes to the atmospheric characterization of such planets. By exploring a grid of atmospheric models, we find that the data favour a cloud-free atmosphere with an abundance of H<inline-formula><tex-math>$_2$</tex-math></inline-formula>S corresponding to <inline-formula><tex-math>$\sim$</tex-math></inline-formula>1─10 <inline-formula><tex-math>$\times$</tex-math></inline-formula> solar metallicity. We additionally place constraints on the atmospheric abundances of other molecular species. Assuming cloud-free models, super-solar abundances for CH<inline-formula><tex-math>$_4$</tex-math></inline-formula> and NH<inline-formula><tex-math>$_3$</tex-math></inline-formula> are ruled out at 3.6<inline-formula><tex-math>$\sigma$</tex-math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><tex-math>$4.6\sigma$</tex-math></inline-formula>, respectively. Our results are consistent with previous suggestions that L 98-59 d is a super-Earth with possible disequilibrium production of H<inline-formula><tex-math>$_2$</tex-math></inline-formula>S driven by volcanic outgassing from the surface. Future studies combining multiple observations with different facilities may be able to further constrain the atmospheric composition of this planet. This work underscores the promise of atmospheric characterization of super-Earth exoplanets using high-resolution spectroscopy with ground-based facilities.

[3] 利用高光谱分辨率对超级地球L 98-59 d进行地面大气特征研究

利用地基高分辨率透射光谱对系外行星大气的特性研究传统上主要集中在大质量、近轨道的行星，如热木星。本工作旨在通过研究温带超级地球L 98-59 d（~1.5 R_⊕，~1.9 M_⊕）的大气组成，将该技术扩展到更小、更温和的行星。我们利用Gemini-South望远镜上的IGRINS获得的高分辨率透射光谱，展示了利用地基设施对超级地球进行大气特性研究的可行性，并确认了此前JWST对L 98-59 d大气中硫化氢（H2S）存在的初步推断，显著性为≲3.9σ（B~390）。这是首次在超级地球大气中通过地基观测推断出分子物种，显示了8米级望远镜光谱仪对这类行星大气特性研究的灵敏度。通过探索一组大气模型，我们发现数据更支持无云大气，H2S丰度约为1─10倍太阳金属丰度。我们还对其他分子物种的大气丰度进行了约束。在假设无云模型下，CH4和NH3的超太阳丰度分别以3.6σ和4.6σ被排除。我们的结果与此前L 98-59 d为超级地球且可能存在由火山喷发驱动的H2S非平衡生成的观点一致。未来结合多台设施的多次观测，有望进一步约束该行星的大气组成。本工作强调了利用地基高分辨率光谱对超级地球系外行星进行大气特性研究的前景。

[4] L 98-59: A Benchmark System of Small Planets for Future Atmospheric Characterization

2021-10-00

The M3V dwarf star L 98-59 hosts three small (R &lt; 1.6 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>) planets. The host star is bright (K = 7.1) and nearby (10.6 pc), making the system a prime target for follow-up characterization with the Hubble Space Telescope (HST) and the upcoming James Webb Space Telescope (JWST). Herein, we use simulated transmission spectroscopy to evaluate the detectability of spectral features with HST and JWST assuming diverse atmospheric scenarios (e.g., atmospheres dominated by H<SUB>2</SUB>, H<SUB>2</SUB>O, CO<SUB>2</SUB>, or O<SUB>2</SUB>). We find that H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CH<SUB>4</SUB> present in a low mean molecular weight atmosphere could be detected with HST in one transit for the two outermost planets, while H<SUB>2</SUB>O in a clear steam atmosphere could be detected in six transits or fewer with HST for all three planets. We predict that observations using JWST/NIRISS would be capable of detecting a clear steam atmosphere in one transit for each planet and H<SUB>2</SUB>O absorption in a hazy steam atmosphere in two transits or less. In a clear, desiccated atmosphere, O<SUB>2</SUB> absorption may be detectable for all three planets with NIRISS. If the L 98-59 planets possess a clear, Venus-like atmosphere, NIRSpec could detect CO<SUB>2</SUB> within 26 transits for each planet, but the presence of H<SUB>2</SUB>SO<SUB>4</SUB> clouds would significantly suppress CO<SUB>2</SUB> absorption. The L 98-59 system is an excellent laboratory for comparative planetary studies of transiting multiplanet systems, and observations of the system via HST and JWST would present a unique opportunity to test the accuracy of the models presented in this study.

[4] L 98-59：未来大气特征研究的小型行星基准系统

M3V矮星L 98-59拥有三颗小型（R < 1.6 R⊕）行星。母星亮（K = 7.1）且距离近（10.6 pc），使该系统成为Hubble Space Telescope（HST）和即将发射的James Webb Space Telescope（JWST）后续特征分析的优选目标。本文利用模拟透射光谱，评估在不同大气情景（如以H2、H2O、CO2或O2为主的大气）下，HST和JWST对光谱特征的探测能力。我们发现，在低平均分子量大气中，H2O和CH4可在两颗最外侧行星的一次凌星中被HST探测到，而在清晰的水蒸气大气中，三颗行星均可在六次或更少凌星中被HST探测到H2O。我们预测，JWST/NIRISS观测可在每颗行星的一次凌星中探测到清晰的水蒸气大气，在两次或更少凌星中探测到有薄雾的水蒸气大气中的H2O吸收。在清晰、干燥的大气中，NIRISS有望对三颗行星均探测到O2吸收。如果L 98-59行星拥有清晰、类金星大气，NIRSpec可在每颗行星26次凌星内探测到CO2，但若存在H2SO4云则CO2吸收将被显著抑制。L 98-59系统是凌星多行星系统比较行星学的极佳实验室，通过HST和JWST观测该系统将为检验本研究模型的准确性提供独特机会。

[5] Hubble WFC3 Spectroscopy of the Rocky Planet L 98-59 b: No Evidence for a Cloud-free Primordial Atmosphere

2022-11-00

We are using archived data from HST of transiting exoplanet L 98-59 b to place constraints on its potentially hot atmosphere. We analyze the data from five transit visits and extract the final combined transmission spectrum using Iraclis. Then we use the inverse atmospheric retrieval code TauREx to analyze the combined transmission spectrum. There is a weak absorption feature near 1.40 μm and 1.55 μm in the transmission spectrum, which can be modeled by a cloudy atmosphere with abundant hydrogen cyanide (HCN). However, the unrealistically high abundance of HCN derived cannot be explained by any equilibrium chemical model with reasonable assumptions. Thus, the likeliest scenario is that L 98-59 b has a flat, featureless transmission spectrum in the WFC3/G141 bandpass due to a thin atmosphere with high mean molecular weight, an atmosphere with an opaque aerosol layer, or no atmosphere, and it is very unlikely for L 98-59 b to have a clear hydrogen-dominated primary atmosphere. Due to the narrow wavelength coverage and low spectral resolution of HST/WFC3 G141 grism observation, we cannot tell these different scenarios apart. Our simulation shows future higher precision measurements over wider wavelengths from the James Webb Space Telescope can be used to better characterize the planetary atmosphere of L 98-59 b.

[5] 哈勃WFC3对岩石行星L 98-59 b的光谱观测：未发现无云原始大气的证据

我们利用 HST 存档数据对凌日系外行星 L 98-59 b 的潜在高温大气进行约束。我们分析了五次凌日观测数据，并使用 Iraclis 提取了最终合并的透射光谱。随后，我们使用反演大气检索代码 TauREx 对合并的透射光谱进行分析。在透射光谱中，1.40 μm 和 1.55 μm 附近存在微弱吸收特征，可用富含氢氰（HCN）的多云大气模型进行拟合。然而，推导出的 HCN 丰度异常高，无法通过任何合理假设下的平衡化学模型解释。因此，最可能的情景是 L 98-59 b 在 WFC3/G141 波段具有平坦、无特征的透射光谱，原因可能是其大气稀薄且平均分子量高，或存在不透明气溶胶层，或根本没有大气，并且 L 98-59 b 拥有清晰的氢主导原始大气的可能性极低。由于 HST/WFC3 G141 色散元件观测的波长覆盖范围窄且光谱分辨率低，我们无法区分这些不同情景。我们的模拟显示，未来詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜更高精度、覆盖更宽波段的观测可用于更好地刻画 L 98-59 b 的行星大气。

[6] Detailed Architecture of the L 98-59 System and Confirmation of a Fifth Planet in the Habitable Zone

2025-09-00

The L 98-59 system, identified by TESS in 2019, features three transiting exoplanets in compact orbits of 2.253, 3.691, and 7.451 days around an M3V star, with an outer 12.83 day nontransiting planet confirmed in 2021 using ESPRESSO. The planets exhibit a diverse range of sizes (0.8─1.6 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>), masses (0.5─3 M<SUB>⊕</SUB>), and likely compositions (Earth-like to possibly water-rich), prompting atmospheric characterization studies with Hubble Space Telescope and JWST. Here, we analyze 16 new TESS sectors and improve radial velocity (RV) precision of archival ESPRESSO and HARPS data using a line-by-line framework, enabling stellar activity detrending via a novel differential temperature indicator. We refine the radii of L 98-59 b, c, and d to 0.837 ± 0.019 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>, 1.329 ± 0.029 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>, and 1.627 ± 0.041 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>, respectively. Combining RVs with transit timing variations (TTVs) of L 98-59 c and d from TESS and JWST provides unprecedented constraints on the masses and eccentricities of the planets. We report updated masses of 0.46 ± 0.11 M<SUB>⊕</SUB> for b, 2.00 ± 0.13 M<SUB>⊕</SUB> for c, and 1.64 ± 0.07 M<SUB>⊕</SUB> for d, and a minimum mass of 2.82 ± 0.19 M<SUB>⊕</SUB> for e. We additionally confirm L 98-59 f, a nontransiting super-Earth with a minimal mass of 2.80 ± 0.30 M<SUB>⊕</SUB> on a 23.06 day orbit inside the Habitable Zone. The TTVs of L 98-59 c and d (&lt;3 minutes, P<SUB>TTV</SUB> = 396 days) constrain the eccentricities of all planets to near-circular orbits (e ≲ 0.04). An internal structure analysis of the transiting planets reveals increasing water-mass fractions (<inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>f</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>H</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msub><mml:mi>O</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:math> </inline-formula>) with orbital distance, reaching <inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>f</mml:mi><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mi>H</mml:mi><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:mi>O</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub><mml:mo>≍</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.16</mml:mn></mml:math> </inline-formula> for L 98-59 d. We predict eccentricity-induced tidal heating in L 98-59 b with heat fluxes comparable to those of Io, potentially driving volcanic activity.

[6] L 98-59系统的详细结构及宜居带第五颗行星的确认

L 98-59 系统由 TESS 于2019年发现，包含三颗绕 M3V 恒星运行、轨道周期分别为2.253、3.691 和 7.451天的凌星系外行星，2021年又通过 ESPRESSO 确认了一颗轨道周期为12.83天的非凌星行星。该系统行星的半径（0.8─1.6 R_⊕）、质量（0.5─3 M_⊕）和可能的组成（类地到可能富水）多样，促使人们利用哈勃空间望远镜和 JWST 开展大气特性研究。本文分析了16个新的 TESS 观测区段，并利用逐线分析框架提升了 ESPRESSO 和 HARPS 存档数据的径向速度（RV）精度，通过一种新颖的微分温度指标实现了恒星活动去趋势。我们将 L 98-59 b、c、d 的半径精确到 0.837 ± 0.019 R_⊕、1.329 ± 0.029 R_⊕ 和 1.627 ± 0.041 R_⊕。将 RV 与 TESS 和 JWST 的 L 98-59 c、d 的凌时变化（TTV）结合，首次对行星的质量和偏心率给出前所未有的约束。我们报告 b、c、d 的最新质量分别为 0.46 ± 0.11 M_⊕、2.00 ± 0.13 M_⊕ 和 1.64 ± 0.07 M_⊕，e 的最小质量为 2.82 ± 0.19 M_⊕。我们还确认了 L 98-59 f，这是一颗轨道周期为23.06天、位于宜居带内的非凌超地球，最小质量为 2.80 ± 0.30 M_⊕。L 98-59 c 和 d 的 TTV（<3分钟，P_TTV = 396天）将所有行星的偏心率约束在近圆轨道（e ≲ 0.04）。对凌星行星的内部结构分析显示，随轨道距离增加水质量分数（f_H2O）递增，L 98-59 d 达到 f_H2O ≍ 0.16。我们预测 L 98-59 b 存在偏心率引起的潮汐加热，其热流与木卫一相当，可能驱动火山活动。

[7] A tentative detection of a potentially rocky exoplanet's atmosphere from spectral features

2021-01-00

We are moving from an era of exoplanet discovery into an era of exoplanet characterization, but small (less than 1.5 Rearth), potentially-rocky planets orbiting bright, nearby stars that are suitable for atmospheric characterization studies are relatively scarce. Amongst these candidates for atmospheric characterization is L 98-59 c. This 1.35 Rearth planet is one of three known to orbit the bright (H = 7.4), nearby (10.6 pc), M3V star L 98-59. HST/WFC3 observed a transit of L 98-59 c and revealed the first tentative signs of an atmosphere on a predominantly-rocky exoplanet. However, this detection was of fairly low signal-to-noise, and we are pursuing the collection of additional observations. If confirmed, this first detection of an atmosphere on a potentially rocky exoplanet from spectral features would enable us to measure the chemical composition of the atmosphere. This planet could become a touchstone exoplanet for atmospheric studies and a key planet for demonstrating the precision of JWST.

[7] 可能岩石系外行星大气的初步光谱特征探测

我们正从系外行星发现的时代进入系外行星特性研究的时代，但适合进行大气特性研究的小型（小于1.5 Rearth）、可能为类岩石行星且环绕明亮、近距离恒星的行星相对稀少。在这些适合大气特性研究的候选体中，L 98-59 c 是其中之一。这颗1.35 Rearth的行星是已知环绕明亮（H = 7.4）、近距离（10.6 pc）、M3V型恒星L 98-59的三颗行星之一。HST/WFC3观测了L 98-59 c的一次凌日，并首次揭示了在以岩石为主的系外行星上存在大气的初步迹象。然而，这一探测的信噪比较低，我们正在进行更多观测以确认。如果得到证实，这将是首次通过光谱特征在可能为岩石行星的系外行星上探测到大气，从而使我们能够测量其大气的化学组成。这颗行星有望成为大气研究的基准系外行星，并成为展示JWST精度的关键目标。

[8] Atmospheric characterisation of HIP 67522 b with VLT/CRIRES+: VLT/CRIRES+ suggests a heavier planet and hints at deuterium fractionation

2026-06-00

Context. Young transiting exoplanets provide unique opportunities to probe planetary atmospheres during critical early phases of evolution when atmospheric escape and contraction are most active. HIP 67522 b, a 17 Myr old hot Jupiter with an extraordinarily low bulk density (&lt;0.20 g cm<SUP>−3</SUP>), represents an ideal target for high-resolution transmission spectroscopy. Aims. We aim to constrain the mass and characterise the atmospheric composition, thermal structure, and dynamics of HIP 67522 b using ground-based high-resolution near-infrared spectroscopy with VLT/CRIRES+, complementing recent JWST observations. Methods. We obtained 92 high-resolution spectra (R ≍ 10<SUP>5</SUP>) with VLT/CRIRES+ in the K2166 band during a transit on 30 January 2025. We applied cross-correlation techniques and Bayesian nested sampling retrievals to constrain molecular abundances, temperature structure, and atmospheric dynamics. Results. We detected H<SUB>2</SUB>O at 20σ and CO at 5σ, confirming the extremely extended atmosphere of this low-mass giant. A velocity offset of −2.9 ± 0.2 km s<SUP>−1</SUP> indicates day-to-night winds. The rotation velocity has been constrained to &lt;1.8 km s<SUP>−1</SUP> at 3σ, consistent with tidal locking. The retrieval analysis suggests a planetary mass of 27.7<SUB>−5.5</SUB><SUP>+5.9</SUP> Earth masses and statistically favours a two-temperature atmospheric structure with a discrete change at mbar pressures over an isothermal profile. This mass is twice as high as the mass estimated from JWST atmospheric observations and inconsistent at 3σ, casting doubt on the actual planetary density of the planet. No matter the choice of atmospheric model, we derived a supersolar C/O ratio that is about 1.5 times solar, along with a supersolar metallicity that might further increase if the atmosphere is cloudy, which is a degeneracy that our data alone cannot resolve. We report a tentative 2σ detection of HDO with an extreme enrichment factor of ∼1000 relative to the protosolar D/H ratio. If confirmed, this would be the first detection of deuterium in an exoplanet atmosphere and would require an intense escape rate to confirm its presence.

[8] 利用VLT/CRIRES+对HIP 67522 b的大气特征研究：VLT/CRIRES+暗示行星质量更大并提示氘分馏

背景：年轻的凌星系外行星为研究行星在演化早期关键阶段（大气逃逸和收缩最为活跃时）的大气提供了独特机会。HIP 67522 b 是一颗年龄为 17 Myr、体密度极低（<0.20 g cm−3）的热木星，是高分辨率透射光谱观测的理想目标。目标：我们旨在利用 VLT/CRIRES+ 地基高分辨率近红外光谱，结合近期 JWST 观测，约束 HIP 67522 b 的质量并刻画其大气成分、热结构和动力学特征。方法：我们于 2025 年 1 月 30 日凌星期间，在 K2166 波段使用 VLT/CRIRES+ 获得了 92 个高分辨率光谱（R ≍ 10^5）。我们应用了交叉相关技术和贝叶斯嵌套采样检索方法，约束分子丰度、温度结构和大气动力学。结果：我们以 20σ 显著性探测到 H2O，以 5σ 探测到 CO，证实了该低质量巨行星具有极为膨胀的大气。速度偏移为 −2.9 ± 0.2 km s−1，表明存在昼夜面风。自转速度被约束在 <1.8 km s−1（3σ），与潮汐锁定一致。检索分析表明行星质量为 27.7−5.5+5.9 地球质量，并在统计上支持大气存在 mbar 压力下离散变化的双温结构，而非等温结构。该质量值是 JWST 大气观测估算质量的两倍，且在 3σ 范围内不一致，对该行星的实际密度提出了质疑。无论采用哪种大气模型，我们都得到了约为太阳值 1.5 倍的超太阳 C/O 比，以及超太阳金属丰度；如果大气中存在云，金属丰度可能进一步升高，但这一退化性仅凭本次数据无法解决。我们报告了 HDO 的 2σ 候选探测，其丰度相对于原太阳 D/H 比极度富集（约 1000 倍）。若被证实，这将是首次在系外行星大气中探测到氘，并需要极高的大气逃逸率来解释其存在。

8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] High-resolution transmission spectroscopy of the super-Earth 55 Cnc e

2021-10-00

The comparison of exoplanets with well-studied Solar System planets can provide answers to the fundamental questions regarding their evolutionary path. The study of exoplanet atmospheres via high-resolution transmission spectroscopy has shown great success, especially for Jupiter-type planets. In the last years, this study also continued with promising investigations towards the atmospheric characterization of terrestrial exoplanets. One possible target for such investigation is the super Earth-sized exoplanet 55 Cnc e, which is one of the most studied terrestrial exoplanets to date. Currently, there are two possible atmospheric scenarios for 55 Cnc e: Either there is a heavy-weighted envelope with a significant amount of water or there is no gaseous envelope at all i.e. a rocky surface similar to Mercury, of our Solar System.Here, we present high-resolution (R = 120 000) transit observation of this planet, acquired with the PEPSI instrument at the Large Binocular Telescope, aiming to search for absorption signatures from various species in the puzzling atmosphere of 55 CNC e.

[1] 超级地球 55 Cnc e 的高分辨率透射光谱

将系外行星与太阳系中研究充分的行星进行比较，有助于回答其演化路径的基本问题。通过高分辨率透射光谱研究系外行星大气已取得巨大成功，尤其是类木星行星。近年来，这一研究也在向类地系外行星的大气特性探索方向取得了有前景的进展。55 Cnc e是一颗超地球大小的系外行星，也是迄今研究最充分的类地系外行星之一，目前对其大气存在两种可能情景：要么存在一个富含水的大质量包层，要么完全没有气体包层，即类似于太阳系中的水星那样的岩石表面。本文展示了利用大双筒望远镜上的PEPSI仪器获得的该行星高分辨率（R = 120 000）凌日观测，旨在搜索55 Cnc e大气中各种组分的吸收特征。

[2] JWST COMPASS: NIRSpec/G395H Transmission Observations of the Super-Earth TOI-776 b

2025-03-00

We present two transit observations of the ∼520 K, 1.85 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>, 4.0 M<SUB>⊕</SUB> super-Earth TOI-776 b with JWST NIRSpec/G395H, resulting in a 2.8─5.2 μm transmission spectrum. Producing reductions using the ExoTiC-JEDI and Eureka! pipelines, we obtain a median transit depth precision of 34 ppm for both visits and both reductions in spectroscopic channels 30 pixels wide (∼0.02 μm). We find that our independent reductions produce consistent transmission spectra; however, each visit shows differing overall structure. For both reductions, a flat line is preferred for Visit 1 while a flat line with an offset between the NRS1 and NRS2 detectors is preferred for Visit 2; however, we are able to correct for this offset during our modeling analysis following methods outlined in previous works. Using PICASO forward models, we can rule out metallicities up to at least 100× solar with an opaque pressure of 10<SUP>−3</SUP> bars to ≥3σ in all cases; however, the exact lower limit varies between the visits, with Visit 1 ruling out ≲100× solar while the lower limits for Visit 2 extend beyond ∼350× solar. Our results add to the growing list of super-Earth atmospheric constraints by JWST, which provide critical insight into the diversity and challenges of characterizing terrestrial planets.

[2] JWST COMPASS：对超级地球TOI-776 b的NIRSpec/G395H透射观测

Context. 55 Cnc e is an ultra-short period super-Earth transiting a Sun-like star. However, the data at hand do not provide evidence that circumstellar material with a variable grain mass per unit area causes the observed variability. [4] On the Effect of Stellar Activity on Low-resolution Transit Spectroscopy and the use of High Resolution as Mitigation

2022-05-00

We present models designed to quantify the effects of stellar activity on exoplanet transit spectroscopy and atmospheric characterization at low (R = 100) and high (R = 100,000) spectral resolution. We study three model classes mirroring planetary system archetypes: a hot Jupiter around an early-K star (HD 189733 b); a mini-Neptune around an early-M dwarf (K2-18 b); and terrestrial planets around a late-M dwarf (TRAPPIST-1). We map photospheres with temperatures and radial velocities (RV) and integrate specific intensity stellar models. We obtain transit spectra affected by stellar contamination, the Rossiter-McLaughlin effect (RME), and center-to-limb variations (CLV). We find that, at low resolution, for later-type stars, planetary water features become difficult to distinguish from contamination. Many distributions of unocculted active regions can induce planetary-like features of similar amplitudes in the case of a late-M dwarf. Atmospheric characterization of planets around late-type stars will likely continue to suffer from degeneracy with stellar activity unless active regions' parameters can be constrained using additional information. For the early-K star, stellar contamination mostly manifests itself through a slope at optical wavelengths similar to Rayleigh scattering. In all cases, contamination induces offsets in measured planet radii. At high resolution, we show that we can determine the origin of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO detection signals and lift the degeneracy observed at low resolution, provided sufficient planet RV variation during transit and adequate correction for the RME and CLV when required. High-resolution spectroscopy may therefore help resolve issues arising from stellar contamination for favorable systems.

[4] 恒星活动对低分辨率凌星光谱的影响及高分辨率作为缓解手段的应用

我们提出了旨在量化恒星活动对系外行星凌日光谱和大气表征影响的模型，涵盖低（R = 100）和高（R = 100,000）光谱分辨率。我们研究了三类反映行星系统原型的模型：围绕早K型恒星的热木星（HD 189733 b）；围绕早M型矮星的迷你海王星（K2-18 b）；以及围绕晚M型矮星的类地行星（TRAPPIST-1）。我们绘制了具有不同温度和径向速度（RV）的光球，并整合了特定强度的恒星模型。我们获得了受恒星污染、Rossiter-McLaughlin效应（RME）和中心到边缘变化（CLV）影响的凌日光谱。我们发现，在低分辨率下，对于晚型恒星，行星水特征变得难以与污染区分。在晚M型矮星的情况下，许多未被遮挡的活跃区分布可诱发与行星特征幅度相当的信号。除非能利用额外信息约束活跃区参数，否则围绕晚型恒星的行星大气表征很可能继续受到与恒星活动的退化影响。对于早K型恒星，恒星污染主要表现为类似瑞利散射的光学波段斜率。在所有情况下，污染都会引入行星半径测量的偏移。在高分辨率下，我们展示了只要凌日过程中行星RV变化足够大，并对RME和CLV进行充分校正，就能确定H<sub>2</sub>O和CO探测信号的起源，并消除低分辨率下的退化。因此，高分辨率光谱有望帮助解决有利系统中由恒星污染引发的问题。

Context. Collecting a large variety of exoplanetary atmosphere measurements is crucial to improve our understanding of exoplanets. In this context, it is likely that the field would benefit from broad species surveys, particularly using transit spectroscopy, which is the most successful technique of exoplanetary atmosphere characterization so far. <BR /> Aims: Our goal is to develop a model-unbiased technique using transit spectroscopy to analyze every qualified atomic spectral line in exoplanetary transit data, and search for relative absorption, that is, a decrease in the flux of the line when the planet is transiting. <BR /> Methods: We analyzed archive data from HDS at Subaru, HIRES at Keck, UVES at VLT, and HARPS at LaSilla to test our spectral survey methodology.
