[7] TSD: An inverse problem approach for recovering the exoplanetary atmosphere transmission spectrum from high-resolution spectroscopy

2025-09-00

Our ability to observe, detect, and characterize exoplanetary atmospheres has grown by leaps and bounds over the last 20 years, aided largely by developments in astronomical instrumentation; improvements in data analysis techniques; and an increase in the sophistication and availability of spectroscopic models. Over this time, detections have been made for a number of important molecular species across a range of wavelengths and spectral resolutions. Ground-based observations at high resolution are particularly valuable due to the high contrast achievable between the stellar spectral continuum and the cores of resolved exoplanet absorption features. However, the model-independent retrieval of such features remains a major hurdle in data analysis, with traditional methods being limited by both the choice of algorithm used to remove the non-exoplanetary components of the signal, as well as the accuracy of model template spectra used for cross-correlation. Here we present a new algorithm TSD (Transmission Spectroscopy Decomposition) formulated as an inverse problem in order to minimize the number of assumptions and theoretically modelled components included in the retrieval. Instead of cross-correlation with pre-computed template exoplanet spectra, we rely on high spectral resolution and instrument stability to distinguish between the stellar, exoplanetary, and telluric components and velocity frames in the sequence of absorption spectra taken during multiple transits. We demonstrate the performance of our new method using both simulated and real K band observations from ESO's VLT/CRIRES+ instrument, and present results obtained from two transits of the highly-inflated super-Neptune WASP-107 b which orbits a nearby K7V star.

[7] TSD：一种通过高分辨率光谱反演恢复系外行星大气透射光谱的方法

在过去20年里，借助天文仪器的发展、数据分析技术的改进以及光谱模型的日益复杂和普及，我们观测、探测和刻画系外行星大气的能力取得了巨大进步。在此期间，多个重要分子的探测已覆盖了不同波长和光谱分辨率。地基高分辨率观测尤为有价值，因为它能在恒星光谱连续体与分辨出的系外行星吸收特征的核心之间实现高对比度。然而，这些特征的模型无关检索仍是数据分析中的主要难题，传统方法受限于去除非系外行星信号的算法选择，以及用于互相关的模型模板光谱的准确性。本文提出了一种新的算法TSD（Transmission Spectroscopy Decomposition，透射光谱分解），将其表述为一个反问题，以最小化检索中包含的假设和理论建模成分。我们不再与预先计算的系外行星模板光谱进行互相关，而是依赖高光谱分辨率和仪器稳定性，在多次凌日获得的吸收光谱序列中区分恒星、系外行星和地球大气成分及速度框架。我们利用模拟数据和ESO VLT/CRIRES+仪器的实际K波段观测，展示了新方法的性能，并给出了对绕近邻K7V恒星运行的高度膨胀超海王星WASP-107 b两次凌日观测的结果。

[8] Robustness measures for molecular detections using high-resolution transmission spectroscopy of exoplanets

2023-06-00

Ground-based high-resolution transmission spectroscopy has emerged as a promising technique for detecting chemicals in transiting exoplanetary atmospheres. Despite chemical inferences in several exoplanets and previous robustness studies, a robust and consistent detrending method to remove telluric and stellar features from transmission spectra has yet to be agreed upon. In this work, we investigate the robustness of metrics used to optimize principle component analysis (PCA)-based detrending for high-resolution transmission spectra of exoplanets in the near-infrared. As a case study, we consider observations of the hot Jupiter HD 189733 b obtained using the CARMENES spectrograph on the 3.5 m CAHA telescope. We confirm that optimizing the detrending parameters to maximize the signal-to-noise ratio (S/N) of a cross-correlation signal in the presence of noise has the potential to bias the detection significance at the planetary velocity of optimization. However, we find that optimization using the difference between a signal-injected cross-correlation function and the direct cross-correlation function (CCF) is more robust against over-optimization of noise and spurious signals. We additionally examine the robustness of weighting the contribution of each order to the final CCF, and of S/N calculations. Using a prescribed robust methodology, we confirm H<SUB>2</SUB>O in the atmosphere of HD 189733 b (S/N = 6.1). We then investigate two further case studies, of exoplanets HD 209458 b and WASP-76 b, confirming OH in the atmosphere of WASP-76 b (S/N = 4.7), and demonstrating how non-robust methods may induce false positive or inflated detections. Our findings pave the way towards a robust framework for homogeneous characterization of exoplanetary atmospheres using high-resolution transmission spectroscopy in the near-infrared.

[8] 利用高分辨率透射光谱进行系外行星分子探测的稳健性度量

地基高分辨率透射光谱已成为探测凌日系外行星大气中化学成分的有前景技术。尽管已有多颗系外行星的化学推断及相关稳健性研究，目前尚未就如何从透射光谱中去除大气和恒星特征的稳健一致的去趋势方法达成共识。在本工作中，我们研究了用于优化基于主成分分析（PCA）去趋势的指标在近红外高分辨率系外行星透射光谱中的稳健性。作为案例，我们分析了使用CARMENES光谱仪在3.5米CAHA望远镜上获得的热木星HD 189733 b的观测数据。我们证实，在存在噪声的情况下，优化去趋势参数以最大化互相关信号的信噪比（S/N）有可能在优化的行星速度处对探测显著性产生偏差。然而，我们发现，采用信号注入互相关函数与直接互相关函数（CCF）之差进行优化，对噪声和伪信号的过度优化更具稳健性。我们还考察了对每个阶次对最终CCF贡献加权及S/N计算的稳健性。采用规定的稳健方法，我们确认在HD 189733 b大气中探测到H2O（S/N = 6.1）。随后，我们研究了HD 209458 b和WASP-76 b两个案例，确认在WASP-76 b大气中探测到OH（S/N = 4.7），并展示了非稳健方法可能导致假阳性或虚高探测。我们的研究为利用近红外高分辨率透射光谱对系外行星大气进行同质化表征建立了稳健框架。

[1] Line Lists for High Resolution Studies of Exoplanets

2021-06-00

The ExoMol project (www.exomol.com) provides comprehensive line lists for the study of exoplanets and other hot bodies. As part of the ERC-funded ExoMolHD project these line lists are being refactored to facilitate their use in characterization of exoplanets using high resolution Doppler shift spectroscopy. This technique is based the use of MARVEL (measured active rotation vibration energy level) to obtain empirical energy levels and illustrated by a recent study on formaldhyde (H$_2$CO). For formaldehyde we analysed of 16 596 non-redundant transitions from 43 experimental sources and resulted in the determination of 5029 empirical energy levels. As part of this work new measurements were performed in Bologna yielding high-accuracy rotational transitions within the ground, \nub{3}, \nub{4} and \nub{6} vibrational states, significantly improving the overall accuracy of the network. Eleven artificial transitions determined from effective Hamiltonian fits were added to the network to both fix the gap between ortho and para levels, and to join free-standing networks of transitions to the main network. The empirical energies were used to update the AYTY ExoMol line list for formaldehyde. This yielded 367 779 transition frequencies determined using the empirical energy levels of which 183 673 lie above the dynamic HITRAN intensity cutoff. The generation of line lists which combine empirically-determined transition wavenumbers with {\it ab initio} transition dipoles is suitable for high resolution studies of both the Earth's and exoplanetary atmospheres. High resoltion line lists for other molecules starting with AlO, VO and the isotopologues of H$_3^+$ are currently being prepared. <P />A. R. {Al-Derzi}, S. N. Yurchenko, J. Tennyson, M. Melosso, Ningjing Jiang, C. Puzzarini, L. Dore, T. Furtenbacher, R. Tobias and A G. Császár, 2021, J. Quant. Spectrosc. Radiat. Transf., doi = {10.1016/j.jqsrt.2021.107563

[1] 用于系外行星高分辨率研究的谱线列表

ExoMol项目（www.exomol.com）为系外行星及其他高温天体的研究提供了全面的谱线表。作为ERC资助的ExoMolHD项目的一部分，这些谱线表正在重构，以便于利用高分辨率多普勒位移光谱对系外行星进行特性刻画。这一技术基于MARVEL（实测主动转动-振动能级）获得经验能级，并以最近对甲醛（H$_2$CO）的研究为例。对于甲醛，我们分析了来自43个实验来源的16,596个非冗余跃迁，确定了5029个经验能级。作为该工作的一部分，在博洛尼亚进行了新测量，获得了基态、\nub{3}、\nub{4}和\nub{6}振动态内的高精度转动跃迁，显著提升了整体网络的准确性。通过有效哈密顿量拟合确定的11个人工跃迁被加入网络，用于弥合ortho和para能级之间的间隙，并将独立的跃迁网络连接到主网络。经验能级被用于更新甲醛的AYTY ExoMol谱线表。由此得到的367,779个跃迁频率是基于经验能级确定的，其中183,673个高于HITRAN动态强度阈值。结合经验跃迁波数和从头算跃迁偶极矩生成的谱线表，适用于地球和系外行星大气的高分辨率研究。其他分子的高分辨率谱线表（如AlO、VO及H$_3^+$的同位素体）目前正在编制中。<P />A. [2] Line mixing study of carbon monoxide near 4 . 7 μm broadened by nitrogen, helium, and hydrogen

2022-11-00

We present quantitative, broadband absorbance measurements of the fundamental rovibrational band of carbon monoxide (CO) between 1965 and 2230 cm<SUP>-1</SUP> in bath gases of nitrogen (N<SUB>2</SUB>), helium (He), and hydrogen (H<SUB>2</SUB>). These measurements are then compared to two constructed models capable of reproducing the effects of line mixing present in our measured results. The static cell measurements were taken using a narrow-linewidth, broad-scan external-cavity quantum-cascade laser at pressures of 15-35 atm and temperatures of 293, 453 (CO/H<SUB>2</SUB>) and 802 K (CO/N<SUB>2</SUB>, CO/He). Our first line mixing approach, based on the modified exponential gap (MEG) law with a fitted inter-branch factor, shows improved agreement with the measured spectra across different pressures and broadening partners relative to purely Lorentzian models. At the elevated temperatures, similar agreement is observed; however, a mismatch is present between extrapolated HITRAN broadening parameters and those observed in the measured spectra. This is likely due to the known deficiency of the single power law over large temperature ranges, and hence a minor scaling of the line-by-line temperature-dependence exponents is incorporated that is supported by previous studies in the literature. Our second, more empirical line mixing approach involves extracting MEG line mixing parameters through a direct fit to the measured spectra. The Direct Fit method bypasses the need for known line shape parameters and produces even stronger agreement with measured data, with a CO/H<SUB>2</SUB> root-mean-square error of 0.4% at the highest-number-density condition of 293 K and 35 atm. The line mixing models presented and discussed will be useful for interpreting future satellite and telescope observations of CO from deeper, higher-number-density layers of exoplanetary gas-giant atmospheres, consisting of mainly H<SUB>2</SUB> and He.

[2] 4.7 μm附近一氧化碳被氮、氦和氢展宽的谱线混合研究

我们报告了在氮气（N2）、氦气（He）和氢气（H2）背景气体中，碳一氧化物（CO）基本转动-振动带在1965至2230 cm-1范围内的定量宽带吸收测量。随后将这些测量结果与两种能够再现我们观测到的谱线混合效应的模型进行了比较。静态池测量采用窄线宽、宽扫描的外腔量子级联激光器，在15-35 atm压力和293、453 K（CO/H2）及802 K（CO/N2、CO/He）温度下进行。我们的第一种谱线混合方法基于带有拟合分支间因子的修正指数间隙（MEG）定律，相较于纯洛伦兹模型，在不同压力和展宽伴侣下与实测光谱的符合度更高。在高温下也观察到类似的符合度，但HITRAN外推展宽参数与实测光谱存在不匹配。这很可能是由于单一幂律在大温度范围内的已知不足，因此我们引入了基于文献支持的谱线温度依赖指数的微小缩放。我们的第二种更为经验性的谱线混合方法是通过直接拟合实测光谱提取MEG谱线混合参数。直接拟合法无需已知谱线形状参数，并且与实测数据的符合度更高，在293 K、35 atm最高数密条件下，CO/H2的均方根误差为0.4%。所提出和讨论的谱线混合模型将有助于解释未来卫星和望远镜对主要由H2和He组成的系外气态巨行星大气深层、高数密层中CO的观测。

[3] Adapting ExoMol line lists for high-resolution studies: methane as an example

2024-07-00

The ExoMol database (Tennyson+ 2020 J. Quant. Spectrosc. Radiat. Transf., 225, 107228) provides extensive line lists and key molecular properties applicable across a broad of temperatures: including energy levels, and transitions, opacities, lifetimes, partition functions, and other relevant information. The database is widely used to interpret spectra from JWST (James Webb Space Telescope) and elsewhere. <P />The ExoMol methodology relies on the quantum mechanical treatment of the problem for each molecule with electronic motion represented by potential energy and dipole moment surfaces which provide inputs for nuclear motion programs (DUO, TROVE, DVR3D, EVEREST), enabling the generation of line lists. These line lists are designed to model molecular spectra over a wide range of temperatures for both parent and isotopically-substituted molecules. ExoMol data is being widely used to interpret JWST observations, for example leading to the detection of sulfur dioxide in exoplanet WASP-39b (Alderson+ 2023 Nature, 664, 6994) and ammonia in cool brown dwarf WISEJ1828 (Barrado+ 2023 Nature, 624, 263). The current database contains line lists and opacities for approximately a hundred molecules (250+ isotopologues), see data release ExoMol 2024 (Tennyson+ 2024 J. Quant. Spectrosc. Radiat. Transf., submitted). <P />The ExoMolHD (ExoMol in High Definition) project delivers accurate molecular line lists tailored for the studies of exoplanetary atmospheres in the current era of high-resolution (R=100000) observations. ExoMolHD combines this theoretical approach with extensive sets of accurate experimental data to enhance the applicability of these line lists for high-resolution studies. <P />A key molecule is methane, CH_4. Methane plays a vital role in astrochemistry. It is one of the dominant constituents of the atmospheres of brown dwarfs, it has been detected in hot atmospheres of exoplanets and is considered a biosignature on terrestrial planets. The ExoMol methodology was used to produce a complete and accurate line list for methane, the recent Exomol MM (MARVELous Methane) line list (Yurchenko+ 2024 Mon. Not. Roy. Astron. Soc., 528, 3719). In particular, over 50 billion transitions were calculated with the variational program TROVE (Theoretical ROVibrational Energies) and subsequently improved by incorporating empirical energy levels computed with the MARVEL (Measured Active Rotational-Vibrational Energy Levels) algorithm of Furtenbacher+ (2007 J. Molec. Spectrosc., 245, 115) from assigned measured transitions and associated uncertainties collected from the literature (Kefala+ 2024 J. Quant. Spectrosc. Radiat. Transf., 316, 108897). More than 80000 experimental transitions were collected from 96 sources to derive over 23000 energy levels with full assignments and uncertainties. Finally, over 1 million line positions with experimental accuracy were derived from this procedure. <P />The ExoMol/ExoMolHD are (a) expanding our representation of the effects of pressure on molecular spectra using a mixture of full quantum mechanical treatments, semi-empirical models and machine learning; (b) constructing line lists for important isotopically substituted molecules (McKemmish+ 2024 RAS Tech. Instr., in preparation), (c) extending calculations into the ultraviolet with a particular focus on modeling the temperature-dependence of photodissociation as photochemistry provides a key driver of atmospheric processes in many exoplanets.

ExoMol 数据库（Tennyson+ 2020 J. Quant. Spectrosc. Radiat. Not. Roy. Astron. Soc., 528, 3719）。具体来说，使用变分程序 TROVE（Theoretical ROVibrational Energies）计算了超过 500 亿个跃迁，并通过结合 MARVEL（Measured Active Rotational-Vibrational Energy Levels）算法（Furtenbacher+ 2007 J. Instr., in preparation）；（c）将计算扩展到紫外波段，特别关注光解作用的温度依赖性建模，因为光化学过程是许多系外行星大气过程的关键驱动力。

[4] Influence of molecular collisions on the atmospheric modelling: new data and updates in spectroscopic databases

2024-09-00

The advent of new-generation space missions (such as WFIRST and ARIEL) as well as high-performance observatories both space-born and ground-based (such as JWST and ELTs) should start to answer fundamental questions about the formation, composition and properties of exoplanets. A reliable interpretation of the observations will rely on a huge amount of spectroscopic data, with a particular emphasis on high-resolution spectroscopic data.Because of the extreme conditions characteristic of exoplanetary atmospheres (high temperatures and high levels of insolation), much information on molecular processes and their spectroscopic signatures is not available. In particular, lack of adequate procedures to measure or calculate collisional line-shape parameters for billions of transitions required limits severely the quality of the atmospheric modelling and retrievals. Line-shape profiles and pressure-broadening coefficients for "exotic" molecular species constitute a basis for cross-sections calculations which are further used to calculate atmospheric opacities. It was shown recently [1] that account of pressure-broadening effect on the spectral line shapes is essential to enhance our capacity for studying exoplanets. Several spectroscopic databases, such as HITRAN [2], GEISA [3], ExoMol [4], TheoReTS [5] and MoLList [6]) provide extensive line lists of positions and intensities of isolated spectral transitions, some of which contain many billions of lines. However, the associated pressure-broadening and shift parameters, as well as their temperature dependences, remain poorly determined or completely missing. Therefore, there is a huge demand for robust theoretical approaches and estimates that could provide line-shape parameters for a large range of excitations covering wide ranges of temperatures and pressures and large variety of perturbers.First steps in this direction have been made recently with developing at least approximate theoretical approaches [7] to getting pressure-broadening parameters for most infrared-active molecules detected or expected in exoplanetary atmospheres. Rotationally independent estimates of pressure-broadening coefficients have been generated for more than 50 active species (see Table) and 12 perturbers (Ar, CH4, CO, CO2, H2, H2O, He, N2, NH3, NO, O2 and self). Full data sets have been put in a specifically designed for this purpose prototype database COLLINE [8] for testing, and one-value data have been selected for each molecular pair for including in the 2024 release [4] of ExoMol.Table: Exomolecules with new line-broadening coefficients. We provide also some examples showing the influence of different pertubers on the cross-sections calculated for active molecules. [4] 分子碰撞对大气建模的影响：光谱数据库中的新数据与更新

新一代空间任务（如WFIRST和ARIEL）以及高性能的空间和地面天文台（如JWST和ELTs）的出现，将开始解答有关系外行星形成、组成和性质的基本问题。对观测结果的可靠解释依赖于大量光谱数据，尤其是高分辨率光谱数据。由于系外行星大气的极端条件（高温和高照射），关于分子过程及其光谱特征的信息严重不足。特别是，缺乏适当的程序来测量或计算数十亿跃迁所需的碰撞线型参数，严重限制了大气建模和检索的质量。线型轮廓和压强展宽系数对于“非常规”分子物种来说，是计算截面进而计算大气不透明度的基础。最近的研究[1]表明，考虑压强展宽对光谱线型的影响对于提升我们研究系外行星的能力至关重要。多个光谱数据库，如HITRAN[2]、GEISA[3]、ExoMol[4]、TheoReTS[5]和MoLList[6]，提供了大量孤立光谱跃迁的位置和强度线表，其中一些包含数十亿条谱线。然而，相关的压强展宽和位移参数及其温度依赖性仍然测定不足或完全缺失。因此，迫切需要稳健的理论方法和估算，能够为广泛激发态、宽温压范围和多种扰动体提供线型参数。最近在这一方向上已取得初步进展，开发了至少近似的理论方法[7]，用于获得大多数在系外行星大气中已探测或预期的红外活性分子的压强展宽参数。已为50多种活性物种（见表）和12种扰动体（Ar, CH4, CO, CO2, H2, H2O, He, N2, NH3, NO, O2及自展宽）生成了与转动无关的压强展宽系数估算。完整数据集已被放入专为此目的设计的原型数据库COLLINE[8]进行测试，并为每对分子选择了一个数值用于2024年ExoMol[4]发布。表：具有新展宽系数的系外分子。AlCl AlH AlO AsH3 BeH C3 CaF CaH CaO CaOH CH2 CH3 CP CrH CS FeO KCl KF KOH LiCl LiF LiH LiH+ MgF MgH MgO NaCl NaF NaH NaOH NH NS PF3 PH PN PO PO2 PS ScH SH SH3 SiC SiH SiH2 SiH3 SiO SiO2 SiS TiF TiH TiO VO 本文描述了新生成的线展宽数据的主要特征、其在COLLINE数据库中的组织方式，以及部分数据被选入ExoMol的过程。我们还提供了一些例子，展示不同扰动体对活性分子计算截面的影响。最后将概述进一步扩充系外行星分子数据库的方法。参考文献[1] J.J. [5] The 2024 release of the ExoMol database: Molecular line lists for exoplanet and other hot atmospheres

2024-11-00

The ExoMol database (www.exomol.com) provides molecular data for spectroscopic studies of hot atmospheres. These data are widely used to model atmospheres of exoplanets, cool stars and other astronomical objects, as well as a variety of terrestrial applications. The 2024 data release reports the current status of the database which contains recommended line lists for 91 molecules and 224 isotopologues giving a total of almost 10<SUP>12</SUP> individual transitions. New features of the database include extensive "MARVELization" of line lists to allow them to be used for high resolutions studies, extension of several line lists to ultraviolet wavelengths, provision of photodissociation cross-sections and extended provision of broadening parameters. Some of the in-house data specifications have been rewritten in JSON and moved for conformity with other international standards. Data products, including specific heats, a database of lifetimes for plasma studies, and the ExoMolHR web app which allows exclusively high resolution data to be extracted, are discussed.

[5] 2024年ExoMol数据库发布：用于系外行星及其他高温大气的分子谱线表

ExoMol数据库（www.exomol.com）为高温大气的光谱学研究提供分子数据。这些数据被广泛用于系外行星、冷星及其他天体的大气建模，以及多种地面应用。2024年数据发布报告了数据库的最新状态，目前包含91种分子和224种同位素体的推荐谱线表，总计近10^12条单独跃迁。数据库的新特性包括对谱线表进行大规模“MARVEL化”，以支持高分辨率研究，扩展了若干谱线表至紫外波段，提供了光解截面和更广泛的展宽参数。部分内部数据规范已用JSON重写，并与其他国际标准接轨。数据产品还包括定压热容、用于等离子体研究的寿命数据库，以及ExoMolHR网页应用（可专门提取高分辨率数据）等内容。

[6] ExoMol line lists - XLVII. Rovibronic molecular line list of the calcium monohydroxide radical (CaOH)

2022-11-00

Any future detection of the calcium monohydroxide radical (CaOH) in stellar and exoplanetary atmospheres will rely on accurate molecular opacity data. Here, we present the first comprehensive molecular line list of CaOH covering the $\tilde{A}\, ^2\Pi$-$\tilde{X}\, ^2\Sigma ^+$ rotation-vibration-electronic and $\tilde{X}\, ^2\Sigma ^+$-$\tilde{X}\, ^2\Sigma ^+$ rotation-vibration bands. The newly computed OYT6 line list contains over 24.2 billion transitions between 3.2 million energy levels with rotational excitation up to J = 175.5. It is applicable to temperatures up to T = 3000 K and covers the 0-35 000 cm<SUP>-1</SUP> range (wavelengths λ &gt; 0.29 μm) for rotational, rotation-vibration and the $\tilde{A}\, ^2\Pi$-$\tilde{X}\, ^2\Sigma ^+$ electronic transitions. The strong band around 16 000 cm<SUP>-1</SUP> (λ = 0.63 μm) is likely to be of interest in future astronomical observations, particularly in hot rocky exoplanets where temperatures can become extremely high. The OYT6 line list has been generated using empirically refined $\tilde{X}\, ^2\Sigma ^+$ and $\tilde{A}\, ^2\Pi$ state potential energy surfaces, high-level ab initio transition dipole moment surfaces, and a rigorous treatment of both Renner-Teller and spin-orbit coupling effects, which are necessary for correctly modelling the CaOH spectrum. Post-processing of the CaOH line list has been performed so as to tailor it to high-resolution applications, i.e. by replacing calculated energy levels with more accurate empirically derived values (where available), hence improving the accuracy of the predicted line positions in certain regions. The OYT6 line list is available from the ExoMol data base at www.exomol.com and the CDS astronomical data base.

[6] ExoMol谱线表 - XLVII. 钙羟基自由基（CaOH）的转动-振动-电子分子谱线表

未来在恒星和系外行星大气中探测到羟基钙自由基（CaOH）将依赖于准确的分子不透明度数据。本文首次给出了CaOH的全面分子谱线表，涵盖$\tilde{A}\, ^2\Pi$-$\tilde{X}\, ^2\Sigma ^+$转动-振动-电子和$\tilde{X}\, ^2\Sigma ^+$-$\tilde{X}\, ^2\Sigma ^+$转动-振动带。新计算的OYT6谱线表包含超过242亿条跃迁，涉及320万个能级，转动激发最高至J = 175.5。该谱线表适用于温度高达T = 3000 K，覆盖0-35000 cm^-1（波长λ > 0.29 μm）范围的转动、转动-振动及$\tilde{A}\, ^2\Pi$-$\tilde{X}\, ^2\Sigma ^+$电子跃迁。16 000 cm^-1（λ = 0.63 μm）附近的强带在未来天文观测中可能具有重要意义，尤其是在温度极高的热类地系外行星中。OYT6谱线表基于经经验修正的$\tilde{X}\, ^2\Sigma ^+$和$\tilde{A}\, ^2\Pi$态势能面、高水平从头算跃迁偶极矩面，并严格处理了Renner-Teller和自旋-轨道耦合效应，这对于正确建模CaOH光谱是必要的。谱线表后处理针对高分辨率应用进行了优化，即在可用区域用更精确的经验能级替换计算能级，从而提高了部分区域谱线位置的准确性。OYT6谱线表可从ExoMol数据库（www.exomol.com）和CDS天文数据库获取。

[7] Line mixing studies of diatomic rovibrational fundamental bands in the infrared

2023-00-00

Laser absorption spectroscopy enables quantitative, non-intrusive, and short-time-scale measurements of gaseous systems, especially at extreme temperature and pressure conditions where traditional sensors may fail. As we develop more powerful energy systems and optical metrology facilities, the need for accurate sensing in these extreme conditions has grown to include studies of combustion systems, propulsion facilities, and more recently, even exoplanetary bodies in space. Unfortunately, at higher pressure conditions, collisional "line mixing" effects cause the traditional absorption models that inform most absorption-based sensing techniques to fail. The characterization and modeling of these line mixing effects are therefore important to maintain accurate sensing across environments of interest. This dissertation will focus on the experimental measurement and modeling of broadband mid-infrared light absorption across elevated pressure and temperature conditions, especially where collisional line mixing effects are significant. In this work, I present quantitative, broadband absorbance measurements of the fundamental rovibrational band of carbon monoxide (CO) between 1965 and 2230 cm-1 in bath gases of nitrogen (N2), helium (He), and hydrogen (H2), and nitric oxide (NO) between 1700 to 2000 cm-1 in N2. These measurements were taken using a static cell and a narrow-linewidth, broad-scan external-cavity quantum-cascade laser at pressures of 15-35 atm and temperatures of 293, 453 (CO/H2) and 802 K (CO/N2, CO/He, NO/N2). The measurements are then compared to multiple constructed models capable of reproducing the effects of line mixing present in the measured results. The first line mixing approach, based on the modified exponential gap (MEG) law with fitted inter-branch factors, shows improved agreement with the measured spectra across different pressures and broadening partners relative to purely Lorentzian models. The NO model required two fitted inter-branch factors to achieve satisfactory agreement, necessary due to its additional Q-branch structure relative to that of CO. At the elevated temperatures, similar agreement is observed; however, for CO, a mismatch is present between extrapolated HITRAN broadening parameters and those observed in the measured spectra. This is likely due to the known deficiency of the single power law over large temperature ranges, and hence a minor scaling of the line-by-line temperature-dependence exponents is incorporated that is supported by previous studies in the literature. The second, more empirical line mixing approach involves extracting MEG line mixing parameters through a direct fit to the measured spectra. The Direct Fit method bypasses the need for known line shape parameters and produces even stronger agreement with measured CO data, with a CO/H2 root-mean-square error of 0.4% at the highest-number-density condition of 293K and 35atm. This approach is slightly less effective for modeling NO due to its additional spin-split lines, but satisfactory agreement is still demonstrated with constraints placed on the spin-split MEG parameters. Finally, the last line mixing approach utilizes the Energy Corrected Sudden (ECS) scaling law, which eliminates the need for any additional fitted factors, and produces reasonable agreement across the measured NO spectra, excluding the Q-branch. In the NO Q-branch peak, the ECS model overpredicts the measured data by about 7%, possibly due to the presence of additional collisional coupling between the NO spin-split lines. The line mixing models presented will be useful across a number of applications, from accurate NO thermometry in hypersonic propulsion test facilities on the ground to the interpretation of future infrared observations of CO in exoplanetary gas giant atmospheres deep in space.

[7] 红外中双原子分子转动-振动基带的谱线混合研究

激光吸收光谱能够对气体系统进行定量、非侵入性和短时间尺度的测量，尤其适用于传统传感器可能失效的极端温度和压力条件。随着更强大的能量系统和光学计量设施的发展，对这些极端条件下精确传感的需求已扩展到燃烧系统、推进设施，甚至最近的太空系外行星体研究。不幸的是，在高压条件下，碰撞“谱线混合”效应会导致大多数基于吸收的传感技术所依赖的传统吸收模型失效。因此，对这些谱线混合效应的表征和建模对于在感兴趣的各种环境中保持精确传感至关重要。本论文聚焦于在高压高温条件下，尤其是谱线混合效应显著时，宽带中红外光吸收的实验测量与建模。本文展示了在氮气（N2）、氦气（He）、氢气（H2）背景气体中，1965至2230 cm-1范围内一氧化碳（CO）基态转动-振动带，以及在N2中1700至2000 cm-1范围内一氧化氮（NO）的定量宽带吸收测量。这些测量采用静态池和窄线宽、宽扫描外腔量子级联激光器，在15-35 atm压力和293、453（CO/H2）及802 K（CO/N2、CO/He、NO/N2）温度下进行。随后将测量结果与多种能够再现谱线混合效应的模型进行比较。第一种谱线混合方法基于修正指数间隙（MEG）定律并拟合分支间因子，相较于纯洛伦兹模型，在不同压力和展宽伴侣下与实测光谱的一致性更好。NO模型需要两个分支间因子以获得满意一致性，这是由于其相较于CO具有额外的Q分支结构。在高温下也观察到类似一致性，但对于CO，HITRAN展宽参数的外推与实测光谱存在不符，这可能是单一幂律在大温区范围内已知的不足，因此引入了基于文献支持的谱线温度依赖指数微调。第二种更为经验性的谱线混合方法是通过直接拟合实测光谱提取MEG谱线混合参数。Direct Fit方法无需已知的谱线形参数，对CO数据的拟合一致性更高，在293K、35atm最高数密条件下CO/H2均方根误差为0.4%。该方法对NO的建模略逊一筹，原因在于其额外的自旋分裂谱线，但通过对自旋分裂MEG参数的约束，仍能获得满意一致性。最后一种谱线混合方法采用能量修正突变（ECS）定律，无需额外拟合因子，在NO实测光谱（除Q分支外）表现出合理一致性。在NO Q分支峰，ECS模型对实测数据高估约7%，可能是由于NO自旋分裂谱线间存在额外碰撞耦合。所提出的谱线混合模型将在多种应用中具有价值，从地面高超声速推进试验设施中NO精确测温，到未来对深空系外气态巨行星大气中CO红外观测的解释。

[8] Provision of high-definition pressure-broadening data for spectroscopic studies of exoplanetary atmospheres: ExoMol Diet 1.2

2022-09-00

Spectroscopic studies of exoplanetary atmospheres by the next generation space missions, such as the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) and Atmospheric Remote-sensing Exoplanet Large-survey (ARIEL), will rely on spectroscopic data available at mid-infrared and visible/infrared wavelengths for the bulk of already detected or expected molecules. While in gas giants the main perturbers are hydrogen (H2) and helium (He), due to the large variety of exoplanet atmospheres to be probed, a whole range of other broadeners are also of interest, including carbon dioxide (CO2), nitrogen (N2), oxygen (O2), water vapor (H2O), carbon monoxide (CO), nitric monoxide (NO), methane (CH4) and ammonia (NH3). Moreover, because of the elevated temperatures encountered, not only "exotic" molecules but also molecular ions have to be considered as optically active species.Several spectroscopic databases (e.g., HITRAN [1], GEISA [2], ExoMol [3], TheoReTS [4] and MoLList [5]) provide extensive line lists of positions and intensities of isolated spectral transitions, some of which contain many billions of lines. However, the associated pressure-broadening and shift parameters, as well as their temperature dependences, remain poorly determined or completely missing (see Figure). In addition, due to the specific conditions of hot atmospheres and chemical reactions which lead to the formation of spectroscopically active species, laboratory studies are extremely scarce in both infrared/microwave (IR/MW) and visible/ultraviolet(UV) regions. Therefore, there is a huge demand for robust theoretical approaches and estimates that could provide line-shape parameters for a large range of excitations covering wide ranges of temperatures and pressures and large variety of perturbers.Figure: Availability of line broadening parameters in spectroscopic databases: missing (white), limited coverage (light green) and good coverage (dark green). In this work, we introduce recipes for getting reasonable estimates of pressure line-broadening parameters and their temperature dependences for IR/MW (vibrotational) and UV (vibronic) transitions for molecules relevant to exoplanetary atmospheres including "exotic" neutral and ionic species (such as NO+, HCO+, H3O+, HeH+ and H3+, NH4+).The existing classical, quantum-mechanical and semi-classical theoretical approaches to collisional line-broadening and shifting used for the IR/MW region require pre-computed reliable potential-energy surfaces and CPU-costly calculations for each collisional pair. Here we suggest a simple theoretical expression requiring a minimal set of input parameters (kinetic molecular properties and the character of the leading term in the intermolecular interaction potential) [6]. Good consistency with available measurements over the temperature range 200-3000 K is demonstrated for NO and OH colliding with rare-gas atoms and non-polar molecules. Not only active neutral molecules but also molecular ions such as NO+, HCO+, H3O+, HeH+ (which have permanent dipole moments) and H3+, NH4+ (with only transition dipole moments) can be treated by this approach.In addition to the recipe for getting estimates, for the IR/MW region we consider traditional semiclassical calculation methods combined with accurate variational wavefunctions for large-scale calculations: a new program for computing line widths and shifts, based on the use of ExoMol ro-vibrational wavefunctions to compute the key (reduced) matrix elements. Given the large number of molecules, transitions, broadeners and range of temperatures required, we also explore the use of machine learng methods trained on data for selected systems to compute comprehensive and very large rotation-vibration and rovibronic pressure-broadening parameters.For the UV region, where the lack of the line-shape data is especially critical, more accurate models, motivated by the known effect of the strongly dominating adiabatic collisions and based on the Fourier-integral/phase-shift theory, are introduced to take account of real molecular trajectories and very different interaction potentials of the active molecule in the initial and final states. Examples of applications for line lists from the ExoMol database are given.The theoretical models presented will be incorporated into an updated ExoMol diet [7] to provide default line-broadening parameters when none are available from other sources and will be used to develop stand-alone approaches for automatic, large-scale production of collisional widths/shifts for line lists containing billions of transitions for molecules of interest for atmospheres of exoplanets. Tennyson, S.N. Yurchenko, A. F. Al-Refaie et al., J. Quant. Spectrosc. Radiat. Transfer 255, 107228 (2020)[4] A.V. Nikitin, Y.L. Babikov, V. G. Tyuterev, J. Mol. Spectrosc. 327, 138 (2016)[5] P.F. Transfer 203, 490 (2017)

[8] 为系外行星大气光谱研究提供高分辨率压力展宽数据：ExoMol Diet 1.2

下一代空间任务（如James Webb Space Telescope（JWST）和Atmospheric Remote-sensing Exoplanet Large-survey（ARIEL））对系外行星大气的光谱学研究，将依赖于中红外和可见/红外波段已探测或预期分子的光谱数据。对于气态巨行星，主要的展宽体是氢（H2）和氦（He），但由于未来要探测的系外行星大气类型多样，其他展宽体也很重要，包括二氧化碳（CO2）、氮气（N2）、氧气（O2）、水蒸气（H2O）、一氧化碳（CO）、一氧化氮（NO）、甲烷（CH4）和氨（NH3）。此外，由于高温环境，不仅“非常规”分子，还需考虑分子离子作为光学活性物种。多个光谱数据库（如HITRAN [1]、GEISA [2]、ExoMol [3]、TheoReTS [4]和MoLList [5]）提供了大量孤立光谱跃迁的线位置和强度表，其中部分数据库包含数十亿条谱线。然而，相关的压强展宽和频移参数及其温度依赖性仍然测定不足或完全缺失（见图）。此外，由于高温大气的特殊条件和化学反应导致的光谱活性物种形成，实验室在红外/微波（IR/MW）和可见/紫外（UV）波段的研究极为稀缺。因此，亟需稳健的理论方法和估算，能够为广泛的激发态、温度、压强和展宽体提供线型参数。图：光谱数据库中线展宽参数的可用性：缺失（白色）、覆盖有限（浅绿色）、覆盖良好（深绿色）。本工作介绍了为系外行星大气相关分子（包括“非常规”中性和离子物种，如NO+、HCO+、H3O+、HeH+和H3+、NH4+）的IR/MW（振转）和UV（电子振动）跃迁获得合理压强展宽参数及其温度依赖性的估算方法。现有的经典、量子力学和半经典理论方法用于IR/MW区的碰撞展宽和频移，需要预先计算可靠的势能面，并对每一对碰撞体进行高计算量的计算。我们提出了一种简单的理论表达式，仅需最少的输入参数（分子的动力学性质和分子间相互作用势的主导项特性）[6]。该方法在200-3000 K温度范围内，对NO和OH与稀有气体原子及非极性分子的碰撞展现出与实验数据良好的一致性。不仅适用于活性中性分子，也适用于如NO+、HCO+、H3O+、HeH+（具有永久偶极矩）和H3+、NH4+（仅有跃迁偶极矩）等分子离子。此外，针对IR/MW区，我们还考虑了结合精确变分波函数的传统半经典计算方法，用于大规模计算：开发了基于ExoMol转动-振动波函数计算关键（约化）矩阵元的新程序，用于计算谱线宽度和频移。鉴于所需分子、跃迁、展宽体和温度范围极其庞大，我们还探索了利用针对特定体系训练的数据的机器学习方法，来计算全面且极大的转动-振动和转动-电子振动压强展宽参数。对于UV区，因线型数据极度缺乏，我们引入了更精确的模型，这些模型基于已知的绝热碰撞主导效应和傅里叶积分/相移理论，能够考虑分子在初末态下真实的运动轨迹和截然不同的相互作用势。给出了ExoMol数据库线表的应用实例。所提出的理论模型将被整合进更新版ExoMol diet [7]，为其他来源无法提供参数时提供默认线展宽参数，并用于开发独立方法，实现对包含数十亿跃迁的线表自动大规模生成碰撞展宽/频移参数，这些分子对系外行星大气研究具有重要意义。参考文献：[1] I.E. 8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] A new method to correct for host star variability in multiepoch observations of exoplanet transmission spectra

2022-10-00

Transmission spectra of exoplanets orbiting active stars suffer from wavelength-dependent effects due to stellar photospheric heterogeneity. WASP-19b, an ultra-hot Jupiter (T<SUB>eq</SUB> ~ 2100 K), is one such strongly irradiated gas-giant orbiting an active solar-type star. We present optical (520-900 nm) transmission spectra of WASP-19b obtained across eight epochs, using the Gemini Multi-Object Spectrograph (GMOS) on the Gemini-South telescope. We apply our recently developed Gaussian Processes regression based method to model the transit light-curve systematics and extract the transmission spectrum at each epoch. We find that WASP-19b's transmission spectrum is affected by stellar variability at individual epochs. We report an observed anticorrelation between the relative slopes and offsets of the spectra across all epochs. This anticorrelation is consistent with the predictions from the forward transmission models, which account for the effect of unocculted stellar spots and faculae measured previously for WASP-19. We introduce a new method to correct for this stellar variability effect at each epoch by using the observed correlation between the transmission spectral slopes and offsets. We compare our stellar variability corrected GMOS transmission spectrum with previous contradicting MOS measurements for WASP-19b and attempt to reconcile them. We also measure the amplitude and timescale of broad-band stellar variability of WASP-19 from TESS photometry, which we find to be consistent with the effect observed in GMOS spectroscopy and ground-based broad-band photometric long-term monitoring. Our results ultimately caution against combining multiepoch optical transmission spectra of exoplanets orbiting active stars before correcting each epoch for stellar variability.

[1] 多历元系外行星透射光谱观测中校正母星变化性的新方法

围绕活跃恒星运行的系外行星的透射光谱会受到恒星光球非均匀性导致的波长相关效应影响。WASP-19b是一颗超热木星（T_eq ~ 2100 K），围绕一颗活跃的类太阳恒星运行。我们利用Gemini-South望远镜的Gemini多目标光谱仪（GMOS），在八个历元获得了WASP-19b的光学（520-900 nm）透射光谱。我们应用最近开发的基于高斯过程回归的方法，对凌星光变系统误差进行建模，并在每个历元提取透射光谱。我们发现，WASP-19b的透射光谱在各个历元均受到恒星活动的影响。我们报告了所有历元的光谱相对斜率与偏移量之间的反相关关系。该反相关与前向透射模型的预测一致，该模型考虑了WASP-19先前测量的未被凌星遮挡的恒星黑子和亮斑的影响。我们提出了一种新方法，通过利用观测到的透射光谱斜率与偏移量的相关性，在每个历元校正恒星活动效应。我们将校正后的GMOS透射光谱与先前对WASP-19b存在矛盾的MOS测量进行了比较，并尝试调和这些结果。我们还利用TESS光度测量了WASP-19的宽带恒星活动幅度和时间尺度，发现与GMOS光谱和地基宽带光度长期监测结果一致。我们的结果最终提醒，在合并活跃恒星系外行星的多历元光学透射光谱前，需对每个历元进行恒星活动校正。

[2] Gemini/GMOS optical transmission spectroscopy of WASP-121b: signs of variability in an ultra-hot Jupiter?

2021-06-00

We present ground-based, spectroscopic observations of two transits of the ultra-hot Jupiter WASP-121b covering the wavelength range ≍500-950 nm using Gemini/GMOS. We use a Gaussian process framework to model instrumental systematics in the light curves, and also demonstrate the use of the more generalized Student's-T process to verify our results. We find that our measured transmission spectrum, whilst showing overall agreement, is slightly discrepant with results obtained using HST/STIS, particularly for wavelengths shortwards of ≍650 nm. In contrast to the STIS results, we find evidence for an increasing bluewards slope and little evidence for absorption from either TiO or VO in our retrieval, in agreement with a number of recent studies performed at high-resolution. We suggest that this might point to some other absorbers, particularly some combination of recently detected atomic metals, in addition to scattering by hazes, being responsible for the excess optical absorption and observed vertical thermal inversion. Our results are also broadly consistent with previous ground-based photometry and 3D GCM predictions, however, these assumed different chemistry to our retrievals. In addition, we show that the GMOS observations are repeatable over short periods (days), similar to the HST/STIS observations. Their difference over longer periods (months) could well be the result of temporal variability in the atmospheric properties (i.e. weather) as predicted by theoretical models of ultra-hot Jupiters; however, more mundane explanations such as instrumental systematics and stellar activity cannot be fully ruled out, and we encourage future observations to explore this possibility.

[2] Gemini/GMOS 对 WASP-121b 的光学透射光谱：超热木星中变异性的迹象？

我们利用Gemini/GMOS在约500-950 nm波段，对超热木星WASP-121b的两次凌日进行了地基光谱观测。我们采用高斯过程框架对光变曲线中的仪器系统误差进行建模，并进一步演示了更通用的Student's-T过程用于验证结果。我们发现，所测得的透射光谱总体上与HST/STIS的结果一致，但在约650 nm以下波段存在轻微差异。与STIS结果相反，我们在检索中发现蓝端斜率增加的证据，且几乎未发现TiO或VO的吸收，这与近期多项高分辨率研究结果一致。我们认为，这可能指向其他吸收体，特别是最近探测到的原子金属的某种组合，以及薄雾的散射，共同导致了光学波段的过量吸收和观测到的垂直温度反转。我们的结果也与先前的地基光度观测和3D GCM预测大致一致，但这些假设的化学组成与我们的检索不同。此外，我们显示GMOS观测在短时间尺度（天）内具有可重复性，类似于HST/STIS观测。长时间尺度（月）上的差异很可能是理论模型预测的超热木星大气性质（即天气）随时间变化的结果；然而，仪器系统误差和恒星活动等常规解释也不能完全排除，我们鼓励未来观测进一步探讨这一可能性。

[3] Stellar Models Also Limit Exoplanet Atmosphere Studies in Emission

2025-08-00

Stellar contamination has long been recognized as a major bottleneck in transmission spectroscopy, limiting our ability to accurately characterize exoplanet atmospheres—particularly for terrestrial worlds. In response, significant observational efforts have shifted toward emission spectroscopy as a potentially more robust alternative, exemplified by initiatives such as the 500 hr JWST Rocky Worlds Director's Discretionary Time program. However, the extent to which emission spectroscopy may be affected by stellar effects remains mostly unexplored, in stark contrast with the extensive exploration and mitigation work for transmission spectroscopy. In this study, we assess the impact of imperfect knowledge of stellar spectra on exoplanet atmospheric retrievals from emission spectroscopy. At 12.8 μm, none of the considered bare surface types—basalt, ultramafic, Fe oxidized, and granitoid—can be reliably distinguished when accounting for the 3σ model precision between SPHINX and PHOENIX. At 15.0 μm, only the granitoid surface is distinguishable from all others above this threshold. These results show that stellar model uncertainty alone substantially limits our ability to constrain surface composition from photometric data, even before including other sources of uncertainty, such as stellar radius. Also, we find that current 15 μm eclipse depth estimations using different stellar models introduce a 60 ppm difference for M8 and 20 ppm for M5 stars. This model discrepancy leads to a degeneracy in retrieved planetary albedos and weakens constraints on the presence of an atmosphere. We therefore recommend that future JWST secondary eclipse observations systematically include stellar mid-IR spectroscopy to mitigate these uncertainties.

[3] 恒星模型同样限制了系外行星大气发射研究

恒星污染长期以来被认为是透射光谱的主要瓶颈，限制了我们准确表征系外行星大气的能力，尤其是类地行星。为此，观测工作已大幅转向发射光谱，作为一种更稳健的替代方案，典型例子如500小时JWST Rocky Worlds主任酌情时间项目。然而，发射光谱受恒星效应影响的程度仍基本未被探索，这与透射光谱的广泛研究和缓解工作形成鲜明对比。在本研究中，我们评估了对恒星光谱认知不完善对发射光谱系外行星大气检索的影响。在12.8 μm波段，考虑SPHINX和PHOENIX模型3σ精度后，所考虑的所有裸露表面类型（玄武岩、超镁铁、氧化铁和花岗岩）均无法可靠区分。在15.0 μm波段，只有花岗岩表面能在该阈值上与其他类型区分。这些结果表明，仅恒星模型的不确定性就已显著限制了我们通过光度数据约束表面成分的能力，甚至在考虑其他不确定性来源（如恒星半径）之前。此外，我们发现当前利用不同恒星模型估算的15 μm食深度，在M8型恒星间差异为60 ppm，M5型为20 ppm。这种模型差异导致所检索的行星反照率存在简并性，并削弱了对大气存在性的约束。因此，我们建议未来JWST二次凌日观测系统性地包含恒星中红外光谱，以缓解这些不确定性。

[4] The Influence of Stellar Chromospheres and Coronae on Exoplanet Transmission Spectroscopy

2025-02-00

A main source of bias in transmission spectroscopy of exoplanet atmospheres is magnetic activity of the host star in the form of stellar spots, faculae, or flares. However, the fact that main-sequence stars have a chromosphere and a corona and that these optically thin layers are dominated by line emission may alter the global interpretation of the planetary spectrum has largely been neglected. Using a JWST NIRISS/SOSS data set of hot Jupiter HAT-P-18 b, we show that even at near-IR and IR wavelengths, the presence of these layers leads to significant changes in the transmission spectrum of the planetary atmosphere. Accounting for these stellar outer layers thus improves the atmospheric fit of HAT-P-18 b and increases its best-fit atmospheric temperature from <inline-formula> </inline-formula> K to <inline-formula> </inline-formula> K, a value much closer to the predicted equilibrium temperature of ∼852 K. Our analysis also decreases the best-fit abundance of CO<SUB>2</SUB> by almost an order of magnitude. The approach provides a new window to the properties of chromospheres/corona in stars other than our Sun.

[4] 恒星色球层和日冕对系外行星透射光谱的影响

系外行星大气透射光谱中的一个主要偏差来源是宿主恒星的磁活动，如恒星黑子、亮斑或耀斑。然而，主序星具有色球层和日冕，这些光学上稀薄的层由谱线发射主导，这一事实可能会改变对行星光谱的整体解释，但这一点在很大程度上被忽视了。利用JWST NIRISS/SOSS对热木星HAT-P-18 b的数据集，我们展示了即使在近红外和红外波段，这些层的存在也会导致行星大气透射光谱发生显著变化。因此，考虑这些恒星外层可以改善HAT-P-18 b的大气拟合，并将其最佳拟合大气温度从<inline-formula> </inline-formula> K提高到<inline-formula> </inline-formula> K，这一数值更接近预测的平衡温度约852 K。我们的分析还将CO2的最佳拟合丰度降低了近一个数量级。这一方法为研究除太阳以外恒星的色球层/日冕性质提供了新的窗口。

[5] 3D Biases in General Circulation Models of HD189733 b and Comparisons to MIRI Secondary Eclipse Observations

2025-01-00

The study of exoplanet atmospheres has evolved over time from 1-dimensional (1D) radiative transfer models to incredibly complex and computationally intensive three-dimensional (3D) general circulation models (GCMs). Both 1D forward and retrieval models remain popular because of their simplicity which directly translates to less computational requirements and faster modeling. However, previous studies have modeled detectable, explicitly 3D phenomena in transmission spectroscopy of exoplanet atmospheres due to day-night temperature gradients (Caldas et al., 2019) and chemical species distribution (Pluriel et al. 2020, Wardenier et al., 2023). These 3D phenomena will become increasingly necessary to consider for both retrieval models and forward model predictions of exoplanet spectroscopy. <P />We use a general circulation model (GCM), the SPARC/MITgcm (Showman et al., 2009), to model the atmosphere of the canonical hot Jupiter HD189733b assuming an atmospheric metallicity of 1x solar and 5x solar. We then use the Planetary Intensity Code for Atmospheric Scattering Observations (PICASO; Batalha et al. 2019) to produce thermal emission spectra from the 1D spatial averages of the planet's dayside and from the dayside 3D grid. The spectra are then convolved to the MIRI/LRS bandpass for comparison with James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) observations recently reported by Inglis et al. (2024) who detected quartz clouds in HD189733b's atmosphere. <P />We find noticeable differences in the simulated emission spectrum for a 5x solar GCM when a one-dimensional (1D) spatially averaged pressure-temperature (PT) profile is used as opposed to the full-3D grid. The 1x solar spectra show good agreement between 1D and 3D, but the full-3D spectrum of the 5X solar model has an overall higher total temperature than the 1D-averaged spectrum and, due to less efficient heat redistribution, this heat is concentrated closer to the center of the apparent disk of the planet. When the thermal emission flux is integrated across the disk of the planet, the central grid cells are weighted higher, increasing the contribution of these high-temperature regions to the overall spectrum and raising the total flux above the spectrum created from the 1D-averaged PT profile. In addition, we compare simulated spectra from clear-sky and cloudy scenarios to recent JWST observations to analyze the implications for the possible 3D structure and composition of HD189733b's atmosphere. Cloudy scenarios fit the data much more accurately, especially around 8.7 microns, supporting the quartz cloud discovery by Inglis et al. (2024).

[5] HD189733 b大气环流模型中的三维偏差及与MIRI次生凌星观测的比较

系外行星大气的研究经历了从一维（1D）辐射传输模型到极其复杂且计算量巨大的三维（3D）大气环流模型（GCM）的发展。由于其简单性，一维前向和检索模型仍然流行，计算需求低、建模速度快。然而，已有研究表明，日夜温度梯度（Caldas et al., 2019）和化学物种分布（Pluriel et al. 2020, Wardenier et al., 2023）等可探测的、明确的三维现象会影响系外行星大气的透射光谱。随着观测精度提升，这些三维现象对于检索模型和前向模型预测都将变得越来越重要。 我们使用通用环流模型（GCM）SPARC/MITgcm（Showman et al., 2009）对典型热木星HD189733b的大气进行建模，假设大气金属丰度为1倍和5倍太阳。随后，利用PICASO（Batalha et al. 2019）从行星日面的一维空间平均和日面三维网格生成热发射光谱。将这些光谱卷积到MIRI/LRS波段，以便与Inglis等（2024）最近报道的JWST观测（探测到HD189733b大气中的石英云）进行比较。 我们发现，对于5倍太阳金属丰度的GCM模型，采用一维空间平均压强-温度（PT）剖面与采用完整三维网格时，模拟发射光谱存在明显差异。1倍太阳模型下，一维与三维光谱吻合良好，但5倍太阳模型的完整三维光谱整体温度高于一维平均光谱，并且由于热量再分布效率较低，热量更集中于行星视盘中心。当将热发射通量在行星盘面上积分时，中心网格单元权重更高，这些高温区域对整体光谱的贡献增加，使总通量高于一维平均PT剖面生成的光谱。此外，我们将无云和有云情景下的模拟光谱与最新JWST观测进行比较，分析HD189733b大气可能的三维结构和成分。尤其在8.7微米附近，有云情景与观测数据更为吻合，支持Inglis等（2024）关于石英云的发现。

[6] A Six Year, Low-resolution, Multibroadband Transit Photometry Study of HD 189733b

2023-01-00

Transmission spectroscopy offers an invaluable opportunity to characterize the atmospheres of exoplanets. We present new ground-based optical transmission spectra of the hot Jupiter HD 189733b, derived from nine transits observed over a six year time span (2016-2021) using near-simultaneous $u^{\prime} g^{\prime} r^{\prime} i^{\prime} $ broadband observations. We achieve an average (best) precision of 435 (280) ppm by implementing an optical diffuser on the prime focus spectrograph from the 2.3 m Wyoming Infrared Observatory telescope. The data provide new measurements of the apparent planetary radius with respect to the stellar radius, the spectral index of atmospheric opacity, and the time variability of the two quantities. Our results indicate an enhanced spectral slope in the optical regime ≍2.4 times steeper than would be expected from canonical Rayleigh scattering and that is consistent with earlier measurements of a super-Rayleigh slope (SRS). While the effect of stellar activity on the transmission spectrum complicates the measurement of the spectral slope, our multiepoch data set over six years can measure and average over stellar variations, yielding a mean spectral index of -9.9 ± 4.4. The 1200 K equilibrium temperature of HD 189733b places it in a sweet spot for the formation of SRSs and is consistent with vigorously mixing hazes in the atmosphere. Additionally, we find variations in the depth of the lightcurve during two of the transits, explainable as an increase in occulted star spots during June 2021. Although the star is active, the mean level of stellar activity does not seem to vary dramatically over our six years of observations, leading us to conclude that the variability in stellar activity is modest at most.

[6] HD 189733b六年低分辨率多波段凌日光度研究

透射光谱为研究系外行星大气提供了宝贵机会。我们报告了热木星 HD 189733b 的新地基光学透射光谱，这些数据来自 2016-2021 年间九次凌日观测，采用近同时的 $u^{\prime} g^{\prime} r^{\prime} i^{\prime}$ 宽带观测获得。通过在怀俄明红外天文台 2.3 m 望远镜主焦点光谱仪上使用光学扩散器，我们实现了平均（最佳）435（280）ppm 的精度。数据提供了关于行星表观半径与恒星半径之比、大气不透明度光谱指数及其时间变化的新测量。我们的结果显示，在光学波段的光谱斜率增强，约为标准瑞利散射预期的 2.4 倍，与此前超瑞利斜率（SRS）的测量一致。尽管恒星活动对透射光谱的光谱斜率测量带来复杂性，我们的六年多历元数据集能够测量并平均恒星变化，得到平均光谱指数为 -9.9 ± 4.4。HD 189733b 的平衡温度为 1200 K，正处于 SRS 形成的最佳区间，并与大气中强烈混合的薄雾一致。此外，我们发现两次凌日的光变曲线深度存在变化，可解释为 2021 年 6 月期间被遮挡的恒星黑子增加。尽管该恒星活跃，但我们六年观测期间恒星活动的平均水平并未显著变化，因此我们认为恒星活动的变化幅度至多是适中的。

[7] Assessing Robustness and Bias in 1D Retrievals of 3D Global Circulation Models at High Spectral Resolution: A WASP-76 b Simulation Case Study in Emission

2025-09-00

High-resolution spectroscopy (HRS) of exoplanet atmospheres has successfully detected many chemical species and is quickly moving toward detailed characterization of the chemical abundances and dynamics. HRS is highly sensitive to the line shape and position; thus, it can detect three-dimensional (3D) effects such as winds, rotation, and spatial variation of atmospheric conditions. At the same time, retrieval frameworks are increasingly deployed to constrain chemical abundances, pressure─temperature (P─T) structures, orbital parameters, and rotational broadening. To explore the multidimensional parameter space, we need computationally fast models, which are consequently mostly one-dimensional (1D). However, this approach risks introducing interpretation bias since the planet's true nature is 3D. We investigate the robustness of this methodology at high spectral resolution by running 1D retrievals on simulated observations in emission within an observational framework using 3D global circulation models of the quintessential HJ WASP-76 b. We find that the retrieval broadly recovers conditions present in the atmosphere, but that the retrieved P─T and chemical profiles are not a homogeneous average of all spatial and phase-dependent information. Instead, they are most sensitive to spatial regions with large thermal gradients, which do not necessarily coincide with the strongest emitting regions. Our results further suggest that the choice of parameterization for the P─T and chemical profiles, as well as Doppler offsets among opacity sources, impact the retrieval results. These factors should be carefully considered in future retrieval analyses.

[7] 在高光谱分辨率下评估三维GCM的一维检索的稳健性与偏差：WASP-76 b发射模拟案例研究

系外行星大气的高分辨率光谱（HRS）已成功探测到多种化学物质，并正迅速向化学丰度和动力学的详细刻画迈进。HRS对谱线形状和位置高度敏感，因此能够探测三维（3D）效应，如风、旋转和大气条件的空间变化。同时，检索框架越来越多地被用于约束化学丰度、压强─温度（P─T）结构、轨道参数和旋转展宽。为了探索多维参数空间，我们需要计算速度快的模型，因此目前大多为一维（1D）模型。然而，这种方法有引入解释偏差的风险，因为行星的真实属性是三维的。我们通过在观测框架下，利用典型热木星WASP-76 b的三维全球环流模型，对模拟发射观测进行一维检索，研究该方法在高光谱分辨率下的稳健性。我们发现，检索大致能够恢复大气中存在的条件，但所检索到的P─T和化学剖面并不是所有空间和相位相关信息的均匀平均。相反，它们对具有大热梯度的空间区域最为敏感，而这些区域不一定与最强发射区重合。我们的结果进一步表明，P─T和化学剖面的参数化选择，以及不透明度源之间的多普勒偏移，会影响检索结果。这些因素在未来的检索分析中应予以仔细考虑。

[8] Correcting Exoplanet Transmission Spectra for Stellar Activity with an Optimized Retrieval Framework

2024-01-00

The chromatic contamination that arises from photospheric heterogeneities, e.g., spots and faculae on the host star presents a significant noise source for exoplanet transmission spectra. If this contamination is not corrected for, it can introduce substantial bias in our analysis of the planetary atmosphere. We utilize two stellar models of differing complexity, StARPA (Stellar Activity Removal for Planetary Atmospheres) and ASteRA (Active Stellar Retrieval Algorithm), to explore the biases introduced by stellar contamination in retrieval under differing degrees of stellar activity. We use the retrieval framework TauREx3 and a grid of 27 synthetic, spot-contaminated transmission spectra to investigate potential biases and to determine how complex our stellar models must be in order to accurately extract the planetary parameters from transmission spectra. The input observation is generated using the more complex model (StARPA), in which the spot latitude is an additional, fixable parameter. This observation is then fed into a combined stellar-planetary retrieval, which contains a simplified stellar model (ASteRA). Our results confirm that the inclusion of stellar activity parameters in retrieval minimizes bias under all activity regimes considered. ASteRA performs very well under low-to-moderate activity conditions, retrieving the planetary parameters with a high degree of accuracy. For the most active cases, characterized by larger, higher-temperature contrast spots, some minor residual bias remains due to ASteRA neglecting the interplay between the spot and the limb-darkening effect. As a result of this, we find larger errors in retrieved planetary parameters for central spots (0°) and those found close to the limb (60°) than those at intermediate latitudes (30°).

[8] 利用优化检索框架校正系外行星透射光谱中的恒星活动影响

由主星光球不均匀性（如黑子和亮斑）引起的色散污染，是系外行星透射光谱中的重要噪声源。如果不加以校正，这种污染会对行星大气分析引入显著偏差。我们利用两种复杂度不同的恒星模型——StARPA（Stellar Activity Removal for Planetary Atmospheres）和ASteRA（Active Stellar Retrieval Algorithm），探讨在不同恒星活动水平下，恒星污染对检索结果的偏差影响。我们采用TauREx3检索框架和27组合成的黑子污染透射光谱网格，研究潜在偏差，并确定恒星模型的复杂度需求，以便从透射光谱中准确提取行星参数。输入观测由更复杂的StARPA模型生成，其中黑子纬度为可固定参数。随后将该观测输入包含简化恒星模型（ASteRA）的恒星-行星联合检索。结果证实，在所有活动水平下，检索中引入恒星活动参数都能最小化偏差。ASteRA在低至中等活动条件下表现良好，能高精度检索行星参数。对于最活跃的情况（即黑子面积大、温度反差高），由于ASteRA忽略了黑子与边缘变暗效应的相互作用，仍存在轻微残余偏差。因此，中心黑子（0°）和靠近边缘（60°）的黑子所检索的行星参数误差大于中间纬度（30°）的情况。

8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] HST and TESS Point to a Highly Spotted Photosphere for HD189733A

2024-08-00

Transmission spectra can be affected by stellar photospheric heterogeneities that alter transit depths. The HD189733 system provides a clear example of this challenge, with features variously interpreted as signatures of scattering by haze in the planetary atmosphere or unocculted starspots. Here, we use three Hubble datasets to directly infer the spot covering fraction of HD189733A and explore the evidence for photospheric heterogeneity in the out-of-transit spectra. We model the stellar spectrum using 1─3 spectral components, finding that a two-component model (photosphere and spot) is preferred for all datasets. We find photospheric and spot temperatures of ~5295 K and ~3222 K, respectively, which are consistent across datasets. The spot covering fraction is large and varies between ~38% and ~47%. Combined with time-domain insights from TESS data revealing HD189733A's 1.4% peak-to-peak variability, our findings imply that the majority of the spots must be distributed axisymmetrically, e.g., in a densely filled latitudinal band or at the poles. This work underscores the importance of considering axisymmetric spot distributions in analyses of transmission spectra and the unique role that TESS plays in this effort.

[1] HST和TESS揭示HD189733A具有高度斑点的光球层

透射光谱可能受到恒星光球层非均匀性的影响，从而改变凌星深度。HD189733 系统就是这一挑战的典型例子，其特征曾被分别解释为行星大气中薄雾散射或未被遮挡的恒星黑子。在本研究中，我们利用三组哈勃数据直接推断 HD189733A 的黑子覆盖率，并探讨凌星外光谱中光球层非均匀性的证据。我们采用 1─3 个光谱分量对恒星光谱建模，发现所有数据集均更倾向于两分量模型（光球层和黑子）。我们得到的光球层和黑子温度分别约为 5295 K 和 3222 K，且在各数据集间一致。黑子覆盖率较高，变化范围约为 38% 至 47%。结合 TESS 数据揭示的 HD189733A 1.4% 峰-峰变化，我们的结果表明大多数黑子必须轴对称分布，例如密集分布于某一纬度带或极区。本工作强调了在透射光谱分析中考虑轴对称黑子分布的重要性，以及 TESS 在该领域的独特作用。

[2] 3D Biases in General Circulation Models of HD189733 b and Comparisons to MIRI Secondary Eclipse Observations

2025-01-00

The study of exoplanet atmospheres has evolved over time from 1-dimensional (1D) radiative transfer models to incredibly complex and computationally intensive three-dimensional (3D) general circulation models (GCMs). Both 1D forward and retrieval models remain popular because of their simplicity which directly translates to less computational requirements and faster modeling. However, previous studies have modeled detectable, explicitly 3D phenomena in transmission spectroscopy of exoplanet atmospheres due to day-night temperature gradients (Caldas et al., 2019) and chemical species distribution (Pluriel et al. 2020, Wardenier et al., 2023). These 3D phenomena will become increasingly necessary to consider for both retrieval models and forward model predictions of exoplanet spectroscopy. <P />We use a general circulation model (GCM), the SPARC/MITgcm (Showman et al., 2009), to model the atmosphere of the canonical hot Jupiter HD189733b assuming an atmospheric metallicity of 1x solar and 5x solar. We then use the Planetary Intensity Code for Atmospheric Scattering Observations (PICASO; Batalha et al. 2019) to produce thermal emission spectra from the 1D spatial averages of the planet's dayside and from the dayside 3D grid. The spectra are then convolved to the MIRI/LRS bandpass for comparison with James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) observations recently reported by Inglis et al. (2024) who detected quartz clouds in HD189733b's atmosphere. <P />We find noticeable differences in the simulated emission spectrum for a 5x solar GCM when a one-dimensional (1D) spatially averaged pressure-temperature (PT) profile is used as opposed to the full-3D grid. The 1x solar spectra show good agreement between 1D and 3D, but the full-3D spectrum of the 5X solar model has an overall higher total temperature than the 1D-averaged spectrum and, due to less efficient heat redistribution, this heat is concentrated closer to the center of the apparent disk of the planet. When the thermal emission flux is integrated across the disk of the planet, the central grid cells are weighted higher, increasing the contribution of these high-temperature regions to the overall spectrum and raising the total flux above the spectrum created from the 1D-averaged PT profile. In addition, we compare simulated spectra from clear-sky and cloudy scenarios to recent JWST observations to analyze the implications for the possible 3D structure and composition of HD189733b's atmosphere. Cloudy scenarios fit the data much more accurately, especially around 8.7 microns, supporting the quartz cloud discovery by Inglis et al. (2024).

[2] HD189733 b大气环流模型中的三维偏差及与MIRI次生凌星观测的比较

系外行星大气的研究经历了从一维（1D）辐射传输模型到极其复杂且计算量巨大的三维（3D）大气环流模型（GCM）的发展。由于其简单性，一维前向和检索模型仍然流行，计算需求低、建模速度快。然而，已有研究表明，日夜温度梯度（Caldas et al., 2019）和化学物种分布（Pluriel et al. 2020, Wardenier et al., 2023）等可探测的、明确的三维现象会影响系外行星大气的透射光谱。随着观测精度提升，这些三维现象对于检索模型和前向模型预测都将变得越来越重要。 我们使用通用环流模型（GCM）SPARC/MITgcm（Showman et al., 2009）对典型热木星HD189733b的大气进行建模，假设大气金属丰度为1倍和5倍太阳。随后，利用PICASO（Batalha et al. 2019）从行星日面的一维空间平均和日面三维网格生成热发射光谱。将这些光谱卷积到MIRI/LRS波段，以便与Inglis等（2024）最近报道的JWST观测（探测到HD189733b大气中的石英云）进行比较。 我们发现，对于5倍太阳金属丰度的GCM模型，采用一维空间平均压强-温度（PT）剖面与采用完整三维网格时，模拟发射光谱存在明显差异。1倍太阳模型下，一维与三维光谱吻合良好，但5倍太阳模型的完整三维光谱整体温度高于一维平均光谱，并且由于热量再分布效率较低，热量更集中于行星视盘中心。当将热发射通量在行星盘面上积分时，中心网格单元权重更高，这些高温区域对整体光谱的贡献增加，使总通量高于一维平均PT剖面生成的光谱。此外，我们将无云和有云情景下的模拟光谱与最新JWST观测进行比较，分析HD189733b大气可能的三维结构和成分。尤其在8.7微米附近，有云情景与观测数据更为吻合，支持Inglis等（2024）关于石英云的发现。

[3] The Hubble/STIS near-ultraviolet transmission spectrum of HD 189733 b

2023-03-00

The benchmark hot Jupiter HD 189733 b has been a key target, used to lay out the foundations of comparative planetology for giant exo-planets. As such, HD 189733 b has been extensively studied across the electromagnetic spectrum. Here we report the observation and analysis of three transit light curves of HD 189733 b obtained with Hubble/STIS in the near-ultraviolet (NUV), the last remaining unexplored spectral window to be probed with present-day instrumentation for this planet. The NUV is a unique window for atmospheric mass-loss studies owing to the strong resonance lines and large photospheric flux. Overall, from a low-resolution analysis (R = 50) we found that the planet's NUV spectrum is well characterized by a relatively flat baseline, consistent with the optical-infrared transmission, plus two regions at ~2350 and ~2600 Å that exhibit a broad and significant excess absorption above the continuum. From an analysis at a higher resolution (R = 4700), we found that the transit depths at the core of the magnesium resonance lines are consistent with the surrounding continuum. We discarded the presence of Mg II absorption in the upper atmosphere at a ~2-4σ confidence level, whereas we could place no significant constraint for Mg I absorption. These broad absorption features coincide with the expected location of Fe II bands; however, solar-abundance hydrodynamic models of the upper atmosphere are not able to reproduce the amplitude of these features with iron absorption. This scenario would require a combination of little to no iron condensation in the lower atmosphere super-solar metallicities and a mechanism to enhance the absorption features (such as zonal wind broadening). The true nature of this feature remains to be confirmed.

[3] HD 189733 b的哈勃/STIS近紫外透射光谱

基准热木星HD 189733 b一直是巨型系外行星比较行星学研究的关键目标，因此在整个电磁波段都得到了广泛研究。本文报告了利用Hubble/STIS在近紫外（NUV）波段获得的HD 189733 b三次凌日光变观测与分析，这是目前仪器条件下该行星尚未探索的最后一个光谱窗口。NUV波段因其强烈的共振线和高光球通量，是研究大气逃逸的独特窗口。总体而言，低分辨率分析（R = 50）显示，该行星的NUV光谱表现为相对平坦的基线，与光学-红外透射一致，但在约2350和2600 Å两个区域出现了宽且显著的超出连续谱的吸收增强。高分辨率分析（R = 4700）显示，镁共振线中心的凌日深度与周围连续谱一致。我们以约2-4σ置信度排除了上层大气中Mg II吸收的存在，而对Mg I吸收则无法给出显著约束。这些宽吸收特征与Fe II带的预期位置一致，然而，太阳丰度的上层大气流体动力学模型无法用铁吸收再现这些特征的幅度。这种情形需要下层大气中铁凝结极少或不存在、金属丰度超太阳，以及某种增强吸收特征的机制（如纬向风展宽）。该特征的真实本质仍有待确认。

[4] Retrieved atmospheric properties of the sub-stellar object VHS 1256 b with HST, VLT, and JWST spectra

2024-10-00

Motivated by the observed ~30% variations in flux from the L7 dwarf VHS 1256 b, we subjected its time-resolved Hubble Space Telescope (HST) WFC3 spectra (measured in two epochs: 2018 and 2020), as well as medium-resolution Very Large Telescope (VLT) X-shooter and Early Release Science James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) spectra to a suite of both standard Bayesian (nested sampling) and machine-learning (random forest) retrievals. We find that both HST and VLT data require vertically varying abundance profiles of water in order to model the spectra accurately. Despite the large flux variations observed in the HST data, the temporal variability cannot be attributed to a single varying atmospheric property. The retrieved atmospheric quantities are consistent with being invariant across time. However, we find that model grids offer relatively poor fits to the measured HST spectra and are unsuitable for quantifying the temporal variability of atmospheric properties. Additionally, our analysis of JWST spectra using model grids indicates consistency in terms of the retrieved properties across different wavelength channels. Despite the temporal variability in flux, the retrieved properties between HST and VLT, as well those as between HST and JWST, are consistent within the respective posterior uncertainties. Such an outcome bodes well for future retrieval analyses of exoplanetary atmospheres, which are expected to exhibit weaker flux variations.

[4] 利用HST、VLT和JWST光谱对亚恒星天体VHS 1256 b的大气性质进行检索

受 L7 矮星 VHS 1256 b 观测到的约 30% 通量变化的启发，我们对其 Hubble Space Telescope (HST) WFC3 时序光谱（2018 年和 2020 年两个历元）、中等分辨率 Very Large Telescope (VLT) X-shooter 以及 Early Release Science James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) 光谱进行了标准贝叶斯（嵌套采样）和机器学习（随机森林）检索分析。我们发现，无论是 HST 还是 VLT 数据，都需要水的垂直丰度分布变化才能准确拟合光谱。尽管 HST 数据中观测到较大的通量变化，但这种时间变化不能归因于单一的大气性质变化。检索得到的大气参数在时间上是一致的。然而，我们发现模型网格对 HST 光谱的拟合较差，不适合量化大气性质的时间变化。此外，我们对 JWST 光谱的模型网格分析显示，不同波段通道检索结果一致。尽管存在通量的时间变化，HST 与 VLT 之间以及 HST 与 JWST 之间的检索结果在各自后验不确定度范围内是一致的。这一结果对未来系外行星大气的检索分析具有积极意义，预计这些大气的通量变化会更弱。

[5] Parameter degeneracies associated with interpreting HST WFC3 transmission spectra of exoplanetary atmospheres

2025-04-00

The Wide Field Camera 3 (WFC3) instrument on the Hubble Space Telescope has provided an abundance of exoplanet spectra over the years. These spectra have enabled analysis studies using atmospheric retrievals to constrain the properties of these objects. However, follow-up observations from the JWST have called into question some of the results from these older datasets, and highlighted the need to properly understand the degeneracies associated with retrievals of WFC3 spectra. In this study, we perform atmospheric retrievals of 38 transmission spectra from WFC3 and use model comparison to determine the complexity required to fit the data. We explore the effect of retrieving system parameters such as the stellar radius and planet's surface gravity, and thoroughly investigate the degeneracies between individual model parameters - specifically the temperature, abundance of water, and cloud-top level. We focus on three case studies (HD 209458b, WASP-12b, and WASP-39b) in an attempt to diagnose some of the issues with these retrievals, in particular the low retrieved temperatures when compared to the equilibrium values. Our study advocates for the careful consideration of parameter degeneracies when interpreting retrieval results, as well as the importance of wider wavelength coverage to break these degeneracies, in agreement with previous studies. The combination of data from multiple instruments, as well as analysis from multiple data reductions and retrieval codes, will allow us to robustly characterize the atmosphere of these exoplanets.

[5] 解释系外行星大气HST WFC3透射光谱时的参数简并性

哈勃空间望远镜上的宽视场相机3（WFC3）仪器多年来提供了大量系外行星光谱。这些光谱使得通过大气检索分析这些天体的性质成为可能。然而，JWST的后续观测对这些早期数据集的一些结果提出了质疑，并强调了充分理解WFC3光谱检索相关参数退化的重要性。在本研究中，我们对38个WFC3透射光谱进行了大气检索，并通过模型比较确定拟合数据所需的模型复杂度。我们探讨了检索系统参数（如恒星半径和行星表面重力）的影响，并详细研究了各模型参数之间的退化关系，特别是温度、水丰度和云顶高度。我们重点分析了三个案例（HD 209458b、WASP-12b和WASP-39b），试图诊断这些检索中存在的问题，尤其是检索到的温度低于平衡温度的情况。我们的研究建议在解释检索结果时需谨慎考虑参数退化，并强调拓宽波长覆盖范围以打破这些退化的重要性，这与先前研究一致。多仪器数据的结合，以及多种数据处理和检索代码的分析，将有助于我们对这些系外行星大气进行稳健表征。

[6] A Formally Motivated Retrieval Framework Applied to the High-resolution Transmission Spectrum of HD 189733 b

2024-04-00

Ground-based high-resolution spectra provide a powerful tool for characterizing exoplanet atmospheres. However, they are greatly hampered by the dominating telluric and stellar lines, which need to be removed prior to any analysis. Such removal techniques ("preparing pipelines") deform the spectrum; hence, a key point is to account for this process in the forward models used in retrievals. We develop a formal derivation on how to prepare froward models for retrievals, in the case where the telluric and instrumental deformations can be represented as a matrix multiplied element-wise with the data. We also introduce the notion of a "bias pipeline metric," which can be used to compare the bias potential of preparing pipelines. We use the resulting framework to retrieve simulated observations of 1D and 3D exoplanet atmospheres and to reanalyze high-resolution ( ${ \mathcal R }\approx {\rm{80,400}}$ ) near-infrared (0.96─1.71 μm) CARMENES transit data of HD 189733 b. We compare these results with those obtained from a cross-correlation function analysis. With our fiducial retrieval, we find a blueshift of the absorption features of $-{5.51}_{-0.53}^{+0.66}$ km s<SUP>−1</SUP>. In addition, we retrieve a H<SUB>2</SUB>O log<SUB>10</SUB>(VMR) of $-{2.39}_{-0.16}^{+0.12}$ and a temperature of ${660}_{-11}^{+6}$ K. We are also able to put upper limits for the abundances of CH<SUB>4</SUB>, CO, H<SUB>2</SUB>S, HCN, and NH<SUB>3</SUB>, consistent with a subsolar-metallicity atmosphere enriched in H<SUB>2</SUB>O. We retrieve a broadened line shape, consistent with rotation- and wind-induced line broadening. Finally, we find a lower limit for the pressure of an opaque cloud consistent with a clear atmosphere, and we find no evidence for hazes.

[6] 应用于HD 189733 b高分辨率透射光谱的形式动机检索框架

地基高分辨率光谱为系外行星大气特性研究提供了有力工具。然而，强烈的地球大气和恒星谱线极大地影响了观测，需在分析前去除。这类去除技术（“预处理流程”）会改变光谱，因此在检索所用的前向模型中考虑这一过程至关重要。我们对在地球大气和仪器畸变可表示为与数据逐元素相乘的矩阵时，如何为检索准备前向模型进行了形式化推导。我们还引入了“偏差流程度量”概念，可用于比较不同预处理流程的偏差潜力。我们用该框架对 1D 和 3D 系外行星大气的模拟观测进行了检索，并重新分析了 HD 189733 b 的高分辨率（${ \mathcal R }\approx {\rm{80,400}}$）近红外（0.96─1.71 μm）CARMENES 凌日数据。我们将这些结果与互相关函数分析得到的结果进行了比较。在我们的基准检索中，发现吸收特征存在 $-{5.51}_{-0.53}^{+0.66}$ km s−1 的蓝移。此外，我们检索到 H2O 的 log10(VMR) 为 $-{2.39}_{-0.16}^{+0.12}$，温度为 ${660}_{-11}^{+6}$ K。我们还对 CH4、CO、H2S、HCN 和 NH3 的丰度给出了上限，结果与富含 H2O 的亚太阳金属丰度大气一致。我们检索到展宽的谱线形状，与自转和风致展宽一致。最后，我们对不透明云的压力下限进行了约束，结果与无云大气一致，未发现薄雾证据。

[7] Axisymmetric High Spot Coverage on Exoplanet Host HD 189733 A

2024-03-00

Transmission spectroscopy is a powerful tool to study exoplanet atmospheres, which can be affected by the ability of stellar photospheric heterogeneity to mimic or mask exoplanetary spectral signatures. The canonical HD 189733 system provides a textbook example of this spectroscopic discrepancy with features that have been variously interpreted as signatures of scattering by haze in the planetary atmosphere or unocculted spots on the stellar disk. Here, we leverage three archival data sets from the Hubble Space Telescope to directly infer the covering fraction of HD 189733 A and explore the evidence for photospheric heterogeneity in the out-of-transit spectra. We model the stellar spectrum using one to three spectral components in a nested-sampling framework, finding that the two-component model (photosphere and spot) is preferred for all data sets. We find photospheric and spot temperatures of $5295{\pm }_{41}^{43}$ and $3222{\pm }_{116}^{100}$ K, respectively, which are consistent across data sets. The spot covering fraction is large and varies between 38% ± 4% and 47% ± 3%. Combined with time-domain insights from Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite data revealing HD 189733 A's 1.4% peak-to-peak variability, our findings imply that the majority of the spots must be distributed axisymmetrically, e.g., in a densely filled latitudinal band or at the poles. More work with complementary data sets is necessary to investigate those possible arrangements.

[8] Near-infrared transmission spectroscopy of HAT-P-18 b with NIRISS: Disentangling planetary and stellar features in the era of JWST

2024-02-00

The JWST Early Release Observations (ERO) included a NIRISS/SOSS (0.6-2.8 μm) transit of the ~ 850 K Saturn-mass exoplanet HAT-P-18 b. Initial analysis of these data reported detections of water, escaping helium and haze. However, active K dwarfs like HAT-P-18 possess surface heterogeneities - star-spots and faculae - that can complicate the interpretation of transmission spectra, and indeed, a spot-crossing event is present in HAT-P-18 b's NIRISS/SOSS light curves. Here, we present an extensive reanalysis and interpretation of the JWST ERO transmission spectrum of HAT-P-18 b, as well as HST/WFC3 and Spitzer/IRAC transit observations. We detect H<SUB>2</SUB>O (12.5σ), CO<SUB>2</SUB> (7.3σ), a cloud deck (7.4σ), and unocculted star-spots (5.8σ), alongside hints of Na (2.7σ). We do not detect the previously reported CH<SUB>4</SUB> (log CH<SUB>4</SUB> &lt; -6 to 2σ). We obtain excellent agreement between three independent retrieval codes, which find a sub-solar H<SUB>2</SUB>O abundance (log H<SUB>2</SUB>O ≈ -4.4 ± 0.3). However, the inferred CO<SUB>2</SUB> abundance (log CO<SUB>2</SUB> ≈ -4.8 ± 0.4) is significantly super-solar and requires further investigation into its origin. We also introduce new stellar heterogeneity considerations by fitting for the active regions' surface gravities - a proxy for the effects of magnetic pressure. Finally, we compare our JWST inferences to those from HST/WFC3 and Spitzer/IRAC. Our results highlight the exceptional promise of simultaneous planetary atmosphere and stellar heterogeneity constraints in the era of JWST and demonstrate that JWST transmission spectra may warrant more complex treatments of the transit light source effect.

[8] 利用NIRISS对HAT-P-18 b的近红外透射光谱观测：在JWST时代区分行星与恒星特征

JWST早期发布观测（ERO）包括了对约850 K土星质量系外行星HAT-P-18 b的NIRISS/SOSS（0.6-2.8 μm）凌日观测。对这些数据的初步分析报告了水、逃逸氦和薄雾的探测。然而，像HAT-P-18这样的活跃K型矮星存在表面非均匀性——星斑和亮斑——这会使透射光谱的解释变得复杂，实际上，HAT-P-18 b的NIRISS/SOSS光变曲线中存在一次星斑穿越事件。我们在此对HAT-P-18 b的JWST ERO透射光谱以及HST/WFC3和Spitzer/IRAC凌日观测进行了详尽的再分析和解释。我们探测到H2O（12.5σ）、CO2（7.3σ）、云层（7.4σ）和未被遮挡的星斑（5.8σ），同时还发现Na的迹象（2.7σ）。我们未探测到先前报道的CH4（log CH4 < -6，2σ）。三套独立检索代码的结果高度一致，均得出亚太阳H2O丰度（log H2O ≈ -4.4 ± 0.3）。然而，推断的CO2丰度（log CO2 ≈ -4.8 ± 0.4）显著高于太阳值，需要进一步研究其成因。我们还通过拟合活跃区表面重力（磁压效应的代理）引入了新的恒星非均匀性考量。最后，我们将JWST推断结果与HST/WFC3和Spitzer/IRAC进行了对比。我们的结果强调了JWST时代同时约束行星大气和恒星非均匀性的巨大潜力，并表明JWST透射光谱可能需要对凌日光源效应进行更复杂的处理。

outcome — 这个问题后来怎么样了？ / what happened to the question?
premise_status — 它依赖的那个假设怎么样了？ / what happened to its underlying assumption?
confidence 信心度（1 没把握 / 2 一般 / 3 很有把握）
