item_083 · sys_D
Is the main result reported in "A Panchromatic Comparative View of Exoplanet Atmospheres" robust to independent reanalysis with different modeling assumptions?
“系外行星大气的多波段比较视角”一文报告的主要结果，在采用不同建模假设进行独立再分析时是否稳健？
8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] On the Use of Evidence and Goodness-of-fit Metrics in Exoplanet Atmosphere Interpretation

2021-11-00

In exoplanet atmosphere analyses, a suite of retrievals, with and without different chemical components, is often run, with forward models generated across their parameter space. I discuss here potential pitfalls in the interpretation of the statistics of such setups, suggesting a few simple tests to consider when interpreting their results.

[1] 在系外行星大气解释中证据与拟合优度指标的使用

在系外行星大气分析中，通常会运行一系列包含或不包含不同化学组分的检索，并在其参数空间内生成前向模型。本文讨论了此类设置下统计解释中可能存在的陷阱，并提出了解释结果时应考虑的一些简单测试。

[2] Methods for Incorporating Model Uncertainty into Exoplanet Atmospheric Analysis

2024-05-00

A key goal of exoplanet spectroscopy is to measure atmospheric properties, such as abundances of chemical species, in order to connect them to our understanding of atmospheric physics and planet formation. In this new era of high-quality JWST data, it is paramount that these measurement methods are robust. When comparing atmospheric models to observations, multiple candidate models may produce reasonable fits to the data. Typically, conclusions are reached by selecting the best-performing model according to some metric. This ignores model uncertainty in favor of specific model assumptions, potentially leading to measured atmospheric properties that are overconfident and/or incorrect. In this paper, we compare three ensemble methods for addressing model uncertainty by combining posterior distributions from multiple analyses: Bayesian model averaging, a variant of Bayesian model averaging using leave-one-out predictive densities, and stacking of predictive distributions. We demonstrate these methods by fitting the Hubble Space Telescope (HST) + Spitzer transmission spectrum of the hot Jupiter HD 209458b using models with different cloud and haze prescriptions. All of our ensemble methods lead to uncertainties on retrieved parameters that are larger but more realistic and consistent with physical and chemical expectations. Since they have not typically accounted for model uncertainty, uncertainties of retrieved parameters from HST spectra have likely been underreported. We recommend stacking as the most robust model combination method. Our methods can be used to combine results from independent retrieval codes and from different models within one code. They are also widely applicable to other exoplanet analysis processes, such as combining results from different data reductions.

[2] 将模型不确定性纳入系外行星大气分析的方法

系外行星光谱学的一个关键目标是测量大气属性（如化学组分丰度），以便将其与大气物理和行星形成理论联系起来。在高质量JWST数据时代，确保这些测量方法的稳健性至关重要。当将大气模型与观测数据进行比较时，多个候选模型可能都能较好地拟合数据。通常，结论是基于某种指标选择表现最佳的模型，这种做法忽略了模型不确定性，过度依赖特定模型假设，可能导致对大气属性的测量过于自信和/或不准确。本文比较了三种通过结合多次分析的后验分布来处理模型不确定性的集成方法：贝叶斯模型平均、基于留一预测密度的贝叶斯模型平均变体，以及预测分布叠加。我们以不同云和薄雾假设下的模型拟合热木星HD 209458b的Hubble Space Telescope (HST) + Spitzer透射光谱，演示了这些方法。所有集成方法都使检索参数的不确定性变大，但更为真实且符合物理和化学预期。由于以往很少考虑模型不确定性，HST光谱检索参数的不确定性很可能被低估。我们推荐叠加法作为最稳健的模型组合方法。我们的方法可用于结合不同检索代码的结果，也可用于同一代码下不同模型的结果整合。此外，这些方法还可广泛应用于其他系外行星分析流程，如不同数据处理结果的合并。

[3] Assessing Robustness and Bias in 1D Retrievals of 3D Global Circulation Models at High Spectral Resolution: A WASP-76 b Simulation Case Study in Emission

2025-09-00

High-resolution spectroscopy (HRS) of exoplanet atmospheres has successfully detected many chemical species and is quickly moving toward detailed characterization of the chemical abundances and dynamics. HRS is highly sensitive to the line shape and position; thus, it can detect three-dimensional (3D) effects such as winds, rotation, and spatial variation of atmospheric conditions. At the same time, retrieval frameworks are increasingly deployed to constrain chemical abundances, pressure─temperature (P─T) structures, orbital parameters, and rotational broadening. To explore the multidimensional parameter space, we need computationally fast models, which are consequently mostly one-dimensional (1D). However, this approach risks introducing interpretation bias since the planet's true nature is 3D. We investigate the robustness of this methodology at high spectral resolution by running 1D retrievals on simulated observations in emission within an observational framework using 3D global circulation models of the quintessential HJ WASP-76 b. We find that the retrieval broadly recovers conditions present in the atmosphere, but that the retrieved P─T and chemical profiles are not a homogeneous average of all spatial and phase-dependent information. Instead, they are most sensitive to spatial regions with large thermal gradients, which do not necessarily coincide with the strongest emitting regions. Our results further suggest that the choice of parameterization for the P─T and chemical profiles, as well as Doppler offsets among opacity sources, impact the retrieval results. These factors should be carefully considered in future retrieval analyses.

[3] 在高光谱分辨率下评估三维GCM的一维检索的稳健性与偏差：WASP-76 b发射模拟案例研究

系外行星大气的高分辨率光谱（HRS）已成功探测到多种化学物质，并正迅速向化学丰度和动力学的详细刻画迈进。HRS对谱线形状和位置高度敏感，因此能够探测三维（3D）效应，如风、旋转和大气条件的空间变化。同时，检索框架越来越多地被用于约束化学丰度、压强─温度（P─T）结构、轨道参数和旋转展宽。为了探索多维参数空间，我们需要计算速度快的模型，因此目前大多为一维（1D）模型。然而，这种方法有引入解释偏差的风险，因为行星的真实属性是三维的。我们通过在观测框架下，利用典型热木星WASP-76 b的三维全球环流模型，对模拟发射观测进行一维检索，研究该方法在高光谱分辨率下的稳健性。我们发现，检索大致能够恢复大气中存在的条件，但所检索到的P─T和化学剖面并不是所有空间和相位相关信息的均匀平均。相反，它们对具有大热梯度的空间区域最为敏感，而这些区域不一定与最强发射区重合。我们的结果进一步表明，P─T和化学剖面的参数化选择，以及不透明度源之间的多普勒偏移，会影响检索结果。这些因素在未来的检索分析中应予以仔细考虑。

[4] Self-Consistent Constraints for a Population of Hot Jupiters and Implications for Planet Formation and Climate

2022-06-00

Transit and eclipse spectroscopy offer insights into the chemical and radiative properties of extrasolar planet atmospheres, but inferring these properties from their spectra requires data-model comparisons. Bayesian retrieval methods are commonly used to derive atmosphere characteristics from models. Free retrievals make minimal assumptions allowing for flexible data-model fits and potentially illuminating unknown physics, but this also creates the possibility of inferring unphysical atmosphere characteristics. In this work, we combine powerful Bayesian parameter estimation tools with novel grids of self-consistent, one-dimensional, radiative-convective equilibrium models. These models are more restrictive than free retrievals, giving them more predictive power, while maintaining the computational speed that increasingly complex models lack. Using this framework, we determine metallicities, carbon-to-oxygen ratios, and heat redistributions for a population of hot Jupiters from their dayside thermal emission spectra. Our sample is derived from a uniform data-reduction analysis of Hubble Space Telescope WFC3 and Spitzer 3.6/4.5 micron observations sourced from a variety of programs. Results are presented in the context of current trend predictions in literature — metallicity and C/O closely relate to planetary formation theories, and heat redistribution has implications for climate. Finally, we present this grid modeling method as a tool for broad trend predictions that inform observations with JWST and ground-based high-resolution instruments.

[4] 热木星种群的自洽约束及其对行星形成和气候的影响

凌星和掩食光谱学为系外行星大气的化学和辐射特性提供了洞见，但要从光谱中推断这些特性需要数据与模型的对比。贝叶斯检索方法通常用于从模型中推导大气特性。自由检索假设最少，允许灵活的数据-模型拟合，并有可能揭示未知物理过程，但这也可能导致推断出不符合物理的大气特性。在本工作中，我们将强大的贝叶斯参数估计工具与新型自洽的一维辐射-对流平衡模型网格相结合。这些模型比自由检索更具约束性，因此具有更强的预测能力，同时保持了复杂模型所缺乏的计算速度。利用这一框架，我们根据热木星白昼面热发射光谱，确定了其金属丰度、碳氧比和热量再分布。我们的样本来自对哈勃空间望远镜WFC3和Spitzer 3.6/4.5微米观测数据的统一数据处理分析，数据来源于多个项目。结果在当前文献趋势预测的背景下进行展示——金属丰度和C/O与行星形成理论密切相关，热量再分布对气候有影响。最后，我们将这种网格建模方法作为一种工具，用于进行广泛的趋势预测，为JWST和地基高分辨率仪器的观测提供参考。

[5] Self-Consistent Constraints on Elemental Abundances and Heat Redistributions for a Population of Hot Jupiters

2021-01-00

Transit and eclipse spectroscopy offer insights into the chemical and radiative properties of extrasolar planets. Inferring said properties from their spectra necessarily requires data-model comparisons. Typically, Bayesian atmospheric retrieval methods have been used to derive these properties. Retrievals generally make minimal assumptions allowing for more flexibility in the fits and potentially illuminating unknown physics, but this also creates the possibility of unphysical atmospheric determinations. In this work, we combine powerful Bayesian parameter estimation tools with a new grid of self-consistent, one dimensional radiative-convective equilibrium models. Using this framework, we determine metallicities, carbon-to-oxygen ratios, and heat redistributions for a population of hot Jupiters from their dayside thermal emission spectra. Our sample is derived from a uniform data-reduction analysis of spatial scan Hubble Space Telescope WFC3 and Spitzer 3.6/4.5 micron observations sourced from a variety of programs. The self-consistent approach mitigates the possibility of unphysical solutions, resulting in robust atmospheric inferences. We will present our self-consistent constraints on these fundamental atmospheric quantities in the context of current retrieval results and compare to atmospheric composition predictions from planet formation theories. Our nominal model fits to each object will be extrapolated to the James Webb Space Telescope wavelengths, as well as high-resolution, cross-correlation instruments, in order to identify observational setups that best address any current degeneracies.

[5] 热木星种群的元素丰度与热量再分布的自洽约束

凌星和掩食光谱学为系外行星的化学和辐射特性提供了洞见。从光谱推断这些特性必然需要数据与模型的比较。通常，贝叶斯大气检索方法被用于推导这些属性。检索通常只做最小假设，从而在拟合中更具灵活性，并有可能揭示未知物理过程，但这也带来了得到非物理大气解的可能性。在本工作中，我们将强大的贝叶斯参数估计工具与一套新的自洽一维辐射-对流平衡模型网格结合。利用这一框架，我们根据热木星的日面热发射光谱，确定了其金属丰度、C/O比和热量再分布。我们的样本来自对Hubble Space Telescope WFC3和Spitzer 3.6/4.5微米观测数据的统一数据处理分析，这些数据源自多个项目。自洽方法减小了非物理解的可能性，从而获得稳健的大气推断。我们将在当前检索结果的背景下，展示对这些基本大气参数的自洽约束，并与行星形成理论对大气组成的预测进行比较。我们对每个目标的标称模型拟合将外推到James Webb Space Telescope波段，以及高分辨率交叉相关仪器，以确定最能解决当前简并性的观测方案。

[6] On the robustness of exoplanet atmospheric detections: insights from extensive simulations

2025-01-00

The classical picture of our Solar System being the archetypal outcome of planet formation has been rendered obsolete by the astonishing diversity of extrasolar-system architectures. From rare hot-Jupiters to abundant super-Earths and sub-Neptunes, most detected exoplanets have no analogs in our system, and their interior and atmospheric compositions remain largely unknown. Fortunately, new methodologies enable us to analyze exoplanet atmospheres, inferring their compositions, temperatures, dynamics, and even formation pathways. Specifically, ground-based high-resolution Doppler spectroscopy (HRDS) can disentangle spectral-line profiles of weak exo-atmospheric signals from the dominating features of Earth's atmosphere in the observed flux. For over a decade, HRDS has focused on hot Jupiters (close-orbiting gas giants) due to their high signal-to-noise ratio, which makes them ideal laboratories for advancing our knowledge. However, there have been concerns regarding potential biases in exo-atmospheric-detection methods, hindering comparative planetology. Here we propose a modeling framework based on extensive simulations of HRDS exo-atmospheric observations to systematically explore in-silico underlying biases in commonly-used pipelines, particularly under the presence of observational noise. Our findings show that exo-atmospheric detection-significances are highly contingent on details of the analysis-pipeline used, with different techniques responding differently to noise: A given technique may fail to recover a true-signal that is detected by another. Noise effects in the computed significances are non-trivial and pipeline-dependent. Statistical analyses provide a complementary tool to contextualize signal-significances, which will gain in relevance as we move towards studying the atmospheres of smaller, potentially habitable exoplanets with even weaker signals.

[6] 系外行星大气探测稳健性：来自大量模拟的见解

我们太阳系作为行星形成的典型结果的传统观点，已被系外行星系统结构的惊人多样性所取代。从罕见的热木星到大量的超级地球和亚海王星，目前探测到的大多数系外行星在我们的太阳系中都没有对应物，其内部和大气组成在很大程度上仍然未知。幸运的是，新的方法使我们能够分析系外行星大气，推断其组成、温度、动力学，甚至形成路径。具体而言，地基高分辨率多普勒光谱（HRDS）能够将微弱的系外大气信号的谱线轮廓与观测通量中主导的地球大气特征区分开来。十多年来，HRDS 主要聚焦于热木星（近轨道气体巨行星），因为它们具有较高的信噪比，是推进我们知识的理想实验室。然而，关于系外大气探测方法可能存在的偏差一直存在担忧，这阻碍了比较行星学的发展。本文提出了一种基于大量 HRDS 系外大气观测模拟的建模框架，系统性地探索常用数据处理流程在观测噪声存在下的潜在偏差。我们的研究发现，系外大气探测显著性高度依赖于所用分析流程的细节，不同技术对噪声的响应不同：某一技术可能无法恢复被另一技术探测到的真实信号。计算出的显著性中的噪声效应是非平凡且依赖于流程的。统计分析为信号显著性提供了补充工具，随着我们转向研究信号更弱的较小、潜在宜居系外行星的大气，这一工具的重要性将进一步提升。

[7] A re-analysis of equilibrium chemistry in five hot Jupiters

2023-09-00

<BR /> Aims: Studies of chemistry and chemical composition are fundamental to exploring the formation histories of planets and planetary systems. We propose having another look at five targets to better determine their composition and the chemical mechanisms taking place in their atmospheres. We present a re-analysis of five hot Jupiters, combining multiple instruments and using Bayesian retrieval methods. We compare different combinations of molecules present in the simulated atmosphere and various chemistry types, as well as a range of cloud parametrizations. Following up on recent studies questioning the detection of Na and K in the atmosphere of HD 209458b as being potentially contaminated by stellar lines (when present), we study the impact on other retrieval parameters that may lead to misinterpretations of the presence of these alkali species. <BR /> Methods: We used spatially scanned observations from the grisms G102 and G141 of the Wide Field Camera 3 (WFC3) on the Hubble Space Telescope, with a wavelength coverage of ~0.8 to ~1.7 microns. We analyzed these data with the publicly available Iraclis pipeline. We added data from Space Telescope Imaging Spectrograph (STIS) observations to increase our wavelength coverage from ~0.4 to ~1.7 µm. We then performed a Bayesian retrieval analysis with the open-source TauREx using a nested sampling algorithm. We carried out the retrieval, taking into account molecular abundances that vary freely and then with equilibrium chemistry. We explored the influence of including Na and K on the retrieval of the molecules from the atmosphere. <BR /> Results: Our data re-analysis and Bayesian retrieval are consistent with previous studies, but we do find small differences in the retrieved parameters. After all, Na and K have no significant impact on the properties of the planet atmospheres. Therefore, we present here our new best-fit models, taking into account molecular abundances that are allowed to vary freely as well as the equilibrium chemistry. This work is a preparation for a future addition of a more sophisticated representation of the chemistry involved, while taking into account disequilibrium effects such as vertical mixing and photochemistry.

[7] 对五颗热木星的平衡化学进行再分析

目标：化学及化学组成的研究对于探索行星及行星系统的形成历史至关重要。我们对五个目标进行了重新分析，以更好地确定它们的大气组成及其大气中发生的化学机制。我们结合多种仪器并采用贝叶斯检索方法，对五颗热木星进行了重新分析。我们比较了模拟大气中不同分子组合、不同化学类型以及多种云参数化方案。针对近期有研究质疑在HD 209458 b大气中探测到的Na和K可能受到恒星谱线（如存在时）污染的问题，我们研究了这种情况对其他检索参数的影响，这可能导致对这些碱金属存在性的误判。 方法：我们使用了哈勃空间望远镜宽视场相机3（WFC3）G102和G141棱镜的空间扫描观测，波长覆盖约0.8至1.7微米。我们利用公开的Iraclis流程分析了这些数据。我们还加入了空间望远镜成像光谱仪（STIS）的观测数据，将波长覆盖范围扩展到约0.4至1.7微米。随后，我们使用开源TauREx和嵌套采样算法进行了贝叶斯检索分析。我们分别在分子丰度自由变化和化学平衡条件下进行了检索。我们还探讨了在大气检索中包含Na和K对其他分子的影响。 结果：我们的数据重新分析和贝叶斯检索结果与先前研究一致，但在部分检索参数上发现了小的差异。总体而言，Na和K对行星大气性质没有显著影响。因此，我们在此给出新的最佳拟合模型，考虑了分子丰度自由变化和化学平衡两种情况。本工作为未来引入更复杂化学过程的表示做准备，同时考虑非平衡效应，如垂直混合和光化学。

[8] Methods for Incorporating Model Uncertainty into Exoplanet Atmospheric Analysis

2024-02-00

One of the main goals of transit spectroscopy of exoplanets is to measure atmospheric properties such as the abundances of chemical species. In this new era of high data quality from JWST, it is important to ensure that the methods we use to make these measurements are robust. When comparing atmospheric models to observations, it is common for multiple candidate models to produce a reasonable fit to the observed data. The variations in these models may be a result of different treatments of of atmospheric properties such as clouds and hazes, or different codes with small variations in their model implementation approaches. Typically, final conclusions are reached by selecting the best performing model according to some metric. However, this approach ignores model uncertainty in favour of specific assumptions made by the chosen model. In this paper, we compare three different ensemble methods for dealing with model uncertainty by combining posterior distributions from multiple retrievals: Bayesian model averaging, a variant of Bayesian model averaging using the leave-one-out predictive density, and stacking of predictive distributions. We demonstrate each of these methods by conducting a suite of retrievals on the HST+Spitzer transmission spectrum of HD~209458b, using models with a range of cloud and haze prescriptions. We show that our ensemble methods lead to larger but more realistic uncertainties on retrieved parameters that account for inherent model uncertainty. We recommend stacking as the most robust method for combining retrieval results.

[8] 将模型不确定性纳入系外行星大气分析的方法

系外行星透射光谱的主要目标之一是测量大气性质，如化学组分的丰度。在JWST带来的高质量数据新时代，确保我们用于这些测量的方法具有稳健性非常重要。在将大气模型与观测数据进行比较时，通常会有多个候选模型能够较好地拟合观测数据。这些模型之间的差异可能源于对大气属性（如云和薄雾）的不同处理，或是模型实现方式上的细微差别。通常，最终结论是通过根据某种指标选择表现最好的模型得出的。然而，这种方法忽略了模型不确定性，而偏向于所选模型的特定假设。本文比较了三种不同的集成方法，通过结合多个检索的后验分布来处理模型不确定性：贝叶斯模型平均、基于留一法预测密度的贝叶斯模型平均变体，以及预测分布的堆叠。我们通过对HD 209458 b的HST+Spitzer透射光谱进行一系列检索，采用不同的云和薄雾参数化模型，演示了这些方法。我们展示了集成方法会导致检索参数的不确定性增大，但这种不确定性更为真实，能够反映固有的模型不确定性。我们推荐堆叠作为结合检索结果最稳健的方法。

outcome — 这个问题后来怎么样了？ / what happened to the question?
premise_status — 它依赖的那个假设怎么样了？ / what happened to its underlying assumption?
confidence 信心度（1 没把握 / 2 一般 / 3 很有把握）
notes 备注（选填；犹豫在哪里、为什么这么判）
item_087 · sys_C
Do the HST transmission data independently support NH3 or HCN in HD 209458 b, and how does the evidence for each species change under cloud-free, patchy-cloud, and fully cloudy retrieval scenarios?
HST透射数据是否独立支持HD 209458 b中NH3或HCN的存在，在无云、斑块云和全云检索情景下对每种分子的证据有何变化？
8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] Four HD 209458 b transits through CRIRES+: Detection of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and non-detections of C<SUB>2</SUB>H<SUB>2</SUB>, CH<SUB>4</SUB>, and HCN

2024-10-00

Context. HD 209458 b is one of the most studied exoplanets to date. Despite this, atmospheric characterisation studies yielded inconsistent species detections and abundances. Values reported for the C/O ratio range from ≍0.1 to 1.0. Of particular interest is the simultaneous detection of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and HCN reported by some studies using high-resolution ground-based observations, which would require the atmospheric C/O ratio to be fine-tuned to a narrow interval around 1. HCN has however not been detected from recent space-based observations. Aims. We aim to provide an independent study of HD 209458 b's atmosphere with high-resolution observations, in order to infer the presence of several species, including H<SUB>2</SUB>O and HCN. Methods. We observed four primary transits of HD 209458 b at a high resolution (ℛ ≍ 92000) with CRIRES+ in the near infrared (band H, 1.431243─1.837253 μm). After reducing the data with pycrires, we prepared the data using the SysRem algorithm and performed a cross-correlation (CCF) analysis of the transmission spectra. We also compared the results with those obtained from simulated datasets constructed by combining the Exo-REM self-consistent model with the petitRADTRANS package. Results. Combining the four transits, we detect H<SUB>2</SUB>O with a signal-to-noise CCF metric of 8.7σ. This corresponds to a signal emitted at K<SUB>p</SUB> = 151.3<SUB>−23.4</SUB><SUP>+31.1</SUP> km s<SUP>−1</SUP> and blueshifted by −6<SUB>−2</SUB><SUP>+1</SUP> km s<SUP>−1</SUP>, consistent with what is expected for HD 209458 b. We do not detect any other species among C<SUB>2</SUB>H<SUB>2</SUB>, CH<SUB>4</SUB>, CO, CO<SUB>2</SUB>, H<SUB>2</SUB>S, HCN, and NH<SUB>3</SUB>. Comparing this with our simulated datasets, this result is consistent with a C/O ratio of 0.1 and an opaque cloud top pressure of 50 Pa, at a 3 times solar metallicity. This would also be consistent with recent JWST observations. However, none of the simulated results obtained with a bulk C/O ratio of 0.8, a value suggested by previous studies using GIANO-B and CRIRES, are consistent with our observations.

[1] HD 209458 b的四次CRIRES+凌日观测：探测到H2O，未探测到C2H2、CH4和HCN

背景。HD 209458 b 是迄今为止研究最为深入的系外行星之一。尽管如此，大气特征研究在分子种类探测和丰度方面结果并不一致。已报道的 C/O 比值范围从约 0.1 到 1.0。特别值得关注的是，部分基于高分辨率地面观测的研究报告了 H2O 和 HCN 的同时探测，这要求大气 C/O 比值被精确调节到接近 1 的狭窄区间。然而，近期的空间观测并未探测到 HCN。目标。我们旨在利用高分辨率观测对 HD 209458 b 的大气进行独立研究，以推断包括 H2O 和 HCN 在内的多种分子的存在。方法。我们使用 CRIRES+ 在近红外（H 波段，1.431243─1.837253 μm）以高分辨率（ℛ ≍ 92000）观测了 HD 209458 b 的四次凌日。数据经 pycrires 处理后，采用 SysRem 算法进行预处理，并对透射光谱进行了互相关（CCF）分析。我们还将结果与结合 Exo-REM 自洽模型和 petitRADTRANS 软件包构建的模拟数据集进行了比较。结果。结合四次凌日观测，我们以 8.7σ 的信噪比 CCF 指标探测到 H2O。该信号对应于 Kp = 151.3−23.4+31.1 km s−1，蓝移 −6−2+1 km s−1，与 HD 209458 b 的预期一致。我们未探测到 C2H2、CH4、CO、CO2、H2S、HCN 和 NH3 等其他分子。与我们的模拟数据集比较，该结果与 C/O 比值为 0.1、云顶压强为 50 Pa、金属丰度为三倍太阳值的情形一致。这也与近期 JWST 观测结果相符。然而，采用 GIANO-B 和 CRIRES 先前研究建议的整体 C/O 比值 0.8 的所有模拟结果均与我们的观测不符。

[2] The strange case of Na I in the atmosphere of HD 209458 b. Reconciling low- and high-resolution spectroscopic observations

2022-01-00

<BR /> Aims: We aim to investigate the origin of the discrepant results reported in the literature about the presence of Na I in the atmosphere of HD 209458 b, based on low- and high-resolution transmission spectroscopy. <BR /> Methods: We generated synthetic planetary atmosphere models and we compared them with the transmission light curves and spectra observed in previous studies. Our models account for the stellar limb-darkening and Rossiter-McLaughlin (RM) effects, and contemplate various possible scenarios for the planetary atmosphere. <BR /> Results: We reconciled the discrepant results by identifying a range of planetary atmospheres that are consistent with previous low- and high-resolution spectroscopic observations. Either both datasets are interpreted as consistent with a total absence of Na I in the planetary atmosphere (with Hubble Space Telescope data being affected by limb darkening), or the terminator temperature of HD 209458 b has to have an upper limit of about 1000 K. In particular, we find that 1D transmission spectra with lower-than-equilibrium temperatures can also explain the previously reported detection of absorption signal at low resolution due to differential transit depth in adjacent bands, while the cores of the Na I D lines may be masked by the strong RM signal seen at high resolution. We also rule out high-altitude clouds, which would otherwise mask the absorption signal at low resolution, as the source of the discrepancies. <BR /> Conclusions: This work highlights the synergies between different observing techniques, specifically low- and high-resolution spectroscopy, to fully characterise transiting exoplanet systems.

[2] HD 209458 b 大气中 Na I 的奇异案例。协调低分辨率与高分辨率光谱观测

目标：我们旨在基于低分辨率和高分辨率透射光谱，研究文献中关于HD 209458 b大气中Na I存在性的不同结果的起因。方法：我们生成了合成的行星大气模型，并将其与先前研究中观测到的透射光变和光谱进行比较。我们的模型考虑了恒星边缘变暗和Rossiter-McLaughlin（RM）效应，并涵盖了行星大气的多种可能情景。结果：我们通过识别一系列与先前低分辨率和高分辨率光谱观测一致的行星大气，调和了这些不同的结果。两组数据都可以解释为行星大气中完全没有Na I（Hubble Space Telescope数据受边缘变暗影响），或者HD 209458 b的晨昏线温度必须有约1000 K的上限。特别地，我们发现，具有低于平衡温度的1D透射光谱也可以解释先前在低分辨率下报告的吸收信号探测，这是由于相邻波段凌日深度的差异，而Na I D线的线芯可能被高分辨率下强烈的RM信号所掩盖。我们还排除了高空云层作为导致低分辨率下吸收信号被掩盖的原因。结论：本工作强调了不同观测技术，特别是低分辨率和高分辨率光谱之间的协同作用，以全面刻画凌日系外行星系统。

[3] Transmission Spectroscopy for the Warm Sub-Neptune HD 3167c: Evidence for Molecular Absorption and a Possible High-metallicity Atmosphere

2021-01-00

We present a transmission spectrum for the warm (500-600 K) sub-Neptune HD 3167c obtained using the Hubble Space Telescope Wide Field Camera 3 infrared spectrograph. We combine these data, which span the 1.125-1.643 μm wavelength range, with broadband transit measurements made using Kepler/K2 (0.6-0.9 μm) and Spitzer/IRAC (4-5 μm). We find evidence for absorption by at least one of H<SUB>2</SUB>O, HCN, CO<SUB>2</SUB>, and CH<SUB>4</SUB> (Bayes factor 7.4; 2.5σ significance), although the data precision does not allow us to unambiguously discriminate between these molecules. The transmission spectrum rules out cloud-free hydrogen-dominated atmospheres with metallicities ≤100× solar at &gt;5.8σ confidence. In contrast, good agreement with the data is obtained for cloud-free models assuming metallicities &gt;700× solar. However, for retrieval analyses that include the effect of clouds, a much broader range of metallicities (including subsolar) is consistent with the data, due to the degeneracy with cloud-top pressure. Self-consistent chemistry models that account for photochemistry and vertical mixing are presented for the atmosphere of HD 3167c. The predictions of these models are broadly consistent with our abundance constraints, although this is primarily due to the large uncertainties on the latter. Interior structure models suggest that the core mass fraction is &gt;40%, independent of a rock or water core composition, and independent of atmospheric envelope metallicity up to 1000× solar. We also report abundance measurements for 15 elements in the host star, showing that it has a very nearly solar composition.

[3] 温暖亚海王星 HD 3167c 的透射光谱：分子吸收证据及可能的高金属丰度大气

我们利用哈勃空间望远镜宽视场相机3红外光谱仪获得了温暖（500-600 K）亚海王星HD 3167c的透射光谱。我们将这些覆盖1.125-1.643 μm波长范围的数据与Kepler/K2（0.6-0.9 μm）和Spitzer/IRAC（4-5 μm）的宽带凌星测量结果结合。我们发现至少有H2O、HCN、CO2和CH4中的一种分子存在吸收特征（Bayes因子7.4；2.5σ显著性），但数据精度不足以明确区分这些分子。透射光谱排除了金属丰度≤100倍太阳的无云氢主导大气（置信度>5.8σ）。相比之下，假设金属丰度>700倍太阳的无云模型与数据高度一致。然而，在包含云影响的检索分析中，由于与云顶压力的简并性，数据与更宽范围（金属丰度包括亚太阳值）的模型均相容。我们还给出了考虑光化学和垂直混合的自洽化学模型，这些模型的预测与我们的丰度约束大致一致，尽管这主要归因于后者的不确定性较大。内部结构模型表明，无论核心为岩石还是水成分，且大气包层金属丰度高达1000倍太阳，核心质量分数均大于40%。我们还报告了母星15种元素的丰度测量，显示其成分几乎与太阳相同。

[4] Helium Atmospheres May Hide in Current Exoplanet Analysis Frameworks

2026-01-00

The increasing number of detailed exoplanet observations offers an opportunity to refine our analyses and interpretations. Here, we show that atmospheres that appear volatile-rich and/or cloudy may instead be helium-rich. As transmission spectra constrain the atmospheric scale height (H), a He-enriched atmosphere can be misinterpreted as H<SUB>2</SUB>-dominated water-rich to bring the mean molecular weight (μ) to intermediate values (∼4−10) when He/H<SUB>2</SUB> is fixed. Similarly, a cloud deck can reduce the spectral features, and thus the apparent (i.e., cloud-free equivalent) H. We present a proof-of-concept reanalysis of HD 209458 b's JWST transmission spectrum treating He/H<SUB>2</SUB> as a free parameter, resulting in sets of He-rich solutions. We argue that He enhancement must be considered to reliably constrain atmospheric composition, be sensitive to a more diverse planetary population, and ultimately yield robust trends to inform formation and evolution pathways. Looking ahead, we suggest leveraging insights from differences in pressure-broadening effects, outflow measurements, and atmospheric chemistry to disentangle reliably between He-, volatile-rich, and cloudy atmospheres—while recognizing that associated models need targeted upgrades to reach the fidelity level required to this end.

[4] 氦大气可能隐藏在当前系外行星分析框架中

越来越多的系外行星详细观测为我们改进分析和解释提供了机会。本文展示了那些看似富含挥发物和/或多云的大气，实际上可能是富含He的大气。由于透射光谱约束了大气的尺度高度（H），当He/H2固定时，He富集的大气可能会被误解为H2主导的富水大气，从而使平均分子量（μ）达到中间值（约4−10）。类似地，云层也会减弱光谱特征，从而降低表观（即无云等效）H。我们以HD 209458 b的JWST透射光谱为例，进行概念验证性再分析，将He/H2作为自由参数，得到了多组富He解。我们认为，必须考虑He的增强，才能可靠约束大气组成，对更为多样的行星族群保持敏感，并最终获得稳健的趋势以指导形成和演化路径。展望未来，我们建议利用压强展宽效应差异、外流测量和大气化学等方面的见解，来可靠区分He富、富挥发物和多云大气——同时认识到相关模型需要有针对性地升级，以达到所需的精度水平。

[5] Revised chemistry of HD209458 b

2022-07-00

We characterize the atmosphere of the iconic giant planet HD209458 b based on a joint modeling effort of the available observations. <P />HD 209458 b is the first planet with a reported detection of a chemical species in its atmosphere, namely Na I, and also the one with the largest number of reported species (H I, O I, C II, Mg I, Fe II, Ca I, Sc I, H2O, CO, HCN, CH4, C2H2 and NH3). However, recent studies casted doubts on the authenticity of some detections. Most of the discrepant results are based on data obtained with different observing techniques, especially using low- and high-resolution spectroscopy. We performed a joint analysis of archive data to exploit the synergies between multiple observing techniques. <P />We reconciled discrepant results reported in the literature by identifying a range of planetary atmospheres that are consistent with both low- and high-resolution spectroscopic observations. The joint analysis enabled us to place tighter constraints on the chemical composition and physical state of the HD209458 b atmosphere. We discuss our findings providing an updated picture of this planetary system.

[5] HD209458 b的化学成分修订

我们基于对现有观测数据的联合建模，对标志性巨行星HD 209458 b的大气进行了刻画。 HD 209458 b是首个在其大气中探测到化学物质（Na I）的行星，也是已报道物种数量最多的行星（H I、O I、C II、Mg I、Fe II、Ca I、Sc I、H2O、CO、HCN、CH4、C2H2和NH3）。然而，近期研究对部分探测结果的真实性提出了质疑。大多数存在分歧的结果基于不同观测技术获得的数据，尤其是低分辨率和高分辨率光谱。我们对档案数据进行了联合分析，以发挥多种观测技术的协同作用。 我们通过识别一系列同时与低分辨率和高分辨率光谱观测一致的行星大气，调和了文献中报道的分歧结果。联合分析使我们能够对HD 209458 b大气的化学组成和物理状态施加更严格的约束。我们讨论了这些发现，并给出了该行星系统的最新图景。

[7] The Watery Atmosphere of HD 209458 b Revealed by Joint K- and L-band High-resolution Spectroscopy

2025-10-00

We present a joint analysis of high-resolution K- and L-band observations of the benchmark hot Jupiter HD 209458 b from the Keck Planet Imager and Characterizer. One half-night of observations was obtained in each bandpass, covering similar preeclipse phases. The two epochs were then jointly analyzed using our atmospheric retrieval pipeline based on petitRADTRANS to constrain the atmospheric pressure─temperature profile and chemical composition. Consistent with recent results from JWST observations at lower spectral resolution, we obtain an oxygen-rich composition for HD 209458 b (C/O &lt; 10<SUP>−3</SUP> at 95% confidence) and a lower limit on the volatile metallicity similar to the solar value ([(C + O)/H] &gt; −0.2 at 95% confidence). Leveraging the large spectral grasp of the multiband observations, we constrain the atmospheric H<SUB>2</SUB>O mixing ratio to <inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:mi>log</mml:mi><mml:msub><mml:mi>H</mml:mi><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:msub><mml:mi>O</mml:mi><mml:mi>V MR</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>&gt;</mml:mo><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>3.1</mml:mn></mml:math> </inline-formula> at 95% confidence, and obtain 95% upper limits on the atmospheric mixing ratios of CO (&lt;10<SUP>−4.8</SUP>), CH<SUB>4</SUB> (&lt;10<SUP>−4.5</SUP>), NH<SUB>3</SUB> (&lt;10<SUP>−5.8</SUP>), H<SUB>2</SUB>S (&lt;10<SUP>−3.3</SUP>), and HCN (&lt;10<SUP>−5.6</SUP>). The limits on CH<SUB>4</SUB>, NH<SUB>3</SUB>, and HCN are consistent with recent results from JWST transmission spectroscopy, demonstrating the value of multiband, ground-based high-resolution spectroscopy for precisely constraining trace-species abundances in exoplanet atmospheres. The retrieved low-C/O, moderate-metallicity composition for HD 209458 b is consistent with formation scenarios involving late accretion of substantial quantities of oxygen-rich refractory solids and/or ices.

[7] 通过联合K波段和L波段高分辨率光谱揭示HD 209458 b的富水大气

我们对基准热木星 HD 209458 b 的 Keck Planet Imager and Characterizer 获得的高分辨率 K 波段和 L 波段观测进行了联合分析。每个波段各获得了半夜的观测，覆盖了类似的凌前相位。随后，我们利用基于 petitRADTRANS 的大气检索流程对这两个历元的数据进行了联合分析，以约束大气的压强-温度剖面和化学组成。与近期 JWST 低光谱分辨率观测结果一致，我们获得了 HD 209458 b 的富氧组成（C/O < 10^−3，95%置信度）和与太阳值相当的挥发性金属丰度下限（[(C + O)/H] > −0.2，95%置信度）。借助多波段观测的广泛波长覆盖，我们将大气 H2O 混合比约束为 log(H2O_VMR) > −3.1（95%置信度），并获得了 CO（<10^−4.8）、CH4（<10^−4.5）、NH3（<10^−5.8）、H2S（<10^−3.3）和 HCN（<10^−5.6）的大气混合比 95% 上限。CH4、NH3 和 HCN 的上限与近期 JWST 透射光谱结果一致，显示多波段地基高分辨率光谱在精确约束系外行星大气微量组分丰度方面的价值。HD 209458 b 检索得到的低 C/O、中等金属丰度组成与其形成过程中晚期吸积大量富氧难挥发固体和/或冰的情景一致。

[8] Comb Spectroscopy for the Critical Evaluation of Molecular Line Lists: The Astrophysical Molecule HCN

2021-00-00

Hydrogen cyanide (HCN) has been observed in dense interstellar clouds, star-forming regions, comets, and exoplanetary atmospheres. When present with its isomer hydrogen isocyanide (HNC), their respective abundances act as indicators of local temperature and chemistry, and HNC is suggested as a tracer to identify proto-brown dwarfs. Furthermore, their relative abundances and isotopic composition are useful chemical clocks for star formation. Each of these areas of astrophysical exploration relies upon spectroscopic reference data to dependably model observations. Here we will propose a critical experimental evaluation of a newly published ab initio line list for HCN. The results will give confidence to conclusions inferred from infrared and microwave observations. Proposed work will include the accurate measurement of transition intensities across the 1-5 µm wavelength region, including of hot bands and stable isotopic substitutions (e.g., H13CN) using direct frequency comb spectroscopy. Uniquely, we propose to rigorously compare interferometric and dispersive comb spectroscopy methods (dual-comb spectroscopy and cross-dispersed comb spectroscopy) for their utility in providing accurate spectral reference data. Furthermore, we will propose new measurements of H2 and He pressure-broadening coefficients to resolve a discrepancy in the literature. Preliminary measurements at NIST using dual-comb spectroscopy at a wavelength of 1.55 µm suggest good agreement between experiment and theory, but broadband laser-based measurements will be proposed to benchmark theory across a wide range of relevant conditions. Finally, we will explore spectroscopic measurements of HNC at high temperatures. An experimentally validated line list will directly service several NASA missions, including SOFIA, TESS, and JWST. The results will establish observational consistency across the electromagnetic spectrum (e.g., between JWST and ALMA) by ensuring an accurate HCN/HNC potential energy surface and dipole moment surface ─ both of which are required to model radiative transfer in extreme astrophysical environments.

[8] 梳状光谱法对分子谱线列表的关键评估：天体物理分子 HCN

氢氰酸（HCN）已在致密的星际云、恒星形成区、彗星和系外行星大气中被观测到。当其异构体异氰酸氢（HNC）同时存在时，它们各自的丰度可作为局部温度和化学环境的指示器，且HNC被建议作为识别原棕矮星的示踪物。此外，它们的相对丰度和同位素组成是恒星形成的有用化学时钟。这些天体物理探索领域都依赖于可靠的光谱参考数据来对观测进行建模。本文将提出对新近发表的HCN从头算谱线表进行关键实验评估。结果将增强基于红外和微波观测所推断结论的可信度。拟开展的工作包括利用直接频率梳光谱法，精确测量1-5 µm波长区间内的跃迁强度，包括热带和稳定同位素取代（如H13CN）。本项目的独特之处在于，将严格比较干涉型和色散型梳状光谱方法（双梳光谱和交叉色散梳光谱）在提供精确光谱参考数据方面的实用性。此外，我们将提出对H2和He压致展宽系数的新测量，以解决文献中的分歧。NIST利用双梳光谱在1.55 µm波长的初步测量显示实验与理论吻合良好，但我们将提出采用宽带激光测量以在更广泛的相关条件下校准理论。最后，我们将探索高温下HNC的光谱测量。经实验验证的谱线表将直接服务于包括SOFIA、TESS和JWST在内的多个NASA任务。结果将通过确保HCN/HNC势能面和偶极矩面精确，为极端天体物理环境中的辐射传输建模提供必要条件，从而在整个电磁波段（如JWST与ALMA之间）建立观测一致性。

outcome — 这个问题后来怎么样了？ / what happened to the question?
premise_status — 它依赖的那个假设怎么样了？ / what happened to its underlying assumption?
confidence 信心度（1 没把握 / 2 一般 / 3 很有把握）
notes 备注（选填；犹豫在哪里、为什么这么判）
